**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

More pain and suffering, for sure. After all, you taught me to appreciate the finer things.

## `ZagreusHome_0014`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good talk...? Good talk.

打住...？聊不下去了。

## `ZagreusHome_0015`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You'd like that, wouldn't you? Lucky I've got other family who believe in me.

**中文**

你是想那样的吧？所幸我有另一家人信任我。

## `ZagreusHome_0016b`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No wonder they don't like you.

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_0017`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

In that case, I'll spare you the details.

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_0018`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh, I will.

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_0019`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's all right, Father. I'm not feeling very hungry at the moment.

**中文**

没事，父亲。我现在还不饿。

## `ZagreusHome_0020`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You've taught me everything I know. Other than how to fend for myself, I owe Achilles that.

**中文**

我所知道的都是你教会我的。除了如何保护自己，这是阿喀琉斯的功劳。

## `ZagreusHome_0021a`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father, everybody's dead. Give them a break.

**中文**

父亲，大家都是死人了。您就饶了他们吧。

## `ZagreusHome_0022`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I had it out with them already, thanks.

**中文**

我跟她们交过手了，谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_0023`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Wish I could be there to see your face.

**中文**

多么希望当时也在场，可以看到你的表情。

## `ZagreusHome_0024b`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No need to drag the dog into this, Father.

**中文**

别把狗也扯进来，父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_0025b`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm standing right here, Father.

**中文**

我回来了，父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_0026`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

At least he likes the dog.

**中文**

至少他喜欢狗。

## `ZagreusHome_0027`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't know how you can stand it.

**中文**

我都不知道您是怎么熬过来的。

## `ZagreusHome_0028`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Really...? I mean, oh, wait, you're not serious. That famous sense of humor shining through.

**中文**

真的吗…？我是说，等下，原来你开玩笑呢。这强大的幽默感真是让我猝不及防。

## `ZagreusHome_0029`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm going to find Mother. No matter how long it takes me.

**中文**

我要去找母亲。不管要花多久。

## `ZagreusHome_0030`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

He's fast asleep. How long has he been up...? Whatever, I should go.

**中文**

他倒头就睡。有多长时间他是醒着的...？管他呢，我该走了。

## `ZagreusHome_0031`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You keep an eye on Father for me, boy. Don't let him sour up everyone's mood.

**中文**

帮我看好父亲噢，乖乖。可别让他坏了大家的情绪。

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

**EN**

Ready to face the Underworld...

你好，帅哥。

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All set.

Good to go.

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Excellent.

**中文**

太棒了。

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No time to rest.

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_0078`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

If I sit down I won't want to get back up.

**中文**

一坐下来，我就不想站起来了。

## `ZagreusHome_0079`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not as comfy as it looks.

**中文**

没看起来那么舒服。

## `ZagreusHome_0080`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, wait, you're giving me your Codex? I can't accept something like this!

**中文**

等下，你要把你的手稿给我？这我真的受不起！

## `ZagreusHome_0081`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...This is the cheeriest thing I think I've heard from him.

**中文**

...这是我听过的关于他的最令人高兴的事情。

## `ZagreusHome_0082`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Now that you've started singing once again, I figured it was thirst-inducing work! Your music stirs so many souls around here, mate.

**中文**

因为你又开始歌唱了，所以我就觉得你应该会特别容易口渴！朋友，你的音乐打动了这儿的许多灵魂啊。

## `ZagreusHome_0083`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

So mate, you know Eurydice? Well I just happened to run into her out there. Asked me to let you know that she can always hear your songs, no matter how far apart you are. Seemed rather sweet of her, I have to say.

**中文**

这么说，你认识欧律狄克？对了，我上次碰巧遇到她了。让我告诉你，她总是听得到你的歌声，无论和你相隔多远。我不得不说，她真的很贴心。

## `ZagreusHome_0084`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Don't worry, Meg. I eventually managed to get home the painful way after all.

**中文**

没事，墨纪，反正后来我又惨死回家了。

## `ZagreusHome_0085`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Smack guys against walls. Sound advice as ever, Skelly.

**中文**

把人往墙上撞，听起来是不错的建议，骨头。

## `ZagreusHome_0086`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Look for the perfect opportunity to unleash my much-feared temper. Check.

**中文**

在最完美的时机释放我最骇人的怒气。没问题。

## `ZagreusHome_0087`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Happy to be of service there, now you just keep watching that big old pool of blood for us, all right?

**中文**

很荣幸为你效劳，现在你就替我们看着那片大血泊，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0088`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'll give them hell as you once did, Achilles. Though, doubtless many of the shades who stand against me first arrived in Tartarus because of you.

**中文**

我会像你一样让他们好看的，阿喀琉斯。毫无疑问，很多那些在塔耳塔罗斯对抗我的暗灵们都是因为你才在那里的。

## `ZagreusHome_0089`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, do you know anything about a softly-spoken gentlebeing under the guise of Chaos? They approached me recently, and offered me a Boon, of sorts.

**中文**

倪克斯，你认识一位轻声细语说话，名为卡俄斯的吗？最近祂开始接近我，给了我个另类的祝福。

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Whoops.

**中文**

哎呀。

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Whoops!

哎呀！

Erm sorry...!

呃，抱歉...！

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh must've slipped.

**中文**

哦，肯定是忘记了。

Go away.

走开。

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

Out of my way.

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_0096`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Shoo already.

**中文**

躲远点。

## `ZagreusHome_0097`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Out of my sight.

**中文**

离我远点。

## `ZagreusHome_0098`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Piss off.

**中文**

滚。

## `ZagreusHome_0099`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Buhhh....

**中文**

嗷...

## `ZagreusHome_0100`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I... can't get used to that.

**中文**

我...没办法接受。

## `ZagreusHome_0101`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Brrr...

**中文**

咝...

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well...

好了...

## `ZagreusHome_0103`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well, time to go get killed again.

**中文**

嗯，该接着去送死了。

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sigh....

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_0105`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Night and Darkness, guide me.

**中文**

暮夜和黑暗，为我指明方向。

## `ZagreusHome_0106`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

There.

**中文**

那里。

## `ZagreusHome_0107`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you Nyx.

**中文**

谢谢你，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0108`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right.

**中文**

好吧。

## `ZagreusHome_0109`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How dare you, sir.

**中文**

你好大的胆呀，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_0110`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

For you, mate! Thought you might get a kick out of this.

**中文**

给你的，伙计。我觉得你应该会喜欢这个东西。

## `ZagreusHome_0111`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Ms. Dusa? I wanted you to have this. It's nothing really, just... for your hard work.

**中文**

嗯，杜莎小姐？我想给你这个，不是什么值钱的东西，就当……是你辛勤工作的奖励吧。

## `ZagreusHome_0112`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Look what I got for you, boy...!

**中文**

看看我给你带了什么，乖乖…！

## `ZagreusHome_0113`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, uh, Father. I thought, maybe, you know, here.

**中文**

呃，父亲。我觉得，也许，这个，给。

## `ZagreusHome_0114`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Surprise, Hypnos! I got you this.

**中文**

惊喜，修普诺斯！我给你带了这个。

## `ZagreusHome_0115`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, mate, here. I got this for my favorite court musician.

**中文**

嘿，朋友，来。给我最喜欢的宫廷乐师。

## `ZagreusHome_0116`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It isn't much, Than, but I got you something.

**中文**

虽然不是很多，塔纳，但是我有东西要给你。

## `ZagreusHome_0117`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Meg! I, uh, thought I'd better hand this over.

**中文**

嘿，墨纪！我，呃，我觉得最好把它上交给你。

## `ZagreusHome_0118`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Nyx? You've done so much for me, I.. thought you might like this.

**中文**

呃，倪克斯？你为我做了这么多，我……希望这个你能喜欢。

## `ZagreusHome_0119`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey Achilles, look this really isn't much, but... here.

**中文**

嗨阿喀琉斯，虽然不是什么好东西……给你的。

## `ZagreusHome_0120`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, sir, thought this might ease your burden for a while.

**中文**

嘿，先生，我觉得这个可以让你暂时减轻负担。

## `ZagreusHome_0121`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Lord Zeus, erm, Uncle? Please accept this offering, and all my gratitude.

**中文**

众神之王宙斯，嗯，叔叔？请收下这件贡品，以及我的感激之情。

## `ZagreusHome_0122`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Uncle Poseidon, it's been excellent to know you, sir, so, please, this is for you.

**中文**

波塞冬叔叔，很高兴认识您，先生，所以，这是孝敬您的，请笑纳。

## `ZagreusHome_0123`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm indebted to you, Goddess, for all that you and your side of the family have done for me.

**中文**

我很感激你，女神，你和你的家人为我所做的一切。

## `ZagreusHome_0124`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Lady Artemis, I hope that you'll accept this offering, for all your kindness and support.

**中文**

阿尔忒弥斯女士，我希望你能接受这份礼物，谢谢你的好意和支持。

## `ZagreusHome_0125`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Lady Aphrodite, I am truly blessed simply to have made your acquaintance, so, this is for you.

**中文**

阿弗洛狄忒女士，我真的很荣幸认识你，所以，这是给你的。

## `ZagreusHome_0126`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Lord Ares, please accept this offering, for giving me such strength as necessary to succeed.

**中文**

阿瑞斯大人，感谢您赐予我成功所需的力量，这份薄礼请笑纳。

## `ZagreusHome_0127`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Lord Dionysus, it's been great getting to know you, so, please accept this humble offering.

**中文**

迪奥尼索司大人，很高兴认识你，所以，请接受这份谦卑的礼物。

## `ZagreusHome_0128`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Master Chaos, forebear of the gods, it is an honor to have met you. Please, accept this gift.

**中文**

卡俄斯君主，众神之祖，很荣幸认识您。请收下这个礼物。

## `ZagreusHome_0129`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's see here.

**中文**

我们在这边看看。

## `ZagreusHome_0130`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Keepsakes hold power.

**中文**

纪念品封存着力量。

## `ZagreusHome_0131`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nothing here yet.

**中文**

这里什么也没有。

## `ZagreusHome_0132`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Which to choose.

**中文**

选哪个。

## `ZagreusHome_0133`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Which one.

**中文**

哪个。

## `ZagreusHome_0134`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A new addition to the old collection.

**中文**

旧藏品的新成员。

## `ZagreusHome_0135`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Go with that one.

**中文**

选那个。

## `ZagreusHome_0136`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All set.

**中文**

一切就绪。

## `ZagreusHome_0137`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Might help.

**中文**

可能有帮助。

## `ZagreusHome_0138`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Need to find a key.

**中文**

需要找到钥匙。

## `ZagreusHome_0139`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Need a key.

**中文**

需要钥匙。

## `ZagreusHome_0140`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's sealed shut.

**中文**

它被锁死了。

## `ZagreusHome_0141`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Can't open this yet.

**中文**

现在打不开。

## `ZagreusHome_0142`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Locked.

**中文**

被锁住了。

## `ZagreusHome_0143`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

There we go!

**中文**

快看！

## `ZagreusHome_0144`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Open up.

**中文**

打开。

## `ZagreusHome_0145`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That should do it.

**中文**

应该可以了。

## `ZagreusHome_0146`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can do better than that.

**中文**

我可以做得更好。

## `ZagreusHome_0147`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Like to think I learned something back there.

**中文**

觉得在那里学到不少好东西。

## `ZagreusHome_0148`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ouch, all right, again?

**中文**

哎呀，又来？

## `ZagreusHome_0149`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just have to try again.

**中文**

必须得再试一次。

## `ZagreusHome_0150`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Got to keep at it.

**中文**

不能放弃。

## `ZagreusHome_0151`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

One more time.

**中文**

再一次。

## `ZagreusHome_0152`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's go.

**中文**

快点。

## `ZagreusHome_0153`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here we go.

**中文**

我们走。

## `ZagreusHome_0154`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Out we go.

**中文**

我们出去。

## `ZagreusHome_0155`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No turning back.

**中文**

没有回头路。

## `ZagreusHome_0156`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Bye, everyone.

**中文**

再见，各位。

## `ZagreusHome_0157`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Darkness, give me strength.

**中文**

黑暗，给我力量。

## `ZagreusHome_0158`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Night, give me strength.

**中文**

暮夜，给我力量。

## `ZagreusHome_0159`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Darkness is power.

**中文**

黑暗就是力量。

## `ZagreusHome_0160`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Night and Darkness.

**中文**

暮夜和黑暗。

## `ZagreusHome_0161`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're not so bad yourself, mate. Thanks for always being here for me to savagely attack without a second thought.

**中文**

你也不那么坏，伙计。感谢你一直以来承受我毫无顾忌的暴打。

## `ZagreusHome_0162`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm really giving this to you, mate. Now go enjoy it, will you? Once I've finished pummeling you some more just for the sake of it.

**中文**

真的是给你的，伙计。你就收下吧，好吗？实在不行，我先揍你一顿，然后你好好收下可以不。

## `ZagreusHome_0163`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No need to thank me, mate. But don't you dare forget it or I'll attack you repeatedly and without mercy.

**中文**

不用谢我，伙计。但是你要是忘记了，我就要狠狠地揍你，天天揍你！

## `ZagreusHome_0164`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't remember having you on payroll, mate. Who's your supervisor?

**中文**

我不记得地府有你这号人啊，伙计？哪个单位的？

## `ZagreusHome_0165`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

If you insist...!

**中文**

如果你坚持的话...！

## `ZagreusHome_0166`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Smack guys against walls. Sound advice as ever, mate.

**中文**

把敌人打到墙上去。似乎有点道理，伙计。

## `ZagreusHome_0167`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thanks, mate, note to self: Always spend everything I earn instead of ever saving.

**中文**

谢了，伙计，我记着：路上拾到的所有东西，只花不攒。

## `ZagreusHome_0168`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dash like mad instead of standing still. That's not the worst advice, I guess.

**中文**

到处乱冲比傻站着强！我觉得这建议还算可以吧。

## `ZagreusHome_0169`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have to ask you something, mate. What do you even need the pay for, anyway?

**中文**

我一直想问，伙计，你要那些钱来干嘛呢？

## `ZagreusHome_0170`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Those two are at it again. I'd best stay out of this.

**中文**

那两个又在瞎折腾，我最好离得远点。

## `ZagreusHome_0171`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Appreciate the vote of confidence, mate. Maybe you're right and someday I'll get out of here. Hey, if I never come back, you won't just be stuck here forever, will you?

**中文**

感谢你的支持，伙计。也许你是对的，总有一天我会逃出这里。呃，如果我不回来了，你不会一直永远留在这里吧？

## `ZagreusHome_0172`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Come on, mate, won't you say who hired you? Was it Achilles? Couldn't have been Nyx...? Is it that big a deal?

**中文**

来吧，伙计，跟我说说到底是谁雇你来的？是阿喀琉斯吗？难道是倪克斯……？老纠结着不愿说干嘛？

## `ZagreusHome_0173`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Looks like these two are at it again. I'd better stay out of this.

**中文**

...那两个好像又在瞎折腾，我最好离得远点。

## `ZagreusHome_0174`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Looks like these two are busy making friends. I'd better stay out of this.

**中文**

...那两个好像在忙着交朋友，我最好离得远点。

## `ZagreusHome_0175`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mischief, me? I was just going to have a little look through Father's stuff.

**中文**

恶作剧，我？我只是想看看父亲都藏了哪些好东西。

## `ZagreusHome_0176`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Lower your voice, old man, I'm trying to be sneaky here...!

**中文**

小声点，老头。没见我在这边蹑手蹑脚的吗...！

## `ZagreusHome_0177`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I said, shut up, old man!

**中文**

我说了，闭嘴，老头！

## `ZagreusHome_0178`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

His mother's hand, my mother's hand, wait, what?! You're saying this Persephone, that she's my... mother...? But, but Father always said that Nyx was my.... That liar. He lied to me, they both did. All my life!

**中文**

母亲写下的字条，母亲写下的，等等，你说什么？！你是说，这个叫珀尔塞福涅的人，她是……我母亲……？可，可我父亲一直说，说倪克斯才是我的……那个骗子。他骗我，他们两个都在骗我，把我蒙在鼓里！

## `ZagreusHome_0179`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, uh, what's that you're drinking, Meg? Anything good?

**中文**

嘿，呃，你在喝什么呢，墨纪？好喝吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0180`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nice! Well, I'll just leave you to it, then.

**中文**

好的！那么，我就不打扰你啦。

## `ZagreusHome_0181`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What are your sisters up to these days, Meg? I've not seen them around.

**中文**

你的姐妹们最近都在干什么呢，墨纪？都没看到她们。

## `ZagreusHome_0182`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I was just checking up on you, just... let me know if you wanted to talk, for any reason.

**中文**

我就是来看看你，就是…如果你想找人说话的话，告诉我就行了。

## `ZagreusHome_0183`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's nothing, I just thought I'd say, that was well fought back there.

**中文**

没事，我就是想说，刚刚真是一场激烈的战斗呢。

## `ZagreusHome_0184`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Um, Meg...?

**中文**

呃，墨纪...？

## `ZagreusHome_0185`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

There's a good boy... thought you'd like that!

**中文**

乖狗狗…我就知道你喜欢！

## `ZagreusHome_0186`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Only the very best treats for my best friend, right, boy?

**中文**

最好的东西只给最好的朋友，对吧，乖乖？

## `ZagreusHome_0187`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You... you're... Nyx, you're not my mother. All this time, you and my father, you... you lied to me?

**中文**

你…你……倪克斯，你不是我母亲。这么长时间以来，你和我父亲，你们…在骗我？

## `ZagreusHome_0188`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx... I have to ask you something. Are you certain that my search isn't in vain? My mother, what if... what if she's gone?

**中文**

倪克斯……我想问你个问题，你真的确定，我的寻找不是徒劳的吗？万一我的母亲，她……她已经不在了呢？

## `ZagreusHome_0189`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

She has, Nyx. I am grateful that you put us into contact, and explained to her the situation that I'm in.

**中文**

是的，尼克斯。很高兴您让我们有所联系，还向她解释了我的处境。

## `ZagreusHome_0190`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Can you tell me something more of Chaos, Nyx? I... have a lot of questions.

**中文**

能告诉我更多有关卡俄斯的事吗，倪克斯？我…有许多疑问。

## `ZagreusHome_0191`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Chaos seems to have taken somewhat of a liking to me, Nyx. They mentioned you.

**中文**

卡俄斯似乎有点喜欢我了，倪克斯。祂提到了你。

## `ZagreusHome_0192`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, I hate to be a bother, but I wanted to ask you about Chaos once again. Won't you at least try to reach out to them?

**中文**

倪克斯，我这样可能有点烦，但是我还是想再问一下有关卡俄斯的事。你最起码应该尝试一下与祂接触吧？

## `ZagreusHome_0194`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, sometimes I get the impression the Olympians look down on those of us born of the Underworld. Is that my imagination, or, what do you make of it?

**中文**

倪克斯，有时候我会觉得，奥林匹斯众神看不起我们这些冥界出身的人。这只是我的臆想吗，你怎么看呢？

## `ZagreusHome_0195`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, this may be an odd question, but... do you know what the Fates may have in store for me? They are your daughters, right?

**中文**

倪克斯，这个问题可能有点怪，但是……你知道命运之神打算对我做什么吗？毕竟她们可是您的女儿。

## `ZagreusHome_0196`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nonsense, Nyx. I want you to have it. You've always cared for me. I can't ever repay you for that.

**中文**

胡说，倪克斯。这就是给你的。你一直都很关心我。这我永远报答不完。

## `ZagreusHome_0197`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I think of you always, Nyx. You raised me as your own, and I am grateful for it.

**中文**

我永远记得你，倪克斯。你将我当作自己的孩子养大，我很感激。

## `ZagreusHome_0198`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Be that as it may, Nyx! I would still like for you to have it, with my compliments.

**中文**

请不要推脱了，倪克斯！我希望你可以收下这饱含我敬意的礼物。

## `ZagreusHome_0199`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Why, um, I'm just on my way right now, Dusa, but, some other time, for sure! I think I'd like that, really!

**中文**

为什么，我还在路上呢，杜莎。不过，改天一定可以！我也希望，真的！

## `ZagreusHome_0200`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dusa, please, there's no need to apologize! You've been doing a wonderful job, really!

**中文**

杜莎，说什么傻话呢！你的工作做得非常好，真的！

## `ZagreusHome_0201`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Ms. Dusa, I wanted to, uh, cordially invite you to an outing in the lounge? Remember when you asked me about a drink?

**中文**

嗯，杜莎小姐，我想，呃，我诚邀你与我一起去休息室转一转可以吗？还记得之前你问的饮料的事吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0202`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, mate! What do you say we share this over in the lounge?

**中文**

嘿，朋友！把这个东西在休息室里跟大家一起分享怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_0203`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Orpheus, forgive my bluntness here, but I have to ask. Why won't you sing? You were a legend, mate! Our court musician!

**中文**

俄耳甫斯，可能我有点太直白了，但我要问问你。你为什么不想唱歌？你可是个传奇人物啊，伙计！你是我们的宫廷乐师！

## `ZagreusHome_0204`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I, I suppose I have, yeah. But recently I found my drive again, and that's why I have to get out of here.

**中文**

我，我应该有吧。但是最近我又找回来了，这就是为什么我要离开这里。

## `ZagreusHome_0205`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Anything I can do to help you find your muse, mate?

**中文**

有什么我能帮忙的吗，帮你找到自己的缪斯？

## `ZagreusHome_0206`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, you found her? What happened?

**中文**

等等，你找到她了？然后呢？

## `ZagreusHome_0207`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's... I'm sorry to hear that. Thank you for letting me know. She sounds truly wonderful.

**中文**

真是…很抱歉听到这些。谢谢你让我知道。听上去她真的很不错。

## `ZagreusHome_0208`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mate, if you don't mind, I'd love it if you told me more about your muse. Eurydice.

**中文**

朋友，如果你不介意的话，我想听你说说更多你的缪斯的事，欧律狄克。

## `ZagreusHome_0209`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's quite a few. She sounds like she was a real catch all right. I'm sorry for your loss. Thank you for sharing your memory of her with me.

**中文**

那还蛮多的。听上去她确实是一个不错的人。很抱歉你失去了她。感谢你告诉我你与她的事。

## `ZagreusHome_0210`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Inspiring enough to... make you want to sing, again?

**中文**

这点灵感足够……让你再展歌喉吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0211`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Say, Orpheus? I ever tell you I once single-handedly slew a multi-headed bone hydra within the magma fields of Asphodel without so much as breaking a sweat?

**中文**

喂，俄耳甫斯，我有没有告诉过你，有一次我在水仙花平原的岩浆原野中只身一人杀死了一只骸骨九头蛇，气都不带喘的。

## `ZagreusHome_0212`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Orpheus here believes absolutely everything. At first I thought it mean, telling tall tales to him, but it seems to be the only way to cheer him up.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯什么都信。一开始我以为跟他吹牛不太好，可后来发现，这是唯一能让他高兴的方法。

## `ZagreusHome_0213`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey Orpheus? You know about the time I vanquished seven legions of Bloodless warriors with both hands tied behind my back while hopping on one foot? Lord Poseidon dared me to do it, you know.

**中文**

嗨俄耳甫斯？你知不知道，有一回我双手反绑，单脚跳着击败了七个军团的无血僵尸战士？波塞冬大人让我这样做的，你知道吗。

## `ZagreusHome_0214`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Orpheus? I take it you know all about the time I burned my father's hands to a crisp the moment I was born? He grasped me by the feet, and they caught fire! And that's why he still wears those fancy rings: to cover up the hideous scar tissue.

**中文**

嘿俄耳甫斯？你知不知道我出生的时候把我父亲的手都烧糊了？他抓住了我的脚，然后我的脚也着火了！这就是为什么他现在还戴着那些漂亮的戒指环，是为了遮住伤疤呢。

## `ZagreusHome_0215`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey Orpheus, you've noticed the resemblance between me and Dionysus, haven't you, mate? I'll tell you a little secret but you have to promise not to tell... We're really the same god. I love a good feast, don't I?

**中文**

俄耳甫斯，你应该已经注意到我和迪奥尼索司的相似之处了吧？我会告诉你一个小秘密，但是你要保证不要告诉别人…其实我们是同一个神。谁不喜欢丰盛宴席呢？

## `ZagreusHome_0216`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, there's more to it! A bit grim, really, as I'm a little older than I look. You see, the Titans, they once tore me limb from limb, but then my heart was saved, and that's where Dionysus comes from! That is the connection between us, you understand me, mate?

**中文**

噢，还有呢！有点糟糕，其实，我比看起来要老很多。你看，泰坦，他们曾经把我大卸八块，不过我的心脏被救了下来，那就是迪奥尼索司的来历！这就是我们之间的联系，你懂了吗，朋友？

## `ZagreusHome_0217`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey Orpheus, you know I've just been messing with you, with those Dionysus tales, and all that? Just trying to liven things up for us, mate.

**中文**

嘿俄耳甫斯，其实我一直在逗你玩呢，迪奥尼索司那些事情。我只是为了让咱们开心开心。

## `ZagreusHome_0218`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, mate, sometimes I worry that you're taking my tall tales rather seriously. You're not really going about and telling everyone this stuff, are you?

**中文**

你知道吗，朋友，我真的担心你把我说的故事全都当真了。你不会到处跟人说这些事吧？

## `ZagreusHome_0219`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

This is amazing, mate. So does this mean you found your muse?

**中文**

太棒了，伙计。这是不是说明你找到了自己的缪斯？

## `ZagreusHome_0220`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Turns out there's quite a lot of dead to fight down in the Underworld. Though I probably got farther than I would have, thanks to you.

**中文**

即使是在冥界我也有无数的亡灵要战。不过多亏了你，我比原先走得更远了。

## `ZagreusHome_0221`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What are you worried about, Than? You've never given him a reason not to trust you.

**中文**

你在担心什么呢，塔纳？你永远都值得他去信任你。

## `ZagreusHome_0222`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You never spoke of her to me. You must have known... you kept the truth from me, the same as Nyx?

**中文**

你从没跟我提起过她。你一定早就知道…你也不告诉我真相，就跟倪克斯一样？

## `ZagreusHome_0223`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Something tells me Father wouldn't let her take me even if she wanted.

**中文**

我感觉，就算她想，父亲也不会让她带我走的。

## `ZagreusHome_0224`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's just something I found out there that I thought you might like, Than. Don't think anything of it.

**中文**

只不过是我在外面找到的东西，我想你可能会喜欢的，塔纳。可别想多了。

## `ZagreusHome_0225`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

We've been through a lot, Than, and I think we'll be going through a lot more yet. Consider it a token of our kinship.

**中文**

我们经历了很多，塔纳，我觉得以后还要经历更多事。就把他当作是我们亲情的象征吧。

## `ZagreusHome_0226`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Happen to know whom I should thank for the weapons just outside my chambers, Achilles?

**中文**

我房间外面的那些武器是你放的吗，阿喀琉斯？我得谢谢你。

## `ZagreusHome_0227`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I guess word travels faster than I do. Why do you ask, Achilles?

**中文**

看来我的事迹跑地比我本人快得多。问这个干嘛，阿喀琉斯？

## `ZagreusHome_0228`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Some other time, Achilles. Just checking in on you, but I'll be on my way again shortly.

**中文**

下次吧，阿喀琉斯。就是来看看你，我马上就要走了。

## `ZagreusHome_0229`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Come on, Achilles, live a little... such as you're able. I won't tell if you won't tell.

**中文**

拿着吧，阿喀琉斯，别死脑筋啦…这是你应得的。我俩谁也别说出去。

## `ZagreusHome_0230`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Least I can do for all your guidance, sir.

**中文**

感谢你一直以来的教导，老师。

## `ZagreusHome_0231`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I just happen to think you deserve better than you've got down here, Achilles.

**中文**

我只是觉得你确实配得上这些，阿喀琉斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0232`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Hypnos? What would it take for you to let me through the locked door down the hall?

**中文**

嗨，修普诺斯？要怎样才能让我进去大厅那头被锁上的门？

## `ZagreusHome_0233`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nothing for me back there anyway.

**中文**

反正回去也没有我的容身之地。

## `ZagreusHome_0234`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm glad I died, as well, Hypnos! I'm glad I died, as well.

**中文**

我也很高兴我死了，修普诺斯！我也很高兴我死了。

## `ZagreusHome_0235`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm getting used to it. Though I don't think I'd feel particularly welcome anymore if not for you, Hypnos.

**中文**

我已经习惯了。不过如果不是你的话，我也不会觉得自己受到了欢迎，修普诺斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0236`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Great! Just keep dying over and over and everything will be fine.

**中文**

好！你只管死就好啦，事情会越来越好的。

## `ZagreusHome_0237`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Orpheus here hasn't sung a song in I don't know how long. Father basically ignores him now.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯很久没唱歌了，具体多久我也说不上来。父亲基本上已经忽视他的存在了。

## `ZagreusHome_0238`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Don't worry, mate. Father can't make you sing if you don't want to. I might be gone a while, so... take care of yourself.

**中文**

别担心，伙计。如果你不想唱，父亲也没法逼你去唱的。我可能要离开一阵子，所以...你自己保重。

## `ZagreusHome_0239`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I want you to have it, Orpheus. You've brightened up our lives down here more than you know. I only wish that you could see that.

**中文**

希望你能收下，俄耳甫斯。你给我们的生活带来了光明，你自己都不知道。我只希望你可以看到。

## `ZagreusHome_0240`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's nothing, Orpheus. Thank you for all the inspiration through your song. I love your work, mate.

**中文**

没什么，俄耳甫斯。谢谢你的歌给我的鼓舞。我很喜欢你的作品，伙计。

## `ZagreusHome_0241`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanks for all your hard work...!

**中文**

谢谢你付出的所有努力...！

## `ZagreusHome_0242`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

And, there she goes again....

**中文**

你看，她又来了...

## `ZagreusHome_0243`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh! A drink sometime sounds wonderful, Dusa, maybe tomorrow assuming I'm not getting out of here by then...?

**中文**

噢！现在不用啦，杜莎。不过如果我明天不出门的话也许可以…？

## `ZagreusHome_0244`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Dusa...?

**中文**

杜莎...？

## `ZagreusHome_0245`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's quite all right, Dusa. I'm not afraid of him, and neither should you be. You're the best help he's ever found.

**中文**

没事，杜莎。我不怕他，你也不用怕他。你是他能找到的最得力的助手了。

## `ZagreusHome_0246`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Wow. She didn't even wait for my response.

**中文**

...哇。她甚至都没耐心等我的消息。

## `ZagreusHome_0247`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Better than I could have possibly imagined, Nyx. I'm grateful that you found me such a fine replacement.

**中文**

比想象中的要好得多，倪克斯。我很感谢你为我找了一个这么好的替身。

## `ZagreusHome_0248`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx... I ran into Thanatos out there. He aided me, although I think he's rather upset. That I took off like this.

**中文**

倪克斯……我在外面碰到塔纳托斯了。他帮助了我，但是我觉得他好像有点不开心。然后我就走了。

## `ZagreusHome_0249`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I... somehow knew that was the case, Achilles. I wanted you to have this, anyway, just... don't drown your sorrows, as they seem to make you stronger.

**中文**

我…明白你的意思，阿喀琉斯。不管怎么样我都想让你收下这个，只是…别再借酒消愁了，表面上有效，实际上只会让你更忧愁。

## `ZagreusHome_0250`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You've always been there for me, Achilles. Even when Father pushed me away. This is nothing. I don't think I can ever repay you.

**中文**

你一直在支持着我，阿喀琉斯。就算父亲冷眼待我。这没什么的。这恩情我永远报答不完。

## `ZagreusHome_0251`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's yours, Achilles! Though not only do I hope you'll share it, why, I practically insist.

**中文**

都是你的，阿喀琉斯！除了分享之外，我希望你也可以做些别的。

## `ZagreusHome_0252`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, I was wondering if you'd split this with me at the lounge. A small excuse for both ourselves to take a break?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，要不要跟我一起在休息室分享一下？我们都可以稍微休息一下嘛。

## `ZagreusHome_0253`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What's done is done, sir. In my mind you're the greatest warrior who ever lived. So is it glory you still seek, or is it Patroclus?

**中文**

过去的事都过去了，老师。在我心目中你是有史以来最伟大的战士。所以你现在是追寻荣耀，还是普特洛克勒斯？

## `ZagreusHome_0254`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Achilles! I heard from Lady Artemis out there. It seems you have a bit of a fan in her. She wanted me to tell you you're the best! She's not mistaken, by the way.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，阿尔忒弥斯女士跟我说了。她似乎有点中意你。她想让我告诉你，你是最棒的！她真的没弄错。

## `ZagreusHome_0255`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wanted to say, the Lady Aphrodite asked that I relay a message to you, sir. She said that she admires you. Mentioned a certain war.

**中文**

老师，阿弗洛狄忒女士请我帮她给你带个话。她说她很欣赏你，她还提到了一场战争。

## `ZagreusHome_0256`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Neither do I, last time I checked. I'll take all the help I can get trying to break out of here.

**中文**

其实我跟我父亲关系也不好。如果能让我脱离这个地方，我得到的帮助越多越好。

## `ZagreusHome_0257`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

The Underworld itself, my enemy... I'll do my best anticipating its surprises, sir.

**中文**

我的敌人，就是冥界本身…我会好好接受它带来的惊喜的，老师。

## `ZagreusHome_0258`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What can I say, I try.

**中文**

我能怎么说呢，我试了。

## `ZagreusHome_0259`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

A power that once sealed away the Titans... maybe it's a power that can break me out of here. Thank you for helping me, Achilles.

**中文**

曾经驱逐了泰坦的力量…或许这力量能让我逃离这里。谢谢你的帮助，阿喀琉斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0260`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, I... sometimes I fear that Father, if he learns that you've been aiding me, that he will... not be generous with you.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，我…有时候我有点怕，怕我父亲万一知道你一直在帮助我，他会…找你麻烦。

## `ZagreusHome_0261`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I hope you're right.

**中文**

我希望你是对的。

## `ZagreusHome_0262`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, would you... tell me something more of Patroclus? You mentioned you were very close.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，你能不能…告诉我有关普特洛克勒斯的事情？你提到过你们关系不错。

## `ZagreusHome_0263`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Then, surely he must be in the Underworld, somewhere... have you not tried to seek him out? Or asked my father?

**中文**

那他肯定在冥界的什么地方…你没试过去找他吗？或者问问我父亲？

## `ZagreusHome_0264`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's better than nothing.

**中文**

总比没有好。

## `ZagreusHome_0265`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I listen!

**中文**

我听着呢！

## `ZagreusHome_0266`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I want to go lie down.

**中文**

我想躺下来。

## `ZagreusHome_0267`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's the good word.

**中文**

你好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0268`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What are those shades muttering about.

**中文**

那些暗灵在嘟囔什么。

## `ZagreusHome_0269`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Moaning and groaning.

**中文**

又是呻吟又是哀嚎的。

## `ZagreusHome_0270`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Those shades are always milling about.

**中文**

那些暗灵总是漫无目的地瞎转悠。

## `ZagreusHome_0271`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well see you later, then.

**中文**

好了，再见了。

## `ZagreusHome_0272`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You take care.

**中文**

你多保重。

## `ZagreusHome_0273`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Until next time.

**中文**

再见。

## `ZagreusHome_0274`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Till next time.

**中文**

再见。

## `ZagreusHome_0275`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good-bye for now.

**中文**

先再见了。

## `ZagreusHome_0276`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Bye for now.

**中文**

再见。

## `ZagreusHome_0277`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How you holding up, boy, eating well lately? All three mouths full and happy?

**中文**

怎么样，乖乖，有好好吃饭吗？三张嘴都吃饱喝足了吧？

## `ZagreusHome_0278`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, boy, just me again. Here to report it's pretty dangerous out there even without you on guard duty all the time.

**中文**

乖乖，我又来了。就算外头没有你，也还是危险四伏啊。

## `ZagreusHome_0279`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Guess what, boy? Made it all the way to Asphodel last time! I fully understand how come you never liked it there. Now to trek on back, I guess. Take care!

**中文**

你猜怎么着，乖狗狗？我终于到达水仙花平原了！我算是明白你为什么不喜欢那里了。现在我要走了，多保重！

## `ZagreusHome_0280`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How's everything been going lately, boy? What's the good word?

**中文**

最近怎么样，乖狗狗？有什么好消息吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0281`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I suppose you're going to punish her in some cruel fashion, then?

**中文**

那你是不是想变着法儿地惩罚她？

## `ZagreusHome_0282`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's strangely comforting to know.

**中文**

听说后竟然有种异样的欣慰。

## `ZagreusHome_0283`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm open to a change of climate. Even if it gets a bit uncomfortable.

**中文**

气候变化而已。就算有点让人不舒服也无所谓。

## `ZagreusHome_0284`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Looking forward to it.

**中文**

非常期待。

## `ZagreusHome_0285`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How can you... Nyx is the Night Itself, and can do as she pleases, isn't that correct, Father? You think I asked she get involved in all this business?

**中文**

你怎么能……倪克斯是夜的化身，她想做什么都行，是这样的吧，父亲？你觉得是我把她扯进这些事情里的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0286`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Pleasure doing business, then.

**中文**

愉快的交易。

## `ZagreusHome_0287`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Charon, you've been such a reassuring sight, I thought I'd offer you a token of my gratitude.

**中文**

卡戎，你真是个可靠的人啊，我想我应该表达一下谢意了。

## `ZagreusHome_0288`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, you don't have to give me something in return, it was a gift! Though, you're insisting, aren't you.

**中文**

等下，你不用送我东西回礼的，这是给你的礼物！既然你坚持要给我的话。

## `ZagreusHome_0289`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, uh, Meg? Look, I know you're just doing your job out there, and, anyway, found this, thought of you and all that, so... here.

**中文**

嗨，呃，墨纪？我知道，你在外头只是做自己的工作而已，我找到了这个，觉得你会喜欢的，所以……给你了。

## `ZagreusHome_0290`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey I'm just handing over contraband to the appropriate authorities, think nothing of it, Meg. Just doing our jobs, right?

**中文**

嗨，我又来上交违禁品啦，别多想，墨纪。工作而已啦，对吧？

## `ZagreusHome_0291`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's really what I want. And, I sincerely hope we can move past our differences. Not that turning in this contraband has anything to do with that.

**中文**

我真的是这样想的。并且，我真心希望我们能够解决彼此的分歧。不过这违禁品跟这个没有一点关系的。

## `ZagreusHome_0292`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I realize it. And I hope you never change, Meg.

**中文**

我明白。希望你不要改变自己，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_0293`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Flattery never got me anywhere with you to begin with, Meg. Doesn't mean I won't keep trying. I am ever stubborn, aren't I.

**中文**

我的阿谀奉承虽然现在对你没有用，墨纪。但我不会放弃的。我就是这么固执。

## `ZagreusHome_0294`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Got you some more of that contraband you like. Don't suppose I could help you put it away or anything...?

**中文**

我又带来了你喜欢的违禁品。我想我可以帮你把它给收起来…

## `ZagreusHome_0295`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'd hope we're wiser, too.

**中文**

要是我们也能变得更有智慧就好了。

## `ZagreusHome_0296`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just minding my own business.

**中文**

管好你自己的生意。

## `ZagreusHome_0297`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cheer up, everybody.

**中文**

开心点，各位。

## `ZagreusHome_0298`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Dum-de-dum...

**中文**

嘀-嗒-嘀...

## `ZagreusHome_0299`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

La-di-dah...

**中文**

嗒-嘀-嗒...

## `ZagreusHome_0300`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hm-hmm-hmmm.

**中文**

嗯-哼-哼。

## `ZagreusHome_0301`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How many times has it been?

**中文**

都已经多少次了？

## `ZagreusHome_0302`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How many times has it been now?

**中文**

现在都已经多少次了？

## `ZagreusHome_0303`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

As many chances as it takes.

**中文**

不管需要多少次尝试。

## `ZagreusHome_0304`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just need to keep at it.

**中文**

不可以放弃。

## `ZagreusHome_0305`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That many tries so far?

**中文**

到目前为止试了那么多次？

## `ZagreusHome_0306`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Some new decor.

**中文**

一些新的装饰。

## `ZagreusHome_0307`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should liven things a bit.

**中文**

应该可以活跃下气氛。

## `ZagreusHome_0308`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll just set that there.

**中文**

我就把它布置在那边。

## `ZagreusHome_0309`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Looks good I guess.

**中文**

我想应该不错。

## `ZagreusHome_0310`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cheers, Hypnos!

**中文**

谢谢，修普诺斯！

## `ZagreusHome_0311`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you, boy!

**中文**

谢谢你，乖狗狗！

## `ZagreusHome_0312`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm very grateful, Nyx.

**中文**

我十分感激，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0313`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanks so much, sir.

**中文**

非常感谢你，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_0314`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You're welcome, Father.

**中文**

不客气，父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_0315`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cheers, mate.

**中文**

谢谢你，伙计。

## `ZagreusHome_0316`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanks, Ms. Dusa.

**中文**

谢谢，杜莎小姐。

## `ZagreusHome_0317`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I, uh, thanks.

**中文**

我，呃，谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_0318`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you, Than.

**中文**

谢谢你，塔纳。

## `ZagreusHome_0319`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I don't know what to say, mate.

**中文**

我不知道该说什么好，伙计。

## `ZagreusHome_0320`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanks for that, sir.

**中文**

谢谢你，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_0321`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You're too kind, mate.

**中文**

你太好了，伙计。

## `ZagreusHome_0322`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My thanks, Lord Uncle Zeus.

**中文**

谢谢，众神之王宙斯叔叔。

## `ZagreusHome_0323`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I thank you, Uncle.

**中文**

谢谢你，叔叔。

## `ZagreusHome_0324`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Lady Athena, thank you.

**中文**

雅典娜女士，谢谢你。

## `ZagreusHome_0325`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm honored, Aphrodite.

**中文**

我很荣幸，阿弗洛狄忒。

## `ZagreusHome_0326`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Lady Artemis, you honor me.

**中文**

阿尔忒弥斯女士，我太荣幸了。

## `ZagreusHome_0327`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you, Dionysus mate.

**中文**

谢谢，迪奥尼索司。

## `ZagreusHome_0328`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Master Chaos, thank you.

**中文**

感谢您，卡俄斯君主。

## `ZagreusHome_0329`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm grateful.

**中文**

我非常感激。

## `ZagreusHome_0330`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you.

**中文**

谢谢你。

## `ZagreusHome_0331`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Appreciate that.

**中文**

谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_0332`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I won't forget this.

**中文**

我不会忘记的。

## `ZagreusHome_0333`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cheers for that.

**中文**

谢谢你。

## `ZagreusHome_0334`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You're too kind.

**中文**

你太好了。

## `ZagreusHome_0335`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm not listening.

**中文**

我没在听。

## `ZagreusHome_0336`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Must be something worth a damn in here.

**中文**

这里肯定有什么东西价值不菲。

## `ZagreusHome_0337`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mother... why...

**中文**

母亲...为什么...

## `ZagreusHome_0338`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm standing right here, old man.

**中文**

我就在这里，老爸。

## `ZagreusHome_0339`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You know I can hear you, old man.

**中文**

你知道我能听得到吧，老头。

## `ZagreusHome_0340`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's not that orderly really.

**中文**

其实并没有那么整齐。

## `ZagreusHome_0341`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Mirror's grown in power.

**中文**

圣镜的力量增强了。

## `ZagreusHome_0342`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Mirror's even stronger.

**中文**

圣镜变得更强了。

## `ZagreusHome_0343`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can do anything.

**中文**

我可以做任何事。

## `ZagreusHome_0344`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

More ways to grow my strength.

**中文**

还有更多方式来提升我的力量。

## `ZagreusHome_0345`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ready.

**中文**

准备好了。

## `ZagreusHome_0346`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm ready.

**中文**

我准备好了。

## `ZagreusHome_0347`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Again.

**中文**

再一次。

## `ZagreusHome_0348`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Again!

**中文**

再一次！

## `ZagreusHome_0349`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Away we go.

**中文**

我们离开这里。

## `ZagreusHome_0350`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Off we go.

**中文**

我们走。

## `ZagreusHome_0351`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

And, click.

**中文**

然后，咔哒。

## `ZagreusHome_0352`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's try it.

**中文**

我们来试试。

## `ZagreusHome_0353`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This one, why not.

**中文**

这个，为什么不呢。

## `ZagreusHome_0354`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This one then.

**中文**

那就这个。

## `ZagreusHome_0355`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's see what's in here.

**中文**

我们来看看这里都有什么。

## `ZagreusHome_0356`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Got something new to try.

**中文**

有些新的东西可以尝试。

## `ZagreusHome_0357`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's this one here.

**中文**

这是什么东西。

## `ZagreusHome_0358`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's that one there.

**中文**

那是什么东西。

## `ZagreusHome_0359`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That one looks interesting.

**中文**

那个东西看起来很有趣。

## `ZagreusHome_0360`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's this one do.

**中文**

这个东西有什么用。

## `ZagreusHome_0361`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Empty for now.

**中文**

现在是空的。

## `ZagreusHome_0362`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll have to come back later.

**中文**

看来我得之后再来看看。

## `ZagreusHome_0363`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No keepsakes yet.

**中文**

没有纪念品。

## `ZagreusHome_0364`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

OK!

**中文**

好了！

## `ZagreusHome_0365`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should help.

**中文**

应该有帮助。

## `ZagreusHome_0366`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You have my thanks, Lord Ares.

**中文**

我要感谢你，战神阿瑞斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0367`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well that was interesting.

**中文**

好吧，这很有趣。

## `ZagreusHome_0368`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Showed that stupid Hydra, at least.

**中文**

至少让愚蠢的九头蛇见识到了。

## `ZagreusHome_0369`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you, thank you.

**中文**

谢谢，谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_0370`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Someday I'll go even farther.

**中文**

总有一天，我可以走得更远。

## `ZagreusHome_0371`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Did pretty well that time.

**中文**

那一次做得不错。

## `ZagreusHome_0372`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Think that's about as far as I can get for now.

**中文**

感觉这是我目前能走的最远的距离了。

## `ZagreusHome_0373`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Is that... music?

**中文**

那是...音乐吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0374`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No way... Orpheus?

**中文**

不会吧... 俄耳甫斯？

## `ZagreusHome_0375`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You... why...

**中文**

你...为什么...

## `ZagreusHome_0376`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hi, Nyx.

**中文**

嗨，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0377`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Brilliant.

**中文**

漂亮。

## `ZagreusHome_0378`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Noted.

**中文**

知道了。

## `ZagreusHome_0379`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I haven't!

**中文**

我没有！

## `ZagreusHome_0380`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I have not.

**中文**

我没有。

## `ZagreusHome_0381`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You know, I haven't.

**中文**

你知道的，我没有。

## `ZagreusHome_0382`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Right.

**中文**

好了。

## `ZagreusHome_0383`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I haven't, no!

**中文**

我没有，不！

## `ZagreusHome_0384`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's not a bad idea.

**中文**

是不错的主意。

## `ZagreusHome_0385`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

OK!

**中文**

好了！

## `ZagreusHome_0386`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I want you to know we all appreciate you, Dusa. Don't work yourself too hard on my account, OK?

**中文**

我想让你知道，我们都很感谢你，杜莎。不要因为我你就不要命的工作，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0387`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I really am, Dusa. I hope you enjoy it.

**中文**

没错，杜莎。我希望你会喜欢。

## `ZagreusHome_0388`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, I am deadly serious, Dusa, honest. It's for you.

**中文**

噢，我现在可是非常认真的，杜莎，真的。是给你的。

## `ZagreusHome_0389`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yes, yes I am, Ms. Dusa, and I hope you enjoy it. Be seeing you, OK?

**中文**

是的，没错，杜莎小姐，我希望你会喜欢。回头见，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0390`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'd better talk to her. Thank you for the heads-up, Nyx. See you later, I'm sure.

**中文**

我最好跟她谈谈。多谢你的提醒，倪克斯。我们回头见。

## `ZagreusHome_0391`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, he's not so bad, Nyx. Be proud of your sleepy, cheerful son! Who else among us is able to muster that kind of enthusiasm?

**中文**

噢，他没什么不好啦，倪克斯。为你昏昏欲睡、无忧无虑的儿子感到骄傲吧！我们之中还有谁能有这种热情呢？

## `ZagreusHome_0392`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

OK she got me.

**中文**

好吧，她赢了。

## `ZagreusHome_0393`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Damn you, Meg.

**中文**

该死，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_0394`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll get you for that, Meg.

**中文**

我会让你后悔的，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_0395`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ugh. Skeletons.

**中文**

啊，骷髅。

## `ZagreusHome_0396`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Stupid skeleton twins.

**中文**

愚蠢的骷髅双胞胎。

## `ZagreusHome_0397`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Bloodless bastards got me.

**中文**

混账无血僵尸缠着我不放。

## `ZagreusHome_0398`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ugh, that stupid Hydra...

**中文**

啊，那条愚蠢的九头蛇...

## `ZagreusHome_0399`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Urgh, I'll get that Hydra.

**中文**

呃，我一定要搞定那条九头蛇。

## `ZagreusHome_0400`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I hate that Hydra...

**中文**

我讨厌九头蛇...

## `ZagreusHome_0401`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You cheated me. There wasn't anywhere that I could go! I could have gone much farther. All the way to Mother!

**中文**

你耍我。根本没有路可走！我本该继续前进！去找母亲！

## `ZagreusHome_0402`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I can wait. You can't just keep this place on lockdown for eternity. The mortals up there wouldn't stand for it.

**中文**

我可以等。你不可能让整个地方永远封闭下去。上面的凡人可受不了这样。

## `ZagreusHome_0403`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father, what happened to the lounge? The only place for anyone to have a little fun around here, and you seal it shut.

**中文**

父亲，休息室那边怎么回事？那是这里唯一能让大家娱乐放松的地方，您怎么给封起来了？

## `ZagreusHome_0404`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're really blaming me for not minding the dog while I was out, and while you all were here?

**中文**

您的意思是，我出门之后还得管着家里的狗是吧，您坐在这里干嘛呢？

## `ZagreusHome_0405`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You really ripped the lounge to pieces, boy? I hope you didn't get yourself in too much trouble. I'm sorry to have worried you like that.

**中文**

你是真的把休息室搞得一团乱是吗，乖乖？希望你没让自己惹上其他麻烦。真抱歉让你这样担心我。

## `ZagreusHome_0406`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, did you see, boy? The old lounge is good as new again, you ought to check it out! Just, don't annihilate it like you did last time.

**中文**

嗨，看到没有，乖乖？老休息室又重焕新光了，来看看吧！别再跟上次一样搞坏东西就行了。

## `ZagreusHome_0407`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Better than I could have possibly imagined, Nyx. You mentioned there was something more that it could do, once I had grown accustomed. I think I'm ready for that now.

**中文**

比想象中的要好得多，倪克斯。您说过，一旦我习惯了这成长，它还可以做到更多的事。我觉得我已经准备好了。

## `ZagreusHome_0408`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Least we can look forward to the grand reopening.

**中文**

至少我们可以期待再次开启的伟大时刻。

## `ZagreusHome_0409`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cerberus destroyed the lounge again?

**中文**

刻耳柏洛斯又把休息室给毁了？

## `ZagreusHome_0410`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I, uh, just wanted to say, the lounge looks wonderful, as good as new. Better! Hope it's not been too much trouble patching up.

**中文**

我，呃，我只想说，休息室很好看，跟新的一样。更好了！希望修补的时候能别出什么差错。

## `ZagreusHome_0411`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey how'd you get in here?

**中文**

嘿，你是怎么进来的？

## `ZagreusHome_0412`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What...?

**中文**

什么…？

## `ZagreusHome_0413`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Orpheus, you're back! I guess you've heard the latest with me, then. But I'm all right, just, might be gone a while, sorting some things out. So take care, and don't worry, mate. Father can't make you sing if you don't want to.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯，你回来了！我猜你应该听说我的事情了。我还好啦，可能要出去一段时间，解决一些事情。别担心，朋友。如果你不想唱歌，我父亲是没法强迫你的。

## `ZagreusHome_0414`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

If it's any consolation, mate, I'm certain every single undead court musician serving in the throne room of the god of the dead has been through everything you're going through.

**中文**

如果能安慰你的话，伙计，我敢肯定，每一个侍奉死亡之神的活着的宫廷乐师都会经历你现在的处境。

## `ZagreusHome_0415`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, is it really true that Cerberus ripped the lounge up right after I left? He must have gotten in a lot of trouble.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，刻耳柏洛斯他真的在我离开后把休息室毁了吗？他一定给自己惹了不少麻烦吧。

## `ZagreusHome_0416`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, no... I wondered what became of Dusa. She's always worried Father's going to fire her, even without something like this.

**中文**

不会吧……她现在怎么样了？就算没发生这种事，她也一直在担心父亲会不会解雇她。

## `ZagreusHome_0417`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'll try to travel for the both of us, Achilles. I've seen so little other than these walls.

**中文**

我会试着连你的份也一起，阿喀琉斯。除了这些墙壁，我几乎都没见过其他的东西。

## `ZagreusHome_0418`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Achilles must have been incredible back when he was alive.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯活着时肯定很厉害。

## `ZagreusHome_0419`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Aphrodite herself. Wonder if I could get this signed.

**中文**

阿弗洛狄忒呢。怀疑我能否把这个签了。

## `ZagreusHome_0420`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Bronze weapons... made crudely, but they had the right idea.

**中文**

青铜武器...做工粗糙，但他们的想法是对的。

## `ZagreusHome_0421`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Always wanted my own scrying pool.

**中文**

一直想着我自己的水晶湖。

## `ZagreusHome_0422`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Power.

**中文**

力量。

## `ZagreusHome_0423`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My power grows.

**中文**

我的力量在增强。

## `ZagreusHome_0424`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Done.

**中文**

搞定。

## `ZagreusHome_0425`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Looking good.

**中文**

看起来不错。

## `ZagreusHome_0426`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I've no more Darkness.

**中文**

我没有更多黑暗宝石了。

## `ZagreusHome_0427`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I need more Darkness.

**中文**

我需要更多黑暗宝石。

## `ZagreusHome_0428`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's beyond my means.

**中文**

这超出了我的能力范围。

## `ZagreusHome_0429`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can't do that yet.

**中文**

我还做不到。

## `ZagreusHome_0430`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No good.

**中文**

没用。

## `ZagreusHome_0431`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not possible.

**中文**

不可能。

## `ZagreusHome_0432`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nothing happened.

**中文**

什么都没发生。

## `ZagreusHome_0433`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Didn't work.

**中文**

不起作用。

## `ZagreusHome_0434`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nothing.

**中文**

什么都没有。

## `ZagreusHome_0435`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Don't say that, Nyx. Besides! Conflict between Megaera and me has always put the spark in our relationship. We're going to be fine.

**中文**

别这样说，倪克斯。再说了！我跟墨纪拉起冲突的时候还是可以培养感情的。我们没事的。

## `ZagreusHome_0436`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cheer up, boy, it'll be all right.

**中文**

开心点，乖狗狗，一切都会好起来的。

## `ZagreusHome_0437`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Don't even think about it, boy.

**中文**

想都不要想，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusHome_0438`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Where's Cerberus?

**中文**

刻耳柏洛斯在哪里？

## `ZagreusHome_0439`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cerberus must be asleep somewhere.

**中文**

刻耳柏洛斯肯定在哪里睡觉。

## `ZagreusHome_0440`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No sign of Cerberus for now.

**中文**

没见到刻耳柏洛斯的身影。

## `ZagreusHome_0441`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey where's Hypnos?

**中文**

嘿，修普诺斯在哪里？

## `ZagreusHome_0442`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hypnos must be on break.

**中文**

修普诺斯肯定在休息。

## `ZagreusHome_0443`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What happened to Hypnos?

**中文**

修普诺斯出了什么事？

## `ZagreusHome_0444`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Achilles is off duty.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯不在岗位上。

## `ZagreusHome_0445`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Must have missed Achilles.

**中文**

好像错过阿喀琉斯了。

## `ZagreusHome_0446`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Achilles on break I guess.

**中文**

我猜阿喀琉斯在休息。

## `ZagreusHome_0447`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No sign of Dusa.

**中文**

没见到杜莎的身影。

## `ZagreusHome_0448`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Dusa's probably cleaning the rafters.

**中文**

杜莎应该是在擦房梁。

## `ZagreusHome_0449`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Dusa's not here.

**中文**

杜莎不在这里。

## `ZagreusHome_0450`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nyx isn't around.

**中文**

没看到倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0451`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nyx must be someplace else.

**中文**

倪克斯肯定在其他地方。

## `ZagreusHome_0452`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Wonder where Nyx went.

**中文**

好奇倪克斯去哪儿了。

## `ZagreusHome_0453`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's just have a listen.

**中文**

我们来听听看。

## `ZagreusHome_0454`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What are they on about now.

**中文**

他们现在在说什么。

## `ZagreusHome_0455`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What are these shades up to now?

**中文**

这些暗灵现在在忙什么？

## `ZagreusHome_0456`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What do these shades have to say for themselves.

**中文**

这些暗灵要为自己说些什么。

## `ZagreusHome_0457`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey the lounge is open.

**中文**

嘿，休息室开了。

## `ZagreusHome_0458`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can escape through that pink window there.

**中文**

我可以从那扇粉红窗户逃出去。

## `ZagreusHome_0459`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's my way out.

**中文**

那就是我的出路。

## `ZagreusHome_0460`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

There's my escape.

**中文**

我有我的出路。

## `ZagreusHome_0461`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My escape route's back the other way.

**中文**

我的逃离路线很不一样。

## `ZagreusHome_0462`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can escape from the courtyard back there.

**中文**

我可以从后院逃出去。

## `ZagreusHome_0463`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Urgh, you win this round, Meg.

**中文**

啊，这局你赢了，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_0464`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Unf, cheers for that, Meg.

**中文**

呼，谢谢你，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_0465`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ung, damn it, Father.

**中文**

啊，该死，父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_0466`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You called, Father...?

**中文**

你叫我吗，父亲...？

## `ZagreusHome_0467`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All those wretches got the best of me.

**中文**

这些暗灵们占了上风。

## `ZagreusHome_0468`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mmph, that was too much...

**中文**

嗯，太多了...

## `ZagreusHome_0469`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Got just a little greedy I suppose.

**中文**

我觉得有点太贪心了。

## `ZagreusHome_0470`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's what I get for messing with a trove.

**中文**

要得到那件宝贝的欲望扰乱了我的心智。

## `ZagreusHome_0471`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Rngh, that trove should've been mine.

**中文**

哼，那件宝贝本来就应该是我的。

## `ZagreusHome_0472`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That did not go as planned.

**中文**

计划赶不上变化。

## `ZagreusHome_0473`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I've got to stop walking on spikes like that.

**中文**

我不要像这样走在尖刺上。

## `ZagreusHome_0474`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Spikes continue to be painful.

**中文**

被尖刺刺到还是会很疼。

## `ZagreusHome_0475`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Rngh, blasted spike traps everywhere...

**中文**

该死的尖刺陷阱，到处都是...

## `ZagreusHome_0476`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Damn dart traps shot me all to hell.

**中文**

该死的飞镖陷阱，要把我射死了。

## `ZagreusHome_0477`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Stupid dart trap, ungh.

**中文**

愚蠢的飞镖陷阱，哼。

## `ZagreusHome_0478`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I really shouldn't step on dart traps anymore...

**中文**

我真的不该再踩到那些飞镖陷阱了……

## `ZagreusHome_0479`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'll just aim generally for the midsection since the eyes are a lot harder to hit in my experience.

**中文**

我觉得我还是瞄准躯干好了，以我的经验来看，眼睛这个部位还是很难打到的。

## `ZagreusHome_0480`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wreck my father's finely crafted architecture every chance I get. That I can do for sure.

**中文**

一有机会我就打坏我父亲的华美建筑。这个没问题。

## `ZagreusHome_0481`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Achilles? My father's hall back there. I don't suppose you'll let me have myself a look? Lots for me to rummage through, I'm sure.

**中文**

嗨，阿喀琉斯。我父亲的大厅，你应该不会让我进去的吧？我想在里头找一些东西。

## `ZagreusHome_0482`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Understood, sir.

**中文**

了解了，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_0483`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Mortals must have had their stories, though. About what happens to them once they die, and once they end up in this place.

**中文**

凡人们一定也有自己的故事版本吧。关于自己死后会怎样，来了这里会怎样之类的。

## `ZagreusHome_0484`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I hate it when they fight.

**中文**

我讨厌他们打来打去的。

## `ZagreusHome_0485`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They're at it again... because of me.

**中文**

他们又开始了...就因为我。

## `ZagreusHome_0486`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My faith is prone to shakiness at times.

**中文**

我的信念时不时地会动摇。

## `ZagreusHome_0487`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Get on it, Hypnos.

**中文**

加油吧，修普诺斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0488`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Poor Dusa....

**中文**

可怜的杜莎...

## `ZagreusHome_0489`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh no....

**中文**

哦，不...

## `ZagreusHome_0490`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ladies night I guess. Or day? I don't know.

**中文**

女士之夜，不知道现在是不是晚上？

## `ZagreusHome_0491`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They get along at least.

**中文**

至少他们相处得不错。

## `ZagreusHome_0492`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's probably bad news for me.

**中文**

对我来说可能是个坏消息。

## `ZagreusHome_0493`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What was that about.

**中文**

那又是怎么回事。

## `ZagreusHome_0494`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not my business.

**中文**

不关我的事。

## `ZagreusHome_0495`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cheers.

**中文**

开心点。

## `ZagreusHome_0496`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Indeed.

**中文**

确实。

## `ZagreusHome_0497`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

He usually is.

**中文**

他经常这样。

## `ZagreusHome_0498`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well sign me in, then.

**中文**

那算我一个。

## `ZagreusHome_0499`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I guess so!

**中文**

我想也是！

## `ZagreusHome_0500`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Glad to keep things interesting.

**中文**

很高兴让事情保持这种乐趣。

## `ZagreusHome_0501`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yeah I hope so too.

**中文**

是啊，我也这么希望。

## `ZagreusHome_0502`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It was unpleasant, yes.

**中文**

让人很不愉快，是的。

## `ZagreusHome_0503`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You cool off.

**中文**

淡定。

## `ZagreusHome_0504`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Um. Thanks.

**中文**

嗯，谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_0505`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

There isn't much for us to talk about.

**中文**

没有我们说话的余地。

## `ZagreusHome_0506`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Likewise, Nyx.

**中文**

你也是，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0507`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Your faith in me means a lot, Nyx.

**中文**

你对我的信任很重要，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0508`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm getting somewhere, I guess.

**中文**

我觉得自己有点成果了。

## `ZagreusHome_0509`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you, though I've much to learn as yet.

**中文**

谢谢，虽然我还有很多东西要学。

## `ZagreusHome_0510`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think I turned out OK.

**中文**

我觉得我可以的。

## `ZagreusHome_0511`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can't imagine him otherwise.

**中文**

我想象不出他还会是什么样子。

## `ZagreusHome_0512`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They'd better beware of me.

**中文**

他们最好提防着我。

## `ZagreusHome_0513`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I will look the other way if you do, mate. Now, here! Or else!

**中文**

那我就假装不知道，伙计，拿着！不然的话！

## `ZagreusHome_0514`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

If not for you, my friend, I'd have no one to viciously attack with no fear of reprisal whatsoever.

**中文**

如果没有你，我的朋友，我再找不到第二个能让我无忧无虑暴揍他的人了。

## `ZagreusHome_0515`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It is my humble duty to oblige my favorite boy with all the treats as I am able to procure.

**中文**

尽我所能让我最好的伙伴尝够美食是我的责任。

## `ZagreusHome_0516`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's nothing, Nyx. Really.

**中文**

没什么，倪克斯，真的。

## `ZagreusHome_0517`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You lighten up my numerous untimely deaths, Hypnos.

**中文**

在这么多次无奈的死亡中，是你让我有了一丝慰藉，修普诺斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0518`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sure thing, Hypnos! Knock yourself out.

**中文**

没事，修普诺斯！别客气。

## `ZagreusHome_0519`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have a secret, Hypnos. I've died on purpose every single time, just to see you.

**中文**

我有个秘密，修普诺斯。我每次送死都是为了来见你。

## `ZagreusHome_0520`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, I thought you'd say that, Charon mate, but I insist you take it and enjoy, all right?

**中文**

我就料到你会这样说，卡戎老伙计，但是还请你务必收下这件东西，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0521`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm pleased to have you as a friend as well, Charon. The pleasure's mine.

**中文**

能跟你做朋友我也很开心，卡戎。我很高兴。

## `ZagreusHome_0522`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I understand completely, Orpheus. I think we'll both get through this rough patch in due time.

**中文**

我完全明白，俄耳甫斯。我想我们总会渡过艰难时期的。

## `ZagreusHome_0523`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I figured you could do with something more than that this time around, good sir.

**中文**

我想你这次能做的应该不只这些，好先生。

## `ZagreusHome_0524`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How about you keep on helping me not die as quickly as I might have otherwise, deal?

**中文**

那你就帮我让我不要死得那么快，怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_0525`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're more than welcome, sir. For all you've done for me, it's the least I could do.

**中文**

你真的太客气了，先生。你为我做了这么多，这是我应该做的。

## `ZagreusHome_0526`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Come on, sir, what's a priceless bottle of the drink of the gods between friends?

**中文**

来吧，先生，只是朋友间分享的一瓶无价的美酒而已。

## `ZagreusHome_0527`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I thank you, my Lord Uncle Zeus, for your benevolence and your thunderous might.

**中文**

感谢您，众神之王宙斯叔叔，感谢您的仁慈和雷鸣般的力量。

## `ZagreusHome_0528`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Lord Zeus, I bid you please accept my humble offering, for all you've done for me.

**中文**

宙斯大人，请您接受这份薄礼，感谢您为我所做的一切。

## `ZagreusHome_0529`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

This offering is but a trifle, Uncle Zeus, for all your magnanimity.

**中文**

宙斯叔叔，对您的宽宏大量来说，这份贡品简直不值一提。

## `ZagreusHome_0530`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Lord Ares, I honor you for all the strength you've given me to overcome my foes.

**中文**

阿瑞斯大人，我尊敬您，因为您赐予我战胜敌人的力量。

## `ZagreusHome_0531`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Lord Ares! Please accept this token of my deep appreciation for your generous support.

**中文**

阿瑞斯大人！请接受这个礼物，这是我对您的慷慨支持的诚挚谢意。

## `ZagreusHome_0532`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Lord Ares, it is my privilege to have met you, and have learned your death-inflicting way.

**中文**

阿瑞斯大人，很荣幸认识您，并学会了您致人于死地的技能。

## `ZagreusHome_0533`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

My lady Artemis! This is nothing, next to all your grace and favor.

**中文**

我的阿尔忒弥斯女士！比起你的恩典，这根本不值一提。

## `ZagreusHome_0534`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Lady Artemis! I am indebted to you, for the strength and cunning you have given me.

**中文**

阿尔忒弥斯女士！真的要谢谢你，因为你赐予了我力量和智慧。

## `ZagreusHome_0535`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

My lady Artemis, your favor's been invaluable to me. No finer huntress ever walked the surface of the earth, or underneath.

**中文**

我的阿尔忒弥斯女士，您的帮助对我来说就是无价之宝。再好的猎手也没办法在地面或地下行走。

## `ZagreusHome_0536`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

My lady Aphrodite, if not for our acquaintance, I am certain that my days or nights would be much drearier indeed.

**中文**

我的阿弗洛狄忒女士，如果不是认识了你，我的生活一定会沉闷得多。

## `ZagreusHome_0537`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Lady Aphrodite, I'm afraid there really isn't much here worthy of you, but perhaps this can suffice.

**中文**

阿弗洛狄忒女士，这里没有什么配得上你的礼物，但也许这个勉强够得上。

## `ZagreusHome_0538`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dear Aphrodite, surely you have all the offerings you can stand, but I would ask that you accept one more.

**中文**

亲爱的阿弗洛狄忒，我知道你有许多贡品，但我还是想请你再接受一个。

## `ZagreusHome_0539`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

For you, Lady Athena, without whom I would assuredly be lost.

**中文**

给你，雅典娜女士，没有你我肯定会迷路的。

## `ZagreusHome_0540`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have a simple token for you, Goddess. Although it's insufficient thanks for all the aid you've given me.

**中文**

我有一个简单的令牌要给你，女神。但这对于你给我的所有帮助而言，真是不算什么。

## `ZagreusHome_0541`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Goddess Athena, please accept this gift. Your wisdom ever guides me toward the light.

**中文**

雅典娜女神，请接受这份礼物。您的智慧将指引我走向光明。

## `ZagreusHome_0542`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Uncle Poseidon, this here is for you. Would that my father were as easygoing as yourself.

**中文**

波塞冬叔叔，这是孝敬您的。我父亲要是像您一样随和就好了。

## `ZagreusHome_0543`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Mighty Poseidon, greatest of my uncles, arguably, please accept this gift!

**中文**

威猛的波塞冬，我最伟大的叔叔，我想说，请接受这个礼物！

## `ZagreusHome_0544`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Uncle Poseidon, I have a special catch for you! Although it pales next to your sheer awesomeness.

**中文**

波塞冬叔叔，我有一个特别的礼物要送给您！尽管这和您的神力相比不算什么。

## `ZagreusHome_0545`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ungh, blasted river of flame.

**中文**

哦，该死的地狱之河。

## `ZagreusHome_0546`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just couldn't take the heat back there.

**中文**

真的没办法忍受那里的热浪。

## `ZagreusHome_0547`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Urgh that was not a pleasant journey.

**中文**

啊，那不是一段愉快的旅途。

## `ZagreusHome_0548`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

For a good time, beware the fancy glowing bozos on my quest. Check.

**中文**

出门在外，留神浑身发光的特殊怪物。没问题。

## `ZagreusHome_0549`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Say, I was wondering, Achilles. Any news of Orpheus of late? I've not seen him around here in some time. He still paying his dues?

**中文**

那个，我想知道，阿喀琉斯。俄耳甫斯最近有什么动向吗？我已经有段时间没在这里见过他了。他还在还债？

## `ZagreusHome_0550`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I hope so, too. If you do see him, tell him I said hi.

**中文**

我也这样想。如果你见到他，替我问个好。

## `ZagreusHome_0551`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Good to see old Orpheus is back with us. He hasn't changed a bit. Was he always like this?

**中文**

真高兴看到老俄耳甫斯又回来了。他还是一点没变。他一直是这样的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0552`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Orpheus, he's back.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯，他回来了。

## `ZagreusHome_0553`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

He's in trouble.

**中文**

他遇上麻烦了。

## `ZagreusHome_0554`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Poor Orpheus.

**中文**

可怜的俄耳甫斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0555`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Makes sense.

**中文**

有道理。

## `ZagreusHome_0556`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I see.

**中文**

我明白了。

## `ZagreusHome_0557`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanks.

**中文**

谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_0558`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Got it.

**中文**

我知道了。

## `ZagreusHome_0559`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hmm.

**中文**

嗯。

## `ZagreusHome_0560`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well, take care.

**中文**

好吧，你多保重。

## `ZagreusHome_0561`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh...

**中文**

哦...

## `ZagreusHome_0562`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ah.

**中文**

啊。

## `ZagreusHome_0563`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good catching up.

**中文**

很高兴和你叙叙旧。

## `ZagreusHome_0564`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I see...

**中文**

我明白了...

## `ZagreusHome_0565`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Huh...

**中文**

呃...

## `ZagreusHome_0566`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hope Orpheus is OK.

**中文**

但愿俄耳甫斯一切都好。

## `ZagreusHome_0567`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Where'd Orpheus go.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯去哪里了。

## `ZagreusHome_0568`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Where's our court musician?

**中文**

我们的宫廷乐师呢？

## `ZagreusHome_0569`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thought I'd find Meg here.

**中文**

还以为在这里可以找到墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_0570`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Megaera's not around.

**中文**

墨纪拉不在这里。

## `ZagreusHome_0571`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Meg must've gone back already.

**中文**

墨纪应该已经回去了。

## `ZagreusHome_0572`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I've mastered that.

**中文**

我已经掌握了。

## `ZagreusHome_0573`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Reached the limit.

**中文**

已经到极限了。

## `ZagreusHome_0574`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hit the limit.

**中文**

达到极限。

## `ZagreusHome_0575`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Maxed out already.

**中文**

已经到头了。

## `ZagreusHome_0576`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Mirror... at last I see myself for what I am.

**中文**

圣镜... 我终于看到了自己的样子。

## `ZagreusHome_0577`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Every facet of the Mirror, now within my grasp.

**中文**

圣镜的每一面，现在都在我的掌控之中了。

## `ZagreusHome_0578`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Master Chaos? Everyone makes offerings to my relatives upon Olympus. I wish to make an offering to you. Thank you for... everything.

**中文**

卡俄斯君主？每个人都向我在奥林匹斯山上的亲人贡献礼物。我想送您一个礼物。谢谢您所做的一切。

## `ZagreusHome_0579`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Please accept from me this humble gift, O Master Chaos.

**中文**

请收下我的这份微不足道的礼物，伟大的卡俄斯君主。

## `ZagreusHome_0580`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

This is for you, Master Chaos. Everyone beneath the heavens and earth enjoys this stuff.

**中文**

卡俄斯君主，这是给您的礼物。凡界和冥界的每个人都享受它的口味。

## `ZagreusHome_0581`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Master Chaos, I pledge never to forget you or your good graces. May your wisdom and your whim forever guide me.

**中文**

卡俄斯君主，我发誓永远不会忘记您和您的恩宠。愿您的智慧和新奇想法永远指引我。

## `ZagreusHome_0582`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm ever-grateful that my journeys through the Underworld mean that we can get to know each other, Master Chaos.

**中文**

我穿越冥界的旅程让我们可以互相了解，这种经历我会永远铭记，卡俄斯君主。

## `ZagreusHome_0583`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'd better try some other time.

**中文**

我最好找其他时间试试。

## `ZagreusHome_0584`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They seem busy at the moment.

**中文**

他们好像都在忙。

## `ZagreusHome_0585`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Doesn't seem like a good time.

**中文**

看起来不是个好时机。

## `ZagreusHome_0586`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They're in the middle of something.

**中文**

他们都在忙于其他事。

## `ZagreusHome_0587`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

An infernal contract...

**中文**

地狱契约...

## `ZagreusHome_0588`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's that on my desk?

**中文**

什么东西在我桌上？

## `ZagreusHome_0589`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm through with Father's dirty work.

**中文**

我受够了父亲的肮脏勾当。

## `ZagreusHome_0590`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I don't think so.

**中文**

我不这么认为。

## `ZagreusHome_0591`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not a chance.

**中文**

没有丝毫机会。

## `ZagreusHome_0592`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Can't even open this yet.

**中文**

这次还是打不开。

## `ZagreusHome_0593`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right, let's see here.

**中文**

好吧，我们在这边看看。

## `ZagreusHome_0594`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's see what happens.

**中文**

我们来看看发生了什么。

## `ZagreusHome_0595`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

In the name of Hades.

**中文**

以哈迪斯之名。

## `ZagreusHome_0596`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's see what we can do with this place.

**中文**

我们看看能在这里做些什么。

## `ZagreusHome_0597`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

On my authority.

**中文**

以我的权威。

## `ZagreusHome_0598`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Father always said never to take the easy way.

**中文**

父亲总是说，不要想着走捷径。

## `ZagreusHome_0599`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's make some adjustments.

**中文**

我们来做些调整。

## `ZagreusHome_0600`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Time for some meddling.

**中文**

现在来好好折腾折腾。

## `ZagreusHome_0601`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can make life even more miserable here.

**中文**

我可以让这里的生活变得更悲惨。

## `ZagreusHome_0602`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not authorized.

**中文**

未经许可。

## `ZagreusHome_0603`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not enough Heat.

**中文**

热量不足。

## `ZagreusHome_0604`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I don't have enough Heat.

**中文**

我没有足够热量。

## `ZagreusHome_0605`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm not authorized.

**中文**

我不符合条件。

## `ZagreusHome_0606`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm not cleared to do that.

**中文**

我还没准备好怎么做。

## `ZagreusHome_0607`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Do your worst, Father.

**中文**

拿出你的全部本事，父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_0608`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My choice is made.

**中文**

我已经做出选择。

## `ZagreusHome_0609`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Wretches of the Underworld, take heed.

**中文**

冥界的暗灵们，听着。

## `ZagreusHome_0610`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That ought to liven things up.

**中文**

这应该可以活跃一下气氛。

## `ZagreusHome_0611`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's the worst that could happen?

**中文**

可能发生的最糟糕的情况是什么？

## `ZagreusHome_0612`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What could go wrong.

**中文**

可能会出现什么岔子？

## `ZagreusHome_0613`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This ought to be interesting.

**中文**

这应该很有趣。

## `ZagreusHome_0614`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's life without a little pain.

**中文**

没有一点痛苦的人生算什么人生。

## `ZagreusHome_0615`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's with the contract in my chambers, Father? You offering me a job?

**中文**

我房间里的契约是怎么回事，父亲大人？你给我找了份工作？

## `ZagreusHome_0616`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Why would I want my toiling to be more painful than it already is?

**中文**

我干嘛要让自己受更多的苦？

## `ZagreusHome_0617`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's a deal.

**中文**

就这么说定了。

## `ZagreusHome_0618`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father... look, Lord Uncle Zeus, I've been in contact with him, and he wishes to make peace. He asked for me to tell you. To tell you he forgives you for... whatever happened.

**中文**

父亲，宙斯叔叔他……我跟他联系过了，他希望可以和解。他让我这样告诉你。告诉你，他原谅你……之前的作为。

## `ZagreusHome_0619`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What did you do...? What happened between you?

**中文**

你做了什么……？你们之间到底发生过什么？

## `ZagreusHome_0620`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Loud and clear.

**中文**

一清二楚。

## `ZagreusHome_0621`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Urgh, that blasted giant gorgon head.

**中文**

啊，该死的蛇发女妖的巨型头颅。

## `ZagreusHome_0622`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Eugh, not a word of this to Dusa...

**中文**

哦，关于这件事，一个字都不要和杜莎说...

## `ZagreusHome_0623`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Augh, gorgons, what can you do...

**中文**

啊，蛇发女妖，你有什么能耐...

## `ZagreusHome_0624`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Something about a war. Father sent Thanatos to take stock, but you're right, it's been a while now that you mention it. I guess a lot's occurred down here since then.

**中文**

是关于一场战争的。父亲派塔纳托斯去查看情况，不过你说得对，确实过了很长时间。我想从那以后应该发生了很多事情。

## `ZagreusHome_0625`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Everything under control. Sure.

**中文**

一切都在掌握之中。没错。

## `ZagreusHome_0626`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm not so short.

**中文**

我没那么矮。

## `ZagreusHome_0627`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

OK...

**中文**

好吧...

## `ZagreusHome_0628`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Eventually, he says.

**中文**

终于，他说。

## `ZagreusHome_0629`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's very reassuring.

**中文**

确实很令人安心。

## `ZagreusHome_0630`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Achilles...

**中文**

阿喀琉斯...

## `ZagreusHome_0631`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I understand. I think.

**中文**

我觉得自己明白了。

## `ZagreusHome_0632`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Then, where did... never mind.

**中文**

那么，哪里……算了。

## `ZagreusHome_0633`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's... oh.

**中文**

那是...哦。

## `ZagreusHome_0634`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I see. I'm sorry to have troubled you.

**中文**

我明白了。很抱歉给你添麻烦了。

## `ZagreusHome_0635`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's new?

**中文**

有什么新玩意儿？

## `ZagreusHome_0636`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's new.

**中文**

有什么新玩意儿。

## `ZagreusHome_0637`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's in stock?

**中文**

有哪些现货?

## `ZagreusHome_0638`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's in stock.

**中文**

有哪些现货。

## `ZagreusHome_0639`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's up for barter?

**中文**

都有哪些东西可以交换？

## `ZagreusHome_0640`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's for sale.

**中文**

在卖什么。

## `ZagreusHome_0641`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Give me the best deal you've got.

**中文**

给我最实惠的价格。

## `ZagreusHome_0642`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's on the old exchange.

**中文**

旧市场都有什么。

## `ZagreusHome_0643`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Wretched Broker, what's new, my friend?

**中文**

可怜的中间人，你手上有什么新玩意儿？

## `ZagreusHome_0644`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This Broker sure reminds me of a guy who's killed me once or twice.

**中文**

这位中间人让我想起一个杀了我一两次的家伙。

## `ZagreusHome_0645`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's see here.

**中文**

我们在这边看看。

## `ZagreusHome_0646`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll take it!

**中文**

我要它！

## `ZagreusHome_0647`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fair trade.

**中文**

公平的交易。

## `ZagreusHome_0648`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll take it.

**中文**

它是我的了。

## `ZagreusHome_0649`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll buy that.

**中文**

我要把它买下来。

## `ZagreusHome_0650`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll take that deal.

**中文**

我接受交易。

## `ZagreusHome_0651`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Seems like a good deal.

**中文**

看似不错的交易。

## `ZagreusHome_0652`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sure, why not.

**中文**

当然，为什么不呢。

## `ZagreusHome_0653`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Think I'll take it.

**中文**

我打算拿走这件。

## `ZagreusHome_0654`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fair and square.

**中文**

公平交易。

## `ZagreusHome_0655`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You trying to fleece me here?

**中文**

你这是在坑我吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0656`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's a deal!

**中文**

成交！

## `ZagreusHome_0657`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here you go.

**中文**

拿着吧。

## `ZagreusHome_0658`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Bought 'em all up.

**中文**

把它们全部买下来。

## `ZagreusHome_0659`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nothing left to trade for now.

**中文**

没东西可交易了。
