## `ZagreusHome_0660`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll have to come back later.

**中文**

看来我得之后再来看看。

## `ZagreusHome_0661`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fresh out of stock for now.

**中文**

已经卖光了。

## `ZagreusHome_0662`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Scooped up every offer.

**中文**

接受了所有交易条件。

## `ZagreusHome_0663`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Pardon.

**中文**

不好意思。

## `ZagreusHome_0664`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Pardon me!

**中文**

原谅我！

## `ZagreusHome_0665`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oop my mistake.

**中文**

哎呀，是我的失误。

## `ZagreusHome_0666`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Excuse me.

**中文**

对不起。

## `ZagreusHome_0667`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Beg your pardon.

**中文**

请你原谅。

## `ZagreusHome_0668`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sorry.

**中文**

抱歉。

## `ZagreusHome_0669`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sorry!

**中文**

抱歉！

## `ZagreusHome_0670`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My fault.

**中文**

是我的错。

## `ZagreusHome_0671`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's not all it's cracked up to be.

**中文**

并没有外界说的那么好。

## `ZagreusHome_0672`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My condolences.

**中文**

我很同情你。

## `ZagreusHome_0673`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I get it.

**中文**

我明白了。

## `ZagreusHome_0674`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's all this...?

**中文**

这都是什么...？

## `ZagreusHome_0675`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let me guess... they want me to fight all the way through the Underworld, having used the Pact of Punishment in my bedchambers to make the going even more treacherous than it already is, and if I succeed, they'll reward me with some sort of useless trinket?

**中文**

我猜猜……他们是想让我签署卧室里的那个惩罚契约，好让一切都难上加难，接着又让我杀穿冥界，如果我成功了，他们就会奖励我一个没用的小装饰，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0676`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yes, a little bit, if you must know.

**中文**

是的，有点，如果你非要问的话。

## `ZagreusHome_0677`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What in blazes...

**中文**

什么情况...

## `ZagreusHome_0678`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, just so I understand. I literally toiled through hell and back, and my reward is just... a statue of you? Don't get me wrong, it's lovely, it's just... I don't know what I expected.

**中文**

等会，我明白了。我辛辛苦苦在这杀了八百个来回，然后我的奖励就是……就是一尊你的雕像？没别的意思，我觉得这东西挺好的，但是就是……也许本来就没什么好期待的。

## `ZagreusHome_0679`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll temper my expectations just in case.

**中文**

我会降低自己的期待值，以防万一。

## `ZagreusHome_0680`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Another Skelly statue. Why am I not surprised.

**中文**

又一座骨头的雕像。为什么我一点也不惊讶。

## `ZagreusHome_0681`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Skelly, let me ask you something. Did you really commission three nearly-identical statues of yourself to goad me into using the Pact of Punishment?

**中文**

骨头，我想问你点事。你真的想用三个跟你差不多的雕像来煽动我使用惩罚契约？

## `ZagreusHome_0682`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're only saying that to get me to apologize and try and unlock the last of your three identical statues, aren't you.

**中文**

你这么说只是想让我道歉然后给你解锁最后一个雕像吧？

## `ZagreusHome_0683`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I guess they're stuck here then just like we are.

**中文**

我猜他们也和我们现在一样被困在这里了。

## `ZagreusHome_0684`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's another statue of you, Skelly.

**中文**

又一座你的雕像呢，骨头。

## `ZagreusHome_0685`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...It's amazing!!

**中文**

...好奇妙！！

## `ZagreusHome_0686`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You shouldn't have underestimated me, mate. But... I shouldn't have said such awful things about your statues. You must have really gone out of your way to get them here.

**中文**

你这样也太小看我了，伙计。但是……我不该说你雕像的坏话。你一定花了很大功夫才收集了这些东西。

## `ZagreusHome_0687`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We certainly have, Mate. And we certainly do.

**中文**

我们确实这样做了，朋友。而且我们还是会这么做。

## `ZagreusHome_0688`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I need to do what now?

**中文**

我需要知道现在是什么情况？

## `ZagreusHome_0689`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I need to fire up the Pact of Punishment to reveal this.

**中文**

我要一把火烧了惩罚契约，并把这件事揭露出来。

## `ZagreusHome_0690`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I need to use the Pact back in my Chambers.

**中文**

我需要使用密室里的契约。

## `ZagreusHome_0691`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sounds like the Pact in my Chambers is the only way I'm going to earn this thing.

**中文**

好像密室里的契约是我得到这件东西的唯一途径。

## `ZagreusHome_0692`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Pact of Punishment is the key to this, huh.

**中文**

惩罚契约是这件东西的关键，不是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0693`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's some sort of ridiculous challenge huh.

**中文**

这个挑战有点荒唐，对吧？

## `ZagreusHome_0694`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Someone's trying to goad me into using the Pact of Punishment.

**中文**

有人想诱骗我使用惩罚契约。

## `ZagreusHome_0695`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll have to survive the worst the Underworld has to offer before I can open this.

**中文**

想要打开它，我必须先经历冥界最严酷的考验并存活下来。

## `ZagreusHome_0696`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I haven't earned this yet.

**中文**

我还没有得到它。

## `ZagreusHome_0697`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I've yet to earn the right to look upon whatever this is.

**中文**

我还没有资格看它究竟是什么东西。

## `ZagreusHome_0698`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll need to survive the horrors of the Underworld first.

**中文**

我得先通过冥界的可怕考验。

## `ZagreusHome_0699`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I have to earn this I guess.

**中文**

我必须靠本事得到它。

## `ZagreusHome_0700`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Pact is fired up, just have to earn this.

**中文**

契约被付之一炬，我必须靠自己的本事。

## `ZagreusHome_0701`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Earning this is going to be tough.

**中文**

要得到它可不是一件容易的事。

## `ZagreusHome_0702`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Finally did it, what do I get?

**中文**

终于做到了，我得到的是什么东西？

## `ZagreusHome_0703`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I earned this fair and square.

**中文**

我光明正大地得到了它。

## `ZagreusHome_0704`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I have a feeling I know what this is...

**中文**

我感觉自己知道它是什么...

## `ZagreusHome_0705`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What'd I get?

**中文**

我得到了什么？

## `ZagreusHome_0706`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you, thank you, everyone, you're too kind.

**中文**

谢谢，谢谢各位，你们真的太好了。

## `ZagreusHome_0707`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Always wanted a statue of Skelly in my honor.

**中文**

我一直希望拥有一座骨头的雕像。

## `ZagreusHome_0708`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Beautiful craftwork I must say.

**中文**

工艺一流，我必须承认。

## `ZagreusHome_0709`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Exquisite use of form.

**中文**

形态逼真。

## `ZagreusHome_0710`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Breathtaking detail in the rib-cage area.

**中文**

胸腔部分的细节处理让人叹为观止。

## `ZagreusHome_0711`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The subtlety of the expression is a fascinating touch.

**中文**

生动的表情是点睛之笔。

## `ZagreusHome_0712`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

There's so much tension in the shape of it.

**中文**

外形充满张力。

## `ZagreusHome_0713`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Wonderful use of color.

**中文**

颜色的运用非常巧妙。

## `ZagreusHome_0714`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The raw emotion there is rather striking isn't it.

**中文**

它所散发出的饱满的感情令人印象深刻，不是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0715`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I practically can feel the anguish in this piece.

**中文**

我可以真切感受到它的愤怒。

## `ZagreusHome_0716`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The use of light and shadow is exceptional.

**中文**

光影的运用超乎想象。

## `ZagreusHome_0717`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think it represents... the plight of mortal life.

**中文**

我觉得它象征着...凡人的困境。

## `ZagreusHome_0718`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think it signifies the duality in all of us.

**中文**

我觉得它代表了我们每个人的二元性。

## `ZagreusHome_0719`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think it speaks to how our pride can be our downfall?

**中文**

我觉得它表现出骄傲如何使我们堕落？

## `ZagreusHome_0720`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How did they get the fingerbones to look so real.

**中文**

它们的指骨为什么看起来如此惟妙惟肖。

## `ZagreusHome_0721`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Truly the work of a great master.

**中文**

毫无疑问，这是大师的杰作。

## `ZagreusHome_0722`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Didn't catch the name of the artist...

**中文**

不知道出自哪个艺术家之手...

## `ZagreusHome_0723`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Would that there were, mate.

**中文**

是这样子吗，伙计？

## `ZagreusHome_0724`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Great!

**中文**

太好了！

## `ZagreusHome_0725`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Dionysus and I appreciate it, mate.

**中文**

迪奥尼索司和我都非常喜欢它，伙计。

## `ZagreusHome_0726`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I was kidding. Seriously.

**中文**

我在开玩笑呢，真的。

## `ZagreusHome_0727`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What have I done.

**中文**

我都做了什么。

## `ZagreusHome_0728`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's Orpheus' singing...

**中文**

那是俄耳甫斯的歌声...

## `ZagreusHome_0729`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Wait, he's singing about me...

**中文**

等等，他好像在唱我的事迹...

## `ZagreusHome_0730`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Orpheus, sorry to interrupt, it's just... I couldn't help but notice that new ballad that you're playing, there.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯，抱歉打断一下，就是……我注意到你正在演奏的那首新曲子。

## `ZagreusHome_0731`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, the thing is... none of the information in your hymn is accurate. It's nonsense. I was just kidding with you, mate! I tried to make that clear. You get so lively when I tell you my tall tales, you know? You're not angry with me, are you...?

**中文**

呃，问题是…你这些赞歌里的内容，没有一个是对的。都是胡说。那些都是我跟你开玩笑的，朋友！我告诉过你了。因为我跟你讲起那些夸张故事的时候，你就显得很活跃。你不会生我的气了吧…？

## `ZagreusHome_0732`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Um. Cheers.

**中文**

嗯。谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_0733`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I can't be completely sure but, what you said just now I think contained some of the component pieces of a compliment?

**中文**

我虽然不敢肯定，但你刚才说的话里是不是带有一点赞美的意思？

## `ZagreusHome_0734`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Your methods didn't seem to do the trick.

**中文**

你的办法好像不起作用。

## `ZagreusHome_0735`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It says I'm really the bastard son of Zeus, but also indelibly connected to the god of wine, and how your parents tore me to pieces, giving rise to mortals, but that I'm always going to be stuck here.

**中文**

歌里说我其实是宙斯的私生子，跟酒神也有不可分割的联系，还被泰坦们大卸八块，最后复活成为凡人，但我将永远被困在这里。

## `ZagreusHome_0736`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm not worried. Orpheus plays for a very small audience.

**中文**

我觉得不大可能。因为俄耳甫斯的听众并不多。

## `ZagreusHome_0737`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...I hope.

**中文**

...我希望。

## `ZagreusHome_0738`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Almost? I've been rampaging through the worst your realm has to offer. Causing you untold inconvenience and likely a fortune in repair costs. I thought you'd be upset.

**中文**

夸我？我可是在您地盘里迄今为止最恶劣的环境中杀了个死去活来。给您带来各种麻烦，还有一大笔修整费用。我还以为您会难过呢。

## `ZagreusHome_0739`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know you sound especially despicable when you say underlings, don't you?

**中文**

你说下人们这个词的时候听起来真的很卑鄙，知道吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0740`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Plenty of room for improvement...

**中文**

还有很大的进步空间...

## `ZagreusHome_0741`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You've done it now, Hypnos.

**中文**

你已经做到了，修普诺斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0742`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Alas.

**中文**

唉。

## `ZagreusHome_0743`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I found another weapon waiting for me in the courtyard, Achilles. Exagryph. I never entirely believed it was real.

**中文**

我在院子里看到了新的武器，阿喀琉斯。伊格瑞菲。我还以为这东西根本不存在呢。

## `ZagreusHome_0744`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll use it to the best of my ability, sir.

**中文**

我会尽自己所能使用它，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_0745`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, I'm concerned that Father's growing angrier with you. He thinks you've taken sides against him in all this. I don't want anything to happen.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，我担心父亲会生你的气。他觉得你面对这一切时没有跟他站一边。我不想你有什么事。

## `ZagreusHome_0746`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I... fear is for the weak.

**中文**

我...恐惧是弱者的专利。

## `ZagreusHome_0747`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sounds like you've been giving this a lot of thought, there, Meg. How do you figure?

**中文**

听起来你好像对此有什么想法，墨纪。你想说什么？

## `ZagreusHome_0748`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm not competing with you, Meg. I'm trying to get past you, to the surface.

**中文**

我又不是在跟你竞技，我是想从你这里通过而已，墨纪，然后去凡界。

## `ZagreusHome_0749`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nothing wrong with having help.

**中文**

接受帮助本身没有错。

## `ZagreusHome_0750`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm getting used to it, all right. I'll keep going, for as many chances as it takes. I'll look forward to our next appointment out there.

**中文**

我已经习惯了。我会继续前进，次数越多越好。那我就先提前预约跟你的下一次会面了。

## `ZagreusHome_0751`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We'll see.

**中文**

我们等着瞧。

## `ZagreusHome_0752`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'll fight my way out of this place, or earn it. The Pact is nothing to me other than a way to keep things lively.

**中文**

我会从这个地方杀出去，努力争取。这契约对我来说只是保持活力的一种方式罢了。

## `ZagreusHome_0753`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I do indeed.

**中文**

我确实如此。

## `ZagreusHome_0754`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Meg. Oh. I was not expecting company. Were you, just, casually snooping, or... is there something I can help you with?

**中文**

墨纪。噢。你怎么来了？你刚才，是有在偷看嘛，还是……有什么我能帮你的？

## `ZagreusHome_0755`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...She left. Great. Wonderful! I guess we'll just go back to killing each other repeatedly, then.

**中文**

……她走了。好。太好了！我猜我们又要开始不停地互相杀害对方了吧。

## `ZagreusHome_0756`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Megaera, I have to ask you something, please. We really need to talk.

**中文**

墨纪拉，我想问你一件事，可以吗？我们确实得谈谈。

## `ZagreusHome_0757`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I still have feelings for you. At least... I think I do. Isn't there... isn't there someway for us to, you know... work through all of this?

**中文**

我对你还有感情。至少……我这么觉得。难道……难道就没有其他办法，让我们一起……处理好这些事情吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0758`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...I haven't thought that far ahead. But I don't think it has to be this way. I don't hate you, Meg. I don't think I can ever hate you.

**中文**

……我还没想到那么多。但我觉得这也不是唯一的结局吧。我不恨你，墨纪。对你，我怎么也恨不起来。

## `ZagreusHome_0759`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Forget another something in my bedchambers, there, Meg?

**中文**

你忘了我的卧室里还有什么东西吗，墨纪？

## `ZagreusHome_0760`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm sorry. My mother's out there, somewhere. I won't rest until I can find her. You wouldn't understand.

**中文**

对不起。我的母亲就在那里的某个地方。在找到她之前，我是不会罢手的。你不会理解的。

## `ZagreusHome_0761`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What are you saying?

**中文**

你是什么意思？

## `ZagreusHome_0762`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You've never been there, have you? It's... lush? Is that a word? It feels closer to the surface. Closer to the sky.

**中文**

你还从来没去过，对吧？那是…酒吗？这是个词吗？感觉凡界近在咫尺。天空触手可及。

## `ZagreusHome_0763`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Meg, I understand you're under orders to kill me on sight out there, but... do you really have to go so hard on Sisyphus?

**中文**

嘿，墨纪，你收到的命令就是对我杀无赦，但是…你不用对西西弗斯也这样狠吧？

## `ZagreusHome_0764`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

He cheated death. Tricked Thanatos and maybe Father, too.

**中文**

他避免了死亡。戏耍了塔纳托斯，也许还有父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_0765`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What if he's changed? Or... is that impossible. No coming back from certain crimes.

**中文**

如果他改变了呢？噢…这有可能吗。本性难移。

## `ZagreusHome_0766`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You and your sisters really don't get along well, do you.

**中文**

你和你的姐妹确实处得不太好啊。

## `ZagreusHome_0767`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That doesn't sound like a healthy basis for a relationship between siblings.

**中文**

这听起来可不像是什么和谐的姐妹关系。

## `ZagreusHome_0768`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...Fair point. I... didn't mean to sound like I had any meaningful advice, there, Meg. We're stuck with our bloodlines and our relatives, aren't we.

**中文**

…确实。我…并不是让自己听起来像是在说教，墨纪。我们都被自己的血统和亲人给困住了。

## `ZagreusHome_0769`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Alecto's really something else. She must have been a real handful, huh?

**中文**

阿勒克图确实与众不同。她一定很能干吧。

## `ZagreusHome_0770`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

She could learn a thing or two from you, I'm sure. About having a little common decency, perhaps.

**中文**

我肯定她也能从你身上学到点什么。比方说举止得体，礼貌待人之类的。

## `ZagreusHome_0771`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, so, about Tisiphone, is... uh... everything all right with her? Talk of murder, all the time, and all.

**中文**

嗯，那么，关于提西福涅，她……呃……她没什么问题吧？她一直在说杀手杀手的，她没事吧？

## `ZagreusHome_0772`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No time for family or recreation, then, I take it? Pity what happens to those overly obsessed with work.

**中文**

没有家庭，没有娱乐，这样吗？工作狂真的很值得同情啊。

## `ZagreusHome_0773`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Attend away, Father.

**中文**

不要管我，父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_0774`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sorry, Meg.

**中文**

对不起，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_0775`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Huh.

**中文**

呃。

## `ZagreusHome_0776`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanatos is on assignment huh.

**中文**

塔纳托斯在执行任务。

## `ZagreusHome_0777`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Guess Than must be at work.

**中文**

我猜塔纳肯定在工作。

## `ZagreusHome_0778`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Where's Thanatos I wonder.

**中文**

我想知道塔纳托斯在哪里。

## `ZagreusHome_0779`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's not an empty gesture, Than. Besides, if I wasn't trying to stay focused, I'd be drinking it myself.

**中文**

这可不是没意义，塔纳。另外，如果我不是好好做我自己的事，我早就把它喝了。

## `ZagreusHome_0780`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, that's an idea. One of these days or nights, we'll have enough of this stuff to throw a feast to make all of Olympus jealous.

**中文**

这主意不错。总有一天，我们会有足够多的这些东西，全拿出来摆宴席，羡煞奥林匹斯众神。

## `ZagreusHome_0781`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Why I bother? Why, how else can I show you how I feel except by handing you these bottles from time to time?

**中文**

为何？除了时不时给你带来这些东西之外，我还能怎样表达自己的心情呢？

## `ZagreusHome_0782`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Don't be. That feast of ours is really going to be something, Than.

**中文**

不用不用。你说的那宴席，一定能成，塔纳。

## `ZagreusHome_0783`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey, Than... come split this with me for a bit. The coast is clear. The work can wait.

**中文**

嘿，塔纳...过来和我聊聊。没人在监视你，工作也可以先放一放。

## `ZagreusHome_0784`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No, I know exactly what you're saying, Than. I'm sorry that I'm putting you through all of this, it's just... it's something that I have to do. But, I'm glad we know the truth about me, now. Maybe we can move on from there.

**中文**

不，我知道你想说什么，塔纳。我很抱歉让你卷入到这事里头来，就是……这是我必须要做的。但是，我很高兴现在我们都知道我身世的真相了。或许咱们可以从现在开始，更进一步了。

## `ZagreusHome_0785`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You take care, too, Thanatos. Be seeing you.

**中文**

你也保重，塔纳托斯。再见。

## `ZagreusHome_0786`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Meg's sisters...

**中文**

墨纪的妹妹...

## `ZagreusHome_0787`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...I... I think so.

**中文**

...我...我想是吧。

## `ZagreusHome_0788`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sigh

**中文**

叹气

## `ZagreusHome_0789`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, I ran into Meg's sisters out there. We didn't really hit it off. Meg said they're not permitted in the House. Not that I'm complaining, but... what's that about?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，我遇到墨纪的姐妹了。我们似乎不太合得来。墨纪说她们不允许进入到这里来。不是我抱怨，但……这是为什么呢？

## `ZagreusHome_0790`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's what I was afraid of. Although, fear is for the weak.

**中文**

我担心的就是这个。不过，只有弱者才会感到恐惧。

## `ZagreusHome_0791`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I saw, Achilles. I think he's angry with me because of my decision to leave the House. Not that it's any of his business.

**中文**

我看到了，阿喀琉斯。我觉得因为我打算离开这里，所以他在生我的气。但这根本跟他没关系啊。

## `ZagreusHome_0792`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I will. You're right as always, sir.

**中文**

我会的。你总是对的，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_0793`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Urgh, cheers for that, Alecto.

**中文**

啊，谢谢你，阿勒克图。

## `ZagreusHome_0794`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Augh, that wasn't very kind of her, was it.

**中文**

啊，她这样的态度可不怎么友好。

## `ZagreusHome_0795`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Unf, I guess Alecto wins this round.

**中文**

呼，我猜阿勒克图赢了这局。

## `ZagreusHome_0796`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I much prefer Meg's whip, I have to say...

**中文**

我不得不说，我更喜欢墨纪的表现...

## `ZagreusHome_0797`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well I hope she's happy.

**中文**

好吧，我希望她会高兴。

## `ZagreusHome_0798`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll get you back, Alecto.

**中文**

我会报仇的，阿勒克图。

## `ZagreusHome_0799`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That Tisiphone, ughh...

**中文**

那个提西福涅，呃...

## `ZagreusHome_0800`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

She murdered me all right...

**中文**

她简直是在虐待我...

## `ZagreusHome_0801`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Eugh, I miss Meg...

**中文**

哦，我想念墨纪...

## `ZagreusHome_0802`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That... was disturbing.

**中文**

这...太令人心烦了。

## `ZagreusHome_0803`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Was that a murder...?

**中文**

这是一场谋杀吗...？

## `ZagreusHome_0804`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ugh, sorry, Thanatos...

**中文**

呃，不好意思，塔纳托斯……

## `ZagreusHome_0805`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not even Than could get me out of that one.

**中文**

即使塔纳也没办法让我解脱。

## `ZagreusHome_0806`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Than tried to help at least.

**中文**

至少塔纳在努力提供帮助。

## `ZagreusHome_0807`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Than won't be happy about that.

**中文**

塔纳不会为此感到开心的。

## `ZagreusHome_0808`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanatos couldn't save me there.

**中文**

塔纳托斯也没办法把我解救出来。

## `ZagreusHome_0809`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Than tried his best at least...

**中文**

至少塔纳尽力了...

## `ZagreusHome_0810`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Why? You've been out. It's thanks to you the mortals end up down here maybe half the time.

**中文**

为什么？你出去过。多亏了你，那些凡人才有一半的时间都呆在这个地方。

## `ZagreusHome_0811`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No. But I look forward to finding out for myself.

**中文**

不知道。但是我想自己去寻找答案。

## `ZagreusHome_0812`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Don't be ridiculous, Than.

**中文**

别太荒唐了，塔纳。

## `ZagreusHome_0813`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Than, you know, Hypnos, he adores you. Besides just driving you to madness, now and then.

**中文**

塔纳，你知道吗，修普诺斯，他可喜欢你了。虽然他可能时不时让你觉得崩溃。

## `ZagreusHome_0814`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, dying repeatedly can end up bringing people closer, I suppose.

**中文**

嗯，看样子重复死亡可以让大家的关系更加亲密。

## `ZagreusHome_0815`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

He's like an annoying little brother to me.

**中文**

他对我来说就像个恼人的弟弟。

## `ZagreusHome_0816`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's not like that, Than. She's there to slow me down. Get in my way.

**中文**

事情不是那样的，塔纳。她是为了拖慢我的脚步。阻止我的前进。

## `ZagreusHome_0817`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yeah... her sisters are back. They're... hard to deal with. I don't think Meg is on good terms with either one.

**中文**

是啊……她的姐妹们回来了。她们…很难对付。我觉得墨纪跟她们的关系不是很好。

## `ZagreusHome_0818`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Oh.

**中文**

...哦。

## `ZagreusHome_0819`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You call that paradise? It's just a bunch of shades bored out of their former skulls, itching for a chance to feel alive again.

**中文**

你管那叫仙境？不过是一群好战的幽灵，通过战斗来让自己有活着的感觉。

## `ZagreusHome_0820`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You'll find out soon enough.

**中文**

你很快就会知道真相。

## `ZagreusHome_0821`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Than. Come join me in the lounge for old time's sake. The coast is clear. The work can wait.

**中文**

嗨，塔纳。看在咱俩交情的份上，来休息室陪我一下吧。不会有事的。工作待会再做。

## `ZagreusHome_0822`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...See you, Than.

**中文**

……再见，塔纳。

## `ZagreusHome_0823`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Urgh, that's one bad bull.

**中文**

啊，那头讨厌的公牛。

## `ZagreusHome_0824`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ungh, I got the horns.

**中文**

哦，我抓到牛角了。

## `ZagreusHome_0825`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Argh, the dreaded Minotaur.

**中文**

啊，可怕的弥诺陶。

## `ZagreusHome_0826`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Uff, blasted ghost Minotaur.

**中文**

呼，该死的弥诺陶。

## `ZagreusHome_0827`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nghh, that bull hits rather hard.

**中文**

呀，那头公牛的撞击力太强了。

## `ZagreusHome_0828`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Rngh, I swear, that Theseus...

**中文**

嗯，我发誓，那个忒修斯...

## `ZagreusHome_0829`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Khh, losing to Theseus...

**中文**

哈，败给了忒修斯……

## `ZagreusHome_0830`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Guh, that no-good loudmouth Theseus.

**中文**

呜，一无是处的大嗓门忒修斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0831`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ungh, I want a rematch, now.

**中文**

啊，我要再比一场，就现在。

## `ZagreusHome_0832`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Rmm, how could I lose to him.

**中文**

唉，我怎么可能输给他。

## `ZagreusHome_0833`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Probably!

**中文**

或许！

## `ZagreusHome_0834`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh well!

**中文**

哦，好吧！

## `ZagreusHome_0835`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yeah.

**中文**

耶。

## `ZagreusHome_0836`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Maybe!

**中文**

也许！

## `ZagreusHome_0837`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Next time!

**中文**

下一次！

## `ZagreusHome_0838`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sure!

**中文**

当然！

## `ZagreusHome_0839`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Intruders in Elysium? I swear.

**中文**

至福乐土的入侵者？可不是我啊。

## `ZagreusHome_0840`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I didn't!

**中文**

我没有！

## `ZagreusHome_0841`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No.

**中文**

不。

## `ZagreusHome_0842`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sorry, no.

**中文**

对不起，不。

## `ZagreusHome_0843`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nope, none whatsoever.

**中文**

不，完全不可能。

## `ZagreusHome_0844`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hahaha, no.

**中文**

哈哈，不。

## `ZagreusHome_0845`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Did not come up.

**中文**

没有出现。

## `ZagreusHome_0846`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This is a real honor, Hypnos. Thanks?

**中文**

真吓人，修普诺斯。谢谢你？

## `ZagreusHome_0847`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Enjoy it, Hypnos!

**中文**

我很喜欢，修普诺斯！

## `ZagreusHome_0848`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I did, Achilles. It's crawling with great warriors still looking for a fight. It's not exactly what I was expecting.

**中文**

没错，阿喀琉斯。那里满是渴望战斗的伟大战士。跟我期待的不太一样。

## `ZagreusHome_0849`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Their pride. I'll remember that.

**中文**

他们的意得志满，我不会忘记。

## `ZagreusHome_0850`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's just... within Elysium, I happened on a shade, who said he knew you. Said he misses you. Do you know a Patroclus, sir?

**中文**

就是…我在至福乐土，遇到了一个暗灵，他说他认识你。说他想你。你认识普特洛克勒斯吗，老师？

## `ZagreusHome_0851`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

He... I don't know. He seems like he's known better times than these. Is... there something I should say to him, if I run into him again?

**中文**

他…我不知道。他好像经历过比这更好的时光。如果下次我再遇到他，有什么要我跟他说的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0852`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's just... within Elysium, I happened on a shade. It's your Patroclus. He's all right, but he seems like he's known better times than these. Is there something I should say to him, if I run into him again?

**中文**

就是…我在至福乐土，碰到了一个幽灵。就是你说的普特洛克勒斯。他过得不错，不过他好像经历过比这更好的时光。如果下次我再遇到他，有什么要我跟他说的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0853`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I... take care, then, sir.

**中文**

我...请你保重，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_0854`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I understand. What can I do about this, sir? There must be something. But, you have to let me know what's going on.

**中文**

我明白。我能做点什么，老师？一定有什么事。但是，你得让我知道到底怎么回事。

## `ZagreusHome_0855`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You wanted to see Patroclus. But, he's in Elysium, while you... oh, no.

**中文**

你想要见到普特洛克勒斯。但是他在至福乐土，而你…噢不。

## `ZagreusHome_0856`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...All right.

**中文**

...好吧。

## `ZagreusHome_0857`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You have to tell me more about this pact you signed, Achilles. You and Patroclus... so, you're doomed to never be together, then?

**中文**

你得再和我说说你签订的那个契约，阿喀琉斯。你和普特洛克勒斯……就，永远没办法在一起了吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0858`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

There has to be a way for me to make this right. Not just for him, for you.

**中文**

我一定能找到改变这件事的办法。不仅仅为他，也为你。

## `ZagreusHome_0859`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I do. I think.

**中文**

好的。

## `ZagreusHome_0860`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Achilles, please... are you certain that I mustn't talk to Father about the pact you signed for Patroclus? He thinks that you abandoned him.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，拜托...你确定不让我和父亲说起你和普特洛克勒斯所签的契约？他认为你抛弃了他。

## `ZagreusHome_0861`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I will, sir.

**中文**

我会的，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_0862`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

First, you tell me when I can get out of there. The path guarded by Theseus was sealed! Is there even a surface? Is the outside world all a lie?!

**中文**

首先，你得告诉我什么时候才能离开冥界。忒修斯看守的通道是条死路！真的存在凡界这种地方吗？外部世界什么的都是骗人的？！

## `ZagreusHome_0863`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Ongoing Underworld renovations?

**中文**

冥界仍在修整当中？

## `ZagreusHome_0864`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

And then, we'll both find out.

**中文**

到那时，我们都会知道真相。

## `ZagreusHome_0865`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How long have you been waiting to use that line, Father? Wouldn't put it past you to have sent the Bull of Minos to Elysium entirely so we could have this conversation now.

**中文**

你想说这句话很久了吧，父亲？我怀疑你把米诺斯的公牛送到至福乐土就是为了有朝一日能对我说这句话。

## `ZagreusHome_0866`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

He's working for you, isn't he? You think he can stop me.

**中文**

他为你做事对吧？你以为他能拦得住我。

## `ZagreusHome_0867`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Crush the souls of Elysium's Exalted shades to stop them from regenerating over and over. I'll definitely do that next time I'm there.

**中文**

碾碎至福乐土里这些高贵暗灵的魂，不然它们会不停地复活。下次一定记得。

## `ZagreusHome_0868`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Admit it, Father. Theseus was the only hero you were able to recruit to stand against me. All the while he gets to boast about being the champion. What, was Heracles too busy for the task?

**中文**

少来了，父亲。我看你是除了忒修斯之外，根本找不到其他愿意阻止我的英雄吧。这人张口闭口就吹自己是最强战士。我寻思，赫拉克勒斯是太忙没时间噢？

## `ZagreusHome_0869`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They should have asked the expert I suppose.

**中文**

我觉得他们本该去请教一下专家。

## `ZagreusHome_0870`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Hypnos? This is for all the well-meaning advice you've given me. A little something from a certain double-bladed-axe-wielding, bull-headed, oversized monster of a man who you admire?

**中文**

嗨，修普诺斯？感谢你一直以来给我的好建议。是从你的偶像，一位高大威猛挥舞着双头斧的牛头怪物那里拿到的。

## `ZagreusHome_0871`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm counting that one as a victory.

**中文**

我把它当做一次胜利。

## `ZagreusHome_0872`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll make it out of here yet.

**中文**

我会想办法离开这里。

## `ZagreusHome_0873`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That ought to make Theseus shut it for a bit.

**中文**

这应该会让忒修斯消停一会儿。

## `ZagreusHome_0874`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Feels like it's been a while, everybody.

**中文**

感觉过了很长时间啊，各位。

## `ZagreusHome_0875`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Urgh, well that was pretty good, all in all.

**中文**

呃，总的来说还不错。

## `ZagreusHome_0876`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fought to Elysium and back.

**中文**

一路打到至福乐土，然后凯旋归来。

## `ZagreusHome_0877`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not bad at all, really.

**中文**

真的非常不错。

## `ZagreusHome_0878`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Please, everybody, hold your applause.

**中文**

各位，请先不要鼓掌。

## `ZagreusHome_0879`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

But now you're both here, in the Underworld! Can't you go seek him out? Or alter your arrangement with my father?

**中文**

但你们现在都在冥界了啊！你不能去找他吗？或者跟我父亲修改一下你们的协定？

## `ZagreusHome_0880`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll do what I can...

**中文**

我会尽力去做...

## `ZagreusHome_0881`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

The... fallen warrior in Elysium we spoke of. He seems like a good man, though... was he always the despondent sort?

**中文**

那位…我们提到过的至福乐土的陨落的战士。他看上去人不错，但是…他的性格就是那样沮丧吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0882`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hah! I'm restless all the time. I can only imagine.

**中文**

哈！我一直都处于焦躁不安的状态。我只能想象。

## `ZagreusHome_0883`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

He shouldn't, sir. And neither should you.

**中文**

他不应该，先生。你也不应该。

## `ZagreusHome_0884`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, sir... within the Codex, you'd written something, about someone with whom you shared your life... when you were flesh and blood?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，老师…手稿里，你写的一些内容，里头说你在自己有血有肉还活着的时候…跟另一个人共享了自己的生命？

## `ZagreusHome_0885`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh. Of course, sir.

**中文**

哦，当然了，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_0886`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Achilles sir, there's something I've been wondering, about your Codex. The words are yours, and yet it's through my actions that I'm able to absorb them. Is that how mortal scriptures always work?

**中文**

呃，阿喀琉斯老师，关于你的手稿，我有些问题想问一下。这些都是你写的，但是我要通过自己的行动才可以理解其中的意思。凡人的手稿也是这样的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0887`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I see. I think. I'm grateful for it, sir.

**中文**

我知道了，我会心怀感激的，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_0888`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll get right on that.

**中文**

我会立即去做。

## `ZagreusHome_0889`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It doesn't.

**中文**

它不会。

## `ZagreusHome_0890`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father, you always said the Olympians wanted nothing to do with our kind. But doesn't Charon work directly with Hermes? Maybe it's you who wants nothing to do with them.

**中文**

父亲，你总说奥林匹斯那帮人不想跟我们有任何瓜葛。但是卡戎不就跟赫尔墨斯共事吗？所以这只是你自己单方面不想和他们有瓜葛吧。

## `ZagreusHome_0891`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Want me to pitch in? Just give me your blessing and I'll head straight for the surface.

**中文**

要我帮忙吗？给我你的庇佑，然后我立刻就直冲凡界。

## `ZagreusHome_0892`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You're right. I don't.

**中文**

你说对了。我不会。

## `ZagreusHome_0893`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You mean Mother. How would you even know? You said reaching the surface was impossible as well, and I'm in striking distance.

**中文**

你在说母亲。你怎么知道呢？你还说我不可能到达凡界，但我现在近在咫尺。

## `ZagreusHome_0894`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sorry, Father. I'm not afraid of you.

**中文**

对不起，父亲。但我不怕你。

## `ZagreusHome_0895`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ungh, damn you, Father.

**中文**

啊，真该死，父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_0896`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Urgh, Father...

**中文**

啊，父亲...

## `ZagreusHome_0897`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Angh, he's strong, I'll give him that.

**中文**

啊，他很强，我屈服了。

## `ZagreusHome_0898`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ough, he's tough for an old man.

**中文**

噢啊，真是个难缠的老头。

## `ZagreusHome_0899`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Guh, how am I going to beat him.

**中文**

呜，我怎么才能打败他。

## `ZagreusHome_0900`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mmph, got me again, damn it.

**中文**

嗯，又输了，该死。

## `ZagreusHome_0901`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Augh, I'll get you for this, Father.

**中文**

啊，我会让你为此付出代价，父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_0902`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Finally showed you, Father.

**中文**

终于让你见识到了，父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_0903`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Showed you that time, Father.

**中文**

让你见识到了厉害，父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_0904`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I did it! I think?

**中文**

我觉得自己成功了？

## `ZagreusHome_0905`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well, this is going to be awkward.

**中文**

好吧，感觉有点尴尬。

## `ZagreusHome_0906`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm back, huh.

**中文**

我回来了。

## `ZagreusHome_0907`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Father's not back yet, huh.

**中文**

父亲还没回来？

## `ZagreusHome_0908`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Father's still up there.

**中文**

父亲还在那里。

## `ZagreusHome_0909`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Father...

**中文**

父亲...

## `ZagreusHome_0910`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cerberus must be on duty still.

**中文**

刻耳柏洛斯肯定还在坚守岗位。

## `ZagreusHome_0911`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cerberus must still be back up top.

**中文**

刻耳柏洛斯肯定还在上面。

## `ZagreusHome_0912`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cerberus is still at work I guess.

**中文**

我猜刻耳柏洛斯还在工作。

## `ZagreusHome_0913`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No. Wait, what? I just vanquished you in single combat, and that's all you have to say to me?

**中文**

等下，就这？我刚刚打败你了吧，你就没什么其他可说的？

## `ZagreusHome_0914`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You can't be serious. You're going to pretend as though it never happened?

**中文**

你认真的？你就装作无事发生一样？

## `ZagreusHome_0915`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Guess I'll keep fighting you just for the sake of it then.

**中文**

我猜我会和你战斗到底，没有其他选择。

## `ZagreusHome_0916`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

There's a good boy.

**中文**

好样的，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusHome_0917`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Always have more pets for the greatest boy.

**中文**

最厉害的乖狗狗总能得到更多奖励。

## `ZagreusHome_0918`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How's my favorite boy doing, huh?

**中文**

我最心爱的乖狗狗表现如何？

## `ZagreusHome_0919`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Who's the best three-headed boy?

**中文**

最厉害的长着三颗头的是谁？

## `ZagreusHome_0920`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You've always been there for me, boy.

**中文**

你总是在我需要的时候出现，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusHome_0921`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You are the best, you know that, don't you, boy?

**中文**

你是最棒的，你知道吗，乖狗狗？

## `ZagreusHome_0922`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My mean old father's nice to you at least, isn't he, boy?

**中文**

至少我那刻薄的老爸对你很好，不是吗，乖狗狗？

## `ZagreusHome_0923`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You had enough to eat lately, haven't you boy?

**中文**

你最近吃得也不错吧，乖狗狗？

## `ZagreusHome_0924`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You like it when you get pets like this, don't you boy.

**中文**

你特别喜欢我这么摸摸你，是不是，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusHome_0925`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You want more pets, I got more pets here, boy.

**中文**

你想要更多抚摸，我就可以给更多，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusHome_0926`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Always feel better talking to you, boy.

**中文**

和你说话总是令我开心，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusHome_0927`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You don't let anybody in or out of this place, do you, boy.

**中文**

你不可以让任何人出入这个地方，知道吗，乖狗狗？

## `ZagreusHome_0928`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

More pets? More pets.

**中文**

多点抚摸？那就多点。

## `ZagreusHome_0929`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Circumstances call for more pets.

**中文**

现在就是得多摸一摸你。

## `ZagreusHome_0930`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

More pets are certainly in order here.

**中文**

毫无疑问，你需要更多抚摸。

## `ZagreusHome_0931`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You're my good boy! Yes you are.

**中文**

你真是好样的，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusHome_0932`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That thing doesn't look particularly comfortable.

**中文**

那东西看起来尤其不顺眼。

## `ZagreusHome_0933`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'd bring some for you if I could!

**中文**

如果可以的话，我会给你带点过来。

## `ZagreusHome_0934`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Definitely true.

**中文**

千真万确。

## `ZagreusHome_0935`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yes, well...

**中文**

那好吧...

## `ZagreusHome_0936`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Always a pleasure, Hypnos.

**中文**

是我的荣幸，修普诺斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0937`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Blasted Satyrs.

**中文**

该死的萨提尔。

## `ZagreusHome_0938`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Don't want to talk about it.

**中文**

不想谈论它。

## `ZagreusHome_0939`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Why don't you ask Father when he's back.

**中文**

你为什么不等到父亲回来时问他。

## `ZagreusHome_0940`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I guess!

**中文**

我猜！

## `ZagreusHome_0941`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm aware.

**中文**

我意识到了。

## `ZagreusHome_0942`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Perhaps you ought to have me bound next to Prometheus, and have the dreaded Eagle peck my liver for eternity?

**中文**

把我跟普罗米修斯栓一块儿，再派一只大鸟不停地啄食我的内脏就行了。

## `ZagreusHome_0943`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

See you up at the Temple then, I guess.

**中文**

我们神庙见。

## `ZagreusHome_0944`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Something the matter, there, Father? You seem a little quieter than usual. Dare I say even a little somber and remorseful, for some reason?

**中文**

怎么回事，父亲？您今天好像不怎么爱说话。甚至看上去好像有点忧郁，又有点懊恼。

## `ZagreusHome_0945`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Until such place and time, then.

**中文**

到时再见，不见不散。

## `ZagreusHome_0946`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So you're really not going to answer my question about Mother from the other time, until the Underworld renovations are complete?

**中文**

所以你就非得等冥界修整结束之后，再回答我之前问的有关母亲的问题吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0947`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Excellent, can't wait!

**中文**

太好了，我已经迫不及待了。

## `ZagreusHome_0948`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I thought you'd figured out by now that guilting me into abandoning my quest won't work. You're the one who should feel guilty, forcing Cerberus to block my path.

**中文**

你应该知道，光靠让我内疚是不可能让我放弃行动的。你才是那个应该内疚的人，你命令刻耳柏洛斯去阻止我前进。

## `ZagreusHome_0949`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

And there it is again.

**中文**

又是这样。

## `ZagreusHome_0950`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

They're not doing your bidding, yet they're every bit as bothersome as all your wretched servants I've met. I'm surprised you aren't getting along.

**中文**

它们根本不听你指挥，并且跟你手下那些难缠的仆人一样让人恼火。看来你们相处的不大好啊。

## `ZagreusHome_0951`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Pushovers.

**中文**

易如反掌。

## `ZagreusHome_0952`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey good to see you back here, boy. I'm glad you're back on guard duty! Even if it means having to find a way past you. I know you're just doing your job.

**中文**

能回来见到你真好，乖乖。真高兴你又能继续看守了！尽管这说明我得想办法通过你的看守。我知道你只是在做自己的工作而已。

## `ZagreusHome_0953`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Don't worry, sir. I've run into Cerberus up top, all right. Even if he's technically Father's pet, he'd never bare his fangs at me. No matter how much Father might wish otherwise.

**中文**

没事，老师。我已经在上面跟他见过面了。尽管他是父亲的宠物，但他也从没对我凶过。不管父亲有多想他这么做。

## `ZagreusHome_0954`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, at least I know who will be waiting for me the next time I climb all the way to the Temple of Styx and manage my way out.

**中文**

好吧，至少我知道下次我一路爬到冥河神庙并设法出去时，谁会等着我。

## `ZagreusHome_0955`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just a god...

**中文**

只是神而已...

## `ZagreusHome_0956`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, tell me something, sir. Any rumors swirling about the House regarding the outcome of a certain interaction I had with my father recently? Involving a great deal of violence? Because I'd like for everyone to know: I won.

**中文**

跟我讲讲，老师。关于我最近一次跟我父亲交流会面的情况，大家有没有在讨论啊，有没有什么传闻之类的？非常暴力的那种？因为我想让大家都知道：我赢啦。

## `ZagreusHome_0957`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thank you, sir. I didn't know whether I had it in me. As for the other bit, I'll keep it quiet, then, for now. I probably shouldn't be tying my sense of self-worth to praise from friends, acquaintances, and strangers, anyway.

**中文**

谢谢你，老师。我都不知道我自己能成。至于其他部分，我现在就暂时不说了。无论怎样，我可能不应该把我的自我价值感跟朋友、老熟人或者陌生人对我的称赞联系起来。

## `ZagreusHome_0958`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, it's just... I've never slain a living creature, sir. Until my recent journey to the Temple up above. I know they're just Satyrs and vermin, but... it's not the same as sending wretches to the depths.

**中文**

噢，就是…我从来没杀死过一个活着的生物，老师。直到我去到了上面的神殿。我知道它们只是些萨提尔和鼠兽，但是…这跟把死人送去深渊不一样。

## `ZagreusHome_0959`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Now that you mention it, I have to say I'm glad we can accommodate all former-living things. And at least mortals get to rest assured knowing what happens once they die! Must really ease the mind.

**中文**

既然你都提到了，我得说，我很高兴我们能接纳所有活过的东西。至少凡人可以放心，知道他们死后会去什么地方！这样能稳定他们的情绪。

## `ZagreusHome_0960`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Where is everybody...

**中文**

人都到哪里去了...

## `ZagreusHome_0961`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Emptier than usual around here...

**中文**

比原来更加空空荡荡...

## `ZagreusHome_0962`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Father and Cerberus must still be up there.

**中文**

父亲和刻耳柏洛斯肯定还在上面。

## `ZagreusHome_0963`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cerberus...? Father...

**中文**

刻耳柏洛斯...？父亲...

## `ZagreusHome_0964`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No sign of Cerberus or Father.

**中文**

没见到刻耳柏洛斯和父亲的身影。

## `ZagreusHome_0965`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Who's that sniveling shade to your immediate left, there, Father? Already found someone new to do the busywork. And here I thought I was utterly indispensable.

**中文**

你左边那位哭哭啼啼的幽灵是谁，父亲？看来你已经找到其他人来做这些无聊工作了。我还以为我是无可替代的呢。

## `ZagreusHome_0966`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're trying to guilt me into funding the exorbitant furnishings you desire.

**中文**

你想道德绑架我让我为你这些贵得要死的家具出钱？

## `ZagreusHome_0967`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nice.

**中文**

漂亮。

## `ZagreusHome_0968`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, you know, just giving back to the community. Figure if I'm to keep getting dragged back here it might as well be somewhat livable.

**中文**

噢，我只是知恩图报罢了。如果我不断被强行送回这里，这地方说不定真的就适合住人了。

## `ZagreusHome_0969`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well aware, thanks.

**中文**

明白，谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_0970`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Guess what, Father? The lounge is all tidied up. Your House Contractor there is quite efficient once you muster up the necessary funds. You should try it sometime!

**中文**

你猜怎么着，父亲？休息室整理好了。只要钱到位了，你那位承包商办起事来效率过人。有时间你也试试吧！

## `ZagreusHome_0971`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What if I replaced every furnishing in this blasted place, would you treat me any better then?

**中文**

如果我把这破地方的家具全部换新，您能不能对我态度好点？

## `ZagreusHome_0972`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh, it's no trouble at all.

**中文**

这点不算什么。

## `ZagreusHome_0973`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Saw your new Pact of Punishment out in the courtyard, Father. Impressed that you could find a parchment large enough to fit all the unpleasant stipulations. So you're taking even harsher measures to contain me?

**中文**

看到您在院子里的新的惩罚契约了，父亲。真不愧是您啊，能找到那么大的羊皮纸来写那么多的害人条款。所以您现在为了管住我，要开始动用更严格的手段了嘛？

## `ZagreusHome_0974`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father, I met an oak nymph called Eurydice. You know her name, I think. Is it really true what happened between her and Orpheus? They say you almost let her out, so why not me?

**中文**

父亲，我遇到了一位叫欧律狄克的橡树仙女。你应该知道她。她跟俄耳甫斯之间的事是真的吗？他们说你差点让她离开了这里，那你为什么不让我离开？

## `ZagreusHome_0975`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So you tricked him. Or maybe offered him a deal in bad faith. No matter. What's it going to take to waive the separation order between them?

**中文**

你把他耍了。或者说跟他进行了一次不公平交易。无所谓。要怎样才能撤销他们之间的分居令？

## `ZagreusHome_0976`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Tsch.

**中文**

切。

## `ZagreusHome_0977`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father, let's say, hypothetically, that I could pay the dues to waive the separation order between Orpheus and Eurydice. Would you honor the new terms, or spitefully cheat me out of a fair deal?

**中文**

父亲大人，假设我愿意付出代价，撤销俄耳甫斯和欧律狄克之间的分居令。您可以接受新的条款吗，还是会故意骗我答应你的无理要求？

## `ZagreusHome_0978`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We'll see.

**中文**

走着瞧

## `ZagreusHome_0979`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

The weapons in the courtyard. Of course you know about them, by now. I stole them. Why not just take them from me? I'd be stuck.

**中文**

那些武器就在院子里，你现在当然知道它们了。我偷走了它们。你为什么不直接从我这把它们收回去？这样我就无处可逃了。

## `ZagreusHome_0980`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sounds good.

**中文**

听上去不错。

## `ZagreusHome_0981`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Megaera and her sisters ganged up on me that time. I didn't think anyone could talk her into working with Alecto and Tisiphone again. Or even be in the same chamber.

**中文**

墨纪拉和她的姐妹联合起来对付我了。我是真没想到还有谁能说服她跟阿勒克图以及提西福涅合作。甚至说把她们三个放到一个房间。

## `ZagreusHome_0982`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Didn't think things could get any more awkward with her.

**中文**

我可真没想到，跟她一起，事情居然还能变得更尴尬。

## `ZagreusHome_0983`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, wanted to ask you more about the weapons I've been using. They can change shape! So, they've molded themselves to suit me, and remember their past wielders, something like that?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，我想问问你更多有关我在用的这把武器的问题。它们可以变形！所以，它们会为了适应我而改变形态，同时还记得自己的前任主人，这样的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_0984`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

In the mean time, I'll take good care of them.

**中文**

在这段时间里，我会好好关照他们的。

## `ZagreusHome_0985`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, that? It's nothing. Though I'm glad it's made a difference for the better. Our contractor's been doing all the work, I'm just putting in the orders.

**中文**

噢，就这？没什么的。能有好的改变，我也很高兴。都是承包商的功劳，我只不过下几个订单而已。

## `ZagreusHome_0986`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Glad to hear it.

**中文**

很高兴你这么说。

## `ZagreusHome_0987`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, I've been meaning to ask you about the odd skeleton usually standing around in the courtyard. Old acquaintance of yours? A hireling, perhaps?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，我一直想问你，院子里那个奇怪骷髅的事。是你的熟人吗？大概是雇来的吧？

## `ZagreusHome_0988`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't know that that's it. So neither of us has any idea where he's from or how he got there, huh. Can you think of any leads? Nyx doesn't seem to know him, either.

**中文**

我也是一头雾水。看来我俩都搞不清楚他从哪来，怎么来的。你有头绪吗？倪克斯好像也不认识他。

## `ZagreusHome_0989`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not a concern, just a morbid curiosity.

**中文**

不是关心，就是单纯的好奇而已。

## `ZagreusHome_0990`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can!

**中文**

我可以的！

## `ZagreusHome_0991`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's why he's stuck here, huh.

**中文**

呵，难怪他困在这里。

## `ZagreusHome_0992`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Least we have a backup singer if we need one.

**中文**

起码现在我们有了一位后备乐师。

## `ZagreusHome_0993`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I guess there are a lot of gemstones here.

**中文**

这里应该有很多宝石。

## `ZagreusHome_0994`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Now I feel bad.

**中文**

我感觉很难受。

## `ZagreusHome_0995`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ever since? It's not been that long.

**中文**

自打那时起？还没那么久吧。

## `ZagreusHome_0996`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's supposed to be updated every so often but it's always Thanatos.

**中文**

本该每隔段时间就更新一下，但是每次都是塔纳托斯。

## `ZagreusHome_0997`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I wish I did not know that.

**中文**

我要是不知道那件事就好了。

## `ZagreusHome_0998`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Say, Orpheus. You wouldn't happen to know the nymph Eurydice, would you? Tall, woodsy type? I get the impression maybe you know each other.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯。你不会碰巧认识一个叫欧律狄克的仙女吧？高高的，像树一样？从我的经历来看，你们两个可能互相认识。

## `ZagreusHome_0999`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I saw her, mate. In Asphodel. She's doing fine up there! All on her own. The two of you, you used to know each other, then?

**中文**

我见到她了，伙计。在水仙花平原。她过得还不错！只身一人。你们两个应该都认识彼此吧？

## `ZagreusHome_1000`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Ah, well? Don't you have a message I could bring to her, if we should meet again? Something like that?

**中文**

好吧？如果我再遇到她，你就没有什么话想跟她说的吗？没什么想说的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1001`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You can't be serious.

**中文**

你开玩笑吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1002`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Say, Orpheus. You're not going to believe this. I happened on a certain nymph during my journeys recently. It was Eurydice. Tall, woodsy type?

**中文**

俄耳甫斯。你不会相信的。我最近外出的时候，遇到了一位仙女。它叫欧律狄克。高高的，像树一样？

## `ZagreusHome_1003`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I saw her, mate. In Asphodel. She's doing fine up there! All on her own. You said the two of you were very close, isn't that right?

**中文**

我见到她了，伙计。在水仙花平原。她过得还不错！只身一人。你说过你们两个人很亲近，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1004`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Orpheus, I saw Eurydice again. I... couldn't bring myself to do exactly as you asked, I... sort of mentioned you.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯，我又看到欧律狄克了。我…我没能完全按你的要求来，我…提到你了。

## `ZagreusHome_1005`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You seemed to be a bit of a sore subject, yes. What happened to the two of you, if I may ask?

**中文**

嗯，说到你的时候，气氛有点冷。你俩之间到底怎么了，我想问问。

## `ZagreusHome_1006`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll have to get to the bottom of this later.

**中文**

这件事以后再弄清楚吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1007`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You seemed to be a bit of a sore subject, yes. You really almost freed her from the Underworld, huh?

**中文**

嗯，说到你的时候，气氛有点冷。你真的差点就把她带离冥界了，对吧？

## `ZagreusHome_1008`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Take it easy, mate.

**中文**

悠着点，朋友。

## `ZagreusHome_1009`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

She's all right, Orpheus. Seems comfortable within her little place in Asphodel. The only thing that seems to bother her at all is... when I mention you. Is there nothing you can do to patch things up?

**中文**

她还好啦，俄耳甫斯。看上去她在水仙花平原自己的一小块地方过得挺好的。唯一让她感到烦恼的…就是我提到你的时候。你就不能做点什么来弥补一下吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1010`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

But that's just a technicality! I mean, you're here, she's there! So what if you're not breathing anymore, you have all eternity ahead of you. Don't you want to see her again?

**中文**

但是只是理论上是这样！我是说，你在这头，她在那头！既然你是一个死人了，你就有大把大把的时间了。你就不想再见她一面吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1011`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You're not making this easy, Orpheus.

**中文**

你完全是在帮倒忙，俄耳甫斯。

## `ZagreusHome_1012`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I knew she was a talented musician, mate, but you never let on that she is an amazing chef, as well. She's been hospitable to me each time we've met. Though you still seem to be a bit of a sore subject.

**中文**

我知道她曾是一个才华横溢的乐师，伙计，但是你从没提过她惊人的料理手艺。每次见面她都对我很热情。不过一提到你气氛就凝重起来。

## `ZagreusHome_1013`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm working on it, mate.

**中文**

我还在努力呢，伙计。

## `ZagreusHome_1014`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Orpheus, I don't suppose you know how come I keep running into Eurydice in particular out there? For all the shades resting eternally in Asphodel, it seems beyond coincidence I always find the one that's dear to you.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯，你大概不知道为什么我总在外面碰到欧律狄克吧？水仙花平原里那么多的暗灵，我总能找到你爱的那一个，这可不是什么巧合。

## `ZagreusHome_1015`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Fates can't be that cruel...

**中文**

命运对我也太残酷了……

## `ZagreusHome_1016`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Don't torture yourself, mate. You've been through enough. As has she! You still care for one another just don't have a way to get the point across. But I have an idea.

**中文**

别为难自己，伙计。你经历的够多了。她也是啊！你们依旧互相关心，只是心意没法传达给对方。我有个想法。

## `ZagreusHome_1017`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I know...

**中文**

我知道……

## `ZagreusHome_1018`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I got you something, Orpheus. It's a new song. From someone who still cares for you.

**中文**

给你这个，俄耳甫斯。是一首新歌。从依然关心你的人那里得到的。

## `ZagreusHome_1019`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Take all the time you need, mate.

**中文**

没事，慢慢来，伙计。

## `ZagreusHome_1020`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You sing beautifully, mate. If only Eurydice could hear you singing this right now.

**中文**

你唱得真好听，朋友。如果欧律狄克能听到你的歌声就好了。

## `ZagreusHome_1021`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Perhaps she can.

**中文**

也许她可以。

## `ZagreusHome_1022`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's great! Orpheus, what are you waiting for, mate? Go and find your muse out there! You two have lots to catch up on, I think.

**中文**

太好了，俄耳甫斯！你还愣着干什么，伙计？快去找你的缪斯啊！你俩可得好好叙叙旧啊。

## `ZagreusHome_1023`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Why is it always talk of payment around here? You're my mate. And your songs have lifted many spirits here, including mine. Now go, and make the most of this, will you?

**中文**

怎么老是动不动就回报回报的？咱俩谁跟谁啊，你的曲子可以抚慰我和其他人的心灵。去吧，好好抓住这次机会吧！

## `ZagreusHome_1024`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey Orpheus, you're back! How was your trip? I hope it was all right.

**中文**

嗨，俄耳甫斯，你回来了！你的旅行怎么样了？希望还好？

## `ZagreusHome_1025`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You sound despondent again, mate. You must wish you were there right now. I'm sorry you had to come all the way back.

**中文**

你似乎又沮丧起来了，朋友。你一定希望自己能留在那里吧。很抱歉你得从那儿一路回到这里。

## `ZagreusHome_1026`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Great to hear it. I can only imagine.

**中文**

真是个好消息，我只能想象一下是怎么回事了。

## `ZagreusHome_1027`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

This is amazing, mate. You sing quite beautifully, with or without your muse.

**中文**

太棒了，伙计。不管有没有缪斯，你的歌声都是如此动听。

## `ZagreusHome_1028`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Our renowned court musician ought to have the finest means available, I think. I'm pleased you like it, Orpheus. Though, tell me something, how can it play your music when you're not even here?

**中文**

我们著名的宫廷乐师配得上这最好。你能喜欢就好，俄耳甫斯。不过，告诉我，为什么你不在的时候，也能听到你的音乐？

## `ZagreusHome_1029`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Amazing. And a bit unsettling.

**中文**

很神奇，但也有点让人觉得不安。

## `ZagreusHome_1030`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Orpheus. The keepsake that you gave me... it's come in useful, thanks. I can't quite see what's in the container, though. I shouldn't open it, should I?

**中文**

嘿，俄耳甫斯。你给我的纪念品……它确实很好用，谢谢你。虽然我看不到里面装的是什么。我不该打开它，对吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1031`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Err, didn't think so.

**中文**

呃，我没这么想过。

## `ZagreusHome_1032`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Orpheus, the Distant Memory you gave me... it's a bit of your past with Eurydice in there, isn't it. I'm grateful, but... why give me such a precious gift as that?

**中文**

俄耳甫斯，你给我的遥远记忆……是关于你和欧律狄克的过去，是吗？我很感激，但是……为什么要给我这么珍贵的礼物呢？

## `ZagreusHome_1033`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I guess I can see that. Cheers.

**中文**

我应该能看得出来，再见。

## `ZagreusHome_1034`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Orpheus, you're back! Look, don't get on Father's bad side like that and you're going to be fine. Besides... right now I think he's angrier with me.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯，你回来了！听着，只要别惹父亲发火，你就不会有事的。而且……现在更让他生气的人是我。

## `ZagreusHome_1035`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's all right. Father can't make you sing if you don't want to.

**中文**

不要怕。如果您不想唱，父亲大人没法逼着您唱。

## `ZagreusHome_1036`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Say, Orpheus, Eurydice told me your songs, some of them came from her? She seems quite brilliant. You choose your muses well!

**中文**

俄耳甫斯，欧律狄克跟我讲过你的歌，有些歌是来自于她的？她似乎真的很厉害。你的缪斯女神真不赖！

## `ZagreusHome_1037`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

But weren't you the famous one? I don't understand why she doesn't share your renown.

**中文**

但是你不是出名的那个吗？我搞不懂为什么她好像并没有沾到你的光。

## `ZagreusHome_1038`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't exactly know the ways of mortals, mate. But I'm pleased to hear you always giving credit where it's due.

**中文**

我也搞不懂凡人是怎么想的，伙计。但是你把功劳归给应得的人，这就让我感到非常欣慰。

## `ZagreusHome_1039`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I always meant to ask you, Orpheus. Where did you learn to sing like that? Was it really Apollo himself who taught you?

**中文**

俄耳甫斯，有件事我一直想问你：你是从哪儿学的唱歌？是阿波罗本人教你的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1040`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's certainly paid off.

**中文**

还是有回报的。

## `ZagreusHome_1041`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Not really been at the top of my list of late, mate, but who knows. It's something I've been meaning to try at some point, I suppose. Thanks for the words of encouragement!

**中文**

我最近好像没有那么高的兴致，不过也说不准。有时候我也会想着试试看。谢谢你的鼓励！

## `ZagreusHome_1042`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey Orpheus! I got myself a lyre much like yours, except I'm absolutely miserable when I try to play! Got any advice?

**中文**

嗨，俄耳甫斯！我弄到一把里拉琴，跟你的很相似，只是当我试着演奏时根本不是那回事！有什么建议吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1043`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanks for the impromptu lesson, mate.

**中文**

谢谢你的即兴演出课程，伙计。

## `ZagreusHome_1044`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, come now, mate, I'm terrible and you know it. You pulling one over on me as payback?

**中文**

噢，得了吧，朋友。你明明知道我弹得很难听。你这是在故意损我吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1045`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I hope you're right.

**中文**

希望你说得对。

## `ZagreusHome_1046`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I highly doubt that, mate, though thanks for noticing. Creating music's quite rewarding once you get the hang of it!

**中文**

我对此表示怀疑，朋友，不过还是感谢你的关注。一但掌握了其中的诀窍，音乐创作还是很有成就感的！

## `ZagreusHome_1047`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thank you for encouraging me to give it a shot, mate. And for the kind words.

**中文**

感谢你的鼓励和美言，兄弟。

## `ZagreusHome_1048`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have to ask you something, Orpheus. It seems like everything has been all right for you, except... you don't look very happy to me, mate. Is everything all right? Between you and Eurydice, I mean?

**中文**

我要问你一些事情，俄耳甫斯。现在似乎一切都合你心意了，除了……但我看你好像不怎么开心的样子。没什么事吧？你跟欧律狄刻之间还好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1049`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You're doing wonderfully at it, mate.

**中文**

你干得真是太出色了，伙计。

## `ZagreusHome_1050`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Orpheus? I figure that you've heard by now about how I bested the Minotaur Asterius, alongside Theseus, the Champion of Elysium, himself? I don't think I even broke a sweat.

**中文**

嘿，俄耳甫斯？你应该听说了我同时打败了弥诺陶阿斯忒里俄斯和极乐世界的最强战士忒修斯了吧？我觉得我应该是出了汗的。

## `ZagreusHome_1051`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Did he make fun of me just now?

**中文**

……他刚才是不是在嘲笑我？

## `ZagreusHome_1052`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Orpheus, you have to hear this straight from me. I vanquished my own father, on the outskirts of the Temple of Styx. At the surface! I know how this looks, since I'm back here, and so is he. You believe me, don't you?

**中文**

俄耳甫斯，你得好好听我说。我在冥河神庙的外围，击败了我的父亲。在凡界！我知道这是什么样，因为我回来了，他也回来了。你相信我说的话，对吧？

## `ZagreusHome_1053`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fine, all right.

**中文**

好吧，好吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1054`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Let me guess... they want me to fight all the way through the Underworld, having used the Pact of Punishment over there to make the going even more treacherous than it already is, and if I succeed, they'll reward me with some sort of useless trinket?

**中文**

我猜猜……他们想让我启用那个惩罚契约，让环境变得更危险，然后我接着杀穿冥界，如果我成功了，他们就会奖励我一个没用的小饰品。

## `ZagreusHome_1055`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey what happened to the Pact of Punishment?

**中文**

嘿，惩罚契约怎么了？

## `ZagreusHome_1056`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The exit... that's a Pact of Punishment on it.

**中文**

出口……上面有个惩罚契约。

## `ZagreusHome_1057`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Is that the Pact of Punishment...?

**中文**

这是惩罚契约……？

## `ZagreusHome_1058`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What's the giant pact posted out in the courtyard, Father? You offering me a job?

**中文**

院子里那张巨大的契约是什么东西，父亲？提供给我的工作岗位？

## `ZagreusHome_1059`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I need to use the Pact on my way out.

**中文**

我要出去就得使用契约。

## `ZagreusHome_1060`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Pact over there is the key to revealing this.

**中文**

那契约就是找到真相的关键。

## `ZagreusHome_1061`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Who's that new shade there next to Father?

**中文**

父亲大人身边的新幽灵是谁？

## `ZagreusHome_1062`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Something's different about that shade next to Father.

**中文**

父亲大人身边的幽灵不太一样。

## `ZagreusHome_1063`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So, what exactly happened with the formerly pink window over there? Now it's got these sinister orange flames and an enormous Pact of Punishment hanging ominously over the exit. Father stop by?

**中文**

那边那扇粉色窗户之前是怎么回事？现在那里跳动着邪恶的橘黄色火焰，还有一块巨大的惩罚契约挂在出口那边，非常不详。父亲来过？

## `ZagreusHome_1064`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

As likely an explanation as any I guess.

**中文**

我看，什么样的解释都有可能。

## `ZagreusHome_1065`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Calm down, Skelly. I'm sure whatever it is is perfectly normal for a legendary semi-sentient Titan-slaying weapon from the dawn of time.

**中文**

冷静，骨头。很明确地说，在这件杀死泰坦并且拥有一定自我意识的传说级远古武器上发生这样的事情，再正常不过了。

## `ZagreusHome_1066`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's an imaginative theory, mate.

**中文**

那不过是个奇思妙想的理论而已，伙计。

## `ZagreusHome_1067`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I should go check it out.

**中文**

我该去看看。

## `ZagreusHome_1068`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

If I'm to keep seeing my chamber walls, they might as well not be completely barren.

**中文**

如果我总得见我房间的四墙，它们最好不是光秃秃的。

## `ZagreusHome_1069`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey, they're my bedchambers, I can have whatever wall-scrolls I want.

**中文**

嘿，它们是我的卧室墙，我想要什么壁挂就挂什么。

## `ZagreusHome_1070`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should help make my messy chambers even messier.

**中文**

能让我已经杂乱的房间更乱。

## `ZagreusHome_1071`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should make a nice-enough addition to my chambers.

**中文**

这摆在我的房间里应该再合适不过了。

## `ZagreusHome_1072`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ah yes, more comfortable seating I shall never use.

**中文**

啊，不错，这把椅子更舒服，但我永远都不会去坐。

## `ZagreusHome_1073`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This one's for all the solitary shades hanging around forever in the lounge.

**中文**

这是给那些在休息室里独自徘徊的暗灵们准备的。

## `ZagreusHome_1074`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Anything to liven things up even the slightest bit around here.

**中文**

要是能有什么东西能活跃一下这里的气氛就好了，哪怕只有一丁点儿也好。

## `ZagreusHome_1075`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What is it about fountains that makes everything feel a little fancier?

**中文**

不如弄几座喷泉来，让这里变得更漂亮吧？

## `ZagreusHome_1076`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Always hoped we'd have a fountain here precisely in that location.

**中文**

我一直都想在这个地方摆一座喷泉。

## `ZagreusHome_1077`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Who am I to stop the shades there in the lounge from cooking up whatever it is they're making?

**中文**

天知道休息室里的暗灵做的是什么料理，我又干嘛要去阻止他们呢？

## `ZagreusHome_1078`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Whew. It's a big job, having to sweep up after Cerberus sheds everywhere.

**中文**

呼，不得不清理刻耳柏洛斯造成的破坏，这真是个大工程。

## `ZagreusHome_1079`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This ought to make the lounge a little more presentable. Until Cerberus wrecks it again.

**中文**

这样休息室应该就会更宜人了，除非刻耳柏洛斯又来搞破坏了。

## `ZagreusHome_1080`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should make the climate in the lounge a bit more comfortable for some of us.

**中文**

这样一来，对我们其中一部分人而言，休息室的空气就会更舒适了。

## `ZagreusHome_1081`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

There's not much on the menu in the lounge but maybe this will help.

**中文**

休息室的菜单上东西不多，不过这大概能解决问题吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1082`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Huh maybe I can use this to avoid Father's withering gaze and unwanted feedback.

**中文**

哈，也许有了这个，我就能避开父亲严厉的凝视和讨厌的回话了。

## `ZagreusHome_1083`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How about a moody blue color of some sort for the drapery there.

**中文**

给那边的窗帘换上忧郁蓝调吧，怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_1084`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Perhaps a rich green hue for the drapery will feel a little homelier?

**中文**

或许亮绿色的色调会让人更有家的感觉？

## `ZagreusHome_1085`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A crimson color for the drapery is sure to be a better fit for the decor.

**中文**

深红色的窗帘效果肯定会更好。

## `ZagreusHome_1086`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You know what, I changed my mind about the drapery.

**中文**

说真的，窗帘的事情我又改主意了。

## `ZagreusHome_1087`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I miss the way things used to be, dear Drapery.

**中文**

真怀念过去的样子啊，我亲爱的窗帘。

## `ZagreusHome_1088`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The time has come once more to modify the drapery.

**中文**

是时候该再换换窗帘的颜色了。

## `ZagreusHome_1089`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Helps keep things colorful to change the drapery from time to time.

**中文**

时不时给窗帘换个颜色，有助于让生活变得多姿多彩。

## `ZagreusHome_1090`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Either those curtains go, or I do.

**中文**

要么这些窗帘走，要么我走。

## `ZagreusHome_1091`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This ought to help Orpheus keep his boundless libraries of music in order. Just have to fill it in.

**中文**

这应该可以帮助俄耳普斯整理他庞大的音乐库，只要填个表就行了。

## `ZagreusHome_1092`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Paid in full up front, let's see what we get!

**中文**

先把全款付了，看看东西怎么样吧！

**EN**

**EN**

**中文**

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's lighten things up a little around here.

让这里的气氛变得明快一些吧。

**EN**

Let's see how this one turns out.

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right, let's see what we got.

好了，看看成果如何。

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This should help liven the mood a bit.

**中文**

这个应该可以活跃一下气氛。

## `ZagreusHome_1099`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

One more embellishment for the old House.

**中文**

老房子又添了的新家具。

## `ZagreusHome_1100`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should check the music stand to hear this one.

得去乐谱架那边听听这首曲子怎么样。

## `ZagreusHome_1101`

**EN**

Another piece for the music stand.

**EN**

**EN**

**EN**

**中文**

你好，圣殿承包商。

**EN**

**中文**

**EN**

**中文**

你在想什么呢，承包商？

**EN**

Let's see what the Contractor has.

**中文**

**EN**

**EN**

**EN**

What can we do for this stodgy House.

**EN**

看来我得之后再来看看。

**EN**

No more for now huh?

## `ZagreusHome_1126`

**EN**

Looks like Father's changed the terms of our relationship.

**中文**

看上去父亲大人把我们关系的条款改了。

**EN**

It knows I've chosen Stygius.

## `ZagreusHome_1128`

**EN**

It can tell I have Varatha.

**EN**

It's detected Aegis.

**中文**

**EN**

It can sense Coronacht.

**EN**

It sees I chose Exagryph.

**EN**

Should I risk it?

**EN**

Maybe I ought to risk it.

Should I do it?

**EN**

Father's Pact.

**中文**

父亲大人的契约。

## `ZagreusHome_1136`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

New stipulations added to the Pact.

**中文**

新规定加入契约了。

## `ZagreusHome_1137`

**EN**

There's a new condition on the Pact.

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_1138`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's as bad as this gets.

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_1139`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Can't push it any further.

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_1140`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Maximum punishment.

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_1141`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's as far as it goes.

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_1142`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_1143`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No thank you.

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_1144`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No thanks.

不了，谢谢。

**EN**

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_1146`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I don't think so.

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_1147`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm asking for it.

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_1148`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hah, why not.

**中文**

哈，好呀。

## `ZagreusHome_1149`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

**EN**

New music.

Some new music.

新音乐。

**EN**

This one.

## `ZagreusHome_1161`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This.

**EN**

Let's listen.

**EN**

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Stygius... you're revealing your past?

斯第格思……你在向我讲述你的过去？

**EN**

Coronacht... is this what you really are?

**EN**

Aegis... you are as old as time...

**EN**

Varatha... you once were Father's spear...

**EN**

**EN**

**EN**

## `ZagreusHome_1230`

冥府钥匙不够……

**EN**

**EN**

## `ZagreusHome_1238`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It sounds like Father's very cross with you, Orpheus. Won't you consider singing once again? You know his threat of further punishment is not a bluff.

**中文**

好像父亲对你很不满，俄耳甫斯。没想过再展歌喉吗？你知道他威胁要让你吃更大的苦头可不是说说而已。

## `ZagreusHome_1239`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well then how about for me? Or for your other friends among the court?

**中文**

那如果是为了我呢？或者为了你在宫廷里的其他朋友呢？

## `ZagreusHome_1240`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I understand. I'm sorry.

**中文**

我明白了，对不起。

## `ZagreusHome_1241`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Plenty of practice, regular exercise, a positive attitude, the blessings of assorted family members you don't like, and your discarded Titan-slaying weapons I suppose. Nothing special. Why do you ask?

**中文**

我想就是勤加练习、日常锻炼、积极的态度，你看不上眼的各种家人的祝福，还有被你弃之如敝屣的能杀死泰坦的武器。也没什么特别的。为什么这么问？

## `ZagreusHome_1242`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, wait wait wait wait, care to repeat that first part that you said back there? The talent part?

**中文**

等一下，等一下，可以重复一下你的前半句话吗？关于才华的部分？

## `ZagreusHome_1243`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sure. Sorry about your Pact.

**中文**

没问题，我对你的契约表示遗憾。

## `ZagreusHome_1244`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, you mean the bounties that you've offered up to anybody capable of beating Meg and all your other guardians out there? Don't you think that puts a lot of undue pressure on them, knowing that you've turned them into targets?

**中文**

什么，你是指那些恩赐吗，那些不就是用来奖励能够打败墨纪和你的所有其他守护者的人吗？你不觉得你把他们都当成目标会给他们带来不必要的压力吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1245`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What about the bounties for vanquishing you? Who's your replacement going to be?

**中文**

如果是打败你的恩赐呢？谁会取代你呢？

## `ZagreusHome_1246`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Can't blame you, mate. I wouldn't want to be stuck in a chamber with a bunch of legendary, quite possibly malevolent killing artifacts. Anything I can do?

**中文**

这不是你的问题，兄弟。我也不想和一堆传说中的，杀气腾腾的东西共处一室。我能怎么办呢？

## `ZagreusHome_1247`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Uh... sure.

**中文**

呃……好吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1248`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Begging your pardon, my good Shade.

**中文**

不好意思，我的好暗灵。

## `ZagreusHome_1249`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ugh, more Fury Sisters than I bargained for...

**中文**

呃，复仇女神来得比预期的要多啊……

## `ZagreusHome_1250`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Urgh, that's one sister too many...

**中文**

呃，来的姐妹有点太多了吧……

## `ZagreusHome_1251`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mmph, that didn't seem particularly fair.

**中文**

嗯，好像有点不公平。

## `ZagreusHome_1252`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ungh, I guess I should be flattered in a way...

**中文**

呃呃，我有点受宠若惊了……

## `ZagreusHome_1253`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Augh, don't the Furies have somebody else they could be torturing...

**中文**

啊，复仇女神就不能去找其他人的麻烦吗……

## `ZagreusHome_1254`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Show me your power.

**中文**

向我展示你的力量。

## `ZagreusHome_1255`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Remember your past.

**中文**

记起你的过去。

## `ZagreusHome_1256`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Power beyond time.

**中文**

超越时间的力量。

## `ZagreusHome_1257`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Recover your ancient strength.

**中文**

回复你的远古之力。

## `ZagreusHome_1258`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I see you.

**中文**

我看到你了。

## `ZagreusHome_1259`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We are one.

**中文**

我们为一。

## `ZagreusHome_1260`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Live through me again.

**中文**

透过我再次活着吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1261`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Aid me, and live again.

**中文**

帮助我，并以此重生。

## `ZagreusHome_1262`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's as far as we can go.

**中文**

我们只能到这了。

## `ZagreusHome_1263`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's everything.

**中文**

就是这些了。

## `ZagreusHome_1264`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I see everything about you now.

**中文**

我现在能够看见一切属于你的事物。

## `ZagreusHome_1265`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Your entire past.

**中文**

你所有的过去。

## `ZagreusHome_1266`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Our bond is now complete.

**中文**

我们的牵绊完成了。

## `ZagreusHome_1267`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thus we are bound forever.

**中文**

这下，我们就永远联结在一起了。

## `ZagreusHome_1268`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Now you are awake.

**中文**

你醒了。

## `ZagreusHome_1269`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We've reached the limit.

**中文**

我们已经到极限了。

## `ZagreusHome_1270`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I have returned.

**中文**

我回来了。

## `ZagreusHome_1271`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I've returned.

**中文**

我回来了。

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Stygius.

**中文**

斯第格思。

**EN**

Stygius.

**中文**

斯第格思。

瓦拉塔。

瓦拉塔。

埃癸斯。

**EN**

**中文**

埃癸斯。

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**
