**中文**

科罗纳赫特。

## `ZagreusHome_1279`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Coronacht.

**中文**

科罗纳赫特。

## `ZagreusHome_1280`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Exagryph.

**中文**

伊格瑞菲。

## `ZagreusHome_1281`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Exagryph.

**中文**

伊格瑞菲。

## `ZagreusHome_1282`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Care to join me out there?

**中文**

要不要和我一起去？

## `ZagreusHome_1283`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Care to accompany me?

**中文**

愿意陪伴我吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1284`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Never made it up there myself!

**中文**

我还从来没一个人上去过呢！

## `ZagreusHome_1285`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Prices are a little steep there, I must say.

**中文**

说真的，这价格有点太坑了。

## `ZagreusHome_1286`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can't afford any of this.

**中文**

我一样都买不起。

## `ZagreusHome_1287`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What am I, composed entirely of gemstones?

**中文**

怎么，难道我该是宝石做的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1288`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Too pricey for me.

**中文**

对我来说太贵了。

## `ZagreusHome_1289`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Maybe you'll have something more affordable next time.

**中文**

希望下次你能弄来点我能买得起的东西。

## `ZagreusHome_1290`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's a lot of gemstones.

**中文**

这真的是很多宝石。

## `ZagreusHome_1291`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's cooking?

**中文**

在做什么好吃的呢？

## `ZagreusHome_1292`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Head Chef.

**中文**

头厨。

## `ZagreusHome_1293`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Chef.

**中文**

厨师。

## `ZagreusHome_1294`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Greetings, Chef.

**中文**

头厨，您好。

## `ZagreusHome_1295`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's for breakfast?

**中文**

早餐吃什么？

## `ZagreusHome_1296`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's for breakfast? Dinner maybe?

**中文**

早餐吃什么？还是吃晚餐？

## `ZagreusHome_1297`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Something smells good.

**中文**

什么东西好香啊。

## `ZagreusHome_1298`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How's that knife set working out?

**中文**

那套刀具用起来怎样？

## `ZagreusHome_1299`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Those spices working out all right?

**中文**

那些香料用起来还行？

## `ZagreusHome_1300`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cooking's one way to pass the time.

**中文**

做饭可以打发时间。

## `ZagreusHome_1301`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ah, my incredibly expensive fountain.

**中文**

啊，是我贵到天际的喷泉。

## `ZagreusHome_1302`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This is the most decadent thing I've ever bought.

**中文**

这是我买过最奢侈的东西了。

## `ZagreusHome_1303`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I won't ever grow weary of this!

**中文**

这东西怎么看都看不腻！

## `ZagreusHome_1304`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I won't ever grow weary of this... will I?

**中文**

这东西怎么看都看不腻……是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_1305`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Rather soothing really.

**中文**

挺舒缓的。

## `ZagreusHome_1306`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hello, Fountain.

**中文**

你好啊，喷泉。

## `ZagreusHome_1307`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Greetings, Broker.

**中文**

你好，老板。

## `ZagreusHome_1308`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Got anything good?

**中文**

弄来好东西了吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1309`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I've come for questionable deals.

**中文**

我来买点看起来很可疑的东西。

## `ZagreusHome_1310`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Anything good?

**中文**

有什么好东西吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1311`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I could use that.

**中文**

这个可以派上用场。

## `ZagreusHome_1312`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Need more of those, so here.

**中文**

我需要更多那种东西，于是我来了。

## `ZagreusHome_1313`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just what I was looking for.

**中文**

正好是我要找的。

## `ZagreusHome_1314`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Too good to pass up.

**中文**

不能错过啊。

## `ZagreusHome_1315`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's all right, wasn't hungry anyway.

**中文**

没事，反正我也不饿。

## `ZagreusHome_1316`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You watch yourself, old man.

**中文**

你自己小心点，老头。

## `ZagreusHome_1317`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Won't get extra Bounties this way, but why not.

**中文**

这样得不到更多赏金，不过也无妨。

## `ZagreusHome_1318`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Blowing out the Heat Gauge isn't going to do me any favors here.

**中文**

撑爆热度表可没有任何好处。

## `ZagreusHome_1319`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Don't need to turn the Heat Gauge so high to earn those Bounties.

**中文**

就算想要奖励，也没必要把热度表撑到这么高。

## `ZagreusHome_1320`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Won't get Bounties any faster with this extra Heat.

**中文**

就算有这些多余的热度，也没法更快拿到奖励。

## `ZagreusHome_1321`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Time to put in extra work for those Bounties.

**中文**

为了奖励，我得额外加把劲了。

## `ZagreusHome_1322`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

If it's Bounties I'm after, no need to turn the Heat up this high.

**中文**

如果只是想要奖励的话，那其实没必要把热度提得这么高。

## `ZagreusHome_1323`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This one, Contractor. Ought to make the Underworld a better place, I think.

**中文**

就这个吧，承包商，这应该能让冥界变得更加美好。

## `ZagreusHome_1324`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's make it happen. I'll have to keep an eye out for this next I'm out there.

**中文**

开工吧，等我下次出去的时候，我要好好检查检查干得怎么样。

## `ZagreusHome_1325`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I like the sound of this. Work approved and here's the fee, Contractor sir!

**中文**

听起来不错，我批准了。这是你的费用，承包商先生！

## `ZagreusHome_1326`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here you go! I'll be on the lookout for this when I'm in the area.

**中文**

开工了！等到了地方之后，我会好好留意一下的。

## `ZagreusHome_1327`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here! The Underworld could always use a little extra renovating, right?

**中文**

就是它了！冥界总得需要一些小小的翻新工作，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_1328`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I like the sound of this one here, Contractor sir. Go make it happen!

**中文**

在这里造个这种东西，听起来不错。承包商先生，把它给我造出来吧！

## `ZagreusHome_1329`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Work is approved, upon my father's name! Not like he's paying any heed.

**中文**

以我父亲的名义，我批准了！反正他根本不会管这件事。

## `ZagreusHome_1330`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm hereby authorizing this expenditure! And I look forward to seeing the result.

**中文**

本人特此批准这笔经费！希望成果不会让人失望。

## `ZagreusHome_1331`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

House Contractor, my good shade, I've yet another job for your distinguished team.

**中文**

圣殿承包商，我的好暗灵啊，我有一项工作要交给你出色的团队来做。

## `ZagreusHome_1332`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's go with this assignment, Contractor! My father surely would approve.

**中文**

准备开工吧，承包商！我父亲一定会批准的。

## `ZagreusHome_1333`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This looks to be an excellent investment, Contractor! Go on and make it so.

**中文**

这应该是一笔不错的投资，承包商！开始工作吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1334`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The ever-shifting chambers of the Underworld ought be a little livelier with this.

**中文**

有了它的话，不断变化的冥界房间应该就会显得更有生气了。

## `ZagreusHome_1335`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'd say our court musician's suffered quite enough. Besides, I miss his cheerful disposition.

**中文**

我们的宫廷乐师已经受了太多的苦了。另外，我还是想念那个开朗的他。

## `ZagreusHome_1336`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Fated List of Minor Prophecies... that sounds somewhat exciting, I suppose.

**中文**

《命运小预言清单》……听起来感觉有点意思。

## `ZagreusHome_1337`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey that must be the Fated List...

**中文**

嘿，那肯定就是命运清单了……

## `ZagreusHome_1338`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Urgh, blasted heavily armored half-bull...

**中文**

啊，这只穿重甲的混蛋牛头人……

## `ZagreusHome_1339`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Rrng, where can I get armor like that?

**中文**

呃，我要到哪里才能弄到那种护甲啊？

## `ZagreusHome_1340`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nngh, how do I get through all that armor?

**中文**

呃，要怎样才能击穿那种护甲啊？

## `ZagreusHome_1341`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oof, turns out I can like Theseus even less.

**中文**

喔，原来忒修斯还能更招人烦啊。

## `ZagreusHome_1342`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mmrn, Theseus can't just run over me like that...

**中文**

啊，忒修斯别以为撞了我就没事了……

## `ZagreusHome_1343`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Augh, how come he gets the Macedonian...

**中文**

啊，他是怎么得到马其顿号的……

## `ZagreusHome_1344`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ugh, well then I guess I missed my deadline.

**中文**

呃，好吧，我猜已经超过截止日期了。

## `ZagreusHome_1345`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oomph, that was a little too much Heat.

**中文**

噢噢，热度有点高了。

## `ZagreusHome_1346`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nrgh, ran out of time back there I guess.

**中文**

哎，我想可能是没时间了吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1347`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Augh, blasted Pact of Punishment...

**中文**

啊，这该死的惩罚契约……

## `ZagreusHome_1348`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Meg, what is it going to take for you to quit treating me like I'm incompetent? Have I not grown even a little since we met? You were supposed to teach me something; what does that make you?

**中文**

墨纪，怎样才能让你不再把我当作没用的废物？从你认识我到现在，难道我就没有一点成长吗？你本来该教我一些东西；对此你有何想法？

## `ZagreusHome_1349`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're not hearing me, Meg. I know it sounds a little disingenuous considering we keep fighting to the death and all, but... I never meant to hurt you.

**中文**

你好好听我说，墨纪。我们一直以来都是兵戎相见，我知道这听上去有点虚伪，但是说真的……我从没想过要伤害你。

## `ZagreusHome_1350`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Meg, that isn't why I'm here, you know that isn't how I am. Why can't you see it? All I've ever wanted was to prove myself to you.

**中文**

墨纪，这不是我来的原因，你知道我不是这样的人。为什么你还是不明白？我想要做的，只是在你面前证明自己。

## `ZagreusHome_1351`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sometimes I think that I can never really win with you, Meg. If I held back, what would you have thought?

**中文**

有时候，我都认为自己永远不能赢得你的欢心，墨纪拉。如果我退缩了，你又会怎么想？

## `ZagreusHome_1352`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I had to see if I could do it, Meg. I've learned so much from you, I... thought maybe this was a way that I could prove myself to you. And to myself.

**中文**

我必须要试试看，我能不能做到，墨纪。我从你身上学到那么多，我……觉得这可能是向你，也是向我证明自身价值的一种方式。

## `ZagreusHome_1353`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Some of the pressure... wait, what are you going to do?

**中文**

压力……等等，你要干什么？

## `ZagreusHome_1354`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Meg...! She... ah.

**中文**

墨纪…！她… 嗐。

## `ZagreusHome_1355`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey Meg, I was just thinking that you really shouldn't settle for the run-of-the-mill contraband that I've been handing off to you. I got you something absolutely scandalous this time.

**中文**

嘿，墨纪，我刚刚在想，之前给你的违禁品实在是太寻常了，这次我要给你一件真正恶名昭著的违禁品。

## `ZagreusHome_1356`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What... wow... I'll cherish her.

**中文**

什么……哇啊……那我得珍惜她。

## `ZagreusHome_1357`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I had this awful contraband I wanted to turn in as soon as possible, if you don't mind taking it from me, Meg.

**中文**

这件棘手的违禁品就在我手上，如果你不介意从我手上拿走的话，我想尽快上交，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_1358`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm... so the whole contraband thing, you aren't buying it, I guess? Although I see you took the bottle there, now, anyway... I wanted just to talk to you. And patch things up, maybe.

**中文**

呃……所以这件违禁品你是完全不买账咯？虽然我看到你从那里拿走了瓶子，但现在……我只是想和你谈谈。也许还可以修复我们的关系。

## `ZagreusHome_1359`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I... I was naive. I tried not to get too close, but then I did, and said a lot of stupid things, and then... well, we've since tried to kill each other lots of times, there's that. Look, I am sorry, Meg.

**中文**

我…我太天真了。我试着不要走得太近，但我没做到，而且说了很多傻话，然后……然后，我们还多次相恨相杀，就是这样。我真的很抱歉，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_1360`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Uh, yeah. Bye...

**中文**

呃，好吧，再见……

## `ZagreusHome_1361`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Here, Meg. Contraband. I wish I could do over how we started.

**中文**

在这里，墨纪。违禁品。我希望我们可以重新开始。

## `ZagreusHome_1362`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What if I've learned from my mistakes? Have I ever done anything to hurt you since we've been like this? Discounting the unavoidable and frequent fights to the death.

**中文**

如果我从自己的错误中吸取到教训了呢？自从我们变成这样之后，我曾经做过什么伤害你的事情吗？除了很多次不可避免的生死相搏。

## `ZagreusHome_1363`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well... am I worth your time, then, Meg?

**中文**

好吧，那我值得吗，墨纪？

## `ZagreusHome_1364`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I see. Take all the time you need.

**中文**

我知道了，你慢慢来吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1365`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey Meg, you're off duty, aren't you? Look, I was just... hoping you might want to split this with me, best stuff I could find out there, and I would give it all to you, it's just... maybe you wouldn't mind some company. From me.

**中文**

嘿，墨纪，你没有任务，对吗？我，我只是……觉得你可能会想和我把它分一分，这是我能找到的最棒的东西，我可以全部都给你，只是……你介意和别人结伴吗？比如我。

## `ZagreusHome_1366`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're not exactly easy to approach, you know. So... how about it, then? Care for a drink with me?

**中文**

其实，你这人不大好接近，知道吗？那么……可以和我一起喝一杯吗？怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_1367`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

And I think, deep down, you are not the heartless harbinger of retribution that you want for everyone to think you are. But I can keep your secret if you're willing to keep mine, what do you say?

**中文**

你根本就不是一心想着复仇，冷酷无情的人，虽然你希望别人都这么看你。但如果你可以替我保守秘密的话，我也可以保守你的秘密，你觉得如何？

## `ZagreusHome_1368`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No, I... I was just, that's not what I meant at all...

**中文**

不，我……我只是，我根本不是这个意思……

## `ZagreusHome_1369`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yeah. Thanks.

**中文**

好的，谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_1370`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Meg... what a surprise. Again. What is it? ...What's the matter? Wait. Why are you looking at me like that?

**中文**

墨纪……这太令人意外了。再一次。怎么了？……发生了什么？等下。为什么要这样看着我？

## `ZagreusHome_1371`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...I... you really... oh...

**中文**

……我……你真的……哦……

## `ZagreusHome_1372`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Um, Meg, I... what I'm trying to say is, are you... are we good, or...?

**中文**

呃，墨纪，我…我想说的是，你……我们的关系没问题吧，还是……？

## `ZagreusHome_1373`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I... loud and clear, Meg, yes, I understand. So then... what happens now?

**中文**

我……知道了，墨纪，我知道了。那么……接下来要做什么？

## `ZagreusHome_1374`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

However long we keep this up?

**中文**

无论我们要坚持多久？

## `ZagreusHome_1375`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...See you around! ...Yes.

**中文**

……后会有期！……是的。

## `ZagreusHome_1376`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

But... no, wait... not even Than...?

**中文**

但是……不，等一下……连塔纳也不行……？

## `ZagreusHome_1377`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yeah, but... what about you, you're the punisher of jealousy. What if I...

**中文**

是啊，但……你呢，你会惩罚那些善嫉之人。如果我……

## `ZagreusHome_1378`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What do you think of all the lounge decor, there, Meg? Looking a little better these days, wouldn't you say?

**中文**

你对于休息室的装饰有什么看法吗，墨纪？看起来是比以前好一些，不是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1379`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, it's a popular locale for off-duty Underworld officers to take a load off between shifts, and so I figure... least I can do is pitch in and make the place a little better than I left it, right?

**中文**

其实，冥界的差役们不当差时都喜欢来这里，在换班的空档稍微放松一下压力，所以，我觉得……至少我可以做点贡献，在我离开之后让这个地方变得更好，不是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1380`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hoh now let's not get ahead of ourselves, Meg.

**中文**

噢，咱们先别着急好吗，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_1381`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I wasn't trying to do my father any favors, Meg. Those Satyrs seem to hate me just as much as him. My one way out involves a detour straight into their dens.

**中文**

我并不是在帮助我的父亲，墨纪。那些萨提尔对我和我的父亲都恨之入骨。我的出路正好经过他们的巢穴。

## `ZagreusHome_1382`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I suppose not...

**中文**

我不这么想……

## `ZagreusHome_1383`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, while it was brutal squaring off against the three of you, I have to say, Meg... I was happy you were with your sisters. Nothing like a common cause for bringing bitter rivals together, right?

**中文**

你也知道，在和你们三姐妹残酷对峙的时候，我不得不说，墨纪……我为你和你的姐妹们站在一起而感到高兴。只有共同的理想才能把昔日的仇敌凝聚在一起，不是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1384`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So you've patched things up, then? Going to start spending more quality time together and all that?

**中文**

所以说，你们之间的关系和好如初了？然后开始共度美好时光？

## `ZagreusHome_1385`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

But it's a step.

**中文**

但是往前一步了。

## `ZagreusHome_1386`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, you mean the gigantic infernal contract reserved only for extraordinary circumstances? Yeah, I suppose I'm pleased that Father's gone to all that trouble just for me.

**中文**

你是说如此影响深远的地狱契约只适用于特殊情况？是啊，我应该为此感到高兴，父亲竟然为了我这样大费周章。

## `ZagreusHome_1387`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

A lot of things, such as how you refuse to reason with your positively charming sisters, Meg?

**中文**

很多情况？例如，如何拒绝与你迷人的姐妹们讲道理吗，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_1388`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Anything for you, Meg.

**中文**

为你做什么都行，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_1389`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have to ask you for a favor, Meg. And I'm willing to make it worth your while, if you'll hear me out.

**中文**

我得请你帮个忙，墨纪。好好听我说，我保证你的时间不会白花。

## `ZagreusHome_1390`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's about Sisyphus. If I can get my father to alleviate his sentence, would you just... ease off of him, a little bit? You and your sisters. I think he's suffered quite enough under your whips.

**中文**

就是那个西西弗斯。如果我让我的父亲为他减刑，你们能不能不要对他……这么苛刻？你和你的姐妹们。你的鞭子已经让他吃够苦头了。

## `ZagreusHome_1391`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's a deal.

**中文**

就这么说定了。

## `ZagreusHome_1392`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Meg, you know what's going on with Than and me, don't you? I just... want things to be out in the open, between us.

**中文**

墨纪，你知道我和塔纳之间的事情吗？我只是…不希望对我们两人之间的关系遮遮掩掩。

## `ZagreusHome_1393`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's fine? I mean... upon Olympus, jealousy runs rampant, as I'm told. But here it's not even an issue, then?

**中文**

没关系吗？我听说……在奥林匹斯山上，嫉妒之风盛行。但这件事就完全没关系吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1394`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No... he thinks so highly of you, he... he'd never wish to cause you any harm.

**中文**

不应该……他对你的评价很高，他……他从来没想过要伤害你。

## `ZagreusHome_1395`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I will. Thanks, Meg.

**中文**

我会的。谢谢，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_1396`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Battie's doing great, Meg. Reminds me of you every time I think of her. Helps keep me company throughout my little jaunts outside. So you've a thing for bats, then?

**中文**

巴蒂很好，墨纪。每次想到她，我就会回忆起你的点点滴滴。这会让我的旅途不再那么孤单。所以，现在你开始喜欢蝙蝠了？

## `ZagreusHome_1397`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That she is.

**中文**

她确实是。

## `ZagreusHome_1398`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey, I'm back.

**中文**

哎，我回来了。

## `ZagreusHome_1399`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Skelly!

**中文**

骨头！

## `ZagreusHome_1400`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's going on, Skelly?

**中文**

怎么样，骨头？

## `ZagreusHome_1401`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How's it going, Skelly?

**中文**

还好吗，骨头？

## `ZagreusHome_1402`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's new, Skelly?

**中文**

有新鲜事吗，骨头？

## `ZagreusHome_1403`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

If it doesn't involve hacking away at you with reckless abandon, Skelly, I don't want to hear it. But... oh. You're being serious.

**中文**

如果不是关于如何毫不留情地把你干掉，骨头，那我就不想知道。但是……哦，你是认真的。

## `ZagreusHome_1404`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...I know. I can only imagine. Tell me what I can do.

**中文**

……我知道。我只能想象。告诉我我能做点什么。

## `ZagreusHome_1405`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Then you'd be dead, forever? You're asking me to kill you.

**中文**

然后你就死了，永远地死了？你让我杀了你。

## `ZagreusHome_1406`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right... maybe.

**中文**

好吧……也许吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1407`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Farewell, Skelly...

**中文**

再见，骨头……

## `ZagreusHome_1408`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What...

**中文**

什么……

## `ZagreusHome_1409`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It appears I've been the victim of an elaborate prank. Not sure that I appreciate it, Skelly. What if I were to never hit you again, how would you like that?

**中文**

我好像成了精心布局的恶作剧的受害者。我自己都不确定这是好事还是坏事，骨头。万一我再也打不到你了呢，你觉得怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_1410`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Then no more tricks like that again.

**中文**

不准再开这种玩笑了。

## `ZagreusHome_1411`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Skelly, while I know your services have already been paid in full, your sources ought to know you've been doing an excellent job. So much so, I wanted to give you this.

**中文**

骨头，我知道你拿到了全部报酬，你的线人应该也知道你出色地完成了任务。尽管如此，我还是要把这个给你。

## `ZagreusHome_1412`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Skelly, mate... this what I think it is?

**中文**

骨头，伙计……这是我想的那个东西吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1413`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hope you enjoyed that vintage bottle of Ambrosia last time, Skelly, because I got you another. Here!

**中文**

希望你还喜欢上次给你的仙馔密酒，骨头，因为我今天又给你带了一瓶。

## `ZagreusHome_1414`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're my friend, mate. That's all that matters. Although... I don't suppose you're willing to divulge those types of details? Just out of curiosity.

**中文**

你是我的朋友，伙计。这才是最重要的。但是……我觉得你可能不愿意透露这么多细节？只是出于好奇。

## `ZagreusHome_1415`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Drat.

**中文**

靠。

## `ZagreusHome_1416`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

There's quite a lot of renovating to be done, isn't there, Father. Your House Contractor there has shown a wide variety of pending jobs.

**中文**

父亲，我们还有很多修整工作要做，对吗？你的圣殿承包商列出了一张长长的工作清单。

## `ZagreusHome_1417`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Feels pretty good, now that you mention it. Keeps everybody occupied, no?

**中文**

既然您问，那我告诉您，我感觉挺好的。让每个人都有事情做，不是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1418`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nyx, the latest renovations from the Contractor... Father told me you've been supervising all of that. Look, just between us, and I'll not speak of it again, but... thanks. So much.

**中文**

倪克斯，关于承包商最近的整修工作……父亲和我说，由你负责总体监工。我是说，这是我们两个人之间的秘密，我也不会再次提起这件事，但是……我要谢谢你，真的非常感谢。

## `ZagreusHome_1419`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Is this all that the Fates have planned for me...?

**中文**

这就是命运之神给我安排的命运吗……？

## `ZagreusHome_1420`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's have a look at the old Fated List.

**中文**

看看以前的命运清单上是怎么说的吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1421`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What do the Three Fates have in mind?

**中文**

命运三女神到底是怎么想的？

## `ZagreusHome_1422`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Something inevitable I can do?

**中文**

我会去做某些不可避免的事情吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1423`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A prophecy...!

**中文**

预言……！

## `ZagreusHome_1424`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Another prophecy...

**中文**

又有预言……

## `ZagreusHome_1425`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Fates have spoken.

**中文**

命运发言了。

## `ZagreusHome_1426`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Something new in store for me?

**中文**

店里有新货吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1427`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Something's added to the Fated List.

**中文**

命运清单上加入了新的东西。

## `ZagreusHome_1428`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A prophecy's appeared.

**中文**

一个预言出现了。

## `ZagreusHome_1429`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It was inevitable, thanks.

**中文**

这是必然的啊，谢谢了。

## `ZagreusHome_1430`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Who am I to question Fate?

**中文**

哪里轮得到我去质疑命运呢？

## `ZagreusHome_1431`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The prophecy has been fulfilled.

**中文**

预言应验了。

## `ZagreusHome_1432`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That definitely came to pass.

**中文**

这确实算是应验了。

## `ZagreusHome_1433`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Was bound to happen, really.

**中文**

说实在的，怎么都得应验的。

## `ZagreusHome_1434`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can escape through that sinister window there.

**中文**

我可以从那扇看着阴险的窗户逃出去。

## `ZagreusHome_1435`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Looks like Father's not going to make this easy on me.

**中文**

看来父亲大人是不会让这个简单的。

## `ZagreusHome_1436`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No getting out of that one.

**中文**

躲不过这个的。

## `ZagreusHome_1437`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll have to live with that.

**中文**

我只能面对事实了。

## `ZagreusHome_1438`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Kkh, Father...

**中文**

咔，父亲大人……

## `ZagreusHome_1439`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That Condition's non-negotiable.

**中文**

那个条款没有商量的余地。

## `ZagreusHome_1440`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm stuck with that.

**中文**

我被那玩意困住了。

## `ZagreusHome_1441`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I only wish.

**中文**

但愿如此。

## `ZagreusHome_1442`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Each time I die like that, I come back stronger.

**中文**

……每次我像那样丧命，复活之后都会变得更强。

## `ZagreusHome_1443`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Must be the Fated List there on my desk...

**中文**

桌子上应该就是命运清单了……

## `ZagreusHome_1444`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Than, what's your relationship with Ares on Olympus? I know he deals in death himself, but... his approach seems rather different from your own.

**中文**

塔纳，你和奥林匹斯山上的阿瑞斯是什么关系？我知道他也在和亡灵打交道，但……他的方式好像和你差别很大。

## `ZagreusHome_1445`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You mean the rest of the Olympians don't treat you well?

**中文**

你是说奥林匹斯山上的其他神对你不好？

## `ZagreusHome_1446`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Disturbingly all right!

**中文**

太好了！好得让人心烦！

## `ZagreusHome_1447`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What's going on with Charon, by the way, if you can tell me, Than? He's... never been particularly talkative, but he really seems to know the inner workings of this place.

**中文**

那卡戎呢，他怎么样，塔纳，能和我说说吗？他……从来不多说一句话，但他好像很清楚这个地方的很多潜规则。

## `ZagreusHome_1448`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

He must be compensated pretty well for having done this for so long. Tending the influx of new souls and all of that.

**中文**

这么长时间以来，他肯定拿了不少报酬。照顾新来的亡灵之类的。

## `ZagreusHome_1449`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I never thought of it like that.

**中文**

我从来没那么想过。

## `ZagreusHome_1450`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thanatos, I've meant to ask... is it really the case that many mortals have a crippling fear of death?

**中文**

塔纳托斯，其实我是想问一下……是不是很多凡人都非常怕死？

## `ZagreusHome_1451`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Why not? I mean, isn't it kind of a big problem that they have such negative associations, here? Can't we... I don't know, do something to educate them all?

**中文**

为什么？我是说，他们的这种负面联想难道不是一个大问题吗？我们就不能……想办法纠正他们的想法吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1452`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yeah, seriously.

**中文**

没错，说真的。

## `ZagreusHome_1453`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Than? I had another question about death. So... why is it mortals grieve so much about the thing, I mean... it definitely hurts there for a little while, but... it's not that bad.

**中文**

塔纳？我又想问个关于死亡的问题了。那就是……为什么这件事会让凡人如此悲伤？我知道……它确实会暂时造成一些痛苦，但是……也没那么糟糕吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1454`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

But that's absurd. One life? Though... eugh. That's horrible, even to think about.

**中文**

但这也太荒谬了，生命只有一次吗？但是……呃。光是想一想，就觉得好可怕。

## `ZagreusHome_1455`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Didn't know you saw it that way.

**中文**

我不知道你是这么看待它的。

## `ZagreusHome_1456`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey Than? Something's been troubling me still, with what you said, about how mortals look at death. If most of them believe their life is all they have... how do they live? I mean, I can't even imagine what that's like. I'd be a lot more careful, at the very least!

**中文**

嘿，塔纳？有些事一直困扰着我，就是你说的关于凡人如何看待死亡的问题。如果大多数人都坚信他们只有一次生命……他们是怎么生活的？我的意思是，我根本无法想象。起码，我会更加小心翼翼！

## `ZagreusHome_1457`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, you mean that 'there is no escape'? From what? The mortals are up there, not here. Not while they're technically alive in their sense, anyway.

**中文**

什么，你是说“根本没有逃脱的可能”？从哪里逃脱？凡人应该在阳间，不是这里。至少，在他们所理解的活着的时候。

## `ZagreusHome_1458`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...I'm starting to notice that, yes. Though, to a mortal who has yet to die... don't they think of death as some sort of release? You come to end their misery, something like that?

**中文**

……我开始注意到，是的。虽然每个凡人都难逃一死……但他们不也是把死亡当成一种解脱吗？由你来结束他们的不幸，之类的？

## `ZagreusHome_1459`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It really is.

**中文**

确实如此。

## `ZagreusHome_1460`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thanatos, I ought have asked by now, but... what's it like? Up there I mean, outside. You never talk about it, I... almost forget that's where you go, for work.

**中文**

塔纳托斯，我早该问你了，但……那是一种怎样的感受？我是说阳间的生活。你从没提起，我……差点忘了你在那边做事。

## `ZagreusHome_1461`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That sounds awful. If it's that bad, why would all the Olympians chat it up so much?

**中文**

听起来很糟糕。如果真的这么糟糕，为什么奥林匹斯山上的众神都把那说的天花乱坠呢？

## `ZagreusHome_1462`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Huh. Maybe. I hope.

**中文**

呃，也许吧，我希望是。

## `ZagreusHome_1463`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Than, you don't hold a grudge against old Sisyphus, do you? He seems rather apprehensive about you. I understand you met not on the best of terms.

**中文**

塔纳，你对老西西弗斯没有心怀怨恨吧？他好像对你充满怀疑。我知道你们合不来。

## `ZagreusHome_1464`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I only have a faint impression of the man he used to be, although it seems he's changed a lot, since then. Thank you for understanding, anyway.

**中文**

我对他的模糊印象都停留在过去，虽然他后来变了很多。不管怎样，还是要谢谢你的理解。

## `ZagreusHome_1465`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey Than. For all the times you've bailed me out, and just, all the times you've been around for me... I thought I'd get you this. The real thing.

**中文**

嘿，塔纳。每次都是你救我出来，每次你总是守护在我身边……我只能给你这个。货真价实的好东西。

## `ZagreusHome_1466`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What, Thanatos, is this... you're really giving Mort to me? I... I'm deeply honored!

**中文**

什么，塔纳托斯？这是……你真的要把莫特给我吗？！我……我太荣幸了！

## `ZagreusHome_1467`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I hope you're ready for some more of the real thing, there, Than, because I got you some, and I won't be taking 'Tsch, what is this, Zag' for an answer.

**中文**

我希望你能够找到货真价实的东西，塔纳，因为我可以给你一些，而且我保证你在看到以后不会做出“啧，这是什么，扎格”这样的反应。

## `ZagreusHome_1468`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

In order: no, I'm not; by ransacking my father's realm repeatedly; and it's because I like you, Thanatos. In case you still have some misgivings about that.

**中文**

为了……哦，不，这是我在不停洗劫我的父亲的地盘时找到的，而且因为我喜欢你，塔纳托斯。如果还有疑虑，那真的是大可不必。

## `ZagreusHome_1469`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yeah. Look, if you don't feel the same way about me at this point, I would rather know. Cease all these coy gift exchanges, and all that.

**中文**

是吗。现在的情况是，如果你对我并没有相同的感觉，就让我知道你的真实想法。不要再这样扭扭捏捏地交换什么礼物了。

## `ZagreusHome_1470`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Than, wait...!

**中文**

塔纳，等一下……！

## `ZagreusHome_1471`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thanatos, I wanted to apologize for the last time I approached you with a bottle of the real thing, and give the whole exchange another shot. Would that be all right?

**中文**

塔纳托斯，我想为上次给你的那瓶所谓的货真价实的东西道歉，要不我们再交换一次礼物吧。可以吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1472`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't know that it will. I never really know exactly where I stand with you. But I know how I feel. And I'd rather be up front with you, even if it means risking our relationship, such as it is.

**中文**

我也没把握。我根本不知道自己在你心中的位置。但我很清楚自己的感受，所以我宁愿和你直说，即使冒着和你的关系破裂的风险，就像现在这样。

## `ZagreusHome_1473`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yes, that's what I'm saying, Than. I should not have left the way I did, without letting you know. But when you found me, I... think that's when... that's when I knew. Or started to realize, you know?

**中文**

是的，我真的在乎你，塔纳。我不应该不辞而别。但就在你找到我的时候，我……就是那个时候……那个时候我才明白，或者开始意识到，你懂我的意思吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1474`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Maybe we ought to take our time? Unlike the real thing there, that's a commodity we have in good supply. Just know that, if you feel the way I do... you know where to find me. And if not... I'm grateful, anyway.

**中文**

也许我们应该慢慢来？不像那个所谓货真价实的东西，它只不过是件随处可见的商品罢了。别忘了……如果你和我有同样的感受……你就会知道在哪里可以找到我。如果没有……我还是很感激。

## `ZagreusHome_1475`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...You too.

**中文**

…… 你也保重。

## `ZagreusHome_1476`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thanatos...! I'd ask you to come in, but... you're already here. It's really good to see you.

**中文**

塔纳托斯……！我正要叫你进来呢，这不……你就来了。很高兴见到你。

## `ZagreusHome_1477`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I just... don't mean to push you, Than. I know all this is kind of a lot. And I wanted you to know... this isn't some impulsive thing for me. I'll wait for you however long it takes.

**中文**

我只是……不想给你太多压力，塔纳。我知道发生了太多的事。而且我想让你知道……对我来说，在这件事上我不会冲动。我会等你，不管多长时间。

## `ZagreusHome_1478`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Than...! Hahaha, oh, you're right!

**中文**

塔纳……！哈哈哈，你说的没错！

## `ZagreusHome_1479`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Than, look... speak up, already, I don't like it when you're quiet for too long, what's on your mind?

**中文**

嘿，塔纳，听好了……有什么就说什么，我不喜欢你一直这样保持沉默，你究竟在想什么？

## `ZagreusHome_1480`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It is. It is.

**中文**

当然可以了。没问题。

## `ZagreusHome_1481`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Than... hahaha, I'm so glad! It's just... you know that Megaera has been here, too...?

**中文**

塔纳……哈哈哈，我很高兴！只是……你知道吗，墨纪拉也在这里……？

## `ZagreusHome_1482`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hah! She did, did she? That's good! That's good.

**中文**

哈！难道是她？很好！不错。

## `ZagreusHome_1483`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Than, there's something that I meant to tell you. Ask you, rather. You're close with Megaera, so, what I mean is... you know about us, right?

**中文**

塔纳，我想和你说一件事。或者说问你一个问题。你和墨纪拉的关系亲密，所以，我想知道……你对我们都很了解，对吧？

## `ZagreusHome_1484`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, it's just... I figured since you deal with mortals all the time, I know that some of them, they choose a single suitor. It's a valid custom, if it's yours...!

**中文**

说起来有点……我在想，既然你一直和凡人打交道，我知道他们当中的一些人会选择一个追求者。这是非常合理的习惯，如果是你的……！

## `ZagreusHome_1485`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're saying you're not angry with me? Or Meg, for that matter?

**中文**

你是说，你不生我的气？或者生墨纪的气？

## `ZagreusHome_1486`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Sure. Sounds good.

**中文**

……行啊，听起来不错。

## `ZagreusHome_1487`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Skelly, mate! You really shouldn't have. Do you have any idea how hard-to-find this is?!

**中文**

骨头，朋友！你真的不应该那么做，你知道这东西有多难找吗？！

## `ZagreusHome_1488`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sir, is this a mint-condition Shady you just gave to me?! I'm so grateful. I will keep him safe!

**中文**

先生，这全新的沙迪，真的要给我吗？！太感谢了，我会好好保护他的！

## `ZagreusHome_1489`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

An eternity of hard labor seems a little much for Sisyphus. Let's lighten up his burden, then, shall we?

**中文**

让西西弗斯没完没了地干这种苦差事，这太难为他了。给他稍微减减负吧，怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_1490`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I should check in with the House Contractor.

**中文**

我应该去圣殿承包商那里去看看。

## `ZagreusHome_1491`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ought to spend some of these gems with the House Contractor.

**中文**

得去承包商那里花一点宝石了。

## `ZagreusHome_1492`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Wonder what jobs the House Contractor has available.

**中文**

不知道承包商那里有些什么工程呢。

## `ZagreusHome_1493`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I got a new Companion! I can bring it along with my other Keepsake.

**中文**

我得到了新的伴偶！我可以把它和我的另一个纪念物一起带出场。

## `ZagreusHome_1494`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Can't get out of here till I turn up the Heat.

**中文**

我得先提高热度，才能离开这个地方。

## `ZagreusHome_1495`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I know. I'm sorry. But this is something that I have to do. You wouldn't understand.

**中文**

我知道，我很抱歉。但这件事情我必须做，你是不会明白的。

## `ZagreusHome_1496`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Be sure to add those to the list of words you'll eat someday.

**中文**

总有一天，你会为你说的这些话后悔。

## `ZagreusHome_1497`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father, let me ask you something work-related, here. I know the shade called Sisyphus once wronged both you and Thanatos, and has since paid a heavy price. But don't the Fury Sisters now have better things to do than torture him?

**中文**

父亲，我可以问一些和工作有关的问题吗？我知道这个叫西西弗斯的幽灵过去对你和塔纳托斯做了过分的事，他也因此付出了惨重的代价。但除了折磨他，复仇女神们现在没其他事可做了吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1498`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So then if I could somehow find those terms, then theoretically, I could grant him a pardon...

**中文**

所以，如果我可以找到判决书，从理论上来说，我就可以赦免他？

## `ZagreusHome_1499`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's good to know!

**中文**

很高兴你这么说！

## `ZagreusHome_1500`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You really think that has to do with me...? I mean, I hope you're right, and I suppose it's possible, it's just... how can you tell?

**中文**

你真的觉得这和我有关……？我的意思是，我希望你是对的，确实存在这种可能性，但是……你是怎么看出来的？

## `ZagreusHome_1501`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanks, Achilles. That means a lot.

**中文**

谢谢，阿喀琉斯。这对我很重要。

## `ZagreusHome_1502`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I think so. I hope so. It was complicated for a while there, but... I think things are clearer for us, now. Not just in the moment, but in retrospect. Ugh, I'm not making any sense.

**中文**

我想是吧。但愿如此。情况有点复杂，但是……我觉得现在情况已经比较明朗了。不管对于现在，还是过去。啊，我还是说不清楚。

## `ZagreusHome_1503`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

He is.

**中文**

他是。

## `ZagreusHome_1504`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, have you ever run across the shade of Daedalus in all your time? I've found some of his legendary hammers out there, but no sign of him.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，你遇到过狄德勒斯的暗灵吗？我找到了他的传奇战锤，但没看到他的人。

## `ZagreusHome_1505`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Indeed I have.

**中文**

我确实这么做了。

## `ZagreusHome_1506`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You act as though this God Mode is some sort of lever I can simply switch at will if ever I wish to unlock my latent strength, there, mate.

**中文**

你要假装这种神力模式就像开关一样，可以在想要解锁潜藏力量时随意开启，明白了吗，伙计。

## `ZagreusHome_1507`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, Skelly, I still cannot entirely get over that whole permanently-kill-you stunt you pulled on me. You had me deeply worried for a while, all for just a laugh?

**中文**

你知道吗，骨头，我还是对你说的要让我永远死去这句话耿耿于怀。你让我担心了好久，现在就这么一笑了之？

## `ZagreusHome_1508`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's what you call it, then? In any case, I did want to make sure: You sure that you're all right, having to stand around like this?

**中文**

你觉得这算是学知识？无论如何，我都要确定一点：你觉得这样好吗，就这么干站着？

## `ZagreusHome_1509`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well great, I guess!

**中文**

应该还不错吧！

## `ZagreusHome_1510`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh no, not this determinism thing again...

**中文**

不是吧，别再搞这种命中注定的烂梗了……

## `ZagreusHome_1511`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Eurydice...? I know a... anyway, she must have been a real catch! I'm sorry for your loss. I have to go look into something here.

**中文**

欧律狄克……？我知道……无论如何，她肯定是一个不错的人！很抱歉你失去了她。我必须好好调查一番。

## `ZagreusHome_1512`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Laugh

**中文**

大笑

## `ZagreusHome_1513`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Laugh

**中文**

大笑

## `ZagreusHome_1514`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Laugh

**中文**

大笑

## `ZagreusHome_1515`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Chuckle

**中文**

轻笑

## `ZagreusHome_1516`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Chuckle

**中文**

轻笑

## `ZagreusHome_1517`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Meg, Than, what... what are you two... what's going on? Whatever it is, I can explain, or... wait, you're both smiling. Oh... let me just... take this in a moment here.

**中文**

嗳，等下，墨纪，塔纳，你们……你们两个……什么情况？总之，这里发生的事情我都可以解释……等等，你们怎么都在笑。哦……让我……让我先冷静一下。

## `ZagreusHome_1518`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hah! Well then... if this turns out to be some sort of dream, I will be very, very mad. Though... I guess there's one good way to find out.

**中文**

哈！好吧……如果这是个梦的话，那我肯定要气急败坏了。不过……我猜只有一个办法能弄清楚了。

## `ZagreusHome_1519`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Whew, well, now, um, where was I...

**中文**

呼，好了，现在，呃，我刚刚说到哪儿了……

## `ZagreusHome_1520`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...I did. Sometimes I need to slow things down. I'm thankful that we've been on better terms, lately. But, I've been coming on too strong. Forgive me. You're my dear friend. I don't want to do anything to hurt you. Or anybody else.

**中文**

……我真的这么觉得。有时候我需要放慢节奏。我们最近的关系越来越好，我也感到非常欣慰。但我的情绪有点过于激动。请你原谅。你是我的好朋友。我不想伤害你。或者其他人。

## `ZagreusHome_1521`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...I... Meg, I'm deeply flattered, and you're very dear to me, it's just... this isn't what I want from our relationship. I fear I must have led you on. Forgive me. Please say you'll still be my friend?

**中文**

……我……墨纪，我非常荣幸，你对我十分重要，只不过……这不是我想从我们之间的关系中得到的东西。恐怕是我骗了你。原谅我。我们还可以继续做朋友吧？

## `ZagreusHome_1522`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should turn around. My escape route's in the courtyard I just left.

**中文**

应该掉头了，我的逃跑路线就在我刚刚离开的院子里。

## `ZagreusHome_1523`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Whew, well, that was... that was... ahem.

**中文**

哎呀，这，这可真是……这可真是……嗯嗯。

## `ZagreusHome_1524`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Someone's at the desk next to the throne...

**中文**

王座边的桌子后面有什么人……

## `ZagreusHome_1525`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Some sort of special offer for me, my good Shade?

**中文**

有什特别优惠吗，我的好暗灵？

## `ZagreusHome_1526`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Work authorized! A fountain chamber in the depths of Tartarus sounds like an excellent idea.

**中文**

工程批准！塔耳塔罗斯深处装个泉水间听上去是个非常好的主意。

## `ZagreusHome_1527`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, that? Just another job from the House Contractor near as I'm concerned, sir. Not like anything on the list was getting done while it was stuck in the archives, besides!

**中文**

噢，你说那个？对我来说只是圣殿承包商的另一项任务而已，先生。再说了，如果一直放在档案馆里，清单上的事情一件也做不成！

## `ZagreusHome_1528`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I wouldn't mind their help I guess!

**中文**

我大概不介意他们来帮忙吧！

## `ZagreusHome_1529`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, sir? I got a relic of some sort out there... a globule of ancient blood, which formed after I fought with Megaera. Is this really the Titans' blood I found?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯先生？我在外面捡到一样东西……一滴远古之血，我打败墨纪拉之后出现的。我找到的这个真是泰坦之血吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1530`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well! I wasn't about to drink it.

**中文**

好吧！我也没打算喝。

## `ZagreusHome_1531`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, look at what I got out there, Achilles sir! A vintage bottle of Ambrosia, plucked straight from Elysium! I hear this stuff is very good, but... can't even open it. I've no idea what to do with it, right now.

**中文**

嘿，看我拿到了什么，阿喀琉斯先生！一瓶陈酿仙馔密酒，从至福乐土弄到手的！听说这是好东西，但我……怎么也打不开。我现在也不知道该拿它怎么办。

## `ZagreusHome_1532`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sell it or keep it, huh.

**中文**

卖还是留，唉。

## `ZagreusHome_1533`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, sir? You happen to know whether Father authorized the transfer of a Macedonian Tau-Lambda chariot to Elysium in recent memory? I ran into the former hero Theseus, all joyriding about in one of those.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯先生，你有没有印象我的父亲最近授权把马其顿塔乌-拉姆达战车转移到至福乐土？我遇到一位昔日的英雄忒修斯，他当时正驾着其中一辆在兜风。

## `ZagreusHome_1534`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, you mean the golden matching mask? I saw him wearing it, all right. Looked like a proper fool, I thought. What of it, though?

**中文**

什么，你是说他的黄金面具吗？我看他当时是戴着的。我当时还想呢，真是够傻的。那面具有什么不一般的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1535`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That it did most definitely, sir.

**中文**

毫无疑问，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_1536`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, you know, just... the usual struggles of battling toward the surface, generally to some ignoble death, though... wait, you mean between myself and Megaera, don't you.

**中文**

是啊，就那些，只是……往地表上逃跑的常见挣扎，通常是遇到一些愚蠢的死法，尽管……等等，你是说我和墨纪拉之间，不是吗。

## `ZagreusHome_1537`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

And, what if I don't...? I hesitate to even use the word.

**中文**

如果我不是呢……？！我都不知道该不该用这个字眼。

## `ZagreusHome_1538`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you for the advice, Achilles, sir.

**中文**

感谢您的建议，阿喀琉斯老师。

## `ZagreusHome_1539`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, you know, just... the usual struggles of battling toward the surface, generally to some ignoble death, though... wait, you mean between myself and Thanatos, don't you.

**中文**

是啊，就那些，只是……往地表上逃跑的常见挣扎，通常是遇到一些愚蠢的死法，尽管……等等，您是说我和塔纳托斯之间，不是吗。

## `ZagreusHome_1540`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Whoa, just a moment, there, I mean... I've never said something like that, have I?

**中文**

哇，等、等一下，我的意思是……我从没说过那样的话，对吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1541`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Right. You're right, Achilles.

**中文**

对。您说得对，阿喀琉斯。

## `ZagreusHome_1542`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Achilles, I... uncovered a late passage in the Codex, with regard to Thanatos and me. It says, you say, you think that I'm the god of blood? I'm not the god of anything, I think...

**中文**

嘿，阿喀琉斯，我……刚发现手稿中有这么一段，和塔纳托斯还有我有关……它说，应说你写的，你觉得那啥……我是鲜血之神？要我说，我觉得，我……应该什么神也不是……

## `ZagreusHome_1543`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm not the god of anything, all right?

**中文**

我什么的神都不是，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1544`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, it's just... sometimes, I find my thoughts, they wander between those I care about, it's... not just one. You mortals, typically you had a single suitor, did you not...?

**中文**

哦，这只是…… 有时，我发现我的思念，在我关心的事物之间徘徊... 而不仅仅是一个。 凡人们，通常只有一个求婚者，不是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1545`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, you're saying Father, he was never with somebody else besides... my mother?

**中文**

等一下，你是说我的父亲，他从来没有追求过别人，除了……我的母亲？

## `ZagreusHome_1546`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm nothing like him.

**中文**

我跟他一点都不像。

## `ZagreusHome_1547`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Come on, Meg, Father had it coming for some time. What's your concern, besides? The House is safe.

**中文**

好了，墨纪，父亲那件事已经过了很久了。你有什么好担心的呢？圣殿没事的。

## `ZagreusHome_1548`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I will not let that happen.

**中文**

我不会让它发生的。

## `ZagreusHome_1549`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Seemed only fair in the grand scheme of things. He slew his own parents at some point, right? I think the Fates enjoy this sort of thing.

**中文**

总体来说似乎还算公平。他在某个时候杀死了自己的父母，对吗？我觉得命运喜欢这种相应的发展。

## `ZagreusHome_1550`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm planning to.

**中文**

我是那么计划。

## `ZagreusHome_1551`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nyx, I don't suppose you know where Megaera came from...? Is it really true she has no parents, whatsoever...? You've always taken care of all of us, and so... I was just wondering, I guess.

**中文**

倪克斯，您知不知道墨纪拉从哪来的……？她真的是没有父母吗……？您总是这么照顾我们，所以……我只是想知道，吧。

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not enough Titan Blood.

泰坦之血不够。

I need more Titan Blood.

我需要更多的泰坦之血。

Need more Ambrosia here.

需要更多仙馔密酒。

**EN**

Don't have enough Ambrosia.

**EN**

I need to get my hands on more Ambrosia.

## `ZagreusHome_1559`

**EN**

Lacking Ambrosia here.

Need some more Nectar.

需要更多的蜜露。

## `ZagreusHome_1561`

**EN**

Don't have enough Nectar for this.

蜜露还是不够。

## `ZagreusHome_1562`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not enough Nectar.

蜜露不够。

## `ZagreusHome_1563`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Need more Nectar.

**中文**

需要更多的蜜露。

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

There you go, Battie!

去吧，巴蒂！

## `ZagreusHome_1565`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here you go, Shady!

**中文**

去吧，沙迪！

## `ZagreusHome_1566`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Rib, this one's for you!

瑞博，这是给你的！

## `ZagreusHome_1567`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Drink up, there, Mort!

**中文**

全都干了吧，莫特！

## `ZagreusHome_1568`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here you go!

走你！

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

There.

**中文**

好了。

## `ZagreusHome_1570`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Upgraded!

升级完成！

## `ZagreusHome_1571`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Better than new.

焕然一新。

There we go!

开始了！

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Better.

更好了。

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Powered up.

力量提升。

Battie, you're all set!

巴蒂，你准备好了！

Shady, you're all set!

沙迪，你准备好了！

## `ZagreusHome_1577`

Rib, you are all set!

瑞博，你准备好了！

## `ZagreusHome_1578`

**EN**

Mort, you are all set!

莫特，你准备好了！

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I need to turn up the Heat.

我得提升一下热度了。

## `ZagreusHome_1580`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not getting out of here without more Heat.

**中文**

要离开这里，还需要更多热度。

## `ZagreusHome_1581`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I need to add Conditions to the Pact.

**中文**

我得往契约里加点条件。

## `ZagreusHome_1582`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I need to change the Pact before I go.

**中文**

走之前，我得更改一下契约。

## `ZagreusHome_1583`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh bloody hell.

**中文**

噢，真是见了鬼了。

## `ZagreusHome_1584`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I have to modify the Pact.

**中文**

我得改改契约。

## `ZagreusHome_1585`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I need to add more Heat.

**中文**

我得加点热度。

## `ZagreusHome_1586`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A sealed document, regarding Sisyphus?

**中文**

一个封着的，关于西西弗斯的文件？

## `ZagreusHome_1587`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey that's the Court Musician's sentence, long past due...

**中文**

嘿，是宫廷乐师的判决书，他的刑期早就过了……

## `ZagreusHome_1588`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Achilles thinks that I'm the... god of blood?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯认为我是……血之神？

## `ZagreusHome_1589`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Fated List of Minor Prophecies...

**中文**

命运小预言清单..……

## `ZagreusHome_1590`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Fated List...

**中文**

命运清单……

## `ZagreusHome_1591`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Anything on the List?

**中文**

清单上有什么吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1592`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

New details of another prophecy.

**中文**

是另一条预言的新内容。

## `ZagreusHome_1593`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A prophecy's been added to the List.

**中文**

有一则预言加入了清单中。

## `ZagreusHome_1594`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Prophecy fulfilled.

**中文**

预言应验。

## `ZagreusHome_1595`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Another prophecy fulfilled.

**中文**

又一个预言应验。

## `ZagreusHome_1596`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This one's for you, Three Fates!

**中文**

这一个是献给你们的，命运三女神们！

## `ZagreusHome_1597`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's one more prophecy fulfilled.

**中文**

这是又一个预言应验。

## `ZagreusHome_1598`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Pleasure doing business with you, Fates.

**中文**

与你共事很愉快，命运之神。

## `ZagreusHome_1599`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So, tell me something, Father, if you please. Since we're more candid lately in regard to matters of my birthright here, and all... did you and Mother... how to put this? Any brothers or sisters I should know about?

**中文**

那么，我们就开诚布公地说一说吧，父亲大人。既然我们最近能够更坦率地讨论我的继承权问题，那么……你和我的母亲……怎么说好呢，我是不是还有其他的兄弟姐妹？

## `ZagreusHome_1600`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sure, that's fair enough, it's just not all such things occur as planned, as I am told. From tales of your brothers and so forth.

**中文**

当然，你的想法很有道理，但有时候总是事与愿违。至少从你那些兄弟们的传闻里我是这么觉得的。

## `ZagreusHome_1601`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Heartwarming.

**中文**

暖心啊。

## `ZagreusHome_1602`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's what I was afraid of.

**中文**

这就是我怕会发生的。

## `ZagreusHome_1604`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Greetings, Father. Ran into Lady Demeter out there, on my last outing. Or should I say, my grandmother? What's she to you, then, ex-mother-in-law? Source of abject terror? Hard keeping track. Our family's rather complicated.

**中文**

你好，父亲大人。刚刚出去的时候我碰到得墨忒尔女士，或是说，我的外婆 ？她对你来说算是什么？前岳母？或是包袱？我们家族真复杂。

## `ZagreusHome_1605`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Hard to imagine.

**中文**

……难以想象。

## `ZagreusHome_1606`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Question for you, Father. The name Kore happen to mean anything to you, by any chance?

**中文**

给您的问题，父亲大人：有没有可能……科瑞这个名字是不是对您有什么特别的意义？

## `ZagreusHome_1607`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So let's see. Demeter's daughter went missing. She wound up down here. I... happened. Mother left. Demeter, and everyone else on Olympus apparently, has no idea. That about right? Tell me what happened.

**中文**

让我想想。得墨忒尔的女儿失踪了。她后来出现在冥界这里。我……诞生了。母亲走了。得墨忒尔，和奥林匹斯山上的众神，都似乎对这件事毫无头绪。差不多吧？告诉我，究竟发生了什么？

## `ZagreusHome_1608`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...You can't hide the truth forever.

**中文**

……你不可能永远掩盖住真相。

## `ZagreusHome_1609`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, this one's not insipid in the slightest. Let me get this straight. My mother's Persephone. Her mother's Demeter, but her father... evidently was some farm-boy? My red blood comes from him? And my propensity to die I guess.

**中文**

噢，这个问题一点也不无聊。我来理理清楚。我的母亲是珀尔塞福涅。她的母亲叫得墨忒尔，但她的父亲……显然是某个农家男孩？我的身上流着他的红色血液？而且我猜我也会死。

## `ZagreusHome_1610`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Then, Mother, did... she grow older with age? Is she just going to expire as the mortals do? With demigods I hear it can go either way.

**中文**

那，母亲她……难道衰老了？她会像凡人那样死去吗？听说半神都有可能……

## `ZagreusHome_1611`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll have to try that, thanks.

**中文**

我得试试，谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_1612`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Achilles, something has awakened in Varatha... it revealed to me a form I'd never seen. Wielded by a great bearded warrior called Guan Yu. Handsomely dressed! That's no Greek name, is it...

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，瓦拉塔里有什么觉醒了……它向我展示了一个我从没见过的姿态。一位名为关羽的大胡子勇士所有的姿态。他穿的可帅了！但他这不是希腊名吧……

## `ZagreusHome_1613`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Wait, you mean this is an aspect from a time that's yet to come? That hurts my head. Why would it show up all of a sudden, now?

**中文**

等等，您的意思是这是还没有发生的时代的形态？这真让我头疼。它为什么突然的，现在出现？

## `ZagreusHome_1614`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good thing they're on my side.

**中文**

还好他们是站在我这一边的。

## `ZagreusHome_1615`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, you know, just... the usual struggles of battling toward the surface, generally to some ignoble death, though... wait, you mean between me, Meg, and Thanatos, don't you.

**中文**

呃，就那些，只是……逃往地表时经常在缠斗中挣扎，主要是那些愚蠢的死法，尽管……等等，您是在说我、墨纪拉和塔纳托斯之间的事，不是吗。

## `ZagreusHome_1616`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Whoa, just a moment, there, I mean... ugh, I suppose you're not presuming much at all. Follow my heart, though? Where?

**中文**

哇噢，你等一下，我是说……呃，我觉得你的推想毫无道理。追随本心？要追到哪里去？

## `ZagreusHome_1617`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, it's just... I met the goddess Demeter out there. The blessings that she offered, they... just drained the heat and life from everything. I thought she saw to fruits and grains and vegetables and such? Not that I'm complaining.

**中文**

哦，只是……我在外面遇到了得墨忒尔女神。 她提供的祝福，它们……吸取并消耗一切热量和生命。 我以为她的领域是养育水果，谷物和蔬菜等？ 我不是在抱怨。

## `ZagreusHome_1618`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...I will.

**中文**

……我会的。

## `ZagreusHome_1619`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm glad you think so, mate! These tales of my valorous accomplishments do seem to cheer you up, so, I will keep reporting back once I rack up some more.

**中文**

你能这样想，我很高兴，我的朋友！我的这些英勇事迹看样子能让你开心起来，这样的话，我以后有什么新进展就回来给你说说。

## `ZagreusHome_1620`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You don't like it when I pet your other heads, huh? No!

**中文**

你不喜欢我摸你其它的头，是么？不愿意啊！

## `ZagreusHome_1621`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here boy, just as long as one of your three heads enjoy these pets!

**中文**

来，小家伙，只要你任何一头喜欢抚摸！

## `ZagreusHome_1622`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

If I don't pet your other heads, don't bite my arm off, deal?

**中文**

如果我不摸你其它头，那就别把我的手咬掉，同意吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1623`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not everybody knows exactly how to give you pets and live to tell of it, right, boy?

**中文**

不是所有摸过你的人，都有命告诉别人要怎么摸你才对，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_1624`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Varatha... who's this... Guan Yu?

**中文**

瓦拉塔……这……关羽……是谁？

## `ZagreusHome_1625`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Grant me the aspect of Guan Yu!

**中文**

赐给我关羽的姿态！

## `ZagreusHome_1626`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Guan Yu.

**中文**

关羽。

## `ZagreusHome_1627`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Guan Yu...!

**中文**

关羽……！

Ambrosia, then, why not.

## `ZagreusHome_1684`

**EN**

Trade you this Titan Blood.

就用这瓶泰坦之血来和你交易吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1685`

**EN**

Here's Titan Blood, then.

好了，泰坦之血归你了。

**EN**

I'll take the Special, please.

**中文**

这件特殊物品我要了。

## `ZagreusHome_1687`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's too good a deal to pass up.

**中文**

这个好东西可不能错过。

## `ZagreusHome_1688`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll take that deal.

**中文**

我接受交易。

## `ZagreusHome_1689`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Give me the current Special.

**中文**

我要限时优惠的那个商品。

## `ZagreusHome_1690`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good deal, here's the fee.

**中文**

成交，给你钱。

## `ZagreusHome_1691`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I like the sound of this. Work approved and here's the fee, good Contractor!

**中文**

听起来不错，我批准了。这是你的费用，承包商先生！

## `ZagreusHome_1692`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I like the sound of this one here, good Contractor. Go make it happen!

**中文**

在这里造个这种东西，听起来不错。承包商先生，把它给我造出来吧！

## `ZagreusHome_1693`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Contractor, I would ask that you pursue this job. I'll be excited to see the result!

**中文**

承包商，你来继续做这份工作吧，我已经迫不及待想要看到成果了！

## `ZagreusHome_1694`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Your asking price, good Contractor. Well worth the fruits of your hard work, I'm sure.

**中文**

这是给你的费用，承包商先生，肯定配得上你的工作成果。

## `ZagreusHome_1695`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Since Father's not paying us any heed, what say with go with this assignment, Contractor?

**中文**

反正父亲根本不会管这件事，你看这个项目如何，承包商？

## `ZagreusHome_1696`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I grant you funding and autonomy to do this job upon your earliest convenience, Contractor.

**中文**

我现在给予你资金和自主权，让你可以尽快完成这次的工作，承包商。

## `ZagreusHome_1697`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right! If this isn't a good expenditure of resources, then I don't know what is.

**中文**

太好了！如果这都不能算好钢用在刀刃上，那我也不知道什么能算了。

## `ZagreusHome_1698`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

On my authority, I'd like it if you did this job for me, is that all right?

**中文**

我特许你来为我完成这次的工作，怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_1699`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Contractor, work approved.

**中文**

工作批准了，承包商。

## `ZagreusHome_1700`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think we'll go with this.

**中文**

那就开始吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1701`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Contractor, I've another job.

**中文**

承包商，有新工作要给你了。

## `ZagreusHome_1702`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's go with this one, then.

**中文**

来做这个吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1703`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This should be good I think.

**中文**

这应该会很不错。

## `ZagreusHome_1704`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well, I don't see why not.

**中文**

嗯，有何不可呢？

## `ZagreusHome_1705`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No need to overthink this one I guess.

**中文**

我看这件事不用想得太多。

## `ZagreusHome_1706`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How 'bout this, Contractor?

**中文**

这个如何，承包商？

## `ZagreusHome_1707`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's not our custom to hang brightly colored things in here, though why not try these on for size.

**中文**

我们这里还不怎么习惯使用这种明亮的配色，不过试一试也无妨。

## `ZagreusHome_1708`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hanging bright-colored explosives all about my chambers strikes me as a fine idea!

**中文**

在我的房间里挂满色彩鲜艳的爆炸物，听起来是个好主意！

## `ZagreusHome_1709`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I positively cannot wait to see what sorts of foul creatures I'll dredge up with this!

**中文**

我已经迫不及待地想看看用这个能捞出什么鬼东西了！

## `ZagreusHome_1710`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A fine hallway requires a fine rug, I always say! I say it sometimes...

**中文**

我常说，好走廊要配好地毯！我确实说过……

## `ZagreusHome_1711`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh we absolutely need to get this rug, right over there.

**中文**

噢，这块地毯一定要整上，就放在那里。

## `ZagreusHome_1712`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

By my decree, this House is not a house without this rug.

**中文**

我下令，这座圣殿不能没有这块地毯。

## `ZagreusHome_1713`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

If there's a finer rug to set back there, I'll likely end up getting it as well.

**中文**

如果有块更好的地毯的话，我无论如何都要得到它。

## `ZagreusHome_1714`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What time is it? That's right: Rug-switching time!

**中文**

现在是几点？没错，是换地毯的点！

## `ZagreusHome_1715`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hrm I don't know let's try a different rug.

**中文**

嗯，我也说不好，不如换块地毯试试吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1716`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's try a different rug there, if you please.

**中文**

我们换一块地毯看看吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1717`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I've had a change of heart about that rug if you don't mind.

**中文**

如果你不介意的话，我想换掉那块地毯。

## `ZagreusHome_1718`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think we both know that it's time to change the rug.

**中文**

我们都知道，是时候该换一下地毯了。

## `ZagreusHome_1719`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

If anybody needs to see their own reflection down the hall, well, now they can!

**中文**

如果有谁想在大厅里看看自己的倒影的话，现在就可以去了！

## `ZagreusHome_1720`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let us hereby have better seating, on which I shall never rest, you mark my words!

**中文**

尽管我永远不会在上面休息，让我们在这附近来点更好的座位，你们都注意我的话！

## `ZagreusHome_1721`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We'll have only the finest artwork in this House, and I shall be the self-appointed judge!

**中文**

在这座大殿里我们只会有最好的艺术品，我就是审判官——自封的！

## `ZagreusHome_1722`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It seems my princely duty to commission this fine portrait of myself!

**中文**

是时候来一幅本王子自己的画像了！

## `ZagreusHome_1723`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Since this is Father's House it's only fair the walls be slathered with his sour look.

**中文**

既然这里是父亲的圣殿，那墙上挂着他那张臭脸也说得过去。

## `ZagreusHome_1724`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I've no idea who this is but she's so elegant she ought adorn these halls, if you ask me!

**中文**

我不知道这是哪位，但她是如此高雅，肯定会让这些厅堂蓬荜生辉，我敢打包票！

## `ZagreusHome_1725`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This piece has the power to embarrass several members of this House all in one swoop.

**中文**

这幅画能一下子让好几个人觉得浑身不自在。

## `ZagreusHome_1726`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh gods, am I becoming hopelessly obsessed with Theseus...?

**中文**

众神啊，我就非得被忒修斯纠缠不休吗……？

## `ZagreusHome_1727`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Every self-respecting House needs a portrait of a girl from Crete.

**中文**

没有克里特美女画像的圣殿，不是什么好圣殿。

## `ZagreusHome_1728`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'd like to see the depths of Tartarus adorn those walls back there!

**中文**

我乐意看着这塔耳塔罗斯的深渊是如何装点那些墙壁的！

## `ZagreusHome_1729`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This artful piece of terrifying Asphodel should add some atmosphere.

**中文**

这幅水仙花平原的吓人画作应该能制造点氛围。

## `ZagreusHome_1730`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I've not seen the famous Heracles about, and so, let's have a marble effigy of him, instead!

**中文**

我从来没见过有名的英雄赫拉克勒斯，所以，何不来一座他的大理石雕像呢！

## `ZagreusHome_1731`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A commemorative hammer adds a touch of elegance to any gloomy hall.

**中文**

这把有纪念意义的锤子，给这阴暗的大厅增添了一丝优雅的氛围。

## `ZagreusHome_1732`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Father always wanted a sundial, though that aside, let's have a sundial back there, posthaste!

**中文**

父亲一直想要一个日晷，那么事不宜迟，咱们就在那儿摆一个吧！

## `ZagreusHome_1733`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm oddly smitten with this green-tinged orb. Who wouldn't be?

**中文**

不知为何，我被这淡绿色的球体迷住了，谁不会呢？

## `ZagreusHome_1734`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's switch things up on that display back there.

**中文**

咱们把那边的东西换一换吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1735`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think we'd better go with this option instead.

**中文**

我觉得这样比较好。

## `ZagreusHome_1736`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What say we change things up again, all right?

**中文**

要不然咱们再换一次，怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_1737`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's shift some things around if you don't mind.

**中文**

没问题的话，咱们就把这些东西重新折腾一下吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1738`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think we ought to mix things up a bit.

**中文**

我觉得混搭比较好。

## `ZagreusHome_1739`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's try this on for size instead, OK?

**中文**

不如来试试这个吧，怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_1740`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A hideous rendition of a grizzled skeleton seems suitable decor.

**中文**

一副逼真吓人的骷髅架子，好像跟这里的风格很搭啊。

## `ZagreusHome_1741`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This thought-provoking piece seems like a decent conversation-starter for the hall.

**中文**

这件发人深省的作品，应该能让大厅里的人打开话匣子。

## `ZagreusHome_1742`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A nice floral arrangement ought to lighten up the mood a little bit.

**中文**

漂亮的插花应该会让这里的气氛活跃起来。

## `ZagreusHome_1743`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm sure nobody's going to mind some lovely flowers there.

**中文**

我敢肯定，没人会对这些可爱的鲜花有意见。

## `ZagreusHome_1744`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Back there, let's change the color theming up a little bit.

**中文**

好了，咱们改变一下颜色，让这里的气氛变得活跃一些吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1745`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This color ought to suit the hall back there.

**中文**

这个颜色应该和大厅的风格很搭。

## `ZagreusHome_1746`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We should redecorate the hall back there like so.

**中文**

应该把大厅也重新装饰一下。

## `ZagreusHome_1747`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This is the color suited for our hall back there.

**中文**

这个颜色比较适合大厅。

## `ZagreusHome_1748`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's try this color on for size instead.

**中文**

还是试试这个颜色吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1749`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'd like to see it in this color once again.

**中文**

我想再看看它这个颜色。

## `ZagreusHome_1750`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This color seemed to suit the hall quite well.

**中文**

这颜色好像挺适合大厅的。

## `ZagreusHome_1751`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's change the colors up back there again.

**中文**

再把颜色换一换吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1752`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

For Cerberus, only the greatest bed will do.

**中文**

对刻耳柏洛斯来说，它只肯睡最舒服的床。

## `ZagreusHome_1753`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This seems a suitably fine resting-pad for Cerberus.

**中文**

这张垫子应该挺适合刻耳柏洛斯的。

## `ZagreusHome_1754`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Excuse me while I swap your bedding for you, boy.

**中文**

不好意思，乖狗狗，我要给你换床垫啦。

## `ZagreusHome_1755`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's change the bedding up for Cerberus.

**中文**

给刻耳柏洛斯换张床吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1756`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This other bedding seems a better fit.

**中文**

这张床垫好像更适合一点。

## `ZagreusHome_1757`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hard to decide which bedding type to choose.

**中文**

我也不知道改选什么样的床垫。

## `ZagreusHome_1758`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A marble bust of some old man seems just the thing to liven this place up.

**中文**

摆上一尊什么老人半身像，这地方的气氛应该就能活跃起来了。

## `ZagreusHome_1759`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think those columns still could use some sprucing up.

**中文**

我觉得那几根柱子也要装饰一番。

## `ZagreusHome_1760`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Was thinking it'd be good to change those columns up a bit.

**中文**

这些柱子最好也整点新花样。

## `ZagreusHome_1761`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Changed my mind about those columns there.

**中文**

我觉得那些柱子的样式应该改一改。

## `ZagreusHome_1762`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's go back to an older style for those columns.

**中文**

给这些柱子来些经典的款式吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1763`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cerberus was never keen on playing ball, but on the other hand, who doesn't like a ball?

**中文**

刻耳柏洛斯不喜欢玩球，不过话说回来，真有人不喜欢玩球吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1764`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

If Hypnos is going to be sleeping on the job, he ought to do it in the lap of luxury.

**中文**

如果修普诺斯打算上班打盹的话，那也应该给他弄得舒服一些才行。

## `ZagreusHome_1765`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They say that only Heracles himself can lift these things? I'll take them, then!

**中文**

他们说只有赫拉克勒斯才能举得动这些东西？那我全要了！

## `ZagreusHome_1766`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The hall to Father ought to look more sinister, I think.

**中文**

从大厅到父亲这段路，应该给人更加凶险的感觉才对。

## `ZagreusHome_1767`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A bit more mood lighting in the main hall is certainly in order, isn't it?

**中文**

在大厅里来点氛围光照还是很不错的，对吧？

## `ZagreusHome_1768`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

These undead flower petals ought to add an extra touch of elegance I think.

**中文**

这些永生花的花瓣，应该能给这里额外增添一丝优雅气息。

## `ZagreusHome_1769`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Can't have too many undead flower petals around here.

**中文**

永生花的花瓣越多越好。

## `ZagreusHome_1770`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's get some undead flower petals over there, posthaste!

**中文**

那边再来点永生花的花瓣，别磨蹭！

## `ZagreusHome_1771`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I won't be needing these but anyone emerging from the Pool of Styx might like them I suppose.

**中文**

这些东西我已经用不着了，不过那些冥河里钻出来的人应该会喜欢。

## `ZagreusHome_1772`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's set some decadent old tombs right smack in the middle of the hall back there.

**中文**

咱们在大厅正中间摆几尊古墓吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1773`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should make my bedchambers a little more presentable I guess.

**中文**

应该让我的卧室变得更舒服一点才对。

## `ZagreusHome_1774`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I have this crippling compulsion to commission objects I am never, ever going to sit on.

**中文**

我现在特别想弄一些我永远都不会坐上去的椅子来。

## `ZagreusHome_1775`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Always wanted my own entertainment table, and I can finally afford one, I suppose.

**中文**

我一直都想有一张自己的游戏桌，现在我应该买得起了。

## `ZagreusHome_1776`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

If some big light-refracting orb won't fill the lounge with revelry, I don't know what will.

**中文**

如果这么大的折光水晶球都不能让休息室充满欢乐的气氛，那我也没办法了。

## `ZagreusHome_1777`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The lounge could use a decent rug I think.

**中文**

休息室里应该摆条不错的地毯。

## `ZagreusHome_1778`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This stately rug should match the lounge decor.

**中文**

这条富丽堂皇的地毯很适合休息室的风格。

## `ZagreusHome_1779`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The lounge's regulars might like this rug I guess.

**中文**

休息室的常客应该会喜欢这条地毯的。

## `ZagreusHome_1780`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A serviceable rug for a serviceable lounge.

**中文**

耐用的地毯，耐用的休息室。

## `ZagreusHome_1781`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The wretched broker has to stand all day... or float? Anyway, maybe this helps.

**中文**

这个残魂商人是不是整天都在站着……还是飘着？不管怎样，这个大概都能派上用场。

## `ZagreusHome_1782`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It can't be eaten, as I understand, but it can be displayed for all to see!

**中文**

我觉得这东西不能吃，不过可以展出来给大家看看！

## `ZagreusHome_1783`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cerberus won't touch these things, although some shades seem to enjoy them fine.

**中文**

这些东西刻耳柏洛斯压根碰都不碰，不过有些暗灵好像很喜欢。

## `ZagreusHome_1784`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Think this rug's really going to tie the whole room together.

**中文**

我觉得这条地毯能让整个房间的风格都协调起来。

## `ZagreusHome_1785`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's take that down for now.

**中文**

现在先把这个摘下来吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1786`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's pack that up for now.

**中文**

现在先把这个收起来吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1787`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Teardown approved, Contractor.

**中文**

我批准拆迁了，承包商。

## `ZagreusHome_1788`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I miss the way things used to be with that.

**中文**

真怀念过去的样子啊。

## `ZagreusHome_1789`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's go without that for a bit, all right?

**中文**

看看没了这些会怎么样吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1790`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's bring that back I think.

**中文**

还是把它装回去吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1791`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's put that up again.

**中文**

把那东西再弄回去吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1792`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's see how this looks again.

**中文**

再来看看这东西怎么样吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1793`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's put this back up, why not.

**中文**

把它重新弄回去吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1794`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's go ahead with this again.

**中文**

再看看有了这些是什么样。

## `ZagreusHome_1795`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Who needs sleep?

**中文**

谁需要睡觉啊？

## `ZagreusHome_1796`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not even tired, thanks.

**中文**

谢谢，但我一点都不累。

## `ZagreusHome_1797`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Can't sleep.

**中文**

睡不着。

## `ZagreusHome_1798`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll sleep when I'm dead.

**中文**

等我死了再睡。

## `ZagreusHome_1799`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm wide awake.

**中文**

我清醒得很。

## `ZagreusHome_1800`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's never bedtime here.

**中文**

现在可不是睡觉的时候。

## `ZagreusHome_1801`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should visit the Head Chef.

**中文**

该去看看大厨了。

## `ZagreusHome_1802`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Head Chef always wants fish.

**中文**

头厨总是想要鱼。

## `ZagreusHome_1803`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fish delivery for the Head Chef.

**中文**

给头厨送鱼了。

## `ZagreusHome_1804`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Head Chef is going to want this fish.

**中文**

头厨得想要这条鱼。

## `ZagreusHome_1805`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here's what I caught lately.

**中文**

这是我最近抓到的。

## `ZagreusHome_1806`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here's what I caught.

**中文**

这是我抓到的。

## `ZagreusHome_1807`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here's all I caught.

**中文**

这是我抓到的东西。

## `ZagreusHome_1808`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Freshly caught just for you, Chef.

**中文**

为你带来新鲜的食材，大厨。

## `ZagreusHome_1809`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here's the latest catch, Chef.

**中文**

新抓的食材，大厨。

## `ZagreusHome_1810`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here's the catch of the day.

**中文**

都是今天抓的。

## `ZagreusHome_1811`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here's the catch of the day... or night?

**中文**

都是今天……或者说今晚抓的。

## `ZagreusHome_1812`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Trade you these horrid things.

**中文**

这些破烂东西都卖给你了。

## `ZagreusHome_1813`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Please take these, will you, Chef?

**中文**

这些东西都收下吧，大厨。

## `ZagreusHome_1814`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I come to you bearing aquatic beasts.

**中文**

我给你带来了这些生猛海鲜。

## `ZagreusHome_1815`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here, you can have these, Chef.

**中文**

来，全给你了，大厨。

## `ZagreusHome_1816`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Do what you will with these, my good Chef.

**中文**

随便你怎么处理这些都行，我的好大厨。

## `ZagreusHome_1817`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Some water-dwelling beasts for you, good Chef.

**中文**

一些水里游的，大厨。

## `ZagreusHome_1818`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This is my latest haul.

**中文**

都是新鲜的。

## `ZagreusHome_1819`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Look at what I caught, Chef!

**中文**

看看我都抓了什么，大厨！

## `ZagreusHome_1820`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Why thank you!

**中文**

谢谢你！

## `ZagreusHome_1821`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Pleasure doing business.

**中文**

和你做生意很愉快。

## `ZagreusHome_1822`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm much obliged.

**中文**

非常感谢。

## `ZagreusHome_1823`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Seems fair enough.

**中文**

公平合理。

## `ZagreusHome_1824`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fair market value.

**中文**

价格很合理。

## `ZagreusHome_1825`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Totally worth it.

**中文**

物有所值。

## `ZagreusHome_1826`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right, then!

**中文**

好嘞！

## `ZagreusHome_1827`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We're settled up.

**中文**

完事了。

## `ZagreusHome_1828`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Always a pleasure.

**中文**

非常愉快。

## `ZagreusHome_1829`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Till next time!

**中文**

下次见！

## `ZagreusHome_1830`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Use them wisely, my friend.

**中文**

好好使用，我的朋友。

## `ZagreusHome_1831`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Done deal!

**中文**

交易完成！

## `ZagreusHome_1832`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well...?

**中文**

嗯……？

## `ZagreusHome_1833`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Eh...

**中文**

呃……

## `ZagreusHome_1834`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hrm...

**中文**

嗯……

## `ZagreusHome_1835`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mm...

**中文**

嗯……

## `ZagreusHome_1836`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Scoff

**中文**

嘲笑

## `ZagreusHome_1837`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This is hard.

**中文**

有点难。

## `ZagreusHome_1838`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Need practice.

## `ZagreusHome_1887`

啊，我也希望能再次快得不讲道理啊……

## `ZagreusHome_1891`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Skelly? You look like you know your way around Tartarus. Found some of this ancient Titan blood out there, and I was looking to score some more.

**中文**

喂，骨头，你应该蛮熟悉塔耳塔罗斯的吧。我在那里找到了这些远古泰坦之血，我打算多弄点这种血来。

## `ZagreusHome_1892`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Get out of Tartarus with each of my infernal arms to get more Titan Blood, then? Got it, thank you, mate.

**中文**

用我那些冥府兵器各通过一次塔耳塔罗斯，就能得到更多泰坦之血？明白了，谢谢你了，伙计。

## `ZagreusHome_1893`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well there it is in case you change your mind!

**中文**

好了，我可不希望你再改主意了！

## `ZagreusHome_1894`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hahahaha.

哈哈哈哈。

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Duly noted.

明白了。

## `ZagreusHome_1896`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

## `ZagreusHome_1897`

**EN**

I just prefer to stand, all right?

**中文**

我只是喜欢站着，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1898`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

**中文**

我经常锻炼的，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1899`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Face me yourself, old man.

**中文**

亲自来见我一面吧，老头。

## `ZagreusHome_1900`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

**中文**

**EN**

**中文**

不会真这么难弹吧？

**EN**

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_1903`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

**中文**

我能驾驭得了。

**EN**

**中文**

**EN**

Is it a sword?

**中文**

这是把剑吗？

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

He sure looks tall...

**中文**

他看起来确实很高……

## `ZagreusHome_1907`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Heard it was interesting.

**中文**

我听说很有意思。

## `ZagreusHome_1908`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You there, old man?

**中文**

喂，老头，在吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1909`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Come out of there.

**中文**

快出来吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1910`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Show yourself.

**中文**

## `ZagreusHome_1911`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I know you're in there.

**中文**

我知道你在里头。

## `ZagreusHome_1912`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Say something.

**中文**

说句话啊。

## `ZagreusHome_1913`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hello?

**中文**

喂？

## `ZagreusHome_1914`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Who is this swarthy looking character?

**中文**

这个黑皮肤的家伙又是谁？

## `ZagreusHome_1915`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I bet there were far greater poets.

**中文**

我敢说，以前的诗人肯定要出色得多。

## `ZagreusHome_1916`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hello....?

**中文**

有人吗……？

## `ZagreusHome_1917`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Helloooo?

有——人——吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1918`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I know you can hear me, old man.

**中文**

我知道你能听见，老头。

## `ZagreusHome_1919`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Say something!

**中文**

说话啊！

## `ZagreusHome_1920`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's me! Dusa, I'm impressed you're able to manage this whole lounge all by yourself. How do you do it, what's your secret?

**中文**

没错，是我！杜莎，你能独自管理整个休息室真的是很厉害呀。你是怎么做到的，有什么秘诀吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1921`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Of course not. What's behind schedule, though? This place doesn't seem any worse for wear than usual.

**中文**

当然不会了。不过到底什么工作拖慢了进度？这地方看上去也不比平时差啊。

## `ZagreusHome_1922`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Isn't there something I can do to help...?

**中文**

我应该能帮上什么忙吧……？

## `ZagreusHome_1923`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dusa, wait, before you fly away... there must be something I can do, to help pitch in with everything? Doesn't matter how good you are at your job, what you're describing, that's more than one individual can handle!

**中文**

杜莎，等等，在你飞走之前……这里一定有我能帮上忙的工作吧？就算你再怎么出色，就像你刚才说的，这可不是一个人能做完的工作！

## `ZagreusHome_1924`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

The House Contractor isn't cooperating with you, huh. No wonder so many lounge renovations are clogged up in the works. Though maybe I can grease the wheels a bit?

**中文**

圣殿承包商不配合你的工作啊。难怪这么多休息室翻新工作都滞后了。也许我可以稍微让进度快一点。

## `ZagreusHome_1925`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

She's running herself ragged.

**中文**

她要把自己累趴下了。

## `ZagreusHome_1926`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Ooh, that might be my fault. I've been giving the Contractor lots of work. Some of those lounge orders must be coming from you, right? This place is really shaping up!

**中文**

噢，这可能是我的问题。我让承包商做的工作太多了。其中一些休息室的工作单应该是你下的吧？这地方真是越来越好了啊！

## `ZagreusHome_1927`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just trying to help...?

**中文**

我只是想帮个忙……？

## `ZagreusHome_1928`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

The lounge is really starting to look nice lately, Dusa! All your hard work is really paying off!

**中文**

休息室看起来真是越来越好了，杜莎！这都是你的功劳！

## `ZagreusHome_1929`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm looking forward!

**中文**

期待下次再见！
