## `ZagreusHome_1930`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, no, now's fine, of course, what is it, Dusa? I'm not so busy, by the way, and always happy to check in. Wait, is something wrong?

**中文**

不用，不用，现在就可以说。说吧，杜莎。其实我也不是很忙，来这里看看，我还是很高兴的。等下，有什么问题吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1931`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It was my pleasure, really. All of us use the lounge, don't we? This House can really be a lot, but in the lounge here, things are easier a bit, I think. I'm so glad we have you watching over it.

**中文**

我很荣幸，真的。我们每个人都会用到休息室，不是吗？这座圣殿确实包罗万象，但是到休息室这里，我觉得事情就变得简单起来了。感谢有你来照看这个地方。

## `ZagreusHome_1932`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Take care now!

**中文**

多保重！

## `ZagreusHome_1933`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dusa, you must work harder than anyone in this House, keeping everything so neat and orderly. I don't think it should be thankless work, so, thank you! And I'd like for you to have this.

**中文**

杜莎，你一定是这里工作最认真，最努力的人，这样才能把这里整理得如此整洁整齐。我要好好谢谢你，真的十分感谢！还有，这个是送给你的。

## `ZagreusHome_1934`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, you said it all! Don't mean to put you on the spot or anything. I'm just glad we've had a chance to get to know each other.

**中文**

噢，什么都别说啦！我真的不是故意要让你如此紧张的。只是很高兴我们能彼此认识。

## `ZagreusHome_1935`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

OK, bye...!

**中文**

好吧，再见……！

## `ZagreusHome_1936`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey Dusa? Here's some more of this really, really good Nectar for you! Please keep up the wonderful work, and more importantly, keep being your wonderful self.

**中文**

嗨，杜莎？我这还有一些非常不错的蜜露要给你！还希望你能再接再厉，更重要的是，要坚持做优秀的自己。

## `ZagreusHome_1937`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Please, it's yours! Besides, you must have some time off coming up when you can enjoy it, don't you?

**中文**

这就是给你的！再说了，你肯定能抽出时间好好尝一尝的，不是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1938`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well, you enjoy, all right?

**中文**

嗯，尽情享受吧，知道了吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1939`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dusa, I don't mean to upset you but, I'm worried you're going to burn yourself out if you keep pushing yourself past your limits... are you sure you don't need a break?

**中文**

杜莎，我也不想烦你，但如果你一直这么逼着自己突破极限，我担心你最后会把自己累坏了……你确定不休息一下吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1940`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Please take care of yourself.

**中文**

请务必保重。

## `ZagreusHome_1941`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Dusa? Congratulations on completing your assignment with the lounge. Look at this place! It's better than ever. All thanks to your effort and dedication. Here, this is for you. Something special for the occasion.

**中文**

嗨，杜莎！恭喜你完成了休息室的工作。快看看这里！比以前好多了。感谢你，这全是你的功劳！来，这是给你的，很特别的东西。

## `ZagreusHome_1942`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A Chthonic Companion!! Dusa, really? You're the best!

**中文**

冥界伴偶！！杜莎，真的吗？？你真是太好了！

## `ZagreusHome_1943`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Ambrosia isn't only for the gods, you know, Dusa. It's for anyone we really care about. So, here. Please, enjoy this, or... just add it to the lounge's stash. Whatever suits you.

**中文**

仙馔密酒可不是神专享的，杜莎。它可以用来送给我们真正在乎的人。所以说，请收下吧。不然……直接放到休息室的储柜里也行。看你怎样方便了。

## `ZagreusHome_1944`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're so much more than what you said. I wish you could see that. If only everybody in this House had your spirit.

**中文**

你可比自己说的好多了。我希望你要知道这一点。如果圣殿里的人都能有你这样的精神就好了。

## `ZagreusHome_1945`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It should be more than sometimes that you feel that way, as far as I'm concerned. I just... I've never met anybody like you before. My father always told me not to smile quite as much, but... you're like me.

**中文**

我可不是有时候才会去关心你啊。我只是……从没遇到过像你一样的人。我父亲总教我做一个不苟言笑的人。不过……你跟我很像。

## `ZagreusHome_1946`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Dusa, wait...!

**中文**

杜莎，等等……！

## `ZagreusHome_1947`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I got you more Ambrosia, Dusa, and I hope this is OK. I figure maybe you could use this for the lounge? Special events, guests, that sort of thing?

**中文**

我又给你送仙馔密酒来啦，杜莎，希望你能收下。也许你能在休息室里用到这些，比如说特殊的场合、款待客人之类的。

## `ZagreusHome_1948`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, look, if I listened to everything I was told, my life would be a lot different and a lot worse off, I think! So... who cares what anybody says? I enjoy your company.

**中文**

嗯……你知道吗，如果别人让我干什么，我就干什么，那我的人生可能会完全不一样，变得更糟糕了！所以说……不要太在乎别人的想法。有你陪我，我很高兴。

## `ZagreusHome_1949`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I remember when we first met. You were carrying three bowls for Cerberus and bumped right into me. It all went flying, but you cleaned up half of it before it hit the ground. Then flew away before I got your name. I thought I'd never see you again.

**中文**

第一次和你见面的情形依然历历在目。当时，你端着刻耳柏洛斯的三个碗跟我撞了个满怀。东西全都撞飞了，但是你在东西落地之前就清理了一半。然后，我还没来得及问你名字，你就一溜烟地跑开了。我还以为再也见不到你了。

## `ZagreusHome_1950`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

See you, Dusa.

**中文**

回见，杜莎。

## `ZagreusHome_1951`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dusa, I was thinking that this batch of Ambrosia ought to hold the lounge over for quite a while! Keep things livelier for everyone. And, please take a bottle for yourself, won't you? As a token of my affection.

**中文**

杜莎，我觉得这批仙馔密酒应该够休息室用一阵子的了！让大家都有个好心情。你自己也来一杯吧，怎么样？就当是我的一点心意了。

## `ZagreusHome_1952`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I would be more than that, if it pleased you. You're very wonderful, and... your beauty lights up this House. But I would never risk our friendship, so, if this is where we draw the boundary, please, just let me know.

**中文**

如果你愿意，不只做朋友也可以的。你真的很不错，而且……你的美让整座圣殿蓬荜生辉。但我不能拿我们的友谊来冒这个险，如果你觉得我们应该保持界限，请一定让我知道。

## `ZagreusHome_1953`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I did, indeed. And I am glad to get it off my chest, if you must know! Though... what about you? How do you feel?

**中文**

是啊，我就是这么说的。而且说真的，我很高兴能把这些心里话说出来。你呢？你是怎么想的呢？

## `ZagreusHome_1954`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What? No, no, Dusa, of course not, I... ungh, why is it so damn difficult to share one's feelings with somebody else? I don't want to ruin what we have. If only you could see what's in my heart.

**中文**

什么？没有没有，杜莎，当然不是的……啊，向人诉述衷肠怎么就这么难！我不想让我们之间这份美好的感觉付之一炬。你能明白我的心，我就别无他求了。

## `ZagreusHome_1955`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

See you soon.

**中文**

回头见。

## `ZagreusHome_1956`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Of course, Dusa! Where should we chat? The lounge, or maybe somewhere more private...?

**中文**

当然了，杜莎！我们去哪儿谈呢？休息室，还是其他更私密的地方……？

## `ZagreusHome_1957`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm sorry, Dusa, I... put a lot on you, and... maybe I shouldn't have, since I'm the Prince and everything, as you point out, and... sometimes, I forget myself. I'd never pressure you, it's just... I thought you had these feelings for me, too.

**中文**

很抱歉，杜莎，我……给你太大的负担……或许我不该和你说这些，就像你说的那样，我是王子，我的身份摆在那里……但有时候，我会忘记这些。我从来没想过给你压力，只是……我以为你对我也有相同的感觉。

## `ZagreusHome_1958`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's just what...? Whatever it is, it's OK. You can be yourself with me, Dusa, I promise.

**中文**

只是什么……？你尽管说，没有关系。你可以对我敞开心扉，杜莎，真的。

## `ZagreusHome_1959`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, hahaha, look, there's nothing to apologize for, not at all. In fact... all this is a relief. I've learned a lot from you, too, and I cherish our friendship for what it is. Do you... do you think that we could still be friends? Or, is it better that you didn't see me anymore...?

**中文**

噢，哈哈哈，听着，完全没必要为此道歉。实际上，这对我来说是一种解脱。我也从你的身上学到很多，我珍惜我们之间的友谊。你觉得……你觉得我们还可以做朋友吗？或者，你希望我离你远一点……？

## `ZagreusHome_1960`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I respect that so much, and yes, I promise, Dusa. So, well, then... friends? For all eternity?

**中文**

我尊重你的想法，我答应你，杜莎。那么，还是……朋友？永远的朋友？

## `ZagreusHome_1961`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Bye, Dusa.

**中文**

再见，杜莎。

## `ZagreusHome_1962`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dusa, please! Remember friends for all eternity? That's a long time, so, there's no need for second guessing. I'm glad we had a chance to talk.

**中文**

拜托，杜莎！忘了我们是永远的朋友了吗？时间还长着呢，所以不要胡思乱想。不过我还是很高兴有机会和你聊天。

## `ZagreusHome_1963`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I... look, if you don't want the Ambrosia anymore, and receiving it made you feel uncomfortable in any way, I'll take it back. It was my gift to you, to do with as you please. Are you sure you don't want it?

**中文**

我……听着，如果你不想要这些仙馔密酒，而且收下它们让你觉得内心不安，那我可以收回。这是我送给你的礼物，你可以随便处理。你确定不想要了吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1964`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, now, there's no need to do anything rash like that! That Ambrosia was my gift to you, and it was unconditional. Just try the stuff and see! They say the purer the act of giving, the better it is. Won't you keep it? If not for yourself, then for the lounge?

**中文**

嘿，现在没必要这么草率行事！这些仙馔密酒是我无条件送给你的礼物。你尝一口试试看！据说，赠予的动机越纯洁，它们的味道越醇美。你不留着吗？就算自己不喝，放在休息室里也可以？

## `ZagreusHome_1965`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Um, I... thanks...

**中文**

嗯，我……谢谢……

## `ZagreusHome_1966`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Haha, OK.

**中文**

哈哈，好。

## `ZagreusHome_1967`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey Dusa, something I was wondering, and it's just curiosity, mind you, but... is there somebody else...? I mean, somebody special, whom you're close with, or would like to be. Romantically, I mean. If it's all right to ask.

**中文**

嗨……杜莎，我在想，我就是有些好奇，我想问你……还有没有什么人……？就是，比较特别的人，和你很亲近的，或者想亲近的人。充满浪漫气息那种。这么问你不会介意吧。

## `ZagreusHome_1968`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I definitely know. There's seven kinds of love, or so, isn't that right? It isn't something that's taught extensively down here, though, so... there's still a lot for me to learn, I guess.

**中文**

这个我当然知道。世界上有七种爱，是这样吧？在这种地方可没人会教大家爱是什么。看来我还有很多东西要学习啊。

## `ZagreusHome_1969`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I love you too, Dusa!

**中文**

我也爱你，杜莎！

## `ZagreusHome_1970`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Um, bye! Guess everybody knows.

**中文**

嗯，再见！大概每个人都知道了。

## `ZagreusHome_1971`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Ms. Dusa, I ran into hostile gorgons that resembled you out there in Asphodel. Resembled only in the vaguest sense, I mean, they lacked your charm or social graces, anyway. I just, hope you are not related, since I sort of... you know.

**中文**

呃，杜莎小姐，我在水仙花平原遇到过和你长得很像的蛇发女妖，但她们充满敌意。我是说，有点像，她们没有你的魅力或者说完全不懂社交礼仪。我只是希望，你们之间没有关系，因为……你也知道。

## `ZagreusHome_1972`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So I have your permission to continue hacking down such gorgons with impunity?

**中文**

所以，你同意我可以继续消灭这些蛇发女妖，而不会责怪我吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1973`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I had another question about your relationship to other gorgons, Dusa. So... I notice you're not much for hissing, snakelike noises when you speak, and so on? Is that some sort of special gorgon language or something?

**中文**

杜莎，关于你和其他蛇发女妖的关系，我还有一个问题。就是……我注意到你在说话的时候很少会发出哧哧的声音，像蛇那样的？那是蛇发女妖之间相互交流的一种方式吗，还是什么？

## `ZagreusHome_1974`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, wait, me? I never gave it any thought. I... guess it's from my father. He insisted that he teach me how to speak, himself. Maybe it's in the blood.

**中文**

噢，等一下，你说我吗？我从来没想过这个问题。我猜……应该是因为我父亲的原因吧。他坚持要亲自教我说话。又或者是血液里遗传的？

## `ZagreusHome_1975`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Dusa, this is kind of personal, and you don't have to answer, but I have to know. So... did you... use to have a body, and all that? Because, I've heard of gorgons, well... that you had wings, and such?

**中文**

嘿，杜莎，这个问题有点私密，你可以选择不回答，但我必须弄清楚。就是……你之前……也有身体吗，还有其他的？因为，我听说，蛇发女妖，怎么说好呢……你以前还长着翅膀，之类的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1976`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Scoff Well that was inconsiderate of me.

**中文**

嘲笑 刚刚我真是太轻率了。

## `ZagreusHome_1977`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Ms. Dusa, I, erm, wanted to apologize for when I pried about your past. I just wanted to get to know you better, but... I was insensitive, and I'm sorry.

**中文**

杜莎小姐，我，呃，想向你道歉，我不该打听你的过去。我只是想更了解你。但我太迟钝了，对不起。请你原谅我。

## `ZagreusHome_1978`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

And I'm glad, too.

**中文**

我也很高兴。

## `ZagreusHome_1979`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, it must be pretty handy, being able to turn somebody into stone. Is that something you can just do whenever you please, like... I don't know, sticking out your tongue or something?

**中文**

能动不动就把其他人变成石头，肯定很有趣吧。你随时都可以这么做吗，就像……怎么形容呢，就像把你的舌头伸出来那样轻而易举？

## `ZagreusHome_1980`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Probably for the best that we cannot.

**中文**

或许“不能”是最好的结果。

## `ZagreusHome_1981`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, hah, yes! I made it pretty far! On that account you heard correctly, Dusa, but... turns out I have unfinished business here, so... I should be turning up like this from time to time.

**中文**

噢，哈哈，没错！我确实走了很远，这点你没听错，杜莎。不过……我在这里还有点其他事要忙，所以……我时不时都会来这里待一阵子。

## `ZagreusHome_1982`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It is! It's both OK and great.

**中文**

是的！都很不错，都很好。

## `ZagreusHome_1983`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Have to tell you, Dusa, your job seems really stressful, here. It's a big House! You have to tend to the entire thing?

**中文**

杜莎，不得不说，你的工作看起来是在太辛苦了。偌大的圣殿！你必须方方面面照看到位！

## `ZagreusHome_1984`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm not sure it was ever normal around here, but... I'm sorry I created work for you. Cerberus tore the place apart because of me. Though, maybe I can help you patch things up?

**中文**

我不确定这里也曾正常过吗？不过……很抱歉给你添了麻烦。刻耳柏洛斯因为我才变得那么狂躁。或许，我能帮上什么忙？

## `ZagreusHome_1985`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

And, she's off.

**中文**

她走了。

## `ZagreusHome_1986`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dusa, wherever did you get that Fidi Companion you gave me? You know how much that thing is worth? To me at least, I don't know who collects those anymore, but I sure do!

**中文**

杜莎，你给我的菲迪伴偶是从哪里弄来的？你知道那东西有多高价值吗？至少对我来说是这样，我不知道还有没有人收集这些东西，但我肯定是其中的一个！

## `ZagreusHome_1987`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, wait, you don't mean... no, not from Nyx, herself? Did you, wait, Dusa... you didn't, how to put this, borrow her from Nyx, did you?

**中文**

什么，等等，你不会是说……不，不可能是倪克斯，她本人？你是，等一下，杜莎……你不会，该怎么说合适呢，向倪克斯借的吧？

## `ZagreusHome_1988`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's nice of her. Thank you again!

**中文**

她真不错，再次感谢！

## `ZagreusHome_1989`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Say, Dusa, in your work around the House, you wouldn't happen to have run into a Skelly character, out in the courtyard, there, have you?

**中文**

说吧，杜莎，你平时在圣殿里工作，不可能会到庭院并且正好碰到骨头吧？

## `ZagreusHome_1990`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think so. I hope so?

**中文**

我觉得是这样，还是说，我希望是这样？

## `ZagreusHome_1991`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

She's got this.

**中文**

她没问题的。

## `ZagreusHome_1992`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, thank you, Dusa, I know you and Miss Meg are close, so, I am grateful that we have your blessings. Though, we're kind of... not out in the open with it, necessarily, so if you please, just... keep it quiet for us, would you?

**中文**

嗯，谢谢你，杜莎，我知道你和墨纪小姐的关系很好，所以，我很高兴能够得到你的祝福。不过，我们俩……还没有公开，所以，在必要的时候，你可以……为我们保守秘密吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1993`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Um, bye!

**中文**

嗯，再见！

## `ZagreusHome_1994`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dusa, there is something you should know about me. Me and Nyx, that is. She's not my real mother...! My real mother's called Persephone. She's out there, somewhere. On the surface. That's why I left. Wait, just a moment, are you laughing?

**中文**

杜莎，有些和我有关的事情，我觉得你应该知道。我和倪克斯。其实，她不是我的亲生母亲……！我的亲生母亲是珀尔塞福涅。她现在下落不明，但肯定在阳间。这是我要离开的原因。等一下，你是在笑吗？

## `ZagreusHome_1995`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hah. I hope so, too.

**中文**

哈，我也希望如此。

## `ZagreusHome_1996`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I trust you not to mention this to everybody in the House, but... I have. Found her, that is! I'm... trying to figure out what happens next. Can't really talk about it yet, so... bear with me?

**中文**

我相信你不会对这里的其他人提起这件事，但……我做到了。也就是说我已经找到她了！我……正在弄明白下一步该干什么。我暂时不能透露太多，但……你不介意吧？

## `ZagreusHome_1997`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Dusa, I just can't, right now.

**中文**

杜莎，现在真的不行。

## `ZagreusHome_1998`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I hope it's not been too much, having to care for Cerberus, is it, Dusa? He isn't quite as fearsome as he seems. At least, that goes for two heads out of three!

**中文**

我希望照顾刻耳柏洛斯没有给你带来太大困扰，杜莎。他其实没有看起来那么可怕。至少，三个头和两个头差不了多少。

## `ZagreusHome_1999`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...Cleaning up after him...?

**中文**

……清理它的……？

## `ZagreusHome_2000`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fates be merciful...

**中文**

命运啊，求你仁慈些吧……

## `ZagreusHome_2001`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How's your relationship with Nyx been lately, Dusa? She strikes me as a pretty intense supervisor. You getting on OK?

**中文**

你和倪克斯的关系最近怎么样了，杜莎？她给我的感觉像是一位非常严厉的上司。你们还处得来吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2002`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, no, no no, it's not like that, it's just... a feeling that I've had from glimpses of the interactions that I've seen between you two. I haven't always got along with Nyx, but I know she cares deeply about this House and everyone in it. Including you.

**中文**

嘿，没有没有，不是这样子的，只是……上次无意中看到你们俩之间的互动，给我一种感觉。我也不是总能和倪克斯合得来，但我知道她很在乎这座圣殿和里面的每个人。包括你在内。

## `ZagreusHome_2003`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I may not use those exact words...

**中文**

我不应该说得那么确定……

## `ZagreusHome_2004`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Um, sure!

**中文**

嗯，行！

## `ZagreusHome_2005`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dusa, hey, I wanted to congratulate you! Saw you were chosen as the featured servant of the House, getting your portrait there, right in the lounge for all to see! That's quite an honor!

**中文**

嗨杜莎，我要祝贺你啊！我看到你被选为圣殿里最受欢迎的仆从了，你的画像挂在休息室供大家瞻仰呢！真是莫大的荣誉啊！

## `ZagreusHome_2006`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You're doing great.

**中文**

你做得很好。

## `ZagreusHome_2007`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Enjoying that new rug, there, Broker?

**中文**

喜欢新地毯吗，老板？

## `ZagreusHome_2008`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My aspect of Stygius grows stronger.

**中文**

斯第格思的形态变强了。

## `ZagreusHome_2009`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My aspect of Varatha grows in power.

**中文**

瓦拉塔的形态变强了。

## `ZagreusHome_2010`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My aspect of Coronacht is stronger now.

**中文**

科罗纳赫特的形态变强了。

## `ZagreusHome_2011`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My aspect of Aegis became stronger.

**中文**

埃癸斯的形态变强了。

## `ZagreusHome_2012`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My aspect of Exagryph feels even stronger.

**中文**

伊格瑞菲的形态变强了。

## `ZagreusHome_2013`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

More decor!

**中文**

更多装饰！

## `ZagreusHome_2014`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should look good right there.

**中文**

放在那里应该不错。

## `ZagreusHome_2015`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A fine addition to the bedchambers.

**中文**

卧室里的新东西还挺不错。

## `ZagreusHome_2016`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just what I always wanted!

**中文**

就是我想要的！

## `ZagreusHome_2017`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How about we put this... there.

**中文**

把这个东西放在……那里吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2018`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hi Dusa.

**中文**

嗨，杜莎。

## `ZagreusHome_2019`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey Dusa.

**中文**

嘿，杜莎。

## `ZagreusHome_2020`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey Dusa!

**中文**

嘿，杜莎！

## `ZagreusHome_2021`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Dusa!

**中文**

杜莎！

## `ZagreusHome_2022`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, you mean a vision from Varatha the Eternal Spear? That's... out of the ordinary, and also a bit concerning. So, what did you see?

**中文**

什么，您是说永恒之矛瓦拉塔？ 这……异乎寻常，而且有点令人担忧。 那么，您看到了什么？

## `ZagreusHome_2023`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I see you through the eyes of the crimson phoenix... I don't understand. What's that supposed to mean?

**中文**

我透过绯红凤凰的眼睛看着你…… 我不明白。 那会是什么意思？

## `ZagreusHome_2024`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I see you through the eyes of the crimson phoenix...

**中文**

我透过绯红凤凰的眼睛看着你……

## `ZagreusHome_2025`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles...! Something awakened in Varatha when I spoke the words. It revealed to me a form I'd never seen. Wielded by the warrior called Guan Yu. Handsomely dressed! And just as you described. That's no Greek name, is it...

**中文**

阿喀琉斯……！当我对着瓦拉塔说出那些话的时候，我感觉有什么东西觉醒了。那种形态是我从来没有见过的。一个名叫关羽的勇士挥舞着它。穿着华丽的衣服！就像你说的那样。这不像希腊人的名字，难道是……

## `ZagreusHome_2026`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fear is for the weak.

**中文**

恐惧是弱者的专利。

## `ZagreusHome_2027`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Varatha: I see you through the eyes of the crimson phoenix!

**中文**

瓦拉塔：我透过绯红凤凰的眼睛看着你！

## `ZagreusHome_2028`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A new aspect... who's Guan Yu?

**中文**

新的姿态……关羽是谁？

## `ZagreusHome_2029`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, gods... Skelly, what did you do...?

**中文**

噢，老天啊……骨头，你都做了什么啊……？

## `ZagreusHome_2030`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So you carelessly spoke secret words imparted by the Fates themselves to an infernal weapon forged at the dawn of creation, then what? Say, did you walk up to Aegis, or just shout over your shoulder?

**中文**

这句密语可是命运之神在创世之初对某把冥府兵器说的，你就这么大大咧咧地地对它说了？然后呢？你是走过去对着埃癸斯说的，还是站在这朝它喊的？

## `ZagreusHome_2031`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I miss my fancy sorcery already.

**中文**

我想念我那些花里胡哨的法术了。

## `ZagreusHome_2032`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's the Twin Fists...

**中文**

是双子之拳……

## `ZagreusHome_2033`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Another weapon...

**中文**

又一把武器……

## `ZagreusHome_2034`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Pact can tell I have Malphon this time.

**中文**

从契约上看，我这次要用玛尔封了。

## `ZagreusHome_2035`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Malphon... I see everything you are.

**中文**

玛尔封……我已经看到了你的一切。

## `ZagreusHome_2036`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Malphon: Awaken!

**中文**

玛尔封：苏醒吧！

## `ZagreusHome_2037`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Malphon, reveal your secrets!

**中文**

玛尔封，揭示你的秘密！

## `ZagreusHome_2038`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Grant me the aspect of Talos!

**中文**

赐给我塔罗斯的姿态！

## `ZagreusHome_2039`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Grant me the aspect of Demeter!

**中文**

赐给我得墨忒尔的姿态！

## `ZagreusHome_2040`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Talos.

**中文**

塔罗斯.

## `ZagreusHome_2041`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Talos...!

**中文**

塔罗斯……！

## `ZagreusHome_2042`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Demeter.

**中文**

得墨忒尔。

## `ZagreusHome_2043`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Demeter...!

**中文**

得墨忒尔……!

## `ZagreusHome_2044`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Malphon.

**中文**

玛尔封。

## `ZagreusHome_2045`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Malphon.

**中文**

玛尔封。

## `ZagreusHome_2046`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My aspect of Malphon grows stronger still.

**中文**

玛尔封的形态变强了。

## `ZagreusHome_2047`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Stygius: I see your kingly pardon from a prison of stone!

**中文**

斯第格思：我看见你被王从石狱中赦免！

## `ZagreusHome_2048`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A future aspect... what sort of name is Arthur?

**中文**

未来的姿态……亚瑟是哪来的名字？

## `ZagreusHome_2049`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Grant me the aspect of Arthur!

**中文**

赐给我亚瑟的姿态！

## `ZagreusHome_2050`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Arthur.

**中文**

亚瑟。

## `ZagreusHome_2051`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Arthur...!

**中文**

亚瑟……！

## `ZagreusHome_2052`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Coronacht: I see you drawn in the arms of the seventh avatar!

**中文**

科罗纳赫特：我看到第七化身的手将你拉开！

## `ZagreusHome_2053`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A hidden aspect... Rama, who are you...?

**中文**

是隐藏的形态……罗摩，你是什么人呢……？

## `ZagreusHome_2054`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Grant me the aspect of Rama!

**中文**

赐给我罗摩的形态！

## `ZagreusHome_2055`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Rama.

**中文**

罗摩。

## `ZagreusHome_2056`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Rama...!

**中文**

罗摩……！

## `ZagreusHome_2057`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

There you go, Fidi!

**中文**

去吧，菲迪！

## `ZagreusHome_2058`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fidi, you're all set!

**中文**

菲迪，你准备好了！

## `ZagreusHome_2059`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Access to the administrative chamber, huh...

**中文**

进入管理大厅，嗯……

## `ZagreusHome_2060`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Father always said if I wanted privileged access again, I'd have to earn it.

**中文**

父亲总说，如果我想再次获得特权，就要自己去争取。

## `ZagreusHome_2061`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I knew you thought I'd never do it under any circumstances, Father. That seemed reason enough. Though, fear not, I've no plans to reapply for a position there.

**中文**

我就知道你肯定以为我一辈子都不会那么做，父亲大人，而那就是我的理由。但是你别担心，我可不会申请什么职位。

## `ZagreusHome_2062`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll be sure to say hello.

**中文**

我一定要打个招呼。

## `ZagreusHome_2063`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I got acquainted with the Twin Fists of Malphon recently, sir. Fills me with a kind of battle-hunger I'd not felt before. To think the empty hand could have as much destructive force as any weapon.

**中文**

老师，我最近结识了双子之拳玛尔封。 它使我充满了以前从未有过的战斗渴望。 没想到空手竟然可能具有与任何武器一样大的破坏力。

## `ZagreusHome_2064`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you.

**中文**

谢谢你。

## `ZagreusHome_2065`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, sir! I've uncovered the hidden aspect for Stygius! Nyx gave me the words this time, and a vision of a noble leader among mortals. An entire armored legion of them! Arthur was his name. Will be his name, I guess.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯先生，我发现了斯第格思的隐藏形态！倪克斯这次向我透露了那些话，还有一位高贵的凡人领袖的幻象。那是一整支壮盛的装甲军团！他的名字叫做亚瑟。

## `ZagreusHome_2066`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Does seem that way.

**中文**

看起来是这么回事。

## `ZagreusHome_2067`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, I've revealed the hidden aspect of the Heart-Seeker. Lady Artemis herself told me the words, and... it seems Coronacht belonged to, maybe will belong to somebody called Rama. Peerless with the bow. Even among gods...

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，我发现了索心弓的秘密，是阿尔忒弥斯女士亲口告诉我的。而且……看起来科罗纳赫特曾属于，或者说以后会属于一个叫罗摩的人。这把弓就算是在众神之中，也是举世无双……

## `ZagreusHome_2068`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I have this feeling that he will.

**中文**

我有种感觉，他会的。

## `ZagreusHome_2069`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Of course, sir, don't mention it. You're really stuck having to guard this corridor most of the time, huh?

**中文**

当然了，先生，不用谢我。您真的大部分时间都不得不守卫这个走廊，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2070`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well, see you around.

**中文**

嗯，回头见。

## `ZagreusHome_2071`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, believe me, I don't. But I would like to be able to go where I please around here, and confounding Father is another benefit.

**中文**

哦，相信我，我真的不想。但是我喜欢能够想去哪就去哪，而让父亲头疼是另一个好处。

## `ZagreusHome_2072`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Appreciated, sir.

**中文**

谢谢您，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_2073`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Could never get the blasted thing to work for me...

**中文**

这该死的破玩意怎么都用不了……

## `ZagreusHome_2074`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll just patch this through to the House Contractor, then.

**中文**

直接把这些打包交给圣殿承包商好了。

## `ZagreusHome_2075`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

OK, it should be dispatched over to the House Contractor now.

**中文**

好了，现在应该可以发给圣殿承包商了。

## `ZagreusHome_2076`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Never thought I'd come back here again...

**中文**

真没想到我会再回来……

## `ZagreusHome_2077`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Greetings everyone! Just visiting...

**中文**

大家好啊！我就是随便看看……

## `ZagreusHome_2078`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How's everybody doing?

**中文**

大家最近怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_2079`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hello, I'll only be a moment!

**中文**

大家好，我就在这待一小会！

## `ZagreusHome_2080`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just checking up on things!

**中文**

就是来随便看看而已！

## `ZagreusHome_2081`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just thought I'd say hello!

**中文**

我就是来打个招呼！

## `ZagreusHome_2082`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh don't mind me!

**中文**

噢，就当我不存在！

## `ZagreusHome_2083`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just me, pretend I'm not even here.

**中文**

是我啦，当我不存在就行了。

## `ZagreusHome_2084`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should check that stack of contracts back there.

**中文**

应该去查查那里的契约。

## `ZagreusHome_2085`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Contract I'm looking for should be back there.

**中文**

我要找的契约应该在那里。

## `ZagreusHome_2086`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should check the admin chamber just in case.

**中文**

应该去管理大厅里找找，以防万一。

## `ZagreusHome_2087`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should have a look in the administrative chamber there.

**中文**

应该去管理大厅里看一看。

## `ZagreusHome_2088`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thinking I ought to check the old administrative chamber now.

**中文**

我应该去老管理大厅里看看。

## `ZagreusHome_2089`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I ought to check the old administrative chamber there.

**中文**

我应该去老管理大厅里找找。

## `ZagreusHome_2090`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nyx's Mirror...

**中文**

倪克斯的圣镜...

## `ZagreusHome_2091`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Mirror of Night...

**中文**

夜之圣镜...

## `ZagreusHome_2092`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mirror of Night...

**中文**

夜之圣镜...

## `ZagreusHome_2093`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hello, Mirror...

**中文**

嘿，圣镜。

## `ZagreusHome_2094`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm growing stronger.

**中文**

我变得更强了。

## `ZagreusHome_2095`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can grow stronger.

**中文**

我能变得更强。

## `ZagreusHome_2096`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can be stronger.

**中文**

我能变得更强。

## `ZagreusHome_2097`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can get stronger.

**中文**

我还能更强。

## `ZagreusHome_2098`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can be more.

**中文**

我可以更强。

## `ZagreusHome_2099`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can do better.

**中文**

我可以做得更好。

## `ZagreusHome_2100`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Night, protect me.

**中文**

黑夜啊，庇佑我吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2101`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I feel different.

**中文**

我感觉有点不一样。

## `ZagreusHome_2102`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Felt that I think.

**中文**

我想我感觉到了。

## `ZagreusHome_2103`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Feeling good.

**中文**

感觉很好。

## `ZagreusHome_2104`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ready I think.

**中文**

我想我准备好了。

## `ZagreusHome_2105`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

OK.

**中文**

好了。

## `ZagreusHome_2106`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The power of Darkness.

**中文**

黑暗的力量。

## `ZagreusHome_2107`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Whew, felt that.

**中文**

呼，感觉到了。

## `ZagreusHome_2108`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Think that should do it.

**中文**

这应该就可以了。

## `ZagreusHome_2109`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The power of Night.

**中文**

黑夜的力量。

## `ZagreusHome_2110`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nyx's power....

**中文**

倪克斯的力量……

## `ZagreusHome_2111`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nyx's strength...

**中文**

倪克斯之力……

## `ZagreusHome_2112`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

See you, Mirror...

**中文**

再见，圣镜...

## `ZagreusHome_2113`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Could maybe use some rest...

**中文**

或许我该休息一下……

## `ZagreusHome_2114`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I want to lie down...

**中文**

我想躺一会儿……

## `ZagreusHome_2115`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ungh, I'm late for work... Father's going to kill me.

**中文**

呃啊，我要迟到了……父亲准得弄死我。

## `ZagreusHome_2116`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Going as fast as I can, you hurry up!

**中文**

有多快就多快，搞快点！

## `ZagreusHome_2117`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You better not give me away, old man.

**中文**

你最好别把我晾在这儿，老头。

## `ZagreusHome_2118`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sorry...!

**中文**

对不起……！

## `ZagreusHome_2119`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh, gods....

**中文**

噢，我的天啊……

## `ZagreusHome_2120`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Damn it...

**中文**

可恶……

## `ZagreusHome_2121`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ungh...

**中文**

呃啊……

## `ZagreusHome_2122`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh come on.

**中文**

哦，拜托。

## `ZagreusHome_2123`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Doesn't make any sense.

**中文**

简直是太荒谬了。

## `ZagreusHome_2124`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What...

**中文**

什么……

## `ZagreusHome_2125`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I... ungh.

**中文**

我……呃嗯。

## `ZagreusHome_2126`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll do it later.

**中文**

以后再说吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2127`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

None whatsoever, yes.

**中文**

好啊，完全没问题。

## `ZagreusHome_2128`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I haven't forgotten, no one told me!

**中文**

我没忘，是没人告诉过我！

## `ZagreusHome_2129`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh it's faded...

**中文**

哦，它黯淡下去了……

## `ZagreusHome_2130`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Correct...

**中文**

没错……

## `ZagreusHome_2131`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Can't understand any of this.

**中文**

这一切我都不明白是怎么回事。

## `ZagreusHome_2132`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

OK, I'm done, so... can I go yet, Father?

**中文**

好了，我做完了，那么……我能走了吗，父亲？

## `ZagreusHome_2133`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I... I'm sorry, Father, I just overslept. I must have overslept. How am I even supposed to know exactly when to be here, I mean, every moment every day and night, here, it's all the same!

**中文**

对……对不起，父亲，我只是睡过头了。我肯定是睡过头了。我甚至不知道什么时候该来这儿，我的意思是，这里的白天和晚上，每时每刻都没有任何差别！

## `ZagreusHome_2134`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Right.

**中文**

……好吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2135`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Too lenient? Instead of lambasting me nonstop, have you considered, maybe, training me some more to do this fascinating work to your satisfaction? You'll forgive me if I didn't memorize every laborious detail the last time you performed your monologue about how all this works.

**中文**

仁慈？！你有没有想过，与其不断地责骂我，你不如多训练一下我做你这些“令人着迷”工作直到你满意？你应该原谅我我没记住所有繁琐的细节，因为你上次在说这些怎么操作的时候根本是在单口相声。

## `ZagreusHome_2136`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Then why don't you?! I don't want any of this. I'm not the one who drew the short lot with your brothers and got stuck here forever!! Why am I having to do this senseless parchment-pushing for you?

**中文**

那你还犹豫什么？！这些都不是我想要的。我不像某人，和兄弟几个抽签输了，然后被永远困在这里！！我为什么必须要为你做这些无意义的文书工作？

## `ZagreusHome_2137`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Ugh... at least I can't be late for that job anymore.

**中文**

……呃……起码我再也不会迟到了。

## `ZagreusHome_2138`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Say, Skelly? Don't suppose you'd care to tell me how the infamous Twin Fists of Malphon came to be right here in this very courtyard, would you?

**中文**

骨头，如果我问你，这恶名昭彰的玛尔封双子之拳是怎么来到这里的，你会告诉我吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2139`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right, maybe I will.

**中文**

好吧，我或许会吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2140`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

She has, Nyx. I am grateful that you put us into contact, and explained to her the situation that I'm in. I know you took a considerable risk in reaching out.

**中文**

她遵守了，倪克斯。非常感谢您帮我与她联络，并向她解释了我所处的状况。我知道您这么做冒了很大的风险。

## `ZagreusHome_2141`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I understand.

**中文**

我理解。

## `ZagreusHome_2142`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I heard lately from the goddess Athena, Nyx. She seems to admire you. I thought Olympians didn't like our kind?

**中文**

倪克斯，我最近与女神雅典娜有些来往。 她似乎很敬佩您。 我以为奥林匹斯众神不喜欢我们一类？

## `ZagreusHome_2143`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You make a good team.

**中文**

合作愉快。

## `ZagreusHome_2144`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

The Olympians can't really see me, can they, Nyx? I mean, not very well. I get that they can sense details here and there, but... they don't know the full extent of what's going on. Are we really so far beyond their influence?

**中文**

倪克斯，奥林匹斯众神是不是无法看见我？我是说，至少看不清楚。我知道，他们能感知发生在各处的一些小事，但……他们并不能完整得知发生了什么。我们真的离他们那么远吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2145`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I won't.

**中文**

我不会的。

## `ZagreusHome_2146`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Somehow, I always knew. You know... that you were not my birthmother. I mean, look at Hypnos and Thanatos. There's no way that I could have been related to them, everybody knew, and yet... when I finally learned for certain, it was still a shock.

**中文**

不知为何，我一直都知道这件事情。我是说……你不是我的生母。我的意思是，看看修普诺斯和塔纳托斯，我不可能和他们有血缘关系。这些事情大家早就知道了，可是……当我最后确定这一点的时候，我还是很震惊。

## `ZagreusHome_2147`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No, Nyx, please, it's... quite all right. You may not have given birth to me, but you still cared for me. I never doubt that. Likewise, are you sure it's all right that I continue to address you by name?

**中文**

不，倪克斯，请别这样，现在挺好的。你不是我的生母，但你依然关心我。我从不怀疑这一点。与之相对的，你真的觉得我可以继续直呼你的名字吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2148`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, can you... tell me more about my birthmother? Anything at all. What was she like, when you first met? What did you think of her?

**中文**

倪克斯，你能不能……多给我讲讲我亲生母亲的事？什么事情都行。她当时是什么样子的，你们第一次遇见对方是什么时候？当时你觉得她怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_2149`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Finer qualities? Such as abandoning her family members? Why did she leave? Why won't you tell me?

**中文**

优点？比如说抛弃家人？她当时为什么离开？你们为什么瞒着我？

## `ZagreusHome_2150`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I... I trust you Nyx. I do.

**中文**

我……我信任倪克斯。我说真的。

## `ZagreusHome_2151`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, if I can ever make my way from here, out to the surface... how do you know that I can find her? The instructions you gave me... how can you know for sure that's where she is, that she hasn't left, or...?

**中文**

倪克斯，就算我能一路杀到地面……你怎么知道我能找到她？你给我的提示……你怎么知道她就在那里，她一直没有离开吗，还是说……？

## `ZagreusHome_2152`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll find her, then. I have to.

**中文**

我会找到她的，我必须找到她。

## `ZagreusHome_2153`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I finally made it to the surface, Nyx! Confronted Father, there, out in the cold. I couldn't make it past him, but... I was so close!

**中文**

我终于来到阳间了，倪克斯！在冥界与父亲对抗，饱受冷落。他会这么做，我一点也不惊讶，但是……我就差一点点！

## `ZagreusHome_2154`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Effort and time...

**中文**

时间和精力……

## `ZagreusHome_2155`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, all you've done to help me, with the Olympians, the Mirror, the House Contractor, I... I worry that if Father were to know, that he would turn his wrath upon you. I don't want anything to happen.

**中文**

倪克斯，迄今为止你提供给我的帮助，像是和奥林匹亚神的联系、圣镜、圣殿的承包商……我担心如果父亲知道了这些，他会迁怒于你。我不愿这样的事情发生。

## `ZagreusHome_2156`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Then... if there's really nothing he can do, why go along with his deception? About my birthmother, I mean?

**中文**

那……如果他对你毫无办法，你为什么要和他合谋隐瞒我？我的意思是，隐瞒我的生母一事？

## `ZagreusHome_2157`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Rng. All right.

**中文**

嗯，好吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2158`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Obligated to cooperate... no wonder I thought you two truly were husband and wife.

**中文**

有合作的义务……难怪我把你俩当成一对夫妻了呢。

## `ZagreusHome_2160`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Respectful. Definitely.

**中文**

我很尊敬，真的。

## `ZagreusHome_2161`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have to ask you something, Nyx. What is this debt you owe my father, anyway? Forgive my eavesdropping, it's just, I heard the two of you discussing things, and... it came up.

**中文**

倪克斯，我有一些疑问。你究竟欠了父亲什么？非常抱歉我听到了你们在讨论……只是……

## `ZagreusHome_2162`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It was too much, even for you. So... when father arrived... you welcomed him? He helped?

**中文**

这对你来说实在是太沉重了……所以，当父亲来到的时候……你迎接了他？然后他帮了你？

## `ZagreusHome_2163`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

He has never taken advantage of you? Of your generosity?

**中文**

他没因你的慷慨得寸进尺？

## `ZagreusHome_2164`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm sure we can reconcile our disagreements all the same.

**中文**

我相信，我们还是可以调解一下矛盾的。

## `ZagreusHome_2165`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll make them work for it, at least.

**中文**

起码我会让他们为之努力。

## `ZagreusHome_2166`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, Father can't control the pest problem? Never thought I'd be of any help rampaging through the upper levels of his domain.

**中文**

什么，连父亲也没办法解决这些害虫问题？在他的领地的上层横冲直撞，我从来没想过还能帮上什么忙。

## `ZagreusHome_2167`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yes, I think so, Nyx. Returning here by Father's hand does put a special sting into it, though. So, any invaluable tips on how to vanquish him? Some hidden weaknesses only you know about, something like that?

**中文**

是的，我还好，倪克斯。虽然借父亲之手回来特别伤人。话说，你有宝贵的建议如何打败他吗？比如他身上有什么只有你知道的弱点，诸如此类的？

## `ZagreusHome_2168`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I would. I will.

**中文**

我会的。

## `ZagreusHome_2169`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, I've come to know the god of swiftness, Hermes, though... it seems to me he doesn't work directly with the others on Olympus. Do you know him well?

**中文**

倪克斯，我认识了迅捷之神赫尔墨斯，不过……我看他和奥林匹斯山上的其他诸神没多少来往。你和他熟吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2170`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I get it. Thank you, Nyx.

**中文**

我明白了，谢谢你，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2171`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx... I encountered Demeter. She mentioned her daughter... Persephone. You never said she was my grandmother. Didn't you think... under the circumstances... that was something I should know?

**中文**

倪克斯……我碰到了得墨忒尔。她跟我提起她的女儿……珀尔塞福涅。你从来没说过她是我的外婆。你不觉得……在这种情况下……你应该和我说些什么吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2172`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yes. We're understood...

**中文**

是的，我们心照不宣……

## `ZagreusHome_2173`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You offer something called the Fated List of Minor Prophecies to me, chances are I'm going to pick it up. Though, you make it sound like there's more I should know about it. Should I be concerned?

**中文**

你送我那张所谓的命运小预言清单，我就会把它收下。不过你好像还想和我说些什么，有什么是我应该注意的？

## `ZagreusHome_2174`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's... somehow reassuring.

**中文**

这真是……令人安心啊。

## `ZagreusHome_2175`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thank you, Nyx. Though, they're just minor prophecies, right? The kind that won't necessarily come to pass unless spelled out and coupled with a decent reward to make it worth my while. Not even the Fates are above greasing the wheels of progress, then?

**中文**

谢谢你，倪克斯。但它们只是一些小预言，对吧？那些都不一定会实现，除非你把它说出来，然后给我奖励，让我不觉得是在浪费时间。甚至连命运女神也没有在其中推波逐浪？

## `ZagreusHome_2176`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Some more than others, yes.

**中文**

没错，有的时候情况可能会更严重。

## `ZagreusHome_2177`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No, it's just... the latest renovations from the Contractor... Father told me you've been supervising all of that. Look, just between us, and I'll not speak of it again, but... thanks. So much.

**中文**

倪克斯，关于承包商最近的整修工作……父亲和我说，由你负责总体监工。我是说，这是我们两个人之间的秘密，我也不会再次提起这件事，但是……我要谢谢你，真的非常感谢。

## `ZagreusHome_2178`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm glad you like them, Nyx. Though if you don't, just give the word, and I will have them removed. Probably incinerated for good measure! Now that you mention it, though, how come you are always in this corner in particular?

**中文**

很高兴你喜欢它们，倪克斯。如果你不喜欢，只需要一句话，我就把它们拿走。可能一把火烧干净！话说回来，你为什么一直待在这个特定的角落呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2179`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Makes sense! Mostly.

**中文**

有道理！差不多吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2180`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Don't you ever get lonely down here, Nyx...? For companionship, I mean, because... I mean, just look at you, you're very beautiful, the night herself! There's nothing you can't do. When I was younger, I thought you and father, you know, but... is there not someone else?

**中文**

你在这里不曾感到寂寞吗，倪克斯……？我是说如果有人陪伴，因为……看看你自己，这么迷人，作为黑夜女神本尊！没有什么是你做不到的。小时候，我以为你和父亲，你知道我想说什么，但……你们中间没有其他人吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2181`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I see. Forgive my rudeness...

**中文**

我明白了，请原谅我的无礼……

## `ZagreusHome_2182`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh, I don't know, it honestly felt pretty high up there as victories go. The only shame is cutting Father down to size changes so little around here on its own. He's going to pretend like everything is still OK.

**中文**

噢，我不知道，但胜利确实给人一种高处不胜寒的感觉。唯一的遗憾是，挫败了父亲的威风对这里没有多大的改变。他也假装一切还是原来的老样子。

## `ZagreusHome_2183`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not far enough, yet.

**中文**

但还不够远呢。

## `ZagreusHome_2184`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Something about setbacks building character, I guess? I didn't take you for someone to look on the bright side of things, Nyx.

**中文**

我猜是挫折磨砺了人的个性？我以为你不是那种可以从正面看待事情的人呢，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2185`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No problem whatsoever, I'm sure.

**中文**

一点问题都没有，我敢肯定。

## `ZagreusHome_2186`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, you mean the Fates? And Stygius? I'm honored that they would have taken any notice of me. What is it that they want for me to know?

**中文**

等等，你是说命运女神？还有斯第格思？很荣幸她们会注意到我。她们想和我说什么？

## `ZagreusHome_2187`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I see your kingly pardon from a prison of stone... this must be for one of the weapon's hidden forms. Please tell your daughters I am very grateful, Nyx.

**中文**

汝在石之监狱得到赦免……它肯定和武器的隐藏形态有关。请代我感谢你的几个女儿，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2188`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll take my chances it's the former.

**中文**

我猜是前者。

## `ZagreusHome_2189`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, the words you told me... when I spoke them to Stygius, the blade revealed to me the form of a great leader among men, taking counsel with his loyal retinue about a circular table. But if his reign is inevitable, why would the Fates need me?

**中文**

倪克斯，你教我的那句话……当我对斯第格思说出来时，冥界之刃向我显现出万人拥戴的领袖形态，他正坐在圆桌边听取他的忠诚的随扈的建议。如果他的统治是不可避免的，为什么命运女神还需要我呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2190`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well... thank them for me, please.

**中文**

嗯……帮我谢谢他们。

## `ZagreusHome_2191`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't suppose the name Skelly happens to mean anything to you, does it, Nyx?

**中文**

我猜你应该没听说过“骨头”这个名字吧，倪克斯？

## `ZagreusHome_2192`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, I... well, essentially, he is an animated skeleton who shows up just outside my bedchambers, and lets me practice with my weapons on him. I sort of figured that you put him up to it. So it wasn't you?

**中文**

噢，我……实际上，他是突然出现在我的卧室外面的会动的骷髅人，还让我以他为目标练习使用武器。我还以为是你让他来的，看来和你没有关系？

## `ZagreusHome_2193`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think so? I don't know. He seems all right.

**中文**

我觉得？我不知道啊，他看上去还可以。

## `ZagreusHome_2194`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, if, hypothetically, I felt that some injustice had been dealt to someone who deserved a better lot... say, that they were subjected to too harsh a punishment for all eternity... don't I have some of my father's authority to do something about it?

**中文**

倪克斯，假设说，我感觉某些人遭到了不公平对待，他们应该有更美好的命运……例如，他们被判定要承受过于苛刻的永世惩罚……难道我就不能动用我的父亲的哪怕一点点权限来做点什么吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2195`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

But how can I possibly find anything in there? The place is wall-to-wall pacts and parchments. I've no idea where to look.

**中文**

但我又怎么可能在那里找到些什么？那地方堆满了契约和羊皮纸。我根本不知道该如何下手。

## `ZagreusHome_2196`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yes. We are.

**中文**

是的，没错。

## `ZagreusHome_2197`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, you must know something about why Father broke off ties with the Olympians, don't you?

**中文**

倪克斯，你一定知道一些我父亲与奥林匹亚神决裂的原因，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2198`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Had to ask.

**中文**

我得问问。

## `ZagreusHome_2199`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, I was just busy doing some administrative chamber record-keeping recently, and, wouldn't you know, it somehow slipped my mind where I could find the sentencing parchmentwork for the knave-king Sisyphus, forced to toil out in Tartarus? I ran it by Megaera...

**中文**

倪克斯，我最近都在行政办公室忙于做记录，你知道吗，有时候我在想，在哪里可以找到西西弗斯的判决书呢，就是那位被迫在塔耳塔罗斯做苦力的无赖之王？然后我跟墨纪拉说起这件事……

## `ZagreusHome_2200`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sigma-phi-alpha, lower gamma folder. Got it. Thanks.

**中文**

西格玛——斐——阿尔法区，小写的伽马文件夹。找到了，谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_2201`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I was pleased to see an order pending approval for the release of Orpheus with the House Contractor, Nyx. If Father wants him to start singing again, locking him in Tartarus doesn't seem like the most efficient way to go about it. Besides, I think he's suffered enough.

**中文**

我很高兴听说释放俄耳甫斯和圣殿承包商的命令正等待被批准，倪克斯。如果父亲想要他再次开口唱歌，把他锁在塔耳塔罗斯好像并没用。再者，我觉得他已经受了够多苦。

## `ZagreusHome_2202`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're worried Father doesn't have enough live music to enjoy while brooding over his gloomy court?

**中文**

你在担心父亲在他的阴森的宫殿沉思时没有可欣赏的现场音乐？

## `ZagreusHome_2203`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm just glad to see him back.

**中文**

我很高兴看到他回来。

## `ZagreusHome_2204`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I waived the punishment-sentence for Sisyphus the knave-king, Nyx, except... he chose to stay! He chose to stay, in Tartarus, I think in part because he likes getting to speak with me, from time to time! Also his boulder friend.

**中文**

我撤销了对无赖之王西西弗斯的惩罚，倪克斯，除非……他自己选择留下来！他选择留在塔耳塔罗斯，可能想和我不时地说说话是其中的部分原因！当然还有他的巨石朋友。

## `ZagreusHome_2205`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll look out for him. He's certainly looked out for me.

**中文**

我会留意他的，他肯定也会留意我。

## `ZagreusHome_2206`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Nyx, you ever have a burning urge to find the case files for the circumstances under which our court-musician bound himself to eternal servitude here in this House, and forever lost his opportunity to be with his beloved muse? I was wondering if you had a remedy for that.

**中文**

呃，倪克斯，你是否曾经有迫切地想要找出案件卷宗，看看我们的宫廷乐师出于什么原因要在这座圣殿里永远被奴役，而且和他心爱的缪斯分隔两地？我在想你有没有办法补救。

## `ZagreusHome_2207`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Zeta-pi-omega, upper beta folder. I'll look into it as soon as possible.

**中文**

泽塔——派——欧米伽区，大写的贝塔文件夹。我马上就去找。

## `ZagreusHome_2208`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You don't know how pleased I was to see Orpheus visiting with his muse Eurydice over in Asphodel. Best of all... I don't think he's left for good. He'll just have some time to visit her now and again! Thank you, Nyx, on their behalf, and mine.

**中文**

你没法想象，在看到俄耳甫斯和他的缪斯欧律狄克在水仙花平原相逢时，我有多么高兴。最棒的是……我认为他们不会永远分离。他会时不时地抽空去看欧律狄克！我要代表他们还有我自己谢谢你，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2209`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They were so happy, Nyx.

**中文**

他们当时可开心了，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2210`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I, ah, happened to be looking into our security situation here, just running background checks, that sort of thing, and... I was wondering if you could help me locate an old pact signed by... signed by Achilles, Nyx. It would mean a lot... not just to him, but me.

**中文**

我，呃，碰巧在检查这里的安保情况，背景调查啊之类的，然后……我想问一下，你能不能帮我找一份古老的契约……是阿喀琉斯签署过的契约，倪克斯？这份契约至关重要……不仅仅是对他，对我来说也同样如此。

## `ZagreusHome_2211`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Couldn't be more certain, Nyx.

**中文**

我对此深信不疑，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2212`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I saw Achilles with Patroclus in Elysium, Nyx. They're free to be together, now, thanks to you. If only you could have seen them.

**中文**

我在至福乐土看到阿喀琉斯和普特洛克勒斯在一起，倪克斯。他们现在自由了，在一起了，谢谢你。真希望你也可以去见见他们。

## `ZagreusHome_2213`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father isn't going to be thrilled I've let him out of a binding pact.

**中文**

撕毁一个契约，应该不至于让父亲大发雷霆吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2214`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Don't see Achilles doing that anytime soon...

**中文**

阿喀琉斯一时半会恐怕是不会去做那件事情的……

## `ZagreusHome_2215`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're pleased we're together, Nyx? I thought you'd be concerned that such relationships distract from work responsibilities and such. Although, I'm grateful to have your approval.

**中文**

我们在一起，你很开心吧，倪克斯？我还以为你会担心这种关系会影响工作什么的呢。不过，能得到你的认可我很高兴。

## `ZagreusHome_2216`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I... thanks.

**中文**

我……谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_2217`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're happy Meg and I are friends now, Nyx? I appreciate it, certainly, though... I worry it might get in the way of Meg's ability to do her job.

**中文**

我跟墨纪成为朋友，你很开心吧，倪克斯？当然我也很开心，不过……我担心这样会影响墨纪的工作。

## `ZagreusHome_2218`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It is. You're probably right.

**中文**

是的，也许你说的没错。

## `ZagreusHome_2219`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thank you, Nyx. I think we each have harbored feelings for each other for some time. We've finally been true about them.

**中文**

谢谢你，倪克斯。我觉得我和他一直以来都对彼此有感觉，如今我们终于正视了这份感情。

## `ZagreusHome_2220`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, he's all right. Though, I'll be sure to keep an eye on him.

**中文**

哦，他过得挺好的。不过我会帮你照看他的。

## `ZagreusHome_2221`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's difficult to find a friend down here. Though, I have always had a trusted one in Thanatos, and he in me. I think we have a better understanding of that now.

**中文**

在这种地方能交到朋友真的不容易。不过，我一直以来都很信任塔纳托斯，他对我也一样。我觉得我们现在更加了解彼此了。

## `ZagreusHome_2222`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I will.

**中文**

我会的。

## `ZagreusHome_2223`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

She's my friend, Nyx. Look... I love you very much, you know I do, except... you cannot choose my friends for me! I can't allow that. I know you don't trust her. Don't you trust me?

**中文**

她是我的朋友，倪克斯。听着……我很爱你，你是知道的，但……你不能为我选择该和谁交朋友！我没办法接受。我知道你不相信她。难道你还信不过我吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2224`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

She'll be fine. No one works harder than Dusa.

**中文**

她没问题，没人比杜莎更努力工作。

## `ZagreusHome_2225`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thank you for saying so, Nyx. Though, you mean you were mistaken about me, or about Dusa?

**中文**

感谢你这么说，倪克斯。你是指误会我了，还是误会杜莎了？

## `ZagreusHome_2226`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I will. She'd do the same for me.

**中文**

我会的，换做是她，她也会为我这么做。

## `ZagreusHome_2227`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey Nyx, I would have you know, I'm now proud owner of my very own Chthonic Companion! You really built them to last. Just... not very many of them, apparently. I'd all but given up on ever finding one.

**中文**

嘿，倪克斯，我想告诉你，我现在为能够拥有冥界伴偶而感到自豪！它们是你的杰作，而且很靠得住。但……显然数量太少了。我费尽工夫还想再找到一个。

## `ZagreusHome_2228`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have to say, and I do mean this as a compliment: It's hard for me to imagine Night Incarnate herself fashioning these little keepsakes to be given between friends.

**中文**

我不得不说，而且这是一句赞美：真的很难想象黑夜之神会亲自制作这些在朋友之间送来送去的小信物。

## `ZagreusHome_2229`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

And I just adore them!

**中文**

我爱死他们了！

## `ZagreusHome_2230`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, did you really give Dusa quite possibly the sole surviving epsilon edition Chthonic Companion in the entire Underworld? She's very grateful for it, so much so that she entrusted Fidi to my care. They grow in power that way, right?

**中文**

倪克斯，你真的把据说是整个冥界仅存的艾普西隆级冥界伴偶送给杜莎了吗？她对此感激涕零，甚至把菲迪交给我托管。这样它们的力量就会不断增加，对吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2231`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's very calculating when you put it quite like that...

**中文**

这么看的话，这还真是深谋远虑啊……

## `ZagreusHome_2232`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You have always cared for me and protected me. And given me someone to look up to. This is nothing, Nyx.

**中文**

您一直关心我，并保护我。给了我一位值得仰望的神。我给您的不算什么，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2233`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, if you'll forgive me this transgression for a moment, I would like for you to have this vintage bottle of Ambrosia, please. You can hand it off to Megaera as contraband, but... just in case, I wanted you to have it, first.

**中文**

倪克斯，如果您能暂时原谅我的这种犯罪行为，请容许我献给您这瓶陈年仙馔密酒。您可以将它作为违禁品移交给墨纪拉，但是……这一次，我希望您先拥有它。

## `ZagreusHome_2234`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Though, Father does have questionable taste.

**中文**

虽然，父亲大人的品味确实有些可疑。

## `ZagreusHome_2235`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So glad you liked it, Nyx! And, good timing, too, because... I have for you another bottle, here. They say each one is different in its own respect! And besides, the pleasures of Olympus should be shared. Please, take it, won't you?

**中文**

您喜欢就好，倪克斯！并且，时刻也不错，因为……我这里又有一瓶给您的。据说每一瓶味道都不一样！此外，奥林匹斯山的美味也应该与大家分享。 拜托，收下，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2236`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's quite all right. It never spoils and you have all the time in the world.

**中文**

真没错，上面的内容总是点到为止，时间也都不是问题。

## `ZagreusHome_2237`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, along with giving you this bottle of Ambrosia here, there's something I have meant to say to you. The Three Fates have been very kind to me. Because, in denying me my birthmother... they gave me you. Without your steady presence here, I... can't imagine what it would be like.

**中文**

除了在这里给您这瓶仙馔密酒之外，我还想对您说些话。三位命运女神对我很照顾。因为，让我失去我的生母的同时……她们给我了您。没有您的稳重的母爱在这里，我……无法想象会是什么样。

## `ZagreusHome_2238`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, I mean every word. I love you, Nyx.

**中文**

是么，可我每个词语都是诚意的。 我爱您，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2239`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I... think Chaos wishes to reestablish contact with you, Nyx. The two of you drifted apart from one another, so, I think I can imagine. I lost touch with Thanatos awhile! But that was nothing in comparison to this. Isn't there someway you can reconnect?

**中文**

我猜……卡俄斯是想和你重新取得联系吧，倪克斯。你们两人渐渐疏远了，我可以想象出来。我也曾一度和塔纳托斯互不联络！但和你们比起来根本不算什么。你们没办法再沟通一下吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2240`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Of course, Nyx. Anything. What can I do?

**中文**

当然了，倪克斯。任何事都行。我能做什么？

## `ZagreusHome_2241`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, the Sigil? But I'm not authorized to use it. It's never worked for me, and I have even less authority around here now.

**中文**

等一下，印记？但我没有权限使用它呀。它从来没有对我起过作用，更何况我现在的权限已大不如前。

## `ZagreusHome_2242`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll do everything I can.

**中文**

我会尽我所能。

## `ZagreusHome_2243`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'll do everything I can. Hey maybe you can put in a request with the House Contractor for me? Make it official, right?

**中文**

我会尽量想办法的。对了，或许你可以为我向圣殿承包商提出请求？正式的请求？

## `ZagreusHome_2244`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Please do. Until next time, Nyx...!

**中文**

拜托了。下次见，倪克斯……！

## `ZagreusHome_2245`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It was my pleasure. Take care of each other.

**中文**

不客气，你们互相照顾好对方吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2246`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, you're back! So, how did things with Chaos go? Please tell me it went well?

**中文**

倪克斯，你回来了！卡俄斯怎么样了，一切都还好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2247`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So what happens now? You're back on speaking terms, then... does it mean Chaos will stop by for a visit soon, something like that?

**中文**

那现在怎么样了？话说你也回来了，这是不是意味着……卡俄斯很快就会过来，或是怎样？

## `ZagreusHome_2248`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I see. Well take care of each other, please.

**中文**

我明白了，那么，你们务必互相照顾好对方。

## `ZagreusHome_2249`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, it's just.... Please don't take this the wrong way, Nyx, but I was worried you were being rather harsh to Dusa. You asked her not to speak to me, at all?

**中文**

啊，那只是……请您别误解，倪克斯，我只是担忧您对杜莎太苛刻了。您甚至让她别和我说话，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2250`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Pff! If I'm so important, shouldn't I be the one to choose whom I get to interact with around here?

**中文**

切！如果我真的这么重要，连要和谁做朋友都没办法自己做主吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2251`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Whatever you say...

**中文**

随便你怎么说吧……

## `ZagreusHome_2252`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What happened with Dusa, Nyx? You really fired her because she talked to me? She was my friend!

**中文**

杜莎出了什么事，倪克斯？你真的把她开除了，就因为她和我说话？她是我的朋友！

## `ZagreusHome_2253`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Insubordination, and repeated lack of what? She worked harder than anyone here to keep the House neat and orderly! I'd thought you would have valued that!

**中文**

不服从上级，三番五次还有缺乏什么？为了保持圣殿整洁有序，她比任何人都拼命地工作！我以为你会看到这些！

## `ZagreusHome_2254`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Efficiency... you know who you sound like right now, don't you, Nyx? I can't believe this.

**中文**

效率……你现在的口吻像极了一个人，你知道吗，倪克斯？我简直无法相信。

## `ZagreusHome_2255`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I... rngh.

**中文**

我……呃啊。

## `ZagreusHome_2256`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx... I'm sorry that I raised my voice with you before, it's just... I cared about Dusa, and I know just how much she cared about working here, with you, and all of us... isn't there some way you would reconsider letting her go?

**中文**

倪克斯……我很抱歉之前对着你大呼小叫，只是……我很在乎杜莎，我知道她多想留在这里，和你，和我们在一起……你就不能考虑一下别赶她走吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2257`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, you're saying you fired Dusa for working too hard? Did you give her any warning?

**中文**

等一下，你说你是因为她工作太努力而开除她的？你之前有警告过她吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2258`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Please give her another chance, Nyx. She can learn, she just needs to know what she did wrong. She gave this work everything. She just pushed too hard. Shouldn't something like that be forgivable?

**中文**

请再给她一次机会吧，倪克斯。她愿意学习，她只是需要知道自己做错了什么。为了这份工作，她付出了全部。或许是操之过急。这样的她难道不应该被原谅吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2259`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Scoff Yes...

**中文**

嘲笑 是的……

## `ZagreusHome_2260`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hooray...!

**中文**

太棒了……！

## `ZagreusHome_2261`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well deserved.

**中文**

当之无愧。

## `ZagreusHome_2262`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't often see you interact with Hypnos, Nyx, so I just wanted to make sure... is everything all right?

**中文**

我很少看到你和修普诺斯联系，倪克斯，所以想要问问……你们之间还好吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2263`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

For the time being? How long are we talking? Can't remember the last time I saw you speaking with him, now that you mention it.

**中文**

暂时？那是多久呢？既然你提起来，我都不记得上次你们说话是什么时候了。

## `ZagreusHome_2264`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well, keep it up, Hypnos!

**中文**

很好，继续保持，修普诺斯！

## `ZagreusHome_2265`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Athena sends you her kindest regards, Nyx. Sounds like you have maintained some correspondence since all this began?

**中文**

雅典娜向您致以最诚挚的问候，倪克斯。听起来自从这一切开始之后，你们一直保持着一定程度的往来，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2266`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

But... you told her you're my mother. If she's supposedly so wise... isn't that quite a risk? If she finds out you've been misleading her...

**中文**

但是……您告诉她您是我的母亲。如果她这么聪明……那不是很大的风险吗？如果她发现您在误导她……

## `ZagreusHome_2267`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ah. I see. I guess. I think?

**中文**

啊，我知道了。我猜，我大概知道了？

## `ZagreusHome_2268`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, have you seen Charon lately? Everything all right with him? I can never tell how he's doing.

**中文**

倪克斯，你最近见过卡戎吗？他还好不？我都不知道他现在怎么样。

## `ZagreusHome_2269`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I suppose you are the one to thank for his assistance, when it comes to selling me his wares and such.

**中文**

他之所以会帮助我，我想我应该感谢你，比如他会把自己的商品卖给我。

## `ZagreusHome_2270`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Doesn't answer to me either.

**中文**

也没回答我。

## `ZagreusHome_2271`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Nyx, at first I thought I maybe should ignore this, but I wanted to give you greetings from Lord Ares, who seems rather interested in you.

**中文**

倪克斯，起初我以为我应该忽略这个，但是阿瑞斯阁下向您致以问候，他似乎对您很感兴趣。

## `ZagreusHome_2272`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're not... wait, you're not interested in him, I mean, he's... murderous... polite, though, I don't know.

**中文**

您不是……等等，您不是对他感兴趣……我是说，他是……嗜血……但很礼貌？我不肯定。

## `ZagreusHome_2273`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Anytime...

**中文**

……随时可以……

## `ZagreusHome_2274`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Nyx, erm, Lord Ares wanted you to know, and I believe I quote, that 'I am often thinking of her here'. Take that for what it's worth?

**中文**

啊，倪克斯，嗯，阿瑞斯大人想让您知道一些事情，容我引述：“我在这里经常想起她” 。大概这样吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2275`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I like the positive attitude.

**中文**

我喜欢这种乐观的态度。

## `ZagreusHome_2276`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Help me, Nyx. Or rather, help yourself. Lord Ares seeks for me to put the two of you in contact. I mean... I don't know why I'm getting so defensive, here, now that I think on it.

**中文**

请帮帮我, 倪克斯。 或者，确切的说，帮帮您自己。战神阿瑞斯寻求我帮您们两个联系。我的意思是……我不知道为什么我现在对此事如此抗拒。

## `ZagreusHome_2277`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I hear from them rather a lot, myself....

**中文**

我是常常收到他们的来信，我自己…

## `ZagreusHome_2278`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, did you really start a correspondence with Lord Ares, as he put it to me recently? Is everything all right with you and him?

**中文**

倪克斯，你真的开始与战神阿瑞斯有“来往”吗，就像他最近告诉我的那样？你和他之间没发生什么吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2279`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What of Father? He's forbidden any contact whatsoever!

**中文**

父亲大人怎么办？他禁止任何联系！

## `ZagreusHome_2280`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fair point.

**中文**

有道理。

## `ZagreusHome_2281`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're going to meet Ares, sometime, Nyx? Or maybe you already have?

**中文**

你会偶尔去见阿瑞斯吗，倪克斯？或者你已经这么做了？

## `ZagreusHome_2282`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, I... it's just, Ares, he's... I don't know, he just doesn't seem your type! And isn't he a little young for you?

**中文**

呃，我……只是，阿瑞斯，他……我不知道该怎么说，他应该不是你的菜！对你来说，他不会太年轻了吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2283`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I know. I know. You're right.

**中文**

我知道，我知道，你说得对。

## `ZagreusHome_2284`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Nyx, I... Ares sends you his regards again. Wanted to let you know.

**中文**

呃，倪克斯，我……替阿瑞斯向您问好，他想向您打个招呼。

## `ZagreusHome_2285`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's complicated over there I guess.

**中文**

我猜那里的情况确实很复杂。

## `ZagreusHome_2286`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dusa, hey, hey what's the matter, you seem... something's wrong, what happened? Tell me, please.

**中文**

杜莎，喂，怎么回事，你好像……怪怪的，有什么问题吗？还是告诉我吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2287`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, Dusa, slow down, what is the matter? Nyx really has forbidden you from even speaking with me? Do you know on what grounds?

**中文**

慢点，杜莎，慢点说，怎么回事？倪克斯真的让你连话都不准跟我说吗？到底怎么回事？

## `ZagreusHome_2288`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I need to talk to Nyx about all this.

**中文**

我得和倪克斯谈谈这些。

## `ZagreusHome_2289`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, wait, wait, what exactly happened, your last day or night, what is all this? You're really leaving? Why?

**中文**

等下……等下……等下，到底怎么回事，什么最后一天一晚的，你到底在说什么？你真要走了吗？为什么？

## `ZagreusHome_2290`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Wait! Nyx. Damn it.

**中文**

等下！倪克斯……可恶。

## `ZagreusHome_2291`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Imagine my relief. I'm glad that Nyx has come around. I... understand she was concerned, but... I hope she never looks past all your good qualities like that again.

**中文**

总算松了一口气。真高兴倪克斯能改变主意。我明白她是有点担心，不过……我希望她不要再忽视你的所有优点了。

## `ZagreusHome_2292`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Look, I'm just glad you're back.

**中文**

听着，很高兴你回来了。

## `ZagreusHome_2293`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So, what even happened when you lost your job, Dusa? Did you have someplace else to stay? Was everything all right?

**中文**

那么，丢掉工作之后发生了什么，杜莎？你住在哪里？没发生什么不好的事吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2294`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm surprised you wanted to come back to work after something like that. Wouldn't you be totally fed up? Burned out on the whole thing?

**中文**

发生了这样的事情，之后你还愿意回来工作也挺让我吃惊的。你不觉得烦吗？这些事情没有把你搞得筋疲力尽吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2295`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

But if you go too far beyond your limits, things can get bad. Believe me. I mean, you almost lost the job you care so much about.

**中文**

你太勉强自己也不好。相信我。你看，你差点丢掉自己心爱的工作。

## `ZagreusHome_2296`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sounds good. I will.

**中文**

听上去不错，我会的。

## `ZagreusHome_2297`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How's work lately, Dusa? You seem to have a handle on your new responsibilities, or so I hear from Nyx.

**中文**

最近工作怎么样，杜莎？你似乎对自己的新工作很有信心啊，起码倪克斯是这样说的。

## `ZagreusHome_2298`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Great to hear it, hope you keep it up!

**中文**

很高兴听你这么说，希望你能保持下去！

## `ZagreusHome_2299`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Mirror of Night...

**中文**

夜之圣镜...

## `ZagreusHome_2300`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nyx updated my mirror...

**中文**

倪克斯优化了圣镜...

## `ZagreusHome_2301`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

There's Nyx's work order...

**中文**

这是倪克斯的工作单……

## `ZagreusHome_2302`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Eldest Sigil. Nyx needs it upgraded, huh.

**中文**

远古印记，倪克斯要翻修一下这个。

## `ZagreusHome_2303`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Eldest Sigil should be fully powered up for Nyx.

**中文**

远古印记应该已经充满了能量，可以供倪克斯使用了。

## `ZagreusHome_2304`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

House Contractor, the Eldest Sigil is an ancient relic, isn't it? Surely it could use an update.

**中文**

圣殿承包商，远古印记是一件古老的遗物，对吧？那这东西肯定可以翻新一下。

## `ZagreusHome_2305`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'd like my Darkness back.

**中文**

要是能把黑暗宝石拿回来就好了。

## `ZagreusHome_2306`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How about a new beginning.

**中文**

不如重新开始吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2307`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's try this Mirror stuff again.

**中文**

再来试试看这圣镜吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2308`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Return my Darkness to me.

**中文**

把我的黑暗宝石归还于我。

## `ZagreusHome_2309`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Show me something new.

**中文**

给我看看新东西。

## `ZagreusHome_2310`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How else can I improve?

**中文**

哪里还能再提升一下？

## `ZagreusHome_2311`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What more potential do I have?

**中文**

我还有什么别的潜力？

## `ZagreusHome_2312`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's see the last of them.

**中文**

看看最后的吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2313`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Sisyphus contract. I can buy it out...

**中文**

是西西弗斯的契约，我可以把它买下来……

## `ZagreusHome_2314`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should check back later for the Sisyphus contract.

**中文**

下次再来看看西西弗斯的契约吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2315`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Orpheus' contract. I can release him...

**中文**

是俄耳甫斯的契约，我可以把他解放出来了……

## `ZagreusHome_2316`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It'll take some time for Orpheus' contract to show up.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯的契约还得过一阵子才会出来。

## `ZagreusHome_2317`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

In the name of Hades, I hereby release Sisyphus from the terms of his pact!

**中文**

以哈迪斯之名，我特此将西西弗斯从他的契约中解放出来！

## `ZagreusHome_2318`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

In the name of Hades, I hereby release Orpheus from the terms of his pact!

**中文**

以哈迪斯之名，我特此将俄耳甫斯从他的契约中解放出来！

## `ZagreusHome_2319`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

And I, in turn, am indebted to you, Orpheus, for your calm demeanor and ability to accept life's greatest challenges. You inspire me, and I wish you the best.

**中文**

这就要谢谢你了，俄耳甫斯，你从容自若地接受了人生中最大的挑战。你激励了我。我祝你一切如愿！

## `ZagreusHome_2320`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Orpheus, mate! I have something good for you right here... Ambrosia! All the way from the surface. I want you to have it.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯，来！我给你看个宝贝……仙馔密酒！这东西可是从遥远的地表来的，送给你了。

## `ZagreusHome_2321`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That means a lot coming from you, mate!

**中文**

你这些话对我来说意义重大，伙计！

## `ZagreusHome_2322`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey Orpheus, you know I once wrested this bottle of Ambrosia from the Champions of Elysium, defeating them two against one, just so I could bring it here to you personally?

**中文**

喂，俄耳甫斯，你知不知道，这仙馔密酒是我从至福乐土最强的战士手里抢来的，当时我可是以一打二，想着一定要给你搞到这瓶好酒。

## `ZagreusHome_2323`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Mate, if that is your wish, then it will be my pleasure to oblige. Though, may this gift symbolize the strength of our friendship for an eternity to come. I've learned so much from you.

**中文**

朋友，如果这就是你的愿望，那我马上就让你如愿。希望这份礼物成为我们亲密友情的象征。你教会了我很多东西。

## `ZagreusHome_2324`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father, have you ever considered that Orpheus might be much better as a court musician if you permitted him to see his muse again?

**中文**

父亲大人，你有没有想过，如果俄耳甫斯能见到他的灵感女神，他或许能够将他的音乐才能发挥得更好？

## `ZagreusHome_2325`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So you're forcing him to stay here for eternity just because he signed a contract with you?

**中文**

所以，就因为他和你签了合同，你就可以强迫他在这里待一辈子？

## `ZagreusHome_2326`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Who knows, they might surprise you.

**中文**

谁知道呢，说不定他们会给你个惊喜呢。

## `ZagreusHome_2327`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So what if I did? Just gives him a bit of break from time to time. He's still your court musician, isn't he?

**中文**

是我又怎么样？我只是想让他有一些休息的时间。他还是你的宫廷乐师，不是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2328`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Looking forward to it.

**中文**

我很期待。

## `ZagreusHome_2329`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, now that I have access to the administrative area again, I... well, I've access to the archives, and... I have some authority, to void those, under certain circumstances. So, about your pact...

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，现在我又能到行政区去了，我……可以进档案馆，而且……我还有权在特定情况下使其失去效力。所以，关于你的契约……

## `ZagreusHome_2330`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're sure...? Well then... I'll wait. But I am going to ask you again. We have to find a way to fix this. Patroclus is waiting.

**中文**

你确定吗……？那好吧……我再等等。过段时间我再问你。我们必须想办法解决这个问题。普特洛克勒斯在等着呢。

## `ZagreusHome_2331`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ahem, I, uh, forgot something.

**中文**

咳嗯，我，呃，我忘拿东西了。

## `ZagreusHome_2332`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hm, had to come back for something right quick.

**中文**

嗯，我得赶紧回去把东西拿上。

## `ZagreusHome_2333`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Forgot something again!

**中文**

我又忘拿东西了！

## `ZagreusHome_2334`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thought I'd just pop back in.

**中文**

我想我还是回去好了。

## `ZagreusHome_2335`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ungh, how can something so small be so evil?

**中文**

呃，这么小的东西怎么还能这么讨人厌呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2336`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mmph, that blasted tiny vermin...

**中文**

呃，该死的小鼠兽……

## `ZagreusHome_2337`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Augh, I'll have my vengeance yet, you tiny vermin.

**中文**

啊，我总有一天会报仇的，小鼠兽。

## `ZagreusHome_2338`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oof, well, not my proudest battle...

**中文**

噢哟，这场战斗可没什么值得吹嘘的……

## `ZagreusHome_2339`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Prophecy fulfilled!

**中文**

预言应验！

## `ZagreusHome_2340`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Scratch one off the Fated List.

**中文**

从命运清单上划掉一项。

## `ZagreusHome_2341`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Was only a matter of time I suppose.

**中文**

看起来只是时间问题了。

## `ZagreusHome_2342`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's one prophecy out of the way.

**中文**

又一个预言结束了。

## `ZagreusHome_2343`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I guess this came to pass!

**中文**

这算是应验了吧！

## `ZagreusHome_2344`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What do you know, this came true.

**中文**

真是世事难料啊，这都能成真。

## `ZagreusHome_2345`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No, keep them.

**中文**

不，还是留着吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2346`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I just wanted to see you again, Hypnos.

**中文**

我只是想再见你一面，修普诺斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2347`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well this should help you keep an eye on him.

**中文**

很好，这样你就可以留意他了。

## `ZagreusHome_2348`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Must be the regular feedings.

**中文**

是定期喂食的吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2349`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Orpheus must be in Asphodel.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯应该在水仙花平原。

## `ZagreusHome_2350`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Orpheus is with Eurydice I guess.

**中文**

我猜俄耳甫斯应该跟欧律狄刻在一起。

## `ZagreusHome_2351`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Orpheus is on break.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯在休息呢。

## `ZagreusHome_2352`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Check my record here...

**中文**

看看我的记录吧……

## `ZagreusHome_2353`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just a quick peek.

**中文**

稍微瞄一眼。

## `ZagreusHome_2354`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just minding my own business.

**中文**

只是忙忙我自己的事情而已。

## `ZagreusHome_2355`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How am I doing...

**中文**

我做得怎么样……

## `ZagreusHome_2356`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let me see here.

**中文**

让我看看噢吧

## `ZagreusHome_2357`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Father keeping tabs.

**中文**

父亲还留了标签呢。

## `ZagreusHome_2358`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Every one of my escape attempts...

**中文**

我每一次的逃脱行动……

## `ZagreusHome_2359`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How've I been doing.

**中文**

看看我的战绩好了。

## `ZagreusHome_2360`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's see how I'm doing.

**中文**

看看我做得如何。

## `ZagreusHome_2361`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Check my file here.

**中文**

来看看我的档案吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2362`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Did not go well.

**中文**

不怎么顺利。

## `ZagreusHome_2363`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oof, that one...

**中文**

哦哟，那一次……

## `ZagreusHome_2364`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh, this one...

**中文**

噢，这一次……

## `ZagreusHome_2365`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ehh...

**中文**

呃……

## `ZagreusHome_2366`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Think I remember this one.

**中文**

我还记得这一次。

## `ZagreusHome_2367`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What happened that time...

**中文**

那次发生了什么啊……

## `ZagreusHome_2368`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That was painful.

**中文**

那一次可把我疼坏了。

## `ZagreusHome_2369`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not a good time.

**中文**

那次不太好受。

## `ZagreusHome_2370`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not my best.

**中文**

那次表现得可不怎么样。

## `ZagreusHome_2371`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ouch...

**中文**

哎哟……

## `ZagreusHome_2372`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mmm....

**中文**

嗯……

## `ZagreusHome_2373`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Tsk...

**中文**

啧……

## `ZagreusHome_2374`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Augh...

**中文**

啊……

## `ZagreusHome_2375`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yeah...

**中文**

嗯呢……

## `ZagreusHome_2376`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good.

**中文**

还不错。

## `ZagreusHome_2377`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mm-hm.

**中文**

嗯——嗯。

## `ZagreusHome_2378`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hm.

**中文**

嗯。

## `ZagreusHome_2379`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Did it.

**中文**

成功了。

## `ZagreusHome_2380`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's how it's done.

**中文**

就是这样的。

## `ZagreusHome_2381`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Made it that time.

**中文**

那次成功了。

## `ZagreusHome_2382`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nice.

**中文**

漂亮。

## `ZagreusHome_2383`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not bad.

**中文**

还不赖。

## `ZagreusHome_2384`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not too bad.

**中文**

还行吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2385`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Made it out.

**中文**

成功了。

## `ZagreusHome_2386`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fine work, Zagreus.

**中文**

干得漂亮，扎格列欧斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2387`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good job, boy.

**中文**

我可真厉害呢。

## `ZagreusHome_2388`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What a joke.

**中文**

有够好笑的。

## `ZagreusHome_2389`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The job's number one perk... no thanks.

**中文**

那份工作的第一个福利……还是算了吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2390`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not thirsty.

**中文**

不渴。

## `ZagreusHome_2391`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Still not thirsty.

**中文**

还是不渴。

## `ZagreusHome_2392`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Still definitely not thirsty.

**中文**

还是一点都不渴。

## `ZagreusHome_2393`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mm, no.

**中文**

嗯，不。

## `ZagreusHome_2394`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I've had enough Stygian water, thanks.

**中文**

我已经喝够冥河水了，谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_2395`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Never seen anyone drink this stuff.

**中文**

从没见过任何人喝这东西。

## `ZagreusHome_2396`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can wait till the next Fountain Chamber.

**中文**

我已经等不及要去下一个泉水房间了。

## `ZagreusHome_2397`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No...

**中文**

不……

## `ZagreusHome_2398`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not happening.

**中文**

门都没有。

## `ZagreusHome_2399`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Never.

**中文**

绝对没戏。

## `ZagreusHome_2400`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Everybody hang in there!

**中文**

大家都坚持住！

## `ZagreusHome_2401`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have. Chaos is... not like anyone I've ever met. At first I was a little worried, meeting this vast primordial entity apparently responsible for all creation, but... they seem all right. Chaos and Nyx haven't been talking for a while, though. You don't know what happened there, do you?

**中文**

是真的。 卡俄斯……不像我见过的任何人或神。 起初，我有点担心，遇到这个庞大的，据说创造了整个世界的原始实体，但是……祂似乎还不错。 不过，卡俄斯和倪克斯似乎很久没有这样聊了。 您知道他们之间发生了什么吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2402`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They drifted apart...

**中文**

他们分开了……

## `ZagreusHome_2403`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wanted to ask you more about Nyx and Chaos, sir. So they just drifted apart, I mean... why not just keep in touch? I don't understand. How hard is it just to say hello from time to time?

**中文**

我想向你打听关于倪克斯和卡俄斯的更多消息，先生。所以他们就这么分开了，我是说……为什么他们不保持联系？我不懂。时不时问声好会有多难？

## `ZagreusHome_2404`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, really? I've never heard Hypnos or Thanatos mention any of them, let alone Nyx. They sound like a handful. Where are they, what happened?

**中文**

真、真的吗? 我从未听修普诺斯或塔纳托斯提到过其中任何一个，更不用说倪克斯了。 他们听起来很麻烦。 他们在哪里，发生了什么事？

## `ZagreusHome_2405`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yeah... I can see that.

**中文**

是啊……我都看到了。

## `ZagreusHome_2406`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**
