Oh, just took a bit of tampering with the Sigil in the admin chamber. Whole bunch of Darkness, too. But I could tell that Nyx and Chaos, they... seemed to really miss each other, to the extent they are able to miss anybody.

**中文**

噢，只是改动了一下行政办公室内的印记。还用到了一大堆的黑暗宝石。不过我看得出来，倪克斯和卡俄斯，她们……好像都思念着对方，只要她们还能够思念什么人的话。

## `ZagreusHome_2407`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I couldn't stand it.

**中文**

我没法忍受。

## `ZagreusHome_2408`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're just angry because some of us still want to have anything to do with their parents.

**中文**

你不过是因为我们之中还有人会为自己的父母着想而发怒。

## `ZagreusHome_2409`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My pleasure.

**中文**

非常荣幸。

## `ZagreusHome_2410`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nyx might be with Chaos...

**中文**

倪克斯可能与卡俄斯一起。

## `ZagreusHome_2411`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Maybe Nyx is with Chaos...

**中文**

也许倪克斯与卡俄斯一起。

## `ZagreusHome_2412`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nyx is with Chaos, huh...

**中文**

哦，倪克斯与卡俄斯一起嘛...

## `ZagreusHome_2413`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All my escape attempts...

**中文**

我每一次的逃脱行动……

## `ZagreusHome_2414`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Security Log...

**中文**

是安保日志……

## `ZagreusHome_2415`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's the latest, Security Log?

**中文**

有新消息吗，安保日志？

## `ZagreusHome_2416`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We meet again, Security Log.

**中文**

又见面啦，安保日志。

## `ZagreusHome_2417`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Security Log keeping up?

**中文**

安保日志一直在更新啊？

## `ZagreusHome_2418`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

There is no escape.

**中文**

没人逃得出去。

## `ZagreusHome_2419`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

There is no escape...

**中文**

逃不出去的……

## `ZagreusHome_2420`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Is there no escape?

**中文**

逃不出去吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2421`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What do they have on me.

**中文**

看看有什么关于我的事情。

## `ZagreusHome_2422`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's see what they have on me.

**中文**

让我看看有没有关于我的事情。

## `ZagreusHome_2423`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's check the numbers.

**中文**

来看看这些数字吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2424`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's see my rap sheet.

**中文**

来看看我的记录吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2425`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Check the rap sheet...

**中文**

查看一下记录……

## `ZagreusHome_2426`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My permanent record.

**中文**

我的永久记录。

## `ZagreusHome_2427`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Take a quick look then.

**中文**

稍微看一下就好。

## `ZagreusHome_2428`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Tracking my every move...

**中文**

追踪我的每一次行动……

## `ZagreusHome_2429`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's see some numbers, then.

**中文**

来看看数字吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2430`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I've seen enough.

**中文**

我看够了。

## `ZagreusHome_2431`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Enough snooping for now.

**中文**

暂时先不看了吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2432`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Informative.

**中文**

挺有用的信息。

## `ZagreusHome_2433`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Interesting.

**中文**

很有意思。

## `ZagreusHome_2434`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well, back to not looking at the Security Log.

**中文**

好了，不看这些安保日志了。

## `ZagreusHome_2435`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Farewell, Security Log.

**中文**

再见，安保日志。

## `ZagreusHome_2436`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well, back to not looking at my Permanent Record.

**中文**

好了，不看我的永久记录了。

## `ZagreusHome_2437`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Farewell, Permanent Record.

**中文**

再见了，永久记录。

## `ZagreusHome_2438`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Till next time.

**中文**

下次见吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2439`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's enough numbers.

**中文**

不想再看数字了。

## `ZagreusHome_2440`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Enough numbers for now.

**中文**

暂时不想再看数字了。

## `ZagreusHome_2441`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Enough of that.

**中文**

暂时不想再看了。

## `ZagreusHome_2442`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm good on numbers for now.

**中文**

我暂时不想再看数字了。

## `ZagreusHome_2443`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well, back to it.

**中文**

好吧，回去看看。

## `ZagreusHome_2444`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right then.

**中文**

很好。

## `ZagreusHome_2445`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good info.

**中文**

很有用的信息。

## `ZagreusHome_2446`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good to know.

**中文**

感谢告知。

## `ZagreusHome_2447`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Boons...

**中文**

是祝福……

## `ZagreusHome_2448`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My Boons...

**中文**

我的祝福……

## `ZagreusHome_2449`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Boon records...

**中文**

祝福记录……

## `ZagreusHome_2450`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All my Boons...

**中文**

我所有祝福的记录……

## `ZagreusHome_2451`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Infernal Arms...

**中文**

冥府兵器……

## `ZagreusHome_2452`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My weapons...

**中文**

我的武器……

## `ZagreusHome_2453`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Records of my weapons...

**中文**

是我武器的记录……

## `ZagreusHome_2454`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Weapon records...

**中文**

武器记录……

## `ZagreusHome_2455`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Daedalus Hammer records...

**中文**

狄德勒斯之锤的记录……

## `ZagreusHome_2456`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My Hammer enchantments...

**中文**

我的锻锤附魔记录……

## `ZagreusHome_2457`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Daedalus Hammer enchantments...

**中文**

狄德勒斯之锤的附魔记录……

## `ZagreusHome_2458`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hammer records...

**中文**

是锻锤的记录……

## `ZagreusHome_2459`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Aspects of my weapons...

**中文**

我武器的形态……

## `ZagreusHome_2460`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My Aspect records...

**中文**

武器形态的记录……

## `ZagreusHome_2461`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Records of my Aspects...

**中文**

这里记载着武器的形态……

## `ZagreusHome_2462`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My Weapon Aspects...

**中文**

我的武器形态……

## `ZagreusHome_2463`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Keepsake records...

**中文**

是信物的记录……

## `ZagreusHome_2464`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Records of my Keepsakes...

**中文**

我所有的信物都记在这里……

## `ZagreusHome_2465`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All my Keepsakes....

**中文**

我所有的信物……

## `ZagreusHome_2466`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My Keepsakes, huh...

**中文**

是我的信物，嗯……

## `ZagreusHome_2467`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nothing here yet.

**中文**

这里什么都没有。

## `ZagreusHome_2468`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nothing here.

**中文**

什么都没有。

## `ZagreusHome_2469`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Say, Meg's the featured servant of the House.

**中文**

看来，墨纪是这座圣殿里最受欢迎的仆从。

## `ZagreusHome_2470`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Looks like Meg's the featured servant of the House.

**中文**

看起来，墨纪是这座圣殿里最受欢迎的仆从。

## `ZagreusHome_2471`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Meg's the featured servant of the House again.

**中文**

墨纪又成了这座圣殿里最受欢迎的仆从。

## `ZagreusHome_2472`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Megaera, the featured servant of the House.

**中文**

墨纪拉是这座圣殿里最受欢迎的仆从。

## `ZagreusHome_2473`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Than's the featured servant of the House again.

**中文**

塔纳又成了这座圣殿里最受欢迎的仆从。

## `ZagreusHome_2474`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Than's the featured servant, once again.

**中文**

塔纳又成了这座圣殿里最受欢迎的仆从，他又赢了。

## `ZagreusHome_2475`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Than's got the top honors again I guess.

**中文**

看来，塔纳在荣誉榜上又登顶了。

## `ZagreusHome_2476`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanatos, featured servant of the House.

**中文**

塔纳托斯是这座圣殿里最受欢迎的仆从。

## `ZagreusHome_2477`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Won't ever see my face up on that wall I guess.

**中文**

我猜这面墙上应该不会有我的画像吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2478`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's only ever Thanatos or Meg up there.

**中文**

这上面不是塔纳托斯，就是墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_2479`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You say that like it's a bad thing. Little bit of excess from time to time never hurt anyone I'm sure.

**中文**

你的口气就好像它不是什么好东西一样。偶尔奢侈一下是不会有什么坏处的，我向你保证。

## `ZagreusHome_2480`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

For you, Thanatos. The Olympians might be accustomed to this stuff but I think we should have our share of it down here. At least you should.

**中文**

这是给你的，塔纳托斯。奥林匹斯的众神可能已经喝惯了这种东西，不过我觉得，我们也应该尝尝才对，至少你应该尝尝。

## `ZagreusHome_2481`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

But try it you did! And now, you'll get to try this one for comparison's sake. Each one's supposed to be different you know.

**中文**

再喝一口嘛！再尝一口，就当是比较一下味道吧。你知道吧，每一瓶蜜酒的味道都不一样。

## `ZagreusHome_2482`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yeah, but I wanted to!

**中文**

是啊，可我也想！

## `ZagreusHome_2483`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Probably because Nyx took issue with me chatting with Dusa from time to time. Is that really so unbecoming of me? Not even Father seems to care.

**中文**

可能是因为我时不时与杜莎聊天，倪克斯觉得不太妥当吧？这么做真的很不合适吗？父亲好像根本都不在乎。

## `ZagreusHome_2484`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're telling me I should just start ignoring Dusa from now on, like you do?

**中文**

你是想我从今以后不理杜莎吗，就像你一样？

## `ZagreusHome_2485`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Tsch.

**中文**

啧。

## `ZagreusHome_2486`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm sorry I raised my voice with you last time, Than. I know Nyx puts so much into this place, and has nurtured so many of us here.

**中文**

很抱歉上次和你发脾气了，塔纳。我知道倪克斯很在乎这个地方，我们很多人也都是由她抚养成人的。

## `ZagreusHome_2487`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanks...

**中文**

谢谢……

## `ZagreusHome_2488`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx let her go, Than. Dusa, I mean, she's gone. What do I do?

**中文**

倪克斯把她撵走了，塔纳。我是说，杜莎她走了，我该怎么办？

## `ZagreusHome_2489`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Then she should give Dusa another chance. Has Meg heard? She'll be furious.

**中文**

那她应该再给杜莎一个机会才对。墨纪听说这件事情了吗？她一定会火冒三丈的。

## `ZagreusHome_2490`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Maybe you should.

**中文**

或许你应该试试。

## `ZagreusHome_2491`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx genuinely cares about this House and all of us. She had to come around on something like this. Dusa's one of the hardest workers here. Even if she has room to improve.

**中文**

倪克斯真的很在乎这座圣殿，在乎我们，她不得不处理这样的事情。杜莎差不多算是我们之中工作最勤奋的人了，但即便如此，她也有进步空间。

## `ZagreusHome_2492`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I don't think we have to worry about that.

**中文**

我觉得那件事不用担心。

## `ZagreusHome_2493`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Than, you don't take issue with me being close with Dusa, do you? She brings such warmth to this place... I really appreciate having her here.

**中文**

塔纳，我和杜莎走得很近，你对此不介意吧？她让这里充满了温暖……我很庆幸这里能有她在。

## `ZagreusHome_2494`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yeah, well... thanks, Than.

**中文**

是啊，没错……谢谢你，塔纳。

## `ZagreusHome_2495`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Than? I thought you ought to know that Ares, he... I think he's taken quite an interest in Nyx. Keeps asking me to talk to her on his behalf.

**中文**

塔纳？你应该和阿瑞斯很熟吧，他……我觉得他对倪克斯很感兴趣，他总想让我替他给倪克斯带话。

## `ZagreusHome_2496`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Finally? You don't think Ares has untoward intentions or anything like that?

**中文**

终于啊？你不觉得阿瑞斯有什么不良企图之类的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2497`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Eh, you're probably right...

**中文**

呃，或许你说得没错……

## `ZagreusHome_2498`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It was my pleasure. Say, did Nyx ever let on to you of her relationship with Chaos, at any point?

**中文**

我很荣幸。和我说说吧，倪克斯有没有和你透露过她和卡俄斯之间的关系？

## `ZagreusHome_2499`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Least I could do for all she's done for me.

**中文**

她为我做了这么多，至少这是我能做的事情。

## `ZagreusHome_2500`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Far from it, Than. I got where I needed to go, and if not for your help, maybe I wouldn't have. As for why I'm back... as Father would say, I have some business to attend to, here.

**中文**

才不是呢，塔纳。该去的地方我都去了，而且如果没有你的帮助，或许我根本到不了那些地方。至于我为什么会回来……就像父亲说的那样，我来这里有点事要办。

## `ZagreusHome_2501`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, just how far are you able to shift, anyway, Than? I never could go more than a blade's length in front of me, while you're out there skipping across the entire world probably.

**中文**

嘿，你能瞬间移动多远呢，塔纳？我最多只能瞬移差不多一把刀那么远的距离，而你却能在整个世界之中到处穿梭。

## `ZagreusHome_2502`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can see that.

**中文**

看得出来。

## `ZagreusHome_2503`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Than, my blade, the one I'm borrowing from Father anyway, it... revealed to me an older aspect of itself, of Nemesis. Nyx never mentions her. Have you two ever met?

**中文**

塔纳，我那把刀，就是我从父亲那里借来的那把，它……它向我展示了它更古老的形态，是涅墨西斯的形态。倪克斯从来没提起过她，你见过她吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2504`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm sure she's lovely, then.

**中文**

说真的，她很可爱。

## `ZagreusHome_2505`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Why, Thanatos, you're learning to pry in others' business! The Rail's been working out just fine, thanks. Though, now that you mention it, how well do you know Eris, one of Nyx's daughters, right? Seems she wielded the Rail before me.

**中文**

怎么，塔纳托斯，你也开始打探别人的事情了嘛！那把炮我一直用得很顺手，多谢了。不过既然你提起来的话，你和厄里斯熟吗？她也是倪克斯的女儿，是吧？看起来，她是那把坚炮之前的主人。

## `ZagreusHome_2506`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right, then!

**中文**

好了！

## `ZagreusHome_2507`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, were you ever close with Charon, Than? Seems like no matter how much I try, I can't quite get through to him.

**中文**

嘿，你之前和卡戎融洽相处过吗，塔纳？好像不管我怎么努力，都没办法和他搞好关系。

## `ZagreusHome_2508`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fair enough.

**中文**

说得没错。

## `ZagreusHome_2509`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Than, now hypothetically, if I were to have vanquished Charon in single combat after having, I don't know, say, helped myself to one of his wares at no charge, how long would you say he'd hold it over me?

**中文**

嘿，塔纳，假设说，我拿了卡戎的东西却没给他钱，之后又在一对一的战斗中把他打败了，你觉得这件事情他会记恨多久？

## `ZagreusHome_2510`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

So then, hypothetically, we're squared away!

**中文**

所以说，假设我们被淘汰了！

## `ZagreusHome_2511`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Than, level with me on this. You didn't happen to hire a shifty character called Skelly to assist me here and there, did you?

**中文**

塔纳，实话告诉我吧。你该不会刚好雇了个叫“骨头”的狡猾帮手，来帮我忙这忙那吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2512`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Didn't think so.

**中文**

我不这么认为。

## `ZagreusHome_2513`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Than, you're not going to believe this, but... Charon's been helping me. All on his own, apparently! I was so sure that Nyx or someone put him up to it. That Skelly character I mentioned, Charon hired him!

**中文**

塔纳，或许我这么说你不会信，但……卡戎一直都在帮我。很显然，全是靠他自己的力量！我很确定他是奉倪克斯或某人之命这么做的。我之前说的那个叫骨头的家伙，就是在替卡戎工作！

## `ZagreusHome_2514`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm sure! His helper, Skelly, he's a lot more talkative, and finally let on. Although, he couldn't tell me how come Charon did a thing like this, I mean... we weren't ever close...

**中文**

我很确定！他的帮手骨头是个大嘴巴，他终于说漏嘴了。不过，他还是没告诉我卡戎为什么要做这种事情。我是说……我们两个之前都不熟……

## `ZagreusHome_2515`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Did he really think that far ahead...?

**中文**

他真的已经想了那么远了吗……？

## `ZagreusHome_2516`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Do you remember anything else about my mother, Than? Just, anything. You weren't close, I guess?

**中文**

关于我的母亲，你还记得什么别的事情吗，塔纳？什么事情都行。你们之前不太亲近，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2517`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father isn't from this place, either. How was she any different?

**中文**

父亲也不是在这个地方诞生的，她又有什么不一样的呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2518`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Radiance, huh... doesn't sound like Father at all.

**中文**

容光焕发，呃……听起来一点都不像父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_2519`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Turns out I'm full of surprises, Thanatos. Though, the Three Fates are your sisters, aren't they? Surprised you didn't requisition the List yourself.

**中文**

我就是这样总能给人带来惊喜，塔纳托斯。不过命运三女神不是你的姐妹吗？我很惊讶你居然没自己申请命运清单。

## `ZagreusHome_2520`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can hazard a guess...

**中文**

我有个大胆的假设……

## `ZagreusHome_2521`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

He does, and I did, Than. I think we can stand to see a little less of him if it means he can be happier for the remainder of eternity.

**中文**

是啊，而且确实是我干的，塔纳。如果他在余下的永生之中能更开心的话，就算少见他几面，我觉得也是可以忍受的。

## `ZagreusHome_2522`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What? Orpheus is my mate, Than. And I came to know his muse, Eurydice, as well. They still had feelings for each other, but with no way to communicate.

**中文**

你在说什么呢？俄耳甫斯是我的伙伴，塔纳，我和他的缪斯女神欧律狄刻也很熟。他们依然彼此相爱，只是没办法交流而已。

## `ZagreusHome_2523`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Than, wait...!

**中文**

塔纳，等一下……！

## `ZagreusHome_2524`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

We did what needed to be done. As much as Father likes making examples out of wretched shades, an eternal sentence felt a little excessive in this case. Besides... Sisyphus isn't leaving, anyway. Seems to have found his niche.

**中文**

我们只是做了很久以前就该做的事情。虽然父亲喜欢拿卑鄙的幽灵杀鸡儆猴，但永生永世的惩罚未免过分了点。另外……西西弗斯是不会离开这里的，他似乎已经找到了他该干的事情。

## `ZagreusHome_2525`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Don't think you will.

**中文**

我不觉得你会这么做。

## `ZagreusHome_2526`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You and Achilles ever get to talk much, Than?

**中文**

你和阿喀琉斯聊过什么吗，塔纳？

## `ZagreusHome_2527`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Because he was a warrior. When mortals die a violent death, they're no longer your case, are they?

**中文**

因为他生前是个战士，在战斗中丧命的凡人不归你管，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2528`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know he's more than my trainer, Than. As for how I did it, turns out being the first and only son of Hades has its privileges. Now Achilles gets to be with someone he loves. Who loves him back.

**中文**

你也知道，他可不仅仅是我的教练。至于我是怎么做到的，身为冥王的长子，也是他唯一的儿子，我还是有些特权的。现在，阿喀琉斯应该和他相爱的人在一起了。

## `ZagreusHome_2529`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It is. I think it is.

**中文**

是这样的，我想是的。

## `ZagreusHome_2530`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...I did. Sometimes I need to slow things down. I'm thankful that we've been on better terms, lately. But, I've been coming on too strong. Forgive me. You're my dear friend. I don't want to do anything to hurt you. Or anybody else.

**中文**

……我真的这么觉得，有时候我需要放慢节奏。我们最近的关系越来越好，我也感到非常欣慰。但我的情绪有点过于激动。请你原谅。你是我的好朋友。我不想伤害你。或者其他人。

## `ZagreusHome_2531`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...Be seeing you, too.

**中文**

……再见了。

## `ZagreusHome_2532`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Got to tell you something, Than. You can be pretty mean to Hypnos! And that's coming from someone who's mean to Hypnos a lot. Believe me, I completely understand, but... still.

**中文**

有些话我想和你说，塔纳。对修普诺斯来说，你是个很重要的人，可有时候你对他太刻薄了！说实话，我能明白你的感受，可……就是太过了。

## `ZagreusHome_2533`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yeah, but... pretty much none of this would be here, were it not for Nyx, right? Anyway my point is not that you should go easy on him, but... there must be some way not to get as frustrated. You'll both be better off.

**中文**

是啊，可是……如果不是倪克斯的缘故，这里的一切都不会是这个样子，不是吗？总之，我不是想说你应该对他宽厚点，我只想说……肯定有办法可以不闹得这么僵，你们两个可以相处得更和睦。

## `ZagreusHome_2534`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Probably!

**中文**

或许吧！

## `ZagreusHome_2535`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I can hardly believe it. Hypnos finally came out on top, huh? Even Father seemed pleased with him. That's more than I get.

**中文**

我简直不敢相信，修普诺斯终于打起精神了吗？就连父亲大人似乎都对他很满意，我可真没想过会这样。

## `ZagreusHome_2536`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, that? I noticed you two getting along better recently. So what's your secret?

**中文**

哦，是吗？我发现你们两个的关系最近好了不少，你有什么秘诀吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2537`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Who doesn't have room for improvement, though, right?

**中文**

不过，谁会没有进步的空间呢，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2538`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You and Meg go back a long way, don't you? I forget sometimes you two are pretty close.

**中文**

你和墨纪以前就很熟悉，是吧？有时候，我都忘了你们之前走得很近了。

## `ZagreusHome_2539`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, right. He got stuck in Tartarus after that, under Meg's personal supervision. So you bonded over your shared disdain for Sisyphus?

**中文**

哦，没错，是墨纪亲自下令要把西西弗斯困在塔耳塔罗斯的。所以，你们是因为都很讨厌西西弗斯，才熟悉起来的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2540`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I know what you mean.

**中文**

我明白你的意思。

## `ZagreusHome_2541`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm sometimes at a loss myself, Thanatos. Though one thing I know is it's always really good running into you out there, it's... well, exciting. Sparring with you and all that! And then, I know it means that I might get to see you here.

**中文**

有的时候我自己也不清楚，塔纳托斯。不过有件事情我很清楚，能见到你我总是很高兴。这么说吧……呃，和你讨论各种事情很有意思！而且我知道，只要来这里就能见到你了。

## `ZagreusHome_2542`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Wait! Ugh.

**中文**

等等！呃。

## `ZagreusHome_2543`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Than, tell me something, and forgive the awkward question here, but... are we good? I mean... between us. Any infuriating habits I should know about, that sort of thing?

**中文**

嘿，塔纳，抱歉问你这样一个蠢问题，不过和我说说吧……我们还是朋友吧？我是说……我们两个之间。我有没有什么让你讨厌的习惯，或者类似的东西？

## `ZagreusHome_2544`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So then, we're good. Because I think we're good, but I just wanted to make sure.

**中文**

好了，那我们就还是朋友。我知道我们一直是朋友，但我只是想确认一下。

## `ZagreusHome_2545`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Good.

**中文**

……很好。

## `ZagreusHome_2546`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, I definitely learned my lesson there. But, thanks, I guess. You've opened up a lot, yourself, by the way. Talking to you lately, it's a world apart from the first time. You looked like you were going to slice my head off.

**中文**

哦，当然了，我已经尝到教训了。不过，谢谢你这么说。顺便一提，你和以前相比也开朗了不少。你现在的样子，和我第一次见你的时候简直判若两人。那个时候，你简直像是要把我脑袋砍下来一样。

## `ZagreusHome_2547`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, the option is always available, I guess. Hey, you ever think of growing it out again?

**中文**

哎，我猜你现在也随时可以这么做吧。嘿，你有没有想过再把头发留起来？

## `ZagreusHome_2548`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Definitely.

**中文**

确实如此。

## `ZagreusHome_2549`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Thank you for waiting.

**中文**

……不好意思，久等了。

## `ZagreusHome_2550`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Thank you for waiting. What can I do for you?

**中文**

……不好意思，久等了。有什么能帮忙的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2551`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, looks like I could buy back my right to go in the administrative chamber, there. I never want to go in there again, but... can you think of any reason whatsoever that I should?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，看来我已经可以赎回自己的权利，可以再次进入管理大厅了。我真的一点都不想去那个地方，不过……在你看来，我有没有什么非去不可的理由？

## `ZagreusHome_2552`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Don't know how I'd ever find something in there, but I suppose so, yes.

**中文**

我也不知道到底能不能找到些什么，不过我猜可以吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2553`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I know that it's a painful subject, sir, but can you tell me... what exactly happened, in the war that claimed Patroclus and yourself? He brought it up. Said you refused to fight.

**中文**

我知道这个话题会让你很痛苦，先生，但你可以告诉我……你和普特洛克勒斯参加的那场战争之中，到底发生了什么吗？他和我提起了这件事情，说你当时不想参加战斗。

## `ZagreusHome_2554`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

And after you found out... you sought vengeance for him. Took vengeance.

**中文**

你发现了这件事情之后……决定为他报仇，你踏上了复仇之路。

## `ZagreusHome_2555`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Did you... allow yourself to die? So that you could see Patroclus again, here?

**中文**

你是……有意寻死的吗？这样你就可以在这里与普特洛克勒斯相见了，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2556`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Of course.

**中文**

……当然了。

## `ZagreusHome_2557`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

He's doing relatively well, I think. There's a lot you've told me... that he didn't know. About what led up to his present circumstances, and your own.

**中文**

我觉得，他看起来还不错。你和我说过很多……他之前并不知道的事情，包括他是怎么来到这里的，还有你的事情。

## `ZagreusHome_2558`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, I... I'm sorry, I... just told him about your ashes being intermingled, that whole thing? And about the pact. You told me once to send him your love and regards! This brought him no joy, only questions! You told me not to speak to Father, not to him!

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，我……我很抱歉。我……我只和他说了你们的骨灰被混在一起了，就是这些事情！还有关于契约的事情。你之前和我说要向他传达你对他的爱与敬意！这些话没有让他感到喜悦，只让他问了许多问题！你说这些话不准告诉我父亲，可你没说不准告诉他！

## `ZagreusHome_2559`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I swear I will.

**中文**

我发誓我会的。

## `ZagreusHome_2560`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have a message for you from the fallen warrior over in Elysium, Achilles sir. He says... he simply said to let you know to risk it all.

**中文**

那个至福乐土的阵亡战士让我给你带句话，阿喀琉斯先生。他说……他只说他想让你知道，他要放手一搏了。

## `ZagreusHome_2561`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yes. Is... there something significant about that phrase?

**中文**

是啊，这……这句话有什么特别的含义吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2562`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

He's asking you to take another chance.

**中文**

他希望你不要再错过这次的机会。

## `ZagreusHome_2563`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

About risking it all, sir. Did you come to any decision about what that meant? What you should do?

**中文**

关于放手一搏的事情，先生，你明白他这句话是什么意思了吗？你打算怎么做？

## `ZagreusHome_2564`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Please do.

**中文**

请便吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2565`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Of course, sir, what is it? I'm listening.

**中文**

当然没问题，先生。是什么请求？我听着呢。

## `ZagreusHome_2566`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...Except that fear is for the weak, Achilles, sir.

**中文**

……除非什么呢？只有弱者才会恐惧，阿喀琉斯先生。

## `ZagreusHome_2567`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'll speak with Nyx about it the next chance I get. And I'll be careful, sir. I promise you.

**中文**

下次有机会的话，我和倪克斯谈谈这件事吧。我会小心的，先生，我向你保证。

## `ZagreusHome_2568`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm working on trying to find your pact, Achilles. Just need a little more time to sort it out.

**中文**

我还在找你的契约，阿喀琉斯。我只要再花点时间去找找就行了。

## `ZagreusHome_2569`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should check the admin chamber for the pact Nyx mentioned...

**中文**

或许我应该去管理大厅，找找倪克斯提到的那份契约……

## `ZagreusHome_2570`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should be able to approve a few revisions to that one with the House Contractor I think.

**中文**

我觉得，我应该有权力批准圣殿承包商合同中的一部分修改条款。

## `ZagreusHome_2571`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's the revised Achilles Pact...

**中文**

是修改过的阿喀琉斯的契约……

## `ZagreusHome_2572`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

In the name of Hades, the shade of Achilles is hereby no longer barred from visiting Elysium!

**中文**

我以哈迪斯的名义解除限制，允许阿喀琉斯的亡魂访问至福乐土！

## `ZagreusHome_2573`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles! Your pact with Father is officially amended, sir. Specifically, it no longer has any stipulation barring you from entering Elysium during the indefinite term of your employment. On my authority in the name of Hades, thank you for your service.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯！你和我父亲的契约已经正式修正了，先生。确切地说，在你无限期的任职期间，已经不会再有人禁止你进入至福乐土了。我以哈迪斯之名在此授权，感谢你一直以来的服务。

## `ZagreusHome_2574`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You can simply leave, right now. And, might I add... it's about damn time.

**中文**

你想走就可以走了。而且我得加一句……你也是时候该走了。

## `ZagreusHome_2575`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Incidentally, there's this one glade out in Elysium I highly recommend... go to him, sir. You've waited long enough. Fear is for the weak.

**中文**

顺带一提，我向你强烈推荐在至福乐土中的这片林地……去找他吧，先生，你已经等得够久了。只有弱者才会恐惧。

## `ZagreusHome_2576`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Farewell for now, sir!

**中文**

再见了，先生！

## `ZagreusHome_2577`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Achilles!

**中文**

阿喀琉斯！

## `ZagreusHome_2578`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sir, you're back! How was it in Elysium, was everything all right?

**中文**

先生，你回来啦！至福乐土那边怎么样，一切都还顺利吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2579`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Come on, Achilles, I want details, here! What happened with you two? If you don't mind.

**中文**

来嘛，阿喀琉斯，我想听细节！你们两个怎么样了，不介意和我说说吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2580`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's wonderful to hear. He seemed uplifted in your presence. You must have lots of catching up to do.

**中文**

这可正是个好消息。有你在他身边，他看起来精神多了，你们肯定有很多事情要做吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2581`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Count on it, sir.

**中文**

交给我吧，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_2582`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Achilles might be in Elysium I guess.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯大概在至福乐土吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2583`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Achilles must be with Patroclus now.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯肯定和普特洛克勒斯在一起。

## `ZagreusHome_2584`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Achilles is probably in Elysium.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯大概在至福乐土吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2585`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Achilles!

**中文**

阿喀琉斯！

## `ZagreusHome_2586`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sometimes the law is not so bad.

**中文**

有的时候，法律法规也没那么可恶嘛！

## `ZagreusHome_2587`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, next time you're in Elysium, I thought this might be something you'd enjoy. That you might share.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，等你下次再去至福乐土的时候，我觉得或许你们可以一起享受一下这瓶美酒。

## `ZagreusHome_2588`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You have a Chthonic Companion! And you're giving it to me?!

**中文**

这可是冥府伴偶！你真的要把它给我吗？！

## `ZagreusHome_2589`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's hard to think of anybody more deserving of this than you, sir. So when next you are off duty, or whenever the occasion warrants, please, enjoy, all right?

**中文**

我想象不出还有谁能比你更配得上这件礼物了，先生。等你下次再休息，或者什么时候有空的话，好好享受一下吧，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2590`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, I'm absolutely positive. I would never have been remotely prepared for everything I've had to face, if not for all your guidance. And, I don't just mean the violent stuff.

**中文**

哦，当然了，要不是因为您的教导，我恐怕永远都没办法为注定要面对的事情做好准备。而且， 我指的也不仅仅是打架的那部分。

## `ZagreusHome_2591`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

See, that's why you give such good advice, right there!

**中文**

你看，所以我才说，你总能给我出色的建议！

## `ZagreusHome_2592`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Here, sir! To you, and to Patroclus. May the remainder of eternity more than make up for everything it took for you to come this far.

**中文**

这是给你和普特洛克勒斯的礼物，先生！你不远千里来到这里，希望这种永恒的残片足以弥补你这一路上遇到的艰险。

## `ZagreusHome_2593`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Really? I mean... I meant no offense, of course, it's just... I thought you enjoyed this stuff, no?

**中文**

真的吗？我……我无意冒犯，当然没问题了，只是……你不是挺喜欢这种酒的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2594`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, good. You have much to be proud of, Achilles. You're a great warrior. A great instructor. A great friend.

**中文**

是嘛，那很好，你有很多地方值得自豪，阿喀琉斯。你是个伟大的战士，伟大的导师，伟大的朋友。

## `ZagreusHome_2595`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I mean it.

**中文**

我是说真的。

## `ZagreusHome_2596`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

These adorable little creatures forced to live in a cage ought to lighten the mood.

**中文**

为了活跃这里的气氛，这种可爱的小家伙不得不住在笼子里。

## `ZagreusHome_2597`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Perhaps the mystery of whether it's day or night around here will finally be solved?

**中文**

现在到底是白天还是晚上，或许这个未解之谜终于能解开了。

## `ZagreusHome_2598`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

At least somebody will enjoy it around here, even if it's just a painted skeleton.

**中文**

就算仅仅是涂了色的骨头架子，至少有人会喜欢。

## `ZagreusHome_2599`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The comfort and the safety hazard of a roaring fire seems the perfect touch for our fine lounge, I say.

**中文**

它既让人感到安逸，又有可能引发火灾。对我们的休息室而言，我认为它是个完美的选择。

## `ZagreusHome_2600`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

An intricate mosaic just above my bedchambers should make having to go in feel less bad I think.

**中文**

装饰在卧室棚顶的马赛克图案，应该会让我不得不离开的心情舒缓一下吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2601`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

These decorative laurels ought to give us an appreciable touch of dignity no doubt.

**中文**

毫无疑问，这些桂冠样子的装饰会给我们一种明显的尊严感。

## `ZagreusHome_2602`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I swear, who would purchase laurels, when they could earn them in sporting competition?

**中文**

我想说，要是能直接从运动比赛中赢来这些桂冠的话，还有谁愿意花钱去买呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2603`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know Achilles reasonably well, don't you, Dusa? Seems like he's always stuck there in that hall. At least you get to fly around. Sometimes I think he wishes he was someplace else.

**中文**

你和阿喀琉斯很熟悉，是吗，杜莎？至少你还能到处乱飞，但他却似乎一直被困在那座大厅里。有时候我觉得，他想去的是别的地方。

## `ZagreusHome_2604`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Maybe he does. Maybe you're right, Dusa.

**中文**

或许他是这样，或许你说得没错，杜莎。

## `ZagreusHome_2605`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Me too, Dusa. The happy-for-him part, at any rate! He's a good friend, and he deserved much better than to be stuck here.

**中文**

我也是，杜莎，他的幸福值得这一切麻烦！他是个善良的朋友，不应该一辈子都被困在这个地方。

## `ZagreusHome_2606`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Dusa? Turns out that I may be stopping back from time to time, like this. But, please don't bother picking up my room. I won't be staying long.

**中文**

呃，杜莎？我有可能确实会像这样，时不时地回来一趟。不过，我的房间就不用费心收拾了，反正我也待不了太久。

## `ZagreusHome_2607`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Erm... good-bye.

**中文**

好吧……再见。

## `ZagreusHome_2608`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, it's nothing for you to be concerned about, Dusa. I'm just having a harder time fighting my blasted way out of this place than I anticipated. If you'll excuse my language.

**中文**

哦，你不用担心什么事情，杜莎。我想从这该死的破地方逃出去，可这一路上的考验比我想象得还艰苦。抱歉，我说脏话了。

## `ZagreusHome_2609`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I am. And that stuff you heard about me isn't true.

**中文**

是啊，而且你说的那些关于我的事情，都太夸大了。

## `ZagreusHome_2610`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I would be more than that, if it pleased you. You're very wonderful, and... you light up this House. But I would never risk our friendship, so, if this is where we draw the boundary, please, just let me know.

**中文**

如果你愿意，不只做朋友也可以的。你真的很美妙，而且……你让这整座圣殿蓬荜生辉。但我不能拿我们的友谊来冒这个险，如果你觉得我们应该保持界限，请一定要让我知道。

## `ZagreusHome_2611`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dusa, I can't shake this feeling, so I wanted to apologize to you, because... I think when we first met, I just... I don't think I took it the right way. I never had a fan before! I wanted to live up to your idea of me.

**中文**

杜莎，这种感觉让我挥之不去，我想向你道个歉，因为……我觉得，我们第一次见面的时候，我……我觉得我的做法不太对。以前从来没人崇拜过我！我当时就想成为你心目中的那个样子。

## `ZagreusHome_2612`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

But you didn't even know me, yet. We didn't get to talking for what seemed like ages. You can be difficult to find! Since I can't fly up to the rafters like you can.

**中文**

但那个时候你和我并不熟，我们可能有很多年都没说过话。我没办法像你那样飞上屋顶，有时候根本都找不到你！

## `ZagreusHome_2613`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ah, thank you, Dusa.

**中文**

啊，谢谢你，杜莎。

## `ZagreusHome_2614`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, you got to try it, after all? What did you think, all it's cracked up to be?

**中文**

哦，到最后，你终于喝了吗？你觉得它味道怎么样，名副其实吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2615`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I wouldn't mind at all.

**中文**

我一点都不介意。

## `ZagreusHome_2616`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, Orpheus? I heard from Hermes, messenger and god of travel, and all that? It's odd I'm giving you his message in this case, but he says hey! Admires your musical ability, as well.

**中文**

哦，俄耳甫斯？我从赫尔墨斯，信使之神、旅行之神、还有别的什么神那里听到了消息。由我来给他传话确实很奇怪，不过他让我替他向你致意！他也很欣赏你的音乐才华。

## `ZagreusHome_2617`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm glad you took it so well...

**中文**

很高兴你有这么强的接受能力……

## `ZagreusHome_2618`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good thing that's settled, then.

**中文**

那太好了，这件事就解决了。

## `ZagreusHome_2619`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Orpheus. You know I'm very happy that things turned out well between you and Eurydice, but... looking back, I think I might have overstepped my bounds with you a bit, and wanted to say I'm sorry.

**中文**

嘿，俄耳甫斯。你和欧律狄刻破镜重圆了，我真高兴。不过……仔细想想的话，我觉得我可能太多管闲事了，我对此很抱歉。

## `ZagreusHome_2620`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, it's just... when I met her, and I found out the two of you were having problems, I... well, I just wanted to see you patch things up. But, it wasn't any of my business, and you never asked my help. Neither did she. I could have made things worse.

**中文**

这么说吧……我见到她，听说你们两个之间出了点问题的时候，我……我就想看看能不能想办法改变一下现状。不过这不是我该管的事情，你从来都没要我帮你这个忙，她也没有，我本可能会把事情搞砸也说不定。

## `ZagreusHome_2621`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well, thank you also, mate.

**中文**

嗯，我也得谢谢你，朋友。

## `ZagreusHome_2622`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Orpheus, you said that it was one of life's injustices that Eurydice doesn't get her fair share of the credit for the songs she wrote. Isn't there something we can do?

**中文**

俄耳甫斯，你之前说欧律狄刻创作了那么多歌曲，可她却没得到应有的赞誉，这恐怕太不公平了。你不觉得我们可以做点什么吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2623`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't know... tell them they're wrong? Politely, perhaps not? Or... tell them what's right. Get the word out about what's right? Can't we do that?

**中文**

我也说不好……告诉他们是他们错了？用比较客气的语气，不行吗？或者……告诉他们什么是对的，让外面的人明白什么是对的？这一点我们做不到吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2624`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Then, word will spread. I'm sure of it.

**中文**

这样的话，我敢肯定这个消息会传播开的。

## `ZagreusHome_2625`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Orpheus? I know Father appreciates your songs, to the extent he's capable of appreciating anything. Though, has Nyx ever responded to them? She's always right nearby yet you two don't seem to talk...

**中文**

俄耳甫斯？我知道父亲很喜欢你的歌声，喜欢到了无以复加的程度。不过，倪克斯给过你什么评价吗？她就在你身边，可你们两个似乎都没怎么说过话……

## `ZagreusHome_2626`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That she is.

**中文**

她确实是。

## `ZagreusHome_2627`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's everything... at last I see myself for what I am.

**中文**

就是这样了……至少我能看清自己是什么样的人。

## `ZagreusHome_2628`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's try this.

**中文**

试试这样吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2629`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Maybe this.

**中文**

或者这样吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2630`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Maybe the other.

**中文**

或许那样吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2631`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Maybe this way.

**中文**

或许这样才对。

## `ZagreusHome_2632`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Maybe like so.

**中文**

或许就这样吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2633`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Maybe like this?

**中文**

这样还行吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2634`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Other side.

**中文**

还是另一边吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2635`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This one then?

**中文**

这样呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2636`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How about like this.

**中文**

这样如何？

## `ZagreusHome_2637`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hmm...

**中文**

嗯嗯……

## `ZagreusHome_2638`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's make an adjustment over here again, Contractor.

**中文**

我们再来调整一下这里的样子吧，承包商。

## `ZagreusHome_2639`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'd like to see how that looks the other way again, please.

**中文**

我想看看如果换另一种做法会怎么样，拜托了。

## `ZagreusHome_2640`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We're going to reconfigure that bit once again, Contractor.

**中文**

我们又得重新装饰一下这个地方了，承包商。

## `ZagreusHome_2641`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I wanted to see how this looks again if that's all right.

**中文**

如果可以的话，我想再看看这样看起来如何。

## `ZagreusHome_2642`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How about we make an adjustment right over here if that's OK.

**中文**

如果可以的话，不如我们重新调整一下这个地方吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2643`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Guess what, Contractor? Changed my mind again.

**中文**

你猜怎么着，承包商？我又改主意了。

## `ZagreusHome_2644`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right if we restore that to the way it was before?

**中文**

不如我们恢复到以前的样子吧，怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_2645`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think I want to change things up again right over here.

**中文**

我觉得，我想改变一下这里的装修风格。

## `ZagreusHome_2646`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm thinking we should freshen things a bit right over here.

**中文**

我在想，或许我们应该给这个地方带来点新的变化。

## `ZagreusHome_2647`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Eh, let's see how it looks this other way, all right?

**中文**

呃，我们来看看那样调整结果如何吧？你说呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2648`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Aegis: I see you stand your ground against the serpent's flame!

**中文**

埃癸斯，我看到你傲然屹立在巨蛇的烈焰之中！

## `ZagreusHome_2649`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A future aspect... Beowulf is going to be tough...

**中文**

是来自未来的形态……贝奥武夫一定是个强壮的战士……

## `ZagreusHome_2650`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Grant me the aspect of Beowulf!

**中文**

赐给我贝奥武夫的形态吧！

## `ZagreusHome_2651`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Beowulf.

**中文**

贝奥武夫。

## `ZagreusHome_2652`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Beowulf...!

**中文**

贝奥武夫……！

## `ZagreusHome_2653`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Exagryph: I see your prideful fall down from the heavens to the flames!

**中文**

伊格瑞菲：我看到高傲的你从天堂跌落下来，落入了地狱的烈焰之中！

## `ZagreusHome_2654`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A hidden aspect... this Lucifer won't be needing it.

**中文**

是隐藏的形态……这个路西法肯定已经用不到它了。

## `ZagreusHome_2655`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Grant me the aspect of Lucifer!

**中文**

赐给我路西法的形态！

## `ZagreusHome_2656`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Lucifer.

**中文**

路西法。

## `ZagreusHome_2657`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Lucifer...!

**中文**

路西法……！

## `ZagreusHome_2658`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We know each other, Stygius.

**中文**

我们已经是老朋友了，斯第格思。

## `ZagreusHome_2659`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good to get to know you better, Coronacht.

**中文**

很高兴看到你变得更强了，科罗纳赫特。

## `ZagreusHome_2660`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Always a pleasure, Aegis.

**中文**

这是我的荣幸，埃癸斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2661`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's do this again sometime, Varatha.

**中文**

我们下次再并肩作战吧，瓦拉塔。

## `ZagreusHome_2662`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Appreciate your support, Exagryph.

**中文**

我很感谢你的支持，伊格瑞菲。

## `ZagreusHome_2663`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Glad we could do this, Malphon.

**中文**

真高兴我们成功了，玛尔封。

## `ZagreusHome_2664`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I see this aspect clearly now.

**中文**

我已经可以清楚地看到这个形态了。

## `ZagreusHome_2665`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Our bond is forged forever.

**中文**

我们的羁绊万古长存。

## `ZagreusHome_2666`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I see you truly now.

**中文**

我能真正地看清你了。

## `ZagreusHome_2667`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We are inseparable now.

**中文**

我们现在是形影不离了。

## `ZagreusHome_2668`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ungh... Mother... I have to get back there.

**中文**

呃……母亲……我必须得回到那里。

## `ZagreusHome_2669`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Unff, Father, you...

**中文**

呃，父亲，你……

## `ZagreusHome_2670`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Urgh... Father wanted her to leave, didn't he...

**中文**

呃……父亲想让她离开这里，是吗……

## `ZagreusHome_2671`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nghh... hold that thought, Mother...

**中文**

嗯……请三思，母亲……

## `ZagreusHome_2672`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Augh... Olympus complicating things...

**中文**

啊……奥林匹斯的破事……

## `ZagreusHome_2673`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ungh, I... need to fix this...

**中文**

呃，我……得把它弄好……

## `ZagreusHome_2674`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Grr, she... can't do this to me...

**中文**

呃，她……不能这样对我……

## `ZagreusHome_2675`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Guh... damn it all... again!

**中文**

啊……该死……又是这样！

## `ZagreusHome_2676`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Y... you are the Queen....

**中文**

你……你就是冥后……

## `ZagreusHome_2677`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Urgh, I'm sure Mother's cottage is perfectly fine...

**中文**

呃，我相信母亲的小屋应该挺不错的……

## `ZagreusHome_2678`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wow, Father, you called me by my name! What is this strange sensation that I feel? Perhaps it's this deference of which you speak?

**中文**

哇哦，父亲大人，您称呼了我的名字！我现在这种受宠若惊的奇怪感觉是怎么回事？说不定这就是您说的恭敬？

## `ZagreusHome_2679`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Good. Because I wouldn't want to be put on the spot about something like that. But, thank you anyway, Father. So, uh... what now? I've grown so used to ransacking through your domain, I think I'm really going to miss it.

**中文**

……很好，因为我不想因为那种事情难堪。不过不管怎样，谢谢你，父亲。所以，呃……现在该怎么办？我已经完全习惯把你的领地搅个底朝天了，我以后肯定会怀念这种感觉的。

## `ZagreusHome_2680`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...You shouldn't have lied to me. But, thank you anyway, Father. We have some work ahead of us I think. Speaking of which... what now? I've grown so used to ransacking through your domain, I think I'm really going to miss it.

**中文**

……你没必要骗我啊。不过不管怎样，谢谢你，父亲。我们还有些事要做呢。说到这个……现在该怎么办？我已经完全习惯把你的领地搅个底朝天了，我以后肯定会怀念这种感觉的。

## `ZagreusHome_2681`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Question, Father. Let's say I accept. When next I reach the surface... is it still going to be you waiting up there?

**中文**

我有一个问题，父亲大人。假如我同意了，那下次我到达地面世界的时候……还会是你在那儿等着我吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2682`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So I just battle to the surface and repeatedly kill my own father, then?

**中文**

所以我得一路杀到地面然后不断地杀了我的亲生父亲？

## `ZagreusHome_2683`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hahaha, well! I guess I'll see what I can do.

**中文**

哈哈哈，好吧！我会去看看我能做些什么。

## `ZagreusHome_2685`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...I understand, Father. I only wish I could have understood sooner. But, thank you anyway. So, uh... what now? I've grown so used to ransacking your domain, I think I'm really going to miss it.

**中文**

……我懂了，父亲。我要是能早点知道这些事情就好了。不过不管怎样，谢谢你。所以，呃……现在该怎么办？我已经习惯把你的领地搅个底朝天了，我以后肯定会怀念这种感觉的。

## `ZagreusHome_2686`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How has it been for you so far, Mother? Reacclimating to all this. You said before it was a struggle. Must be very strange, being back. Being in darkness all the time.

**中文**

最近还好吗，母亲？要再次适应这里的一切。你之前说过，在这里的生活很折磨人。如今你又回来了，永远生活在黑暗之中，这种感觉肯定很古怪吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2687`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's good to hear. I have to admit I was a little worried you'd be desperate to return to your cottage by now. This place can be a little much.

**中文**

这就好。不得不说，我刚刚还在担心你会不会特别想回到那座小屋里，这个地方可能有点太压抑了。

## `ZagreusHome_2688`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I did? But... I tried desperately to run away, myself.... Oh.

**中文**

是我吗？可……我自己也一直都想逃跑啊……哦。

## `ZagreusHome_2689`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I could look at this painting all day or night.

**中文**

这幅画我能看一整天，一整夜也行。

## `ZagreusHome_2690`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Can't believe Father's actually almost smiling.

**中文**

真不敢相信，父亲竟然笑了。

## `ZagreusHome_2691`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cerberus looks so happy! Really perked up since Mother's been back.

**中文**

刻耳柏洛斯看上去可真高兴！自从母亲回来后，它就一直很有精神。

## `ZagreusHome_2692`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Father let me hold Gigaros for once.

**中文**

父亲让我拿上了吉加罗斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2693`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Was pretty awkward posing for this though it turned out quite nice.

**中文**

这个姿势可真让人尴尬，不过效果还蛮不错的。

## `ZagreusHome_2694`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Father said I could admire the painting whenever I wanted, so I am!

**中文**

父亲说我可以随时瞻仰这幅画作，所以我就来了！

## `ZagreusHome_2695`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can't entirely get used to seeing us together like this.

**中文**

我到现在还没法习惯这样在一起的生活。

## `ZagreusHome_2696`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mother looks beautiful. And right at home.

**中文**

母亲美丽动人，而且她就在家里。

## `ZagreusHome_2697`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What a mess. What can we do? The gods of Olympus have all been very good to me, for the most part. I don't feel great about deceiving them. They keep trying to help me escape!

**中文**

这可真是一团糟啊，咱们该怎么办？奥林匹斯众神绝大多数时候都对我很好，欺骗他们让我很过意不去，他们可是一直都在帮助我逃走啊！

## `ZagreusHome_2698`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yes, Mother. So what do you intend to do, and how do I fit in?

**中文**

明白了，母亲。所以你打算怎么做呢？我又该怎么帮忙呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2699`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Mother, are you serious? So, we're just going to have a bash with them, and they'll just see you after all this time, and be fine with everything?

**中文**

母亲，你是认真的吗？所以，我们就这么和他们一起庆祝一番，让他们时隔这么久再见你一面，让他们觉得一切都能接受？

## `ZagreusHome_2700`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What are you going to say?

**中文**

你打算怎么说？

## `ZagreusHome_2701`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's... that's all true. As individual statements, at least.

**中文**

这……这倒是真的，至少从个人陈述的角度来看是真的。

## `ZagreusHome_2702`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Your mother won't be happy that you left.

**中文**

你的母亲肯定会对你的离开感到不满。

## `ZagreusHome_2703`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What? No, I never knew that.

**中文**

什么？不，我从来没听说过这种事情。

## `ZagreusHome_2704`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How soon is this family gathering you have in mind going to take place?

**中文**

你策划的这场家族盛宴，要多久之后举行呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2705`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I know what you mean. I'll let you know when it's done.

**中文**

我知道你的意思，等做完了我会让你知道的。

## `ZagreusHome_2706`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Not just amenable, Mother. Positively intrigued. I can't believe you scrambled the invitations in such a way that, only with the lot of them combined will they get all of the details!

**中文**

不仅仅是喜欢，母亲，他们非常感兴趣。我真不敢相信，你居然用这么巧妙的办法发出了请柬。只有所有人把请柬放在一起，才能知道全部信息！

## `ZagreusHome_2707`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You got it, Mother.

**中文**

没问题，母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_2708`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Mother, this family gathering you're planning... are all our relatives welcome? Because I was wondering if I could extend the invitation to a distant ancestor... you know... Chaos.

**中文**

母亲，你策划的这场盛宴……是所有的家人都欢迎吗？我在想，我要不要邀请某位血缘关系较远的先祖……你懂的……卡俄斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2709`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thanks. I doubt Chaos would be able to attend, though would still appreciate the thought.

**中文**

谢谢，我不知道卡俄斯能不能来，不过我还是很喜欢这个初衷。

## `ZagreusHome_2710`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's done, Mother! Father. The invitations. Personally sent to everyone with whom I've been in contact. So, what do we do now?

**中文**

邀请好了，母亲！父亲。我已经把所有的请柬都私下里送给每个遇到过的人了。所以，咱们现在该做什么？

## `ZagreusHome_2711`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

All right.

**中文**

好啊。

## `ZagreusHome_2712`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll say!

**中文**

是啊！

## `ZagreusHome_2713`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Greetings, Father. So! I can just... head on out there again whenever I want? Don't need to stick to any rigid schedule?

**中文**

你好啊，父亲，所以说！我可以……什么时候想出去就出去？不用遵守什么作息时间表吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2714`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

And the Olympians are going to keep helping me, thinking I'm trying to get away from you, to them. We can't keep this up with them forever, you know.

**中文**

而奥林匹斯众神依然会帮助我，他们以为我打算从你手上逃脱，逃到他们那里。你也知道，我们不可能把他们永远蒙在鼓里。

## `ZagreusHome_2715`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, sir, I've revealed the hidden aspect of the Shield of Chaos... thanks to Chaos, themself. Apparently someday it'll protect an armored warrior known as Beowulf from all sorts of dangers... even a dragon! Mortals don't stand much of a chance against those, do they?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯先生，我发现了混沌之盾的秘密……这多亏了卡俄斯本人的指点。以后会有个叫贝奥武夫的战士身披重铠，而它将会保护他免受各种各样的危险……甚至抵挡巨龙的攻击！凡人恐怕不会是龙的对手，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2716`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I doubt he would be any match for you.

**中文**

我很怀疑他到底是不是你的对手。

## `ZagreusHome_2717`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, I discovered the hidden aspect of the Adamant Rail, after Lord Zeus himself told me the waking-phrase. It's from a winged fellow by the name of Lucifer... apparently had a bit of a falling out with his lord father. Wonder how that worked out for him.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯先生，我发现了狮鹫弩炮的秘密，是宙斯大人亲口告诉我的。这个弩炮以前的主人，是个长着翅膀的家伙，叫路西法……看起来，他和他的天主父亲也闹了矛盾，不知道他后来怎么样了。

## `ZagreusHome_2718`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll take it as a warning. Even the Rail has its limits.

**中文**

我就当做个警告吧，就算是弩炮，也是有极限的。

## `ZagreusHome_2719`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, what happened between Nyx and Dusa? Dusa's really gone?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，倪克斯和杜莎之间到底发生了什么？杜莎真的走了吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2720`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Propensity to overwork herself... you really think that's what this was about? I can't shake the feeling it's my fault. Nyx didn't want her talking to me, as though she's beneath me or something! Ugh, what am I going to do.

**中文**

干活实在是过于卖力了……你真的觉得她是因为这个才被撵走的吗？我一直觉得这都是我的错。倪克斯不希望她和我说话，就好像她和我相比是个下等人一样！啊，我到底该怎么办？

## `ZagreusHome_2721`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh, I let her know.

**中文**

哦，我会告诉她的。

## `ZagreusHome_2722`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll talk to her.

**中文**

我要和她交谈。

## `ZagreusHome_2723`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Did you see, sir? Dusa's returned! Nyx must have reconsidered her decision.

**中文**

你看到了吗，先生？杜莎回来了！倪克斯肯定是重新考虑过了。

## `ZagreusHome_2724`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm just glad she's back.

**中文**

我很高兴她回来了。

## `ZagreusHome_2725`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, I meant to ask, did you know that Coronacht the Heart-Seeker once belonged to the centaur, Chiron? Because one of its aspects is his own. He didn't have it back when you trained with him, did he...?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，有件事想问一下。你知道索心弓科罗纳赫特曾是半人马喀戎的东西吗？因为其中一个形态是属于他的。你和他一起训练的时候，这把弓就已经不在他手里了，是吗……？

## `ZagreusHome_2726`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I've had the opportunity to use your aspect of Varatha, sir. So, you once wielded it yourself? It... fills me with a hunger for battle even more so than usual. A bit of your fighting spirit, perhaps.

**中文**

我终于有机会能使用你的瓦拉塔了，先生。所以，这把武器是你以前用过的吗？它……让我心里充满了对战斗的渴望，比平时要更加强烈。我猜，这大概就是你的斗志吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2727`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can't imagine you weren't always a good listener, Achilles.

**中文**

你以前不怎么擅长倾听，我真是难以想象那样的情形，阿喀琉斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2728`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, did you ever use to pray to the gods? Make offerings to them and such, back when you were alive?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，你还活着的时候，向众神祈祷过吗？比如给他们献上祭品什么的？

## `ZagreusHome_2729`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They're helping, definitely, sir.

**中文**

当然了，他们帮上大忙了，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_2730`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That Chthonic Companion you gave me, sir... have to say it's been amazing to see you and Patroclus in action. I thought you said your fighting days were over?

**中文**

关于你送给我的冥府伴偶，先生……我不得不说，看到你和普特洛克勒斯作战的英姿，真是太美妙了。你不是说过，你早就不想再打仗了吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2731`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Most definitely!

**中文**

我很确定！

## `ZagreusHome_2732`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Only you, Father. Good thing I've inherited some of your Fate-given authority, being your son and all. Otherwise some of your most loyal subjects would be bound by exploitive terms for eternity.

**中文**

是你的错觉而已，父亲。事实上，我能成为你的儿子，拥有这一切，这就是命运赐予我的权利。如果我不这么做的话，你那些最忠诚的仆人们永远都会受到那些剥削条款的束缚。

## `ZagreusHome_2733`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You could find a replacement? But it's a pointless question anyway, because he's here! He isn't leaving. He might even do better at his job, now that he has increased ability to step away from it.

**中文**

找个人替代他不就完了？而且这个问题毫无意义，他人明明就在这里！他是不会走的。如今他的力量变强了，足以挣脱束缚了，说不定他干活会干得更好呢。

## `ZagreusHome_2734`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not if those responsibilities matter.

**中文**

如果那些责任很重要的话，就是另一回事了。

## `ZagreusHome_2735`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, Father! I must say ransacking your domain beats having to toil endlessly in the administrative chamber. Far less painful, really.

**中文**

我不得不说，父亲大人！洗劫你的领地远远胜过待在行政办公室里无休止地工作。爽太多了，真的。

## `ZagreusHome_2736`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yes, please, Father, give me back my old, wretched job, won't you?

**中文**

是啊，拜托你了，父亲。让我回去干以前那份糟心的活吧，好不好？

## `ZagreusHome_2737`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How much is the reimbursement fee, anyway?

**中文**

那我到底要赔多少钱呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2738`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Maybe a little of each? Or maybe you ought not second-guess my every motive and decision, constantly, searching for some sinister agenda that has yet to cross my mind?

**中文**

或许两者都有吧？或许，你不应该总是在揣测我做每件事的动机，妄想着那些我根本就没想过的邪恶念头，你说呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2739`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Great. Next time, you can.

**中文**

很好，下一次你肯定做得到。

## `ZagreusHome_2740`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's have a bat's-eye view.

**中文**

让我看看蝙蝠眼中的世界吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2741`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What do you see, bat friends?

**中文**

你们看到什么了，蝙蝠朋友们？

## `ZagreusHome_2742`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You're free, bats!

**中文**

你们自由了，蝙蝠们！

## `ZagreusHome_2743`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You're free, bats! Temporarily.

**中文**

你们自由了，蝙蝠们！暂时自由了。

## `ZagreusHome_2744`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's have a look at all this.

**中文**

让我来好好看看吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2745`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How's this place looking?

**中文**

这地方看起来怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_2746`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The House of Hades...

**中文**

冥王圣殿……

## `ZagreusHome_2747`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Go, bats, enjoy yourselves!

**中文**

去吧，蝙蝠们，好好玩耍去吧！

## `ZagreusHome_2748`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mm, damn it, Charon...

**中文**

呃呃，该死，卡戎……

## `ZagreusHome_2749`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Augh, Charon, I am going to pay you back.

**中文**

啊啊，卡戎，我一定会讨回这笔债的。

## `ZagreusHome_2750`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nrgh, how'd he get so strong?

**中文**

呃啊，他怎么会这么强壮？

## `ZagreusHome_2751`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Eugh, Charon wasn't too agreeable back there.

**中文**

呃啊，卡戎不怎么通情达理啊。

## `ZagreusHome_2752`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mph, he just lost himself a customer.

**中文**

啧，我觉得他要失去一位顾客了。

## `ZagreusHome_2753`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Augh, that's why it's called a Doomstone.

**中文**

呃啊，所以它才会叫末日巨岩吗。

## `ZagreusHome_2754`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oof, damn giant evil crystal.

**中文**

呃啊，那该死的大水晶。

## `ZagreusHome_2755`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Urgh, that blasted Doomstone.

**中文**

呃啊，那该死的末日巨岩。

## `ZagreusHome_2756`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nng, that Sneak is tough...

**中文**

呃啊，伏击者真是难对付……

## `ZagreusHome_2757`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Augh, my back...

**中文**

呃啊，我的后背啊……

## `ZagreusHome_2758`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Argh, I hate that Sneak...

**中文**

呃啊，我恨死那个伏击者了……

## `ZagreusHome_2759`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Eugh, guess Witches don't like company.

**中文**

呃，我猜女巫们不喜欢有人作伴。

## `ZagreusHome_2760`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Augh, don't mess with Witches, then, I guess...

**中文**

呃啊，我猜，我不应该惹火那群女巫才对……

## `ZagreusHome_2761`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Kkh, blasted Witches got me...

**中文**

呃啊，那些该死的女巫把我干掉了……

## `ZagreusHome_2765`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Drink up, Antos!

**中文**

一口喝光，安托斯！

## `ZagreusHome_2766`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Antos, you're all set!

**中文**

安托斯，你准备好了！

## `ZagreusHome_2767`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanatos can float rather than sit, but who knows, it's a change of pace.

**中文**

塔纳托斯可以飘在空中，不需要坐着，不过万一他想坐着试试呢。

## `ZagreusHome_2768`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should soften up that corridor down there.

**中文**

我应该把那条走廊变得怡人一些才对。

## `ZagreusHome_2769`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here, Thanatos, why don't you have yourself this little table?

**中文**

快看，塔纳托斯，不如把这张小桌子送给你吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2770`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Can get a little brisk in here, so this should be exactly what we need.

**中文**

这里应该更欢快一些才对，而这正是我们需要的东西。

## `ZagreusHome_2771`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Can't say no to more seating options around here, just in case.

**中文**

如果能在这里多加一些座位的话，那自然是要加的，以防万一嘛。

## `ZagreusHome_2772`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Wonder how they got Meg to pose with the other two. They look downright civil!

**中文**

不知道他们是怎么让墨纪和另外两个人合影的，她们看起来温文尔雅！

## `ZagreusHome_2773`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nyx deserves a place of honor around here. How about... right over there on that wall?

**中文**

倪克斯应该有个可以受人崇敬的地方才是。不如……就在那边的墙上吧，怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_2774`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oof, sorry Hypnos.

**中文**

哦哦，抱歉，修普诺斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2775`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Tough break.

**中文**

运气真差。

## `ZagreusHome_2776`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey, he did it.

**中文**

嘿，他做到了。

## `ZagreusHome_2777`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good work, Hypnos!

**中文**

干得漂亮，修普诺斯！

## `ZagreusHome_2778`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Makes sense.

**中文**

有道理。

## `ZagreusHome_2779`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That I will.

**中文**

我会的。

## `ZagreusHome_2780`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That it does.

**中文**

没问题。

## `ZagreusHome_2781`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanks for the tip.

**中文**

谢谢你的建议。

## `ZagreusHome_2782`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Um, OK.

**中文**

呃，没问题。

## `ZagreusHome_2783`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll get right on that.

**中文**

交给我就好了。

## `ZagreusHome_2784`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I have.

**中文**

可以。

## `ZagreusHome_2785`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You try it.

**中文**

你试试吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2786`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You calling me squishy?

**中文**

你说我黏糊糊的？

## `ZagreusHome_2787`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, uh, everything all right, there, Hypnos?

**中文**

嘿，呃，一切都还好吧，修普诺斯？

## `ZagreusHome_2788`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm sure he wasn't trying to hurt your feelings. Wait, what am I saying, he's always been harsh with you, hasn't he?

**中文**

我敢肯定，他不是有意想让你难过的。等等，我在瞎说什么呢，他一直都对你很严苛，不是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2789`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll talk to him.

**中文**

我去和他谈谈吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2790`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Come on, Hypnos, I didn't have anything to do with that. You seem like you've been working extra hard, and taking some of the feedback you've been getting to heart. I think Thanatos really appreciates that sort of thing. Who doesn't?

**中文**

你说笑了，修普诺斯，这件事情和我一点关系都没有。你似乎工作得特别卖力，也因此得到了你想要的答复。我觉得塔纳托斯真的很吃这一套，又有谁不吃呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2791`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Aw, thanks Hypnos.

**中文**

啊，多谢了，修普诺斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2792`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hypnos, wanted to say you have really helped the thought of my coming back here time after time, painful death by death, not be inherently abhorrent. Your greetings really help! So I wanted you to have this.

**中文**

修普诺斯，我只想说，我一次一次痛苦地死亡，一次一次回到这里。虽然我不一定讨厌这种安排，但你帮了我不少忙，你的问候真的起了不少作用！所以，我希望你能收下这个。

## `ZagreusHome_2793`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I notice that you haven't yet returned the bottle to me, which I'm going to take as an indication that you're going to hang onto it.

**中文**

话虽这么说，我发现你并没有把那瓶酒还给我，那我就当你打算收下它了。

## `ZagreusHome_2794`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

One more forbidden exchange, Hypnos. Sometimes when I'm out there and about to die, I catch myself thinking... well, at least Hypnos will share one of his deep insights about why it is I utterly failed. So here! For all your generous advice.

**中文**

又到私下交易的时间了，修普诺斯。有的时候，当我奄奄一息的时候， 我不禁会想……至少修普诺斯会告诉我失败的原因。所以，收下这份礼物吧！感谢你慷慨的建议。

## `ZagreusHome_2795`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You what, Hypnos? You what?

**中文**

你怎么了，修普诺斯？你想说什么？

## `ZagreusHome_2796`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll have to give that a shot.

**中文**

我得试试才行。

## `ZagreusHome_2797`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm sure whatever it is he said is completely true, yes, Hypnos.

**中文**

虽然不知道他是怎么说的，但我敢说都是真的。没错，修普诺斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2798`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well that's rather personal, Skelly, though it sounds like you've some thoughts on the subject you'd like to share?

**中文**

那就涉及私人问题了，骨头。不过听起来，你好像想说说你自己对这件事情的看法，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2799`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How am I supposed to keep track of which weapons I've earned these with, then?

**中文**

那我要怎么知道，我用过哪些武器得到过这种远古之血？

## `ZagreusHome_2800`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Skelly, you mentioned the ancient bit of blood I found in Tartarus. Know what I'm supposed to do with this stuff?

**中文**

骨头，你之前提过我在塔耳塔罗斯找到的远古之血，你知道我应该用这玩意做什么吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2801`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Unlock those first, then, huh.

**中文**

先把它们解锁，然后，嗯。

## `ZagreusHome_2802`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, Skelly, you worried something bad is going to happen if I express a bit of generosity from time to time through our somewhat-illicit gift-giving custom around here?

**中文**

怎么了，骨头？你在担心，如果我总是时不时地送给别人点礼物，时不时破坏一下这里的规矩，会不会发生什么不好的事情，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2803`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Maybe I just want to show thanks to my friends for its own sake!

**中文**

或许我只是单纯地想感谢一下我的朋友而已！

## `ZagreusHome_2804`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...I thought you'd never ask. But I'm glad you did.

**中文**

……我还以为你不打算问了呢，不过我很高兴你问了。

## `ZagreusHome_2805`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, what is it you're always staring at out there, anyway, Than? I don't see anything.

**中文**

嘿，你一直在盯着什么看呢，塔纳？我什么都没看到。

## `ZagreusHome_2806`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So you're locked in ruthless battle with a river, searching it for any signs of weakness, that sort of thing? That sounds stressful.

**中文**

所以说，你是和这条河杠上了吗？打算寻找它的弱点之类的吗？这听起来太让人有压力了。

## `ZagreusHome_2807`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Go get them.

**中文**

去追它们吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2808`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm sure his skill did most of the work.

**中文**

我敢肯定，他完成了绝大部分的工作。

## `ZagreusHome_2809`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Maybe this knitted effigy of some sort of surface creature will keep Cerberus occupied.

**中文**

或许某种地表生物为原型的编制玩具，能让刻耳柏洛斯一直有事可做吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2810`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should make the entryway into the lounge a bit more welcoming.

**中文**

应该把休息室的入口变得更热情才对。

## `ZagreusHome_2811`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This should instill a sense of fear in anyone approaching my bedchambers.

**中文**

要是有人敢接近我的卧室，这东西应该会然他们心生畏惧。

## `ZagreusHome_2812`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You've done so much for us, House Contractor. Why don't you do a little something for yourself?

**中文**

你已经为我们做了很多事情了，圣殿承包商，不如也为你自己做点小小的事情吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2813`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Perhaps my chambers will look even better with these blades ominously displayed on either side.

**中文**

或许有了这些不吉利的刀剑装饰，我的卧室看起来会更漂亮吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2814`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You know I've always had a thing for double-bladed axes?

**中文**

你知道吗？我一直都很喜欢双刃大斧。

## `ZagreusHome_2815`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nothing like a mosaic depicting my arch-enemy and the Bull of Minos to spur me on.

**中文**

是我的宿敌与米诺斯的公牛的马赛克挂画，没什么比这幅画更能激励我了。

## `ZagreusHome_2816`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I would like only the brightest-burning, most ornate fireplace available, thanks.

**中文**

我只想要个火烧得最旺、装饰最豪华的壁炉，谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_2817`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This elegant countenance should look perfect above a roaring inferno in the hall back there.

**中文**

如果把这个优雅的装饰品挂在走廊那边熊熊燃烧的地狱烈焰上面，看起来应该再合适不过了。

## `ZagreusHome_2818`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You look a bit uncomfortable, Father. Here, why don't you have a seat?

**中文**

你看起来不怎么舒服啊，父亲，干嘛不找张椅子坐着呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2819`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

If Achilles is going to stand around eternally, it might as well take place on something soft.

**中文**

如果阿喀琉斯真打算一直站着，那也应该找个柔软、舒服的地方站着。

## `ZagreusHome_2820`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think those pedestals over there are just a bit too modest for my tastes.

**中文**

我觉得那边的基座有点太正式了，不太符合我的审美。

## `ZagreusHome_2821`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Perhaps Death Incarnate would like to take a load off every now and then.

**中文**

或许就算是死亡的化身，也希望时不时能休息一下。

## `ZagreusHome_2822`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh I think Thanatos would love this one. Or totally ignore it?

**中文**

哦，我觉得塔纳托斯应该会很喜欢，或许他根本就不会注意到？

## `ZagreusHome_2823`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Where else is Thanatos going to stash loose odds and ends?

**中文**

塔纳托斯还能把零零散散的东西藏在哪里呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2824`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's lighten up that southwest hall a bit.

**中文**

咱们来装点一下西南侧的大厅吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2825`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think we can spruce up the centerpiece of the great hall just a bit.

**中文**

我觉得，我们可以稍微装饰一下这座大厅的核心部分。

## `ZagreusHome_2826`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A painting of the finest spot in all the Underworld seems suitable to have in here.

**中文**

整个冥界最怡人的地方，这幅画挂在这里正合适。

## `ZagreusHome_2827`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Think this is going to be a better look for the lounge seating.

**中文**

这样一来，休息室里的椅子就会更漂亮了。

## `ZagreusHome_2828`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Surely the Three Fates will favor me more once we have this lovely statue in their honor?

**中文**

如果摆上这尊可爱的雕像，来纪念命运三女神的话，她们应该也会对我更青睐有加吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2829`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think the lounge will be better off for these tables here.

**中文**

我觉得有了这些桌子之后，休息室应该会变得更舒服。

## `ZagreusHome_2830`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Head Chef certainly is going to need a place to safely care for all the fish I've been hauling in.

**中文**

我给大厨抓来了这么多鱼，他肯定需要个地方好好照顾它们。

## `ZagreusHome_2831`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A fine display of dead meats seems very much in theme, I have to say!

**中文**

我不得不说，这个以肉类为主题的精美艺术品，和这里的风格还真搭！

## `ZagreusHome_2832`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Now this is just the sort of outlandish purchase that makes me briefly feel slightly better about myself.

**中文**

像这种充满奇异风格的东西，可以让我对自己的感觉稍微变好那么一点点。

## `ZagreusHome_2833`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My mate Lord Dionysus can inspire me to keep a bright and healthy attitude toward life.

**中文**

我的好朋友迪奥尼索司大人总是能激励我，让我对生活保持乐观、健康的态度。

## `ZagreusHome_2834`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Think I've outgrown my other bed, not that there's been a lot of time for sleep of late.

**中文**

我的个子已经很高了，原来的床睡不下了。不过，我最近也没什么时间睡觉。

## `ZagreusHome_2835`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

These ought to add a little sparkle each time I revive from the Pool of Styx.

**中文**

每次我从冥河血池中爬出来的时候，这些东西应该都能活跃一下气氛。

## `ZagreusHome_2836`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You were a great hero, Achilles, and guess what? You still are! And always will be if you ask me. You deserve so much better than just this. I wish there was more that I could do.

**中文**

你生前是个伟大的英雄，阿喀琉斯，而且你知道吗？你现在也是！要我说的话，你以后也会一样伟大。你应得的礼遇远远不止如此，我要是能多做点什么就好了。

## `ZagreusHome_2837`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Other way around.

**中文**

反过来吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2838`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, Father, while I'm sure you don't appreciate all the distractions as of late, your ceaseless toil has had some noticeable effect. Is it my imagination, or are the ongoing Underworld renovations almost complete?

**中文**

老实说，父亲。最近发生了很多让你分心的事情，我知道你肯定不喜欢，但你不停的鞭策确实带来了明显的效果。是我的错觉吗？还是说，冥界的翻修工作马上就要完成了？

## `ZagreusHome_2839`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I wasn't saying it wasn't important, I was just saying maybe there's finally an end to it in sight. What are you going to do with all your spare time, then?

**中文**

我倒不是说它不重要，我只是说，活终于算是要干完了。等你干完活，有空闲的时间的话，你打算干什么？

## `ZagreusHome_2840`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, I was borrowing, OK? Not shoplifting. Why didn't anybody ever tell me Charon was such a monster? I thought the boating mercantile thing made for a peaceful life.

**中文**

呃，我当时是借用，不是扒窃，好吧？为什么从来没人跟我说过卡戎原来是这么个狠角色？我还以为天天摇船的人脾气肯定很好呢。

## `ZagreusHome_2841`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No!

**中文**

不！

## `ZagreusHome_2842`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, boy. I hope all the construction and renovation around here hasn't been too inconvenient for you, lately. But hey, the lounge is just about ready for you to utterly destroy again!

**中文**

嘿，乖狗狗，最近这里到处是翻修工作和施工，希望这没给你带来太多麻烦。不过，你看，休息室马上就要完工了，到时候你就可以再大肆破坏一通了！

## `ZagreusHome_2843`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's enjoy it while it lasts.

**中文**

趁这个机会好好享受一下吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2844`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I hope I haven't been inconveniencing you too much with all the visits to the Temple lately, boy. Say, how do you get back? Charon giving you a lift?

**中文**

我最近总是往圣殿这边跑，希望没给你带来太多麻烦，乖狗狗。你最近还好吗？卡戎有没有载你一程？

## `ZagreusHome_2845`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hahaha, just kidding, boy.

**中文**

哈哈哈，我在开玩笑呢，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusHome_2846`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, after dying in some wretched manner, giving pets to my good boy sure helps me get back on my feet. Thanks for being here for me.

**中文**

说真的，以某种凄惨的方式丧命之后，好好拍拍我的好狗狗确实有助于让我重新振作起来。谢谢你在这里陪着我。

## `ZagreusHome_2847`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Speaking of which...!

**中文**

说到这个……！

## `ZagreusHome_2848`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You like this stuff, don't you, boy? Well, good, because I like sneaking it over to you!

**中文**

你很喜欢这东西，是吧，我的好狗狗？那就好，因为这是特意给你偷偷拿过来的！

## `ZagreusHome_2849`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

This Nectar not fancy enough for you, boy, is that it? You've developed quite the delicate palate at your distinguished age!

**中文**

你觉得这瓶蜜露的味道不够劲儿，是吧，我的好狗狗？你活了这么久，口味也变得很不错了嘛！

## `ZagreusHome_2850`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's right, boy... Ambrosia, just for you. You're going to love it. Pretty much every creature, living or dead, does!

**中文**

没错，我的好狗狗……这仙馔密酒是特地给你的，你肯定会喜欢。几乎所有人都喜欢仙馔密酒，无论是活人还是死人！

## `ZagreusHome_2851`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hits the spot, doesn't it, boy? I know you're caught up in the middle of all this and having to weigh your loyalties more than you'd like. I'm sorry we're putting you through this.

**中文**

你很喜欢吧，我的乖狗狗？我知道，你并不情愿被卷入这件事情之中，总是不得不衡量自己到底应该听谁的吩咐。把你置于这样的窘境之中，我很抱歉。

## `ZagreusHome_2852`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Cerberus, not to get all sentimental on you, but... oh, who am I kidding, look... you've been with me my entire life! I know you're here at home partly because of me, aren't you?

**中文**

刻耳柏洛斯，我倒不是想多愁善感，不过……哦，我这是在说什么傻话呢。你看，你一直都和我在一起生活！我知道，你会待在家里，或多或少是因为我的关系，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2853`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're the best boy in the whole world. Even if heads Beta and Gamma don't much care for pets, do they? I've got more than enough lined up for you, though. Count on it.

**中文**

你真是整个世界最乖的乖狗狗，不过你的第二个头和第三个头不怎么喜欢被摸，是吧？不过，我给你准备了相当丰富的礼物，拭目以待吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2854`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's like we share a bond, man!

**中文**

看起来我们是有羁绊的，伙计！

## `ZagreusHome_2855`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Already bought it, no need to sell me on it, old man.

**中文**

我已经买过了，没必要再卖给我了，老家伙。

## `ZagreusHome_2856`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's really quite cozy.

**中文**

确实很舒服。

## `ZagreusHome_2857`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Looks like it's 7:48...

**中文**

看起来像是七点四十八分……

## `ZagreusHome_2858`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ooh look at all those.

**中文**

哦哦，快看看那些啊。

## `ZagreusHome_2859`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You take good care of them, Head Chef!

**中文**

你得好好照顾它们，大厨！

## `ZagreusHome_2860`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hello, fishies!

**中文**

你们好啊，小鱼们！

## `ZagreusHome_2861`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It is I, fishies!

**中文**

是我来了，小鱼们！

## `ZagreusHome_2862`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Look at them go...

**中文**

看看它们要去哪……

## `ZagreusHome_2863`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fishies looking right at home.

**中文**

这些小鱼就像是在家里一样。

## `ZagreusHome_2864`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Keep an eye on that Chef for me, fishies.

**中文**

记得帮我留意一下大厨，小鱼们。

## `ZagreusHome_2865`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hail, fishies.

**中文**

你们好，小鱼们。

## `ZagreusHome_2866`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How do you do, fishies?

**中文**

你们还好吗，小鱼们。

## `ZagreusHome_2867`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They're really having a good time.

**中文**

它们游得确实很开心。

## `ZagreusHome_2868`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That a Knucklehead in there?

**中文**

那是条拳头鱼吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2869`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hello, Hellfish!

**中文**

你好啊，地狱鱼！

## `ZagreusHome_2870`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Scyllascion swimming around.

**中文**

蛟龙鱼在游来游去。

## `ZagreusHome_2871`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

## `ZagreusHome_2872`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Chrustacean hanging in there I see.

我看到盔虾在游来游去。

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That Charp seems to be doing well.

那条鲤鱼看起来过得挺安逸的。

## `ZagreusHome_2874`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ooh look at the Seamare.

**中文**

哦哦，快看那只异形海马。

## `ZagreusHome_2875`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nice place to be a Gupp.

**中文**

这只加普虫找了个不错的地方。

## `ZagreusHome_2876`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hope that Scuffer likes the scenery.

**中文**

希望那条刺豚鱼喜欢这里的景色。

## `ZagreusHome_2877`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Look at that Stonewhal...!

**中文**

快看那条石背鱼……！

## `ZagreusHome_2878`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That Chlam's doing fine in there.

**中文**

那只刺甲贝过得很舒坦。

## `ZagreusHome_2879`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That Mati seems to be adapting.

**中文**

那条马蒂鱼似乎已经开始适应这里了。

## `ZagreusHome_2880`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Pretty colors on that Projelly.

**中文**

那只异形水母的颜色真漂亮。

## `ZagreusHome_2881`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Look at that Voidskate...

**中文**

快看那条双翅鳐……

## `ZagreusHome_2882`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Some mortals don't believe in ghosts, but I believe in you!

**中文**

有些凡人不相信什么鬼怪，但我相信你！

## `ZagreusHome_2883`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sleep is for the living, am I right?

**中文**

只有活着的人才需要睡觉，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2884`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mistakes are proof that you're doing your best!

**中文**

你犯下的错误，都是你努力过的证明！

## `ZagreusHome_2885`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Remember: Hang in there!

**中文**

记住，要坚持下去！

## `ZagreusHome_2886`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This poster's awful.

**中文**

这张海报实在是太丑了。

## `ZagreusHome_2887`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I don't know how you Shades handle it honestly.

**中文**

老实说，我不知道你们幽灵们是怎么处理这件事的。

## `ZagreusHome_2888`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You shades hang in there.

**中文**

幽灵们，坚持下去。

## `ZagreusHome_2889`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hanging in there still?

**中文**

你们还在努力吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2890`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Everybody hanging in there, I take it?

**中文**

大家都在努力，是这样吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2891`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Don't think I'm welcome in there.

**中文**

我觉得那里的人恐怕不会欢迎我。

## `ZagreusHome_2892`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Father's chambers, always locked.

**中文**

是父亲的房间，总是锁着的。

**EN**

Looks like Meg's the featured Houseservant right now.

Meg's in the spotlight.

**EN**

Good for Megaera I guess.

我猜对墨纪拉来说，是个好消息吧。

**EN**

Megaera's always up there...

## `ZagreusHome_2897`

Well done, then, Meg...

Thanatos, the featured Houseservant...

塔纳托斯成了焦点人物。

## `ZagreusHome_2900`

Good for Thanatos.

对塔纳托斯来说是件好事。

Thanatos does it again.

塔纳托斯又上榜了。

## `ZagreusHome_2902`

**EN**

Fine job, Thanatos.

干得漂亮，塔纳托斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2903`

**EN**

Cerberus, featured Houseservant!

刻耳柏洛斯是最受欢迎的圣殿仆从！

## `ZagreusHome_2904`

**EN**

Excellent work, Cerberus.

**中文**

干得漂亮，刻耳柏洛斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2905`

**EN**

Look who's officially the best boy.

**中文**

看看谁才是最好的狗狗。

## `ZagreusHome_2906`

**EN**

Cerberus in the spotlight.

刻耳柏洛斯成了大家的焦点。

## `ZagreusHome_2907`

**EN**

阿喀琉斯榜上有名了！

## `ZagreusHome_2908`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Achilles in the spotlight!

**中文**

阿喀琉斯成了焦点人物！

## `ZagreusHome_2909`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The featured Houseservant, Achilles!

**中文**

最受欢迎的圣殿仆从是阿喀琉斯！

## `ZagreusHome_2910`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good for Achilles.

**中文**

对阿喀琉斯来说是个好消息。

## `ZagreusHome_2911`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Orpheus, our featured Houseservant!

**中文**

俄耳甫斯是我们最受欢迎的圣殿仆从！

## `ZagreusHome_2912`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Orpheus made the board!

**中文**

俄耳甫斯上榜了！

## `ZagreusHome_2913`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nice work, Orpheus!

**中文**

干得漂亮，俄耳甫斯！

## `ZagreusHome_2914`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Orpheus got on the board!

**中文**

俄耳甫斯榜上有名了！

## `ZagreusHome_2915`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The House Contractor, up in lights!

**中文**

圣殿承包商站在了聚光灯下！

## `ZagreusHome_2916`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

House Contractor, you did it!

**中文**

你做到了，圣殿承包商！

## `ZagreusHome_2917`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fine work from the House Contractor.

**中文**

干得好，圣殿承包商。

## `ZagreusHome_2918`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We're proud of you, House Contractor.

**中文**

我们真为你骄傲，圣殿承包商。

## `ZagreusHome_2919`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey, nice one, Wretched Broker!

**中文**

嘿，干得不错嘛，残魂商人！

## `ZagreusHome_2920`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Wretched Broker did it!

**中文**

残魂商人做到了！

## `ZagreusHome_2921`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fine work, Wretched Broker.

**中文**

干得漂亮，残魂商人。

## `ZagreusHome_2922`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Congratulations, Wretched Broker.

**中文**

恭喜你了，残魂商人。

## `ZagreusHome_2923`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Head Chef pulled it off!

**中文**

大厨拔得头筹了！

## `ZagreusHome_2924`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Head Chef is on the board!

**中文**

大厨榜上有名了！

## `ZagreusHome_2925`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good service from the Head Chef.

**中文**

大厨的工作一直很出色。

## `ZagreusHome_2926`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey, not bad, Head Chef!

**中文**
