嘿，还不赖嘛，大厨！

## `ZagreusHome_2927`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey Dusa's featured Houseservant!

**中文**

嘿，杜莎成了最受欢迎的圣殿仆从！

## `ZagreusHome_2928`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Dusa made the board!

**中文**

杜莎上榜了！

## `ZagreusHome_2929`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey, it's Dusa again!

**中文**

嘿，杜莎又上榜了！

## `ZagreusHome_2930`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Dusa, you did it!

**中文**

杜莎，你成功了！

## `ZagreusHome_2931`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I must be dreaming. Hypnos?!

**中文**

我怕不是在做梦吧，是修普诺斯？！

## `ZagreusHome_2932`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Wow, Hypnos made the board!

**中文**

哇哦，修普诺斯上榜了！

## `ZagreusHome_2933`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nice work, Hypnos.

**中文**

干得漂亮，修普诺斯。

## `ZagreusHome_2934`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hypnos, making us proud.

**中文**

修普诺斯，我们为你感到骄傲。

## `ZagreusHome_2935`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

There must be some mistake...

**中文**

这肯定是搞错了吧……

## `ZagreusHome_2936`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Now who's this dashing fellow?

**中文**

这个风度翩翩的家伙是谁呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2937`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey, what do you know!

**中文**

嘿，这是真的吗！

## `ZagreusHome_2938`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's really me up there.

**中文**

居然真的是我啊。

## `ZagreusHome_2939`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Say, uh, Skelly? How'd you get here, anyway?

**中文**

这么说吧，呃，骨头？你到底是怎么来到这里的？

## `ZagreusHome_2940`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I meant... how did you get to the realm of the dead, in the first place. Lord Hermes whisk you to the entryway? Thanatos scoop you up, Charon give you a lift, what?

**中文**

我是说……你一开始是怎么来到冥界的。是赫尔墨斯带你进来的吗？是塔纳托斯把你接过来的吗？还是卡戎载了你一程之类的？

## `ZagreusHome_2941`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hrm.

**中文**

呵嗯。

## `ZagreusHome_2942`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That Lucky Tooth you gave me, Skelly. First off, thanks again, and secondly... is this even yours? Had a run-in with Hermes, who got me thinking.

**中文**

骨头，关于你给我的那颗幸运牙齿，我首先得谢谢你，然后我想问……那颗牙真的是你的吗？我和赫尔墨斯碰了个面，当时的情形让我不由得怀疑起来。

## `ZagreusHome_2943`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Never said he did. Though since you let on... what's your relationship to him? Working for him?

**中文**

我从来没说过他认识你，但既然你说漏了嘴……你和他之间是什么关系？你再替他卖命吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2944`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fine. Maybe.

**中文**

好吧，大概吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2945`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

All right, fess up, Skelly, who are you working for? I know it's not Achilles. It's not Nyx. Dusa has no idea who you are. My father... no way. Hypnos? Give me a break. So the House is ruled out.

**中文**

行了，实话实说吧，骨头，你到底是在给谁卖命呢？我知道你的主人不是阿喀琉斯，不是倪克斯，杜莎也不认识你。至于我的父亲……门都没有。修普诺斯？我看没戏。整个圣殿的人都可以排除了。

## `ZagreusHome_2946`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How about you tell me who your sources are?

**中文**

不如和我说说你的老大是谁吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2947`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Tell me.

**中文**

说说嘛。

## `ZagreusHome_2948`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Tell me!

**中文**

就说说嘛！

## `ZagreusHome_2949`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Tell me, Skelly.

**中文**

来说说嘛，骨头。

## `ZagreusHome_2950`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Come on, tell me, please?

**中文**

拜托了，就和我说一下嘛？

## `ZagreusHome_2951`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Why won't you tell me?

**中文**

你真的不能告诉我吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2952`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What if I never hit you again?

**中文**

如果我以后再也不打你了呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2953`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So help me, Skelly, if you don't tell me who you're working for, there will be no more violence between us, ever.

**中文**

所以，你得帮我这个忙，骨头。如果你不告诉我你的老大是谁，那我以后就再也不打你了，想都别想了。

## `ZagreusHome_2954`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No mercy, Skelly. Tell me. Or else... it will be only pleasantries between us from here on out.

**中文**

我不做人了，骨头，赶紧告诉我。否则……从现在开始，我就要对你和和气气的了。

## `ZagreusHome_2955`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I promise. So...?

**中文**

我答应你，所以呢……？

## `ZagreusHome_2956`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Charon! Huh.

**中文**

是卡戎！哎哟。

## `ZagreusHome_2957`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What's his angle?

**中文**

他到底想干什么？

## `ZagreusHome_2958`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

And Hermes... he's working for Charon, too?

**中文**

还有赫尔墨斯……他也是在替卡戎办事吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2959`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Fine. Maybe.

**中文**

好吧，大概吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2960`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Skelly, did you tell Charon and Hermes about our little conversation from before, about your sources? Because, I didn't tell them, but... they seemed to know.

**中文**

骨头，你是不是告诉卡戎和赫尔墨斯我们之前谈话的事情了？因为那些话我根本没告诉过他们，可是……他们好像已经知道了。

## `ZagreusHome_2961`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I didn't get you into too much trouble, did I?

**中文**

我没给你惹上太多麻烦吧？

## `ZagreusHome_2962`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Skelly, is it really true you don't want to talk about how you got here? Just curious who you used to be. What you were like. Your real name.

**中文**

骨头，你是真的不想谈谈你是怎么来这里的吗？我只是很好奇你以前的样子，你是个什么样的人，你的真名叫什么。

## `ZagreusHome_2963`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sorry, what I meant was... I was just interested to get to know you better, after all you've done to help. Anything you can share? Trade you for a detail about me!

**中文**

抱歉，我只是想说……你帮了我这么多忙，我希望能更好地了解了解你。有什么可以告诉我的事情吗？要是你愿意说，那我也说一件关于我自己的事情！

## `ZagreusHome_2964`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Skelly, that's... that's incredible! So then... you and your crew, you perished at sea?

**中文**

骨头，这可……这可真是太难以置信了，然后呢……你和你的船员，你们死在海上了吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2965`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, I get it. You got me. Fine, then, Schelemeus, but I don't appreciate the trick.

**中文**

哦，好吧。这次算你赢了，瑟勒米乌斯。不过我可不喜欢你和我开这种玩笑。

## `ZagreusHome_2966`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Skelly... I'm serious. You really won't tell me anything about you? You're just going to leave me to speculate, forever?

**中文**

骨头……我是说真的，你真的不打算和我说说关于你的事情吗？你就打算让我一直这么遐想下去吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2967`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I broke free after all! Sort of.

**中文**

我终于获得自由了！算是有点自由了吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2968`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Contractor's been plenty busy thanks to me.

**中文**

多亏了我的缘故，承包商一直都有事可忙。

## `ZagreusHome_2969`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My extended family seems an interesting lot.

**中文**

我新的家人们看起来都很有趣。

## `ZagreusHome_2970`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good to have friends in low places.

**中文**

在困境之中能有朋友，真的是件好事。

## `ZagreusHome_2971`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Can't believe Lady Athena's fighting for me.

**中文**

我真不敢相信，雅典娜女士在为我而战。

## `ZagreusHome_2972`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Lord Uncle Zeus sure has been generous.

**中文**

宙斯叔叔一直都对我很慷慨。

## `ZagreusHome_2973`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Uncle Poseidon... so different from Father.

**中文**

波塞冬叔叔……和我的父亲一点都不一样。

## `ZagreusHome_2974`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Lady Aphrodite... showing me favor.

**中文**

阿弗洛狄忒女士……她对我很关照。

## `ZagreusHome_2975`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Where there's violence, there's Lord Ares.

**中文**

哪里有暴力，哪里就有阿瑞斯大人。

## `ZagreusHome_2976`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Grateful for your help, Lady Artemis.

**中文**

我很感谢你的帮助，阿尔忒弥斯女士。

## `ZagreusHome_2977`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Never a dull moment with Lord Dionysus around.

**中文**

只要有迪奥尼索司大人在身边，什么时候都不会无聊。

## `ZagreusHome_2978`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Glad to have Lord Hermes on my side.

**中文**

很高兴赫尔墨斯能站在我这边。

## `ZagreusHome_2979`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Lady Demeter seems to have taken a shine to me.

**中文**

得墨忒尔女士似乎很关照我。

## `ZagreusHome_2980`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Olympians aren't all on the best of terms I guess.

**中文**

我猜，奥林匹斯众神之间，也不总是和和睦睦的吧。

## `ZagreusHome_2981`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The gifts of Master Chaos...

**中文**

是来自卡俄斯君主的礼物……

## `ZagreusHome_2982`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think Master Chaos just likes messing with me.

**中文**

我觉得卡俄斯君主只是喜欢和我开玩笑而已。

## `ZagreusHome_2983`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nice to have my ancient legendary weapons there.

**中文**

能使用这些古老的传奇兵器，这可真不错。

## `ZagreusHome_2984`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I know Stygius like the back of my hand.

**中文**

我对斯第格思了如指掌。

## `ZagreusHome_2985`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Coronacht and I are close by now.

**中文**

我和科罗纳赫特的关系很亲密。

## `ZagreusHome_2986`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Aegis has seen me through how many fights?

**中文**

不知我已经和埃癸斯共同经历了多少场战斗呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2987`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Varatha, has there ever been a greater spear?

**中文**

瓦拉塔，真的还有比你更厉害的长矛吗？

## `ZagreusHome_2988`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Malphon's always there for the direct approach.

**中文**

玛尔封的战斗方式一直都直截了当。

## `ZagreusHome_2989`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Exagryph, you and I have been through a lot.

**中文**

伊格瑞菲，我和你经历了很多战斗啊。

## `ZagreusHome_2990`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Guess I have been rather busy as of late.

**中文**

我猜我最近肯定是没怎么闲着。

## `ZagreusHome_2991`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My weapons have revealed their hidden power.

**中文**

我的武器已经显现出了它们隐藏的力量。

## `ZagreusHome_2992`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Where would I be without my friends?

**中文**

要是没有我的朋友，我现在会在哪里呢？

## `ZagreusHome_2993`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Achilles' Codex... I'm getting somewhere with it.

**中文**

阿克琉斯的法典……它应该对我很有用。

## `ZagreusHome_2994`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Was this lot the best you have, Father?

**中文**

你就这点本事吗，父亲？

## `ZagreusHome_2995`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I guess it is a pretty decent statue.

**中文**

我猜这个雕像确实挺不错的。

## `ZagreusHome_2996`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can't believe I actually pulled this off.

**中文**

真不敢相信，我居然真的做到了。

## `ZagreusHome_2997`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Wonder if I can get out even faster.

**中文**

不知道我能不能用更短的时间逃出这里。

## `ZagreusHome_2998`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not even the Pact of Punishment can stop me.

**中文**

就算是惩罚契约，也阻止不了我。

## `ZagreusHome_2999`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I get the perks of getting to slay Father's forces.

**中文**

我的力量足以消灭父亲的仆从。

## `ZagreusHome_3000`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Mirror of Night... it's made me strong.

**中文**

夜之圣镜……它让我变得更加强大。

## `ZagreusHome_3001`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I guess I'm glad Skelly's not permanently dead.

**中文**

骨头并没有彻底死掉，我猜我还是挺高兴的吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3002`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nyx... this was the least I could do.

**中文**

倪克斯……这是我至少应该做的。

## `ZagreusHome_3003`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I guess Sisyphus is even freer than before.

**中文**

我猜，西西弗斯应该比以前还要自由。

## `ZagreusHome_3004`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hope Orpheus and Eurydice can be happy again.

**中文**

希望俄耳甫斯和欧律狄刻能重归于好。

## `ZagreusHome_3005`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

May Achilles and Patroclus make up for lost time.

**中文**

希望阿喀琉斯和普特洛克勒斯能弥补他们错过的一切。

## `ZagreusHome_3006`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Dusa's got everything under control, I hope.

**中文**

希望杜莎能把一切都安排好。

## `ZagreusHome_3007`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Frost Fair Blade... what a weapon...

**中文**

冷艳锯……多么奇妙的武器啊……

## `ZagreusHome_3008`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Holy Excalibur... thank you for your strength.

**中文**

王者之圣剑……谢谢你的力量。

## `ZagreusHome_3009`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Celestial Sharanga... it was my honor.

**中文**

天神之弓沙兰迦……能使用你，我感到很荣幸。

## `ZagreusHome_3010`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Naegling's Board... nothing gets past you.

**中文**

奈格灵之盾……什么样的攻击你都能挡得住。

## `ZagreusHome_3011`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Igneus Eden... where in blazes did Lucifer find you?

**中文**

伊甸之火……路西法到底是在哪里找到你的？

## `ZagreusHome_3012`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm no Orpheus, but I'm no slouch.

**中文**

我不是俄耳甫斯，不过我也不是什么笨蛋。

## `ZagreusHome_3013`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I shall procure all the fishes.

**中文**

我要把所有鱼都钓回来。

## `ZagreusHome_3014`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Charon must have made a fortune off of me.

**中文**

卡戎肯定从我这里赚了不少钱吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3015`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hermes really believed I could pull this off.

**中文**

赫尔墨斯真的相信我做得到。

## `ZagreusHome_3016`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Always happy to oblige Master Chaos.

**中文**

我一直很乐于听从卡俄斯君主的指示。

## `ZagreusHome_3017`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Anything to cut Theseus down to size.

**中文**

只要是能把忒修斯干掉，怎样都好。

## `ZagreusHome_3018`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The ones that tell me which Olympian is waiting for me, and so on? What about them?

**中文**

就是他们对我说，整个奥林匹斯都在等着我诸如此类的话吧？他们怎么样了？

## `ZagreusHome_3019`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Blue and gold reward chambers, huh.

**中文**

金碧辉煌的奖励房间，嗯。

## `ZagreusHome_3020`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's not to like, indeed.

**中文**

是啊，有什么理由不喜欢呢。

## `ZagreusHome_3021`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Dash while spear-spinning, then.

**中文**

那就一边挥舞长枪，一边向前奔跑吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3022`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Look for the little moment when I draw.

**中文**

拉弓的时候得留意最佳时机。

## `ZagreusHome_3023`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Block, then chuck shield in nose.

**中文**

先挡住攻击，然后再一盾砸过去。

## `ZagreusHome_3024`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Dash in with the Twin Fists...

**中文**

手持双子之拳全力狂奔……

## `ZagreusHome_3025`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Listen for the cue when I need to reload...

**中文**

需要重新上弹的时候，要记得留意提示……

## `ZagreusHome_3026`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You mean the Dark Thirst on my weapons, huh...

**中文**

你指的是我的武器上的黑暗渴望吗，嗯……

## `ZagreusHome_3027`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This one's for you, ever-bloodthirsty god of war.

**中文**

这是献给你的，渴望鲜血的战争之神。

## `ZagreusHome_3028`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nice chatting!

**中文**

和你谈得很愉快！

## `ZagreusHome_3029`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good chatting.

**中文**

和你聊得很开心。

## `ZagreusHome_3030`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

See you!

**中文**

下次见！

## `ZagreusHome_3031`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cheers!

**中文**

谢谢！

## `ZagreusHome_3032`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Take care!

**中文**

多保重！

## `ZagreusHome_3033`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Take care, OK?

**中文**

多多保重，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3034`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right I'm off.

**中文**

好了，我得走了。

## `ZagreusHome_3035`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good seeing you.

**中文**

回头见吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3036`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Bye, Dusa.

**中文**

再见，杜莎。

## `ZagreusHome_3037`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

See you, Dusa.

**中文**

下次见，杜莎。

## `ZagreusHome_3038`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Take care, Dusa!

**中文**

保重身体，杜莎！

## `ZagreusHome_3039`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

See you, Hypnos.

**中文**

下次见，修普诺斯。

## `ZagreusHome_3040`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right, Hypnos.

**中文**

好了，修普诺斯。

## `ZagreusHome_3041`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Get some rest, mate.

**中文**

好好休息休息吧，朋友。

## `ZagreusHome_3042`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Bye, Hypnos.

**中文**

再见，修普诺斯。

## `ZagreusHome_3043`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

See you, Than.

**中文**

再见，塔纳。

## `ZagreusHome_3044`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

See you, Thanatos.

**中文**

再见，塔纳托斯。

## `ZagreusHome_3045`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

See you out there.

**中文**

下次见吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3046`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanks, sir.

**中文**

谢谢你，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_3047`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Take care, sir.

**中文**

多保重，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_3048`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you, sir.

**中文**

谢谢你，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_3049`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

See you, boy.

**中文**

下次见，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusHome_3050`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Be good, boy.

**中文**

记得要当个乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusHome_3051`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Back soon, boy.

**中文**

我马上就回来，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusHome_3052`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Take care, boy.

**中文**

多保重，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusHome_3053`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Take care, Meg.

**中文**

多保重，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_3054`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

See you out there?

**中文**

下次见了，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3055`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nice seeing you, Meg.

**中文**

很高兴见到你，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_3056`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

See you soon, Meg.

**中文**

回头见吧，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_3057`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Bye, mate.

**中文**

再见了，朋友。

## `ZagreusHome_3058`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Take care, mate.

**中文**

多保重，朋友。

## `ZagreusHome_3059`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cheers, mate.

**中文**

谢谢你，朋友。

## `ZagreusHome_3060`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Beautiful, mate.

**中文**

你很漂亮，朋友。

## `ZagreusHome_3061`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You sound amazing, mate.

**中文**

你听起来真是棒极了，朋友。

## `ZagreusHome_3062`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Bye, Nyx.

**中文**

再见，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_3063`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you, Nyx.

**中文**

谢谢你，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_3064`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good chat, Father!

**中文**

和你聊得很开心，父亲！

## `ZagreusHome_3065`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well then!

**中文**

那好吧！

## `ZagreusHome_3066`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well all right!

**中文**

好吧！

## `ZagreusHome_3067`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Please make yourselves at home!

**中文**

你在这里千万别拘束！

## `ZagreusHome_3068`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How's it going?

**中文**

最近怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_3069`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you for waiting.

**中文**

谢谢你等我。

## `ZagreusHome_3070`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Appreciate your patience.

**中文**

谢谢你的耐心。

## `ZagreusHome_3071`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How goes it, shades?

**中文**

你们还好吗，幽灵们？

## `ZagreusHome_3072`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's the latest?

**中文**

最近有什么事吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3073`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's new?

**中文**

有什么新鲜事吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3074`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey, welcome!

**中文**

嘿，欢迎！

## `ZagreusHome_3075`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey, I know you!

**中文**

嘿，我认识你！

## `ZagreusHome_3076`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You shades doing all right?

**中文**

你们还好吗，幽灵们？

## `ZagreusHome_3077`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This one!

**中文**

就是它！

## `ZagreusHome_3078`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This one, then.

**中文**

就是它吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3079`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Such meticulous craft.

**中文**

做工真细致。

## `ZagreusHome_3080`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The sense of poise is truly extraordinary.

**中文**

这种镇定自若的感觉，真的很了不起。

## `ZagreusHome_3081`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It looks distinctly different from this angle.

**中文**

从这个角度来看，真是完全不一样。

## `ZagreusHome_3082`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's like a new experience every time I look.

**中文**

每次看的时候，我都能有新的体会。

## `ZagreusHome_3083`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Say, mate? I picked up a painting for our lounge that looked a little like yourself. Reminds me... have you ever even been inside? Should come join us sometime.

**中文**

这么说吧，伙计，我找了一幅画挂在休息室里，画上的人有点像你。说到这个……你有没有进过休息室？你有空就应该过来喝一杯。

## `ZagreusHome_3084`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What if I picked you up bodily and brought you in? Contract have anything to say about that?

**中文**

那如果我把你举起来，强行带到休息室里呢？契约上提到这样的事情了吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3085`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Skelly, how do you know all this stuff, by the way? I mean no offense, but you're a skeleton who just stands around. Yet you have this wealth of knowledge of the Underworld?

**中文**

顺便问一句，骨头，这些事情你到底是怎么知道的？我无意冒犯，你不过是个干站着的骷髅而已，可你却对这个冥界的事情了如指掌，这是怎么回事？

## `ZagreusHome_3086`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

If I must.

**中文**

如果非这样不可的话。

## `ZagreusHome_3087`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Appreciate it, Skelly.

**中文**

我很感谢，骨头。

## `ZagreusHome_3088`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Stand there eternally to be whaled upon at my whim, using whichever weapon suits me at the time?

**中文**

你是指就那么干等着，等我什么时候一时兴起，用我最趁手的武器揍你一顿吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3089`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That Flameater's acclimating well.

**中文**

那条嗜火鱼的适应能力不错嘛。

## `ZagreusHome_3090`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Greetings, river denizens.

**中文**

你们好啊，河里的居民们。

## `ZagreusHome_3091`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Greetings, river denizens. Former.

**中文**

你们好啊，河里的前居民们。

## `ZagreusHome_3092`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How goes it, fishies?

**中文**

你们还好吗，小鱼们？

## `ZagreusHome_3093`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Limitless work, limitless time, am I right?

**中文**

无穷无尽的工作，无穷无尽的时间，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3094`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Together, we can achieve anything my Father wants!

**中文**

只要齐心协力，无论我父亲想怎么样，我们都能做得到。

## `ZagreusHome_3095`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Who wants to hear a humorous jest of some sort?

**中文**

有谁想听我讲个有意思的笑话吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3096`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Remember, work more intelligently, rather than with more ferocity!

**中文**

记住，工作的时候要多靠智慧，不要只靠蛮干！

## `ZagreusHome_3097`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

These wall-scrolls are awful!

**中文**

这些卷轴画可真是太糟糕了！

## `ZagreusHome_3098`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Seriously, you shades keep it up.

**中文**

说真的，幽灵们，你们应该继续保持下去。

## `ZagreusHome_3099`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

At least you're not working with me, though, right?

**中文**

至少你不打算和我一起工作，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3100`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I understand, I think, mate. Although... I think I would have done the same in your position. I'm so sorry that happened. And appreciate you telling me like this.

**中文**

我觉得我明白，朋友。不过……我觉得，如果我是你的话，也会做同样的事。很抱歉，发生那样的事情，也谢谢你能告诉我这些。

## `ZagreusHome_3101`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dusa, I... think that Nyx has some concerns... maybe with me, because it's like you said, she doesn't want us talking here like this. Is she... not satisfied with the work you've been doing, or something?

**中文**

杜莎，我……我觉得倪克斯可能有些想法……关于我的想法。因为就像你说得那样，她不希望我们像这样聊天。她……她对你最近的工作不满意了吗？还是怎么样了？

## `ZagreusHome_3102`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Those sound like reasonable goals, though I just wish that Nyx could find a way to tell you that wasn't so upsetting. Won't you take care of yourself, Dusa, please?

**中文**

这些建议听起来都合情合理，不过我觉得，倪克斯只是在用她的方式告诉你，她并没有生你的气。你可以好好照顾照顾自己吗，杜莎？拜托了。

## `ZagreusHome_3103`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This isn't working...

**中文**

这可行不通……

## `ZagreusHome_3104`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Do you have to work with Hypnos pretty closely as part of your job, Dusa? Feels like I've been running into him a lot lately.

**中文**

和修普诺斯合作，也是你工作的一部分吗，杜莎？我感觉最近总能碰到他。

## `ZagreusHome_3105`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll just keep talking to him so no one else has to.

**中文**

我只要不停地和他说话就行了，这样其他人就没必要这样了。

## `ZagreusHome_3106`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

They have a lot of catching up to do, for sure. Though I think they're going to be all right.

**中文**

他们肯定要好好叙叙旧，不过我觉得他们应该能谈得很好。

## `ZagreusHome_3107`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Ungh, Dusa... it's a little much out there sometimes, I have to say. Different stresses than in here, but still.

**中文**

呃，杜莎……不得不说，有时候我觉得压力有点太大了。虽然和这里的压力不太一样，不过还是会有压力。

## `ZagreusHome_3108`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...I don't know that anyone can do this. No escape from the Underworld and all that. Though, thanks for cheering me up.

**中文**

……据我所知，这种事情还没人做到过，还没人能从冥界逃出去。不过，谢谢你给我打气。

## `ZagreusHome_3109`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I... they do, huh.

**中文**

我……呃，它们是这样的。

## `ZagreusHome_3110`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Can I ask you a couple of purely figurative questions here, Dusa? First, what have I gotten myself into...? And second, what am I going to do...?

**中文**

我可以问你几个完全是假设性的问题吗，杜莎？第一个问题，我给自己惹上了什么麻烦……？第二个问题，我应该怎么做才好……？

## `ZagreusHome_3111`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You've no idea how much I appreciate it.

**中文**

你不知道我有多感激。

## `ZagreusHome_3112`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh! I can definitely relate at least to some of that.

**中文**

哦！我确实能理解你的感受。

## `ZagreusHome_3113`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's more for everybody else than you, Father. Think of it as a tribute to Cerberus! Besides, you really should think about changing up the look of this place from time to time. Keep things fresh!

**中文**

那不是为你准备的，父亲，而是为其他人准备的。你就把它当做是对刻耳柏洛斯的致意吧！另外，你真的应该考虑时不时地换一下这里的装饰，让大家保持新鲜感！

## `ZagreusHome_3114`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Works for me!

**中文**

我觉得可以！

## `ZagreusHome_3115`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father, of all the skeletons and such in your direct employ... you wouldn't happen to have any here around the House, would you? Out in my courtyard, for example?

**中文**

父亲大人，有很多骷髅都是直接听命于你的……在这些骷髅里面，不会刚好有哪个就在这座圣殿里工作吧？比如说，在我的院子里面？

## `ZagreusHome_3116`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I figured.

**中文**

我想也是。

## `ZagreusHome_3117`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Don't look back, huh. I'll try not to. Though, you can't be blamed for doing it yourself, just the one time. What happened to you... it wasn't fair. If you ask me.

**中文**

不要回头，是吗，我尽量别回头吧。不过，你也只不过是回了一次头而已，不应该这么责备自己。要我说的话……你碰上的那种事情根本不公平。

## `ZagreusHome_3118`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...Hey... look. I care about you. Both of you. A lot. I'm happy we're this close... you're this close, rather than being at each other's throats like before. But, I think it's all a little much for me, right now.

**中文**

……嘿……你看，我很在乎你，很在乎你们两个。你看，我很高兴我们的关系变得这么亲密……很高兴你们的关系变得这么亲密，不用像以前那样剑拔弩张。可是，我觉得我稍微有点接受不了。

## `ZagreusHome_3119`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Approved! Wouldn't mind an extra helping of gemstones while I'm ransacking out there.

**中文**

批准了！在外面大杀四方的时候，我是不介意多弄到一些宝石的。

## `ZagreusHome_3120`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh! No, I'm all right, Orpheus. Just a lot on my mind is all, sorting out what's next. Stuff I need to work through on my own.

**中文**

哦！没有，我很好，俄耳甫斯。只是我最近想了很多事情，我得考虑接下来该怎么做，我有些私事得想办法解决。

## `ZagreusHome_3121`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Appreciate it, mate.

**中文**

谢谢你，朋友。

## `ZagreusHome_3122`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

They say he was the strongest demigod who ever lived. Though, I bet you could take him, sir. Big guy like that, you'd be too quick, I'm sure. You ever meet him?

**中文**

大家都说他是最强大的半神。不过，我猜你能打得过他，因为对他那样的大块头来说，你的动作太敏捷了。你见过他吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3123`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm sure he's in no hurry to return.

**中文**

我敢说他不急着回来。

## `ZagreusHome_3124`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You ever think of giving Thanatos a break, Father? He seems to have more than his fair share of work of late.

**中文**

你有没有想过让塔纳托斯休息一下，父亲？他最近的工作似乎已经超出他该干的量了。

## `ZagreusHome_3125`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What wars, what's going on up there? Why don't the other Olympians do something?

**中文**

什么战争？上面到底发生了什么事情？其他的奥林匹斯众神为什么不做点什么？

## `ZagreusHome_3126`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That's certainly a way of looking at it.

**中文**

这么想确实也没问题。

## `ZagreusHome_3127`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Enjoying staring at that ball I got you, boy? No fun if it doesn't fight back, huh...

**中文**

你喜欢我给你的那个球吗，乖狗狗？不过它不会还手，这恐怕就没劲了吧……

## `ZagreusHome_3128`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well, it's there if you change your mind.

**中文**

好吧，如果你改主意的话，它就在那里。

## `ZagreusHome_3129`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

House Contractor? With this gift of Darkness, I decree the Eldest Sigil shall be restored!

**中文**

圣殿承包商？有了这份黑暗的礼物，我有权命令你恢复远古印记！

## `ZagreusHome_3130`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So in pursuing glory... you think you lost it? Forgive me, here, but I just disagree. I settled a family squabble. You single-handedly turned an entire war!

**中文**

所以你觉得，你在追寻荣耀的过程中……失去了它吗？抱歉，但我不同意。我解决的不过是家人之间的小争端，而你却凭一己之力扭转了整个战局！

## `ZagreusHome_3131`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

If not for all your training and advice, I wouldn't have come this far. You prepared me for this. Wasn't that a worthwhile pursuit? Must mean you got some glory after all, then, right?

**中文**

要不是有了你的训练与忠告，我根本走不到这一步。是你让我为这一切做好了准备，这还不算是值得做的事情吗？我猜到头来，一部分荣耀还是属于你的，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3132`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, sir, I have a very important question... why do you detest onions so much? Have they wronged you, somehow?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯先生。我有个非常重要的问题要问……你为什么这么讨厌洋葱呢？它们是怎么惹你了吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3133`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, apparently they're pretty tasty if you don't eat them raw.

**中文**

这么说吧，只要不生吃的话，它们还是很美味的。

## `ZagreusHome_3134`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No. Wait, what? I vanquished you in single combat and finally found Mother, and that's all you have to say to me?

**中文**

不。等等，什么？我刚刚单打独斗打赢了你，而且终于找到了母亲，你现在就只对我说这些？

## `ZagreusHome_3135`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You can't be serious. You're going to pretend as though it never happened? She wants answers, same as I do. She demands you let me pass! Let me go to her again.

**中文**

开玩笑。你要假装根本没这回事？她想要你的答案，我也是。她要让你放我走，让我回到她身边！

## `ZagreusHome_3136`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I know where we can continue this discussion, then...

**中文**

我知道咱们应该怎么继续讨论这个问题……

## `ZagreusHome_3137`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I can't entirely say I do, though if it involves lashings and whatnot, I already get more than my share of that, so... figured I would take the risk. So what do you say?

**中文**

老实说我不太清楚，不知道这么做会不会挨鞭子。我这里的仙馔密酒已经够多了，所以……我觉得我可以冒一下这个险，你觉得呢？

## `ZagreusHome_3138`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

See, I, too, thought she could use the fresh air. Or... however you'd describe the quality of the air on the edge of Tartarus. You know she'd never mean you any harm, right? In her mind... I think she's trying to keep it friendly between us.

**中文**

我也是，我觉得她应该出来呼吸呼吸新鲜空气，或者说……随便你怎么形容塔耳塔罗斯的空气质量吧。她根本没打算伤害你，你是明白的吧？在她心里……我觉得她只希望咱们能好好相处。

## `ZagreusHome_3139`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Appreciate your open-mindedness.

**中文**

谢谢你思想这么开明。

## `ZagreusHome_3140`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Meg, what's going on with Nyx and Dusa, do you know? I think they've been butting heads a bit lately.

**中文**

墨纪，倪克斯和杜莎发生什么事情了，你知道吗？我觉得她们两个最近发生了一些小冲突。

## `ZagreusHome_3141`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What does she care? I talk to everybody around here at least a bit.

**中文**

可这关她什么事情？这里的所有人，我多多少少都聊过了。

## `ZagreusHome_3142`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How is this my fault?

**中文**

这怎么能是我的错呢？

## `ZagreusHome_3143`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Who, you mean Dusa? Like I had anything to do with that. It's Nyx you should be cross with, not me!

**中文**

谁？你说杜莎吗？这件事跟我哪有关系啊。你该找的人是倪克斯，不是我！

## `ZagreusHome_3144`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nrgh...

**中文**

呃啊……

## `ZagreusHome_3145`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You saw Dusa's back, right, Meg? She's getting another chance. Nyx reconsidered!

**中文**

你看到杜莎回来了，是吧，墨纪？倪克斯重新考虑过了！又给了她一次机会！

## `ZagreusHome_3146`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You don't sound all that pleased...

**中文**

你的语气听起来可没那么开心啊……

## `ZagreusHome_3147`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm so proud of Dusa lately, Meg! Chief Custodian of the House, was it? That's quite an honor coming from Nyx.

**中文**

最近我真的为杜莎感到骄傲，墨纪！她成了这座圣殿的首席管家，不是吗？能从倪克斯那里获得这种殊荣，可真了不起。

## `ZagreusHome_3148`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're always looking out for her. Thanks for that.

**中文**

你总是很照顾她，谢谢你。

## `ZagreusHome_3149`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Of course.

**中文**

不客气。

## `ZagreusHome_3150`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You and Thanatos don't work together much anymore, do you, Meg?

**中文**

你和塔纳托斯已经不再共事了，是吗，墨纪？

## `ZagreusHome_3151`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oathbreakers, traitors, and the like? Sending them straight to Tartarus, huh. Must be quite an experience going from living and breathing, to... being dead, to being whisked off by Thanatos, straight to you and your whip.

**中文**

背信弃义之人，叛徒之类的，是吗？把他们直接送到塔耳塔罗斯，哈。我猜这些人从活蹦乱跳到……一命呜呼，到被塔纳托斯带走，再到送到你这里挨鞭子，这肯定是相当曲折的经历吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3152`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You'd think!

**中文**

你说得容易！

## `ZagreusHome_3153`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

If you're asking if I have any regrets about what happened between us, the answer is no. Being with you... any complications there are worth it.

**中文**

如果你想问我，我对咱们之间发生的事情后不后悔的话，我的答案是：不后悔。能和你在一起……无论是什么样的后果，都值得。

## `ZagreusHome_3154`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wasn't planning on sliding back into the insensitive nonsense of youth, so I don't think the second thing will be a problem. But the first... can we really just keep killing each other out there like nothing happened?

**中文**

我并不打算回到那没心没肺，麻木不仁的青葱岁月，所以我觉得第二个条件不成问题。但第一个条件……我们真的能继续互相杀戮，就好像什么都没发生过一样吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3155`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ominous.

**中文**

听起来别有深意。

## `ZagreusHome_3156`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I feel like everybody knows about us, Meg. Even Aphrodite, herself, seemed to know we were involved. It's not like I've been going around boasting. You wanted to keep things quiet, didn't you?

**中文**

我感觉好像所有人都知道咱们之间的事情一样，墨纪，就连阿弗洛狄忒本人似乎都知道了。这倒不是因为我到处宣扬咱们的事情。你希望这一切都不要声张，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3157`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, I could do without the kill-or-be-killed stuff if we're being honest, that part is still a little weird, but otherwise... yes, I mean... I'm happy. You're you.

**中文**

不过，如果说实话的话，我觉得“要么杀人要么被杀”之类的可以去掉，这部分还是有点奇怪。不过除此之外……是啊，我是说……我很开心，你就是你。

## `ZagreusHome_3158`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

But... I know how important your career is to you. This won't create a problem?

**中文**

可是……我知道你的事业对你来说有多么重要。这不会造成问题吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3159`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I refuse.

**中文**

我做不到。

## `ZagreusHome_3160`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No, it's just... I felt terrible, turning you away. It's just... I've been so confused lately, and... I trust you. You're someone I can talk to. I guess I'm... afraid to lose that.

**中文**

不，只是……拒绝你的感觉很糟糕。只是……我最近一直很困惑，而且……我相信你。你是我可以推心置腹的人，我猜……我只是害怕失去这些吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3161`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanks, Meg.

**中文**

谢谢你，墨纪。

## `ZagreusHome_3162`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Come on, if her vocabulary consists of two different although admittedly almost identical words, there's got to be room for growth, there. Besides, I feel like... maybe she's lonely, or something. Has anyone ever tried to teach her anything?

**中文**

拜托，如果她会说的词语里包括两个虽然听起来几乎相同，但其实不一样的词语，那她肯定还可以继续进步。另外我感觉……她会不会是太孤独了之类的。有人试着教过她什么东西吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3163`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You've tried already, haven't you? She's pretty dead-set on the murder thing, then?

**中文**

你已经试过了，是吗？她就是一根筋地对谋杀什么的感兴趣，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3164`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're not too broken up about how things ended between you and Hypnos, are you, Meg?

**中文**

你和修普诺斯之间的结果不太好，你并不太觉得难过，是吗，墨纪？

## `ZagreusHome_3165`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let me know if I can help.

**中文**

如果有什么可以帮忙的地方，尽管和我开口。

## `ZagreusHome_3166`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Have you tried sulking in the lounge?

**中文**

你之前是在休息室里生闷气吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3167`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, you're saying you would feel less ashamed about the outcomes of our fights if I were to gloat more about beating you? How does that make any sense?

**中文**

等等.……你是在说，如果我打败你之后能更加幸灾乐祸的话，你就觉得心里没那么惭愧了，是吗？这哪里说得通了？

## `ZagreusHome_3168`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll keep putting up a fight if it's any consolation.

**中文**

如果这能让你感到慰藉的话，那我就不停地找你打架吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3169`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Meg, did you... did you ever meet the Queen...? Remember anything about her, at all?

**中文**

墨纪，你以前……你以前有没有见过冥后……？还记不记得关于她的事情？

## `ZagreusHome_3170`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How is it that this place has this secret Queen for I don't know how long, then suddenly she just vanishes, and no one speaks a word of it?

**中文**

先是有个避人耳目的冥后，不知道在这里呆了多久。之后她突然消失了，却根本没人提起过她。这地方到底是怎么回事？

## `ZagreusHome_3171`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Long story, but yeah. Boils down to what you said, more or less.

**中文**

这是个复杂的故事，不过是啊，差不多就是你总结的这样。

## `ZagreusHome_3172`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It is.

**中文**

很值得。

## `ZagreusHome_3173`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's her doing, not mine. So you know her? Because... you could have told me. About her. Who she was to me.

**中文**

这是她的功劳，和我无关。所以说，你认识她吗？因为……你本可以告诉我关于她的事情，告诉我她是我的什么人。

## `ZagreusHome_3174`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No, I suppose not. So what does this mean for Nyx, now that she's back? Or for you, for that matter?

**中文**

是啊，我想你说得没错。既然她已经回来了，这对倪克斯来说意味着什么呢？或者说，对你意味着什么呢？

## `ZagreusHome_3175`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hope to find out.

**中文**

希望我能找到答案。

## `ZagreusHome_3176`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, it was all Orpheus, not me. Can't sing like that without a lot of motivation, inner turmoil, who knows what else. Why, don't you like it? This lounge seems better with a little song.

**中文**

嘿，这都归功于俄耳甫斯，和我无关。如果没有内在的动力、充足的情感，或是别的什么东西的话，是没办法唱出那样的歌的。怎么，你不喜欢吗？我觉得休息室里有歌声会更好一些。

## `ZagreusHome_3177`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's a change.

**中文**

确实是个改变。

## `ZagreusHome_3178`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You'll just have to sulk for the both of you, I guess, then, Meg. But if you must know, on my authority I have released Orpheus from the terms of his old, rather unfair agreement with Father, so he can see his wife at least from time to time.

**中文**

我猜，以后你们两个人的闷气，只能由你一个人生了，墨纪。不过如果你真的想知道原委的话，其实是我授权解除了俄耳甫斯与父亲之间相当不公平的古老条款。这样一来，他至少可以时不时地和他的妻子见面了。

## `ZagreusHome_3179`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm doing no such thing. Father wanted me involved in all this business with his pacts, and presiding over the dead, then fine. But I'll do it my way.

**中文**

我这不是作死。父亲希望我能参与契约相关的工作，负责管理那些死人的事情。这没问题，不过我会按照我自己的方式去做。

## `ZagreusHome_3180`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Good thing he never sets foot in here, then, huh? Wait, you don't miss when things were all sad and dreary in here, do you?

**中文**

幸好他从来没来过这个地方，是吧？等等，你可不怀念这里凄风苦雨的日子，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3181`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good!

**中文**

那就好！

## `ZagreusHome_3182`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I guess that's right... but, before you ask, I'm not doing it for him.

**中文**

我猜你说的没错……但是，在你开口之前，我没打算为他做这件事情。

## `ZagreusHome_3183`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We'll make it official, all right.

**中文**

好吧，那咱们就更正式地互相厮杀吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3184`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Seems only fair they should help in some capacity. They all work together on the surface, and, let's face it, they're responsible for a good number of shades pouring into this realm. Least they can do is help shore up the whole 'there is no escape' reputation we've got.

**中文**

他们应该在某种程度上提供帮助，这样才公平。所有的奥林匹斯神都在通力合作，而且老实说，之所以会有那么多幽灵涌入这个地方，都是因为他们的关系。所以，他们至少可以帮助我们，帮忙巩固这里“没人能逃得出去”的名声。

## `ZagreusHome_3185`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Exactly!

**中文**

就是这样！

## `ZagreusHome_3186`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Whoa, back up! Can you elaborate a bit about these wildest fantasies, Meg?

**中文**

哇啊，等一下！这怎么就成了你不切实际的幻想了，可以解释解释吗，墨纪？

## `ZagreusHome_3187`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well, thanks. I guess I am.

**中文**

好吧，谢谢，我想是的。

## `ZagreusHome_3188`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Megaera... you ever been in love with somebody?

**中文**

墨纪拉……你有没有恋爱过？

## `ZagreusHome_3189`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Who?

**中文**

是和谁？

## `ZagreusHome_3190`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Heh.

**中文**

呵呵。

## `ZagreusHome_3191`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I found her, sir! My mother, she's... alive and well, and... ugh, but I just couldn't stay up there, for very long. I have to get back to her!

**中文**

我找到她了，先生！我的母亲，她……还活着，都很好，不过……呃，我没办法在地上的世界呆得太久，我得再回去找她！

## `ZagreusHome_3192`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

She was... she was great! Nothing like Father, more like... you, a little bit, I guess! Though, being on the surface, it just seemed to suck the life right out of me. Even without the wretches of the Underworld trying to do me in.

**中文**

她……她太棒了！和父亲一点都不一样，更像是……你，我猜有点像吧！不过呆在地上世界的时候，明明没有冥界的暗灵在阻挠我，但感觉就像是我的生命要被吸干了一样。

## `ZagreusHome_3193`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It is. I have to speak with her.

**中文**

是啊，我得和她谈谈。

## `ZagreusHome_3194`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No, I failed, sir. No thanks to Father! Urgh, what am I going to do?

**中文**

没，我失败了，都怪父亲阻挠！呃，我现在该怎么办？

## `ZagreusHome_3195`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I hope you're right.

**中文**

希望你说得对。

## `ZagreusHome_3196`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I found her, again, sir, and... we were able to talk some more. But, I have so many questions left for her, and Father's certainly not been of any help...

**中文**

我又见到她了，先生……我们谈了一些其它的事情，可我还是有很多问题要问她，毕竟我父亲肯定是不会回答的……

## `ZagreusHome_3197`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yes. I will.

**中文**

嗯，我会的。

## `ZagreusHome_3198`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's that she didn't even know I was alive. Sounds as though Nyx revived me sometime after I was born, but by that point... Mother had already left, and... they let her be. For reasons I am trying to find out.

**中文**

她当时不知道我还活着。听起来像是我出生的一段时间之后，倪克斯复活了我，但那个时候……母亲已经离开了，而且……他们没人去找她。至于原因，我还在想办法弄清楚。

## `ZagreusHome_3199`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thanks, sir.

**中文**

谢谢你，先生。

## `ZagreusHome_3200`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's so, indeed, sir. Especially for now, at least, I'm going to keep on fighting to the surface. Helps me to vent a bit, and, like Hypnos says, it makes the Underworld a better place! Though indirectly, I suppose.

**中文**

确实如此，先生。至少现在，我打算继续一次次逃到地面世界。这样可以让我缓解压力。而且就像修普诺斯说的那样，这可以让冥界变得更加美好！虽然过程比较间接。

## `ZagreusHome_3201`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you. It's mine as well.

**中文**

谢谢你，我也是这么希望的。

## `ZagreusHome_3202`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, I confess I was perhaps a little nervous for practically the entire thing, but... all in all, I had a good time, and I'm very, very glad it all turned out the way it did. How about yourself?

**中文**

这么说吧，我得承认我整场筵席可能都有点紧张，但……总体来说，我玩得很开心。而且一切能以现在的样子收场，我真的、真的非常高兴。你呢，你怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_3203`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

She really pulled it off! But... for all her confidence, I don't think she knew she had it in her. Because if she did... if she reached out to Olympus sooner... a lot of things could have been different.

**中文**

她真的很了不起！不过……虽然她很有信心，但我不觉得她有稳操胜券的把握。因为如果她……如果她能早一些去找奥林匹斯众神的话……很多事情可能就会完全不一样了。

## `ZagreusHome_3204`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

All thanks to me, sir? I had ample help along the way. For instance, I could not have done it without you. Would have been using an old chair leg as a weapon, I suppose.

**中文**

都多亏了我吗？我这一路上都一直有人帮我。比如说，如果没有你的话，我根本不可能做得到，说不定我还在拿条破椅子腿当武器用呢。

## `ZagreusHome_3205`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yeah, I should go. I'll see you not too long from now.

**中文**

是啊，我得走了，我很快就会回来见你。

## `ZagreusHome_3206`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey sir, meant to say I hope I didn't get you into too much trouble after letting me in Father's bedchambers.

**中文**

嘿，先生，你允许我进入父亲的卧室，希望这没给你带来太多麻烦。

## `ZagreusHome_3207`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mother can be quite persuasive!

**中文**

母亲有时候相当能说会道！

## `ZagreusHome_3208`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No. Wait, what? I vanquished you in single combat and finally found Mother, and that's all you have to say to me?

**中文**

不。等等，什么？我刚刚单打独斗打赢了你，而且终于找到了母亲，你想和我说的只有这些？

## `ZagreusHome_3209`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You can't be serious. You're going to pretend as though it never happened? She wants answers, same as I do. She demands you let me pass! Let me go to her again.

**中文**

开玩笑。你要假装根本没这回事？她想要你的答案，我也是。她要让你放我走，让我回到她身边！

## `ZagreusHome_3210`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Quite a mess you've created, I must say, Father... after being of no help whatsoever when it comes to disentangling it all. Though, this isn't the place to talk, right?

**中文**

不得不说，你真是惹出了相当大的麻烦啊，父亲，而且你在解决问题这方面完全没起到任何作用。不过，这里不是咱们闲聊的地方，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3211`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Count on that.

**中文**

我很期待。

## `ZagreusHome_3212`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles, something I've been wondering... now that I've discovered all the hidden aspects of my weapons... as far as I know, anyway... I can't see any pattern there, at all. Guan Yu, Gilgamesh, Rama, and so on... any idea why them?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，有的时候我不禁在想……如今我已经解锁了所有武器的隐藏形态……据我所知，这些人之间好像根本就没有任何关联，关羽、吉尔伽美什、罗摩、还有其他人……你知道为什么会是他们吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3213`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You were a great warrior! Famous, in your time! It's no surprise to me at all the Fates chose you to have a stint with one of their world-altering weapons.

**中文**

因为你是位伟大的战士啊！在你的时代赫赫有名！命运女神会把你的形态加入到这不断变换的武器之中，我一点都不觉得奇怪。

## `ZagreusHome_3214`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You think the Infernal Arms just want to be popular? Have exciting stories told about them and whatnot?

**中文**

你觉得这些冥府兵器只是希望能为人所知？希望人们能讲起关于它们的传奇故事，类似这样的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3215`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Of course, sir! I've been keeping busy with it, you could say. It's almost complete!

**中文**

当然不介意，我一直在努力完善它的内容，已经基本完成了！

## `ZagreusHome_3216`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Still always more to learn and feel, though, isn't there?

**中文**

不过，总会有更多可以学习、可以感受的东西，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3217`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dusa, in my father's chambers, he... he has a bedside portrait of somebody. Is... is she someone with whom you are familiar?

**中文**

杜莎，我父亲的房间里……挂着一张某个人的肖像画。这个人……这个人你熟悉吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3218`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Don't worry, you're not missing much.

**中文**

别担心，你没错过太多内容。

## `ZagreusHome_3219`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...I think I finally know a little more about how you must feel, Dusa. Working for Father, I mean. I was so intent on running away from here, but... here I am.

**中文**

……我觉得，我终于能稍微理解一些你的感受了，杜莎。我是说，替我父亲工作的感受。我之前一直打算逃出这个地方，可现在……成了这个样子。

## `ZagreusHome_3220`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yeah... thanks, though.

**中文**

是啊……多谢了。

## `ZagreusHome_3221`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dusa, this whole place, it looks immaculate! That's even after all Olympus came here for that giant feast? How did you get everything clean so fast?

**中文**

杜莎，这个地方看起来完美无瑕！奥林匹斯众神这才刚刚来这里，参加了一场隆重的盛宴啊！你是怎么这么快就把这里清扫干净的？

## `ZagreusHome_3222`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well, you did amazing here.

**中文**

哎呀，你可真了不起。

## `ZagreusHome_3223`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's definitely an adjustment, with my mother here. Especially for her! I'm glad the two of you are getting on all right. I guess things will be different, now, but... hopefully, it's for the better, isn't it?

**中文**

我的母亲已经来了，这确确实实就是改变，尤其是对她而言！我很高兴你们两个相处得很好。我觉得现在一切会和以前不太一样，不过……希望能往好的方面变化，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3224`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I guess we are...

**中文**

我猜是这样的……

## `ZagreusHome_3225`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I... well, when you put it that way, yes, I guess it is! We're going to try and make it work like this, for now.

**中文**

我……嗯，如果你这么说的话，是啊，我想是的！我们暂时可以一起工作，一起打理这座圣殿了。

## `ZagreusHome_3226`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dusa... we've been doing everything you said this entire time.

**中文**

杜莎……你说的那些事情，咱们一直都在做。

## `ZagreusHome_3227`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Bye...

**中文**

再见……

## `ZagreusHome_3228`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Have to tell you something, Than. My birthmother... she didn't abandon me. Or at least... she didn't think she did.

**中文**

有件事情得告诉你，塔纳。我的生母……她并没有抛弃我，至少……她并没打算那么做。

## `ZagreusHome_3229`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, it turns out... she never knew I was alive, at all. Complications when I was born. She had been struggling already here, and it was all too much, I guess. By the time Nyx brought me back... my mother was long gone.

**中文**

这么说吧……她根本不知道我还活着。我出生的时候出了很多状况，她一直都处于煎熬之中，我猜她已经承受不了了。等到倪克斯把我救活的时候……我的母亲早就走了。

## `ZagreusHome_3230`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx knew. But, Father must have convinced her that my mother... that it would be for the best if she never found out. About me. So she could live in peace. Away from all of this. And away from Olympus.

**中文**

倪克斯知道。但我父亲肯定说服了她……他们认为我母亲不知情是最好的，那样她就可以过平静的生活，远离这一切。远离奥林匹斯。

## `ZagreusHome_3231`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yeah.

**中文**

是啊。

## `ZagreusHome_3232`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thank you for asking, Than. I don't know how much more I can say right now, given everything. I'm still figuring it out myself. I'm thankful you've been there to help me get to see her from time to time.

**中文**

谢谢你的询问，塔纳。发生了这么多事情，我也不知道该说什么好了，我还在想该怎么办才好。我很感谢你时不时地帮我去见她一面。

## `ZagreusHome_3233`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I hope so.

**中文**

希望如此。

## `ZagreusHome_3234`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I think they have a lot of work ahead of them. As long as they're trying... he's trying... I think I'm good with that, for now. Though, I am glad she's here.

**中文**

我猜他们还有很多事情要做呢，只要他们还愿意努力……他还愿意努力……我觉得现在挺好的，我很高兴她能在这里。

## `ZagreusHome_3235`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh I entirely agree with that.

**中文**

噢，我完全同意。

## `ZagreusHome_3236`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

For now, I think it is. But, you know how they are. How we all are, I guess.

**中文**

我觉得目前是这样。不过，我猜你也知道他们是什么脾气，我们所有人是什么脾气。

## `ZagreusHome_3237`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Bye, Than.

**中文**

再见，塔纳。

## `ZagreusHome_3238`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Something like that. I guess I've gotten pretty decent about escaping from a place that's known for being inescapable. Lets me keep in touch with the other side of the family. Work with you from time to time.

**中文**

差不多就是这样吧。这个地方宣称绝对逃不出去，但我已经是逃出这个地方的行家了。我打算和另一边的家人保持联系，时不时地和你一起共事。

## `ZagreusHome_3239`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh I'm feeling fine. I never thought I was much good at anything, but... if my fated purpose is to be a master of frustrating Father's efforts to make everything strictly perfect, I think I'm OK with that.

**中文**

哦，我挺好的。我一直以为自己没什么长处，不过……如果我命中注定要和我父亲过不去，成为这方面的行家，好让一切尽善尽美的话，那我觉得我是能接受的。

## `ZagreusHome_3240`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Bye, Than.

**中文**

再见，塔纳。

## `ZagreusHome_3241`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I didn't often stop to give it much thought. I just knew I needed to find her... and then as soon as I did, I knew I needed her in my life. In other words, doing you any favors was never a priority, Father. Just ended up that way, I guess.

**中文**

我当时并没考虑过这么多事情，我只知道我必须找到她……而且，当我一见到她的时候，我就知道我的生命中需要她。换句话说，帮不帮你的忙，并不是我需要着重考虑的事情，父亲。我猜，只是结果刚好帮到了你而已。

## `ZagreusHome_3242`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You're welcome.

**中文**

不客气。

## `ZagreusHome_3243`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I do. But she won't have to hide forever.

**中文**

我明白，但她也没必要一直这么躲着。

## `ZagreusHome_3244`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, you know. Just thought I'd check in, see what you were up to. Parchmentwork going all right, I'm sure?

**中文**

呃。我就是感觉我应该来看看你在忙些什么。契约文书之类的一切都还好吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3245`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No thanks!

**中文**

不了，谢谢！

## `ZagreusHome_3246`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father... how are you doing without Mother around? Will she be all right, on Olympus?

**中文**

父亲……母亲不在身边的日子，你过得怎么样？她在奥林匹斯山上能照顾好自己吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3247`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So everything's back to normal, then? That sounds lovely.

**中文**

所以说，一切又恢复原样了，是吗？听起来可真不错。

## `ZagreusHome_3248`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Glad you're handling it fine.

**中文**

很高兴你能接受这件事情。

## `ZagreusHome_3249`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So, are you maintaining better contact now with Uncle Zeus, Uncle Poseidon, and the rest? Oh, and... is it all right for me to speak about them here?

**中文**

所以，现在你和宙斯叔叔、波塞冬叔叔，还有其他人往来得更频繁了吗？哦，我是说……我可以在这里谈论他们的事情吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3250`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, what do you mean, for now?

**中文**

等等，你说的暂时是什么意思？

## `ZagreusHome_3251`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

But, they're family...

**中文**

不过，他们是我的家人……

## `ZagreusHome_3252`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's good to see Mother's back. Not that I was too concerned, but... I was definitely a little concerned.

**中文**

很高兴看到母亲回来了。倒不是因为我很担心她，不过……我确实有点担心。

## `ZagreusHome_3253`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Zagreus.

**中文**

扎格列欧斯。

## `ZagreusHome_3254`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Have another bottle, why don't you, Father? Not like it's going to change anything between us, right? All the Nectar in the Underworld can't change what's already happened. Or change yourself.

**中文**

不如再来一瓶吧，父亲？我倒不是想说，再来一瓶就能改变我们之间的事情了，是吧？就算把整个冥界的蜜露都喝光，也改变不了曾经发生过的事情，也改变不了你这个人。

## `ZagreusHome_3255`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You could start being a better father at any point. There's no one stopping you but you.

**中文**

你随时都可以开始当个更出色的父亲。没人会阻止你的，除了你自己之外。

## `ZagreusHome_3256`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Why? It's just Nectar, Father, lighten up. I get that you're surly about everything, but please at least try toward decency with me! If not for me, then for Mother.

**中文**

怎么了？不过是蜜露而已，父亲，看开点。我知道你对什么事情都没耐心，但你至少应该试着对我和善一些！就算不是为了我，也是为了母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3257`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What are you getting at? You're just going to give up, stick with what's comfortable for you instead of making any real effort at making peace with your son?

**中文**

你想说什么？你不打算努力缓和与儿子的关系，而是打算就这么放弃，只呆在你觉得舒适的地方就好了吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3258`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right. Well, it's a start.

**中文**

好吧，这才刚刚开始。

## `ZagreusHome_3259`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Say, Orpheus! I heard what must have been a song of yours, it went something, something, in the blood, you know that one? It was amazing, mate.

**中文**

我说，俄耳甫斯！我之前听过的那首歌肯定是你写出来的，歌词是鲜血里的什么什么之类的，你知道那首歌吧？真是太好听了，朋友。

## `ZagreusHome_3260`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Would you ever sing it again?

**中文**

那首歌你愿意再唱一次吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3261`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, you're singing In the Blood after all, Orpheus! Thank you for taking my request!

**中文**

嘿，你终于唱起《鲜血之中》这首歌了，俄耳甫斯！谢谢你接受我的请求！

## `ZagreusHome_3262`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm glad to hear it, mate.

**中文**

很高兴听你这么说，朋友。

## `ZagreusHome_3263`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, erm... it all started when my father saw her on Olympus, and... they fell madly in love. He swept her off her feet, and brought her here. The Olympians, they got pretty worried when they saw she was gone. But now it's OK! Everything's out in the open.

**中文**

这么说吧，呃……一切的起因，都是我父亲当年在奥林匹斯山上见到了她……他们坠入了爱河。她无法抗拒他的魅力，跟着他来到了冥界。奥林匹斯众神发现她走了之后非常担心，但现在一切都没问题了！

## `ZagreusHome_3264`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, that! Thing is, Mother ate some... pomegranate seeds when she was here. And we have rules about that stuff, you know! It means she cannot leave this place for very long at all. I'm sure Demeter will understand. And unfreeze everything when her daughter's around.

**中文**

哦，这个啊！问题是，母亲在这里的时候……吃了一些石榴籽，咱们都知道这意味着什么，是吧！这意味着她根本无法长时间离开冥界。 我相信得墨忒尔会理解这一点的。等她女儿在她身边的时候，她就会让地上世界解冻。

## `ZagreusHome_3265`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Forget about me, Orpheus! This isn't about me, it's about Mother. What's important is she's here, where she belongs.

**中文**

不用在乎我的事情了，俄耳甫斯！重点不是我，而是我的母亲。她在这里，她属于冥界，这才是最重要的。

## `ZagreusHome_3266`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Same goes for me, mate. Same goes for me.

**中文**

我也这么觉得，朋友，我也这么觉得。

## `ZagreusHome_3267`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What about the part where you lied to me about Mother, do you apologize for that? Your foul moods, and venting them on me... I've long since come to expect that from you. And I resented you for it. But I always thought... you're honest.

**中文**

关于母亲的事你向我撒了谎，你会为此道歉吗？你的那些臭脾气，还总是冲我发泄……我已经忍受够久的了，为此我真的恨你。但我本来一直以为……你没对我撒谎。

## `ZagreusHome_3268`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, you didn't need to vent all that inner turmoil onto me throughout my life. Though, I think I understand. I only wish I could have understood sooner. So, uh... what now? I've grown so used to ransacking your domain, I think I'm really going to miss it.

**中文**

那你也不必从我出生起就把那种自责向外发泄到我身上吧。不过，我想我现在理解了。我只是希望我能早点理解这一切。好了，呃……现在怎么办？我已经完全习惯把你的领地搅个底朝天了，今后我肯定会怀念这种感觉的。

## `ZagreusHome_3269`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have to say, Mother, that was a clever plan. Convincing Father to finally let somebody have some fun around here.

**中文**

不得不说，母亲，你的计划真是太妙了。你终于说服了父亲，让大家可以在这里开心地玩了。

## `ZagreusHome_3270`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's about Demeter, isn't it. You two were talking half the night. She wants you to come home with her, doesn't she? You'll always be her little one, something like that?

**中文**

是关于得墨忒尔的事情吧？你们昨天聊到了后半夜。她希望你能和她一起回去，是吗？她希望永远能把你当做小女儿，是类似这样的事情吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3271`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So... you're leaving...? No, Mother, you can't be leaving, you just got here!

**中文**

所以……你要走了吗……？不要，母亲！你不能走，你才刚回来呢！

## `ZagreusHome_3272`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well then... when are you going to go?

**中文**

好吧那……你什么时候走呢？

## `ZagreusHome_3273`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't suppose... I can come with you, can I?

**中文**

我猜……我没法和你一起走，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3274`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm going to miss you, Mother.

**中文**

我会想念你的，母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3275`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How could they not be! I mean, look at this place, look at you! This House, this realm of ours... it's not so bad, entirely, is it?

**中文**

他们哪能不嫉妒呢！我是说，看看这个地方吧，看看你吧！这座圣殿，我们的这个王国……其实还不赖嘛，一点都不赖，不是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3276`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Achilles! I need your help.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯！我需要你的帮助。

## `ZagreusHome_3277`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Let me in Father's chambers. Please.

**中文**

让我进父亲的房间吧，拜托了。

## `ZagreusHome_3278`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't know, I don't know! But, if I know Father, and I think I do, he's got to have something of hers in there. Of Mother's! She still doesn't get it, doesn't believe me about him, and... I said I'd prove it. That he cares for her!

**中文**

我不知道，我不知道！不过，我觉得我了解父亲，他的房间里肯定会有关于她的东西，关于我母亲的东西！她还是不相信，不相信我说的关于我父亲的事情，而且……我对她说我会证明给她看的，让她明白他还在乎她！

## `ZagreusHome_3279`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you!!

**中文**

谢谢！！

## `ZagreusHome_3280`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I did, thank you!

**中文**

我找到了，谢谢你！

## `ZagreusHome_3281`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

He's never stopped thinking of her. Ugh, what a fool.

**中文**

他每时每刻都在考虑她的事情，呃啊，真是个傻瓜。

## `ZagreusHome_3282`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I knew it. It's just capes!

**中文**

我知道，这就是披肩！

## `ZagreusHome_3283`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Mother? Was it always a custom in Father's House to secretly give Nectar to those you care about? I guess it also could have been a thing on Olympus. Look, I wanted you to have this. I'm so glad I found you.

**中文**

呃，母亲？在父亲的大殿里有件约定成俗的事，就是把蜜露送给自己在乎的人，这是向来如此吗？我猜这也可能是从奥林匹斯山上传过来的。我想送给你这个。我真的非常高兴自己终于找到你了。

## `ZagreusHome_3284`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oh, Mother, thank you!

**中文**

哦，母亲，谢谢你！

## `ZagreusHome_3285`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, I, for one, am certain this vintage here has journeyed to the right place. Please enjoy it.

**中文**

嗯，就我自己而言，我确信那一季的葡萄来到了对的地方。请你尽情享用。

## `ZagreusHome_3286`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

My careful eye has got to be the green one, hasn't it. To having a discerning eye, Mother!

**中文**

那我一定是用那只绿色的眼睛盯着的。您给了我一只目光敏锐的眼睛，母亲！

## `ZagreusHome_3287`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

If there are other ways of showing one's appreciation than with Nectar and the like, well, they must pale in comparison I'm sure!

**中文**

如果还有除蜜露以外的表达爱意的方式，那么我敢肯定它们都会显得苍白无力！

## `ZagreusHome_3288`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Please don't say that, it's just a gift, all right? Besides... can't we start now? Mother and son type things. If you would like.

**中文**

请别这样说，这只是我送给你的一个礼物，好吗？而且……我们不能从现在开始吗？就是母子关系之类的。如果你愿意的话。

## `ZagreusHome_3289`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

For what it's worth, I happen to think you're off to an excellent start. Nyx did teach me a lot. Achilles, too, and Father, in a way. But there is no replacing your presence. I felt that before we ever met. And now I know for sure.

**中文**

值得一提的是，我认为这正是一个绝佳的开始。倪克斯的确教会了我很多东西。阿喀琉斯也是，还有父亲——以他独特的方式。但没有任何人能替代你。在我们相见之前我就这样感觉，现在我更确定了。

## `ZagreusHome_3290`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Perhaps I am part plant!

**中文**

也许我的一部分也是植物！

## `ZagreusHome_3291`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Mother, I know there may not be many things you miss from living on Olympus, though I wondered if perhaps Ambrosia may be one of them. And got you some, because... well, here.

**中文**

母亲，我知道你对奥林匹斯山并没有太多留恋，但也许仙馔密酒会是个例外？我给你找来了一点，因为……好吧，给你。

## `ZagreusHome_3292`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It is, it is, of course! And, it's my dearest hope that, at some point long from now, when you're relaxing with a bottle of Ambrosia, dare I say perhaps with Father... that these memories will be among the ones that spring to mind.

**中文**

是啊，是啊，当然了！而且我衷心希望，在很久之后当你正享用着一瓶仙馔密酒放松时——或许还是和父亲一起，这些美好的记忆也会涌上你的心头。

## `ZagreusHome_3293`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You're very welcome, Mother.

**中文**

不用客气，母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3294`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Here, Mother. More bottled pleasant memories for you. I know you're glad you've long since left Olympus, but... I'm glad you were there, because the experience of it must have played no small part in making you who you are.

**中文**

母亲，这儿有更多的甜美回忆给您。我知道您对于长久地离开奥林匹斯山感到很高兴，但……我很高兴你曾留在那儿，因为你在那里的经历也造就了如今的你，那是你的一部分。

## `ZagreusHome_3295`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It is yours to do with as you please.

**中文**

悉听尊便。

## `ZagreusHome_3296`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No! I mean, yes, please share it with Father, if he would be willing... I imagine what few pleasant memories he has are all of you.

**中文**

不是！我是说，好的，请你和父亲一同享用，如果他愿意的话……在我的印象里，他仅有的美好回忆都与你有关。

## `ZagreusHome_3297`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Everything is somehow reassuring, coming from you, Mother.

**中文**

不知为何，你的一举一动都很令人安心，母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3298`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I thought about it more, Mother, and I would like very much if you and Father were to share this. Though, maybe don't let on where you got it, just in case?

**中文**

我又想了一下，母亲，我真的十分乐意让你和父亲一起享受这个。不过，以防万一，你能不能别说是我给的？

## `ZagreusHome_3299`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, when it came to the Olympians, you said yourself that there was only so much truth that they could tolerate. Are you sure Father would be tolerant knowing I got this for the both of you?

**中文**

可是，当你谈到奥林匹斯神的时候，你亲口说他们容不下太多真相。我拿来这个给你们，你真的确定父亲会对此置之不理吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3300`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Haha, well! Just as long as someone puts it to good use.

**中文**

哈哈，好吧！只要某人善加利用它就行了。

## `ZagreusHome_3301`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I know. I love you, too, Mother.

**中文**

我知道。我也爱你，母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3302`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mother, you'll forgive my asking but... your mother use to call you by a different name, is that right...?

**中文**

母亲，这么问可能很唐突，不过……外婆以前给你起了另外一个名字，是这样吗……？

## `ZagreusHome_3303`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Your mortal father, right...? So he called you Persephone. What was he like?

**中文**

是你的凡人父亲，是吗……？所以，是他给你起名叫珀尔塞福涅，他是个什么样的人？

## `ZagreusHome_3304`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Demeter mentioned him a bit to me. She seems... I don't know, but I don't sense that she regrets the relationship or anything. Just sad about the outcome I think.

**中文**

得墨忒尔倒是和我提起过他，她看起来……我也说不好，不过我觉得，她对这段关系并不后悔，只是对这样的结果很难过。

## `ZagreusHome_3305`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

At least we've some of that in us.

**中文**

至少我们有一部分那样的品质。

## `ZagreusHome_3306`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Everything was all completely fine, Mother! Well taken care of, even. I look forward to doing it again!

**中文**

一切都非常好，母亲！所有的事情都照顾得很好，我希望能再来一次！

## `ZagreusHome_3307`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Think nothing of it.

**中文**

别放在心上。

## `ZagreusHome_3308`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I, erm... I couldn't make it all the way there, Mother. It's like... without your presence on the surface, I'm unable to hang on for very long at all.

**中文**

我，呃……我已经没办法再回到那里了，母亲。就好像是……你离开了地上世界之后，我就根本没办法再在上面呆太久了。

## `ZagreusHome_3309`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I will. Thank you, Mother.

**中文**

我会的，谢谢你，母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3310`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Do I hate him...? Mother, I don't know that I hate anybody, really. The tales of him and the Olympians rending their immortal Titan parents to bits... I can't relate to how they must have felt. But, I resented him, for sure. And... I don't know if I'll ever get over that, entirely.

**中文**

我恨他吗……？母亲，我觉得我谁都不恨，真的。关于他和奥林匹斯众神的故事，他们合力杀死了他们的父母，那些不朽的泰坦……我这点怒火和他们比起来，恐怕要差远了。不过我对他有怨气，这是肯定的，而且……我也不知道以后能不能完全化解这股怨气。

## `ZagreusHome_3311`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sometimes I think he tried.

**中文**

有时候我觉得他也努力过了。

## `ZagreusHome_3312`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, it's been all right, I guess. I think he's set in some of his ways. But at least we have some sort of understanding now. Even if he acts like everything is still the same when I'm out there on the job.

**中文**

我猜都挺好的吧。我觉得他有些事情很固执，但至少我们多多少少可以相互理解了。即便是我工作的时候，他和以前一点都没变，也不要紧。

## `ZagreusHome_3313`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yes. I'm glad for you.

**中文**

是啊，我为你感到高兴。

## `ZagreusHome_3314`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, so are things with you and Father... everything all right, so far?

**中文**

唔，到现在为止，你和父亲……一切都还好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3315`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

And... what understanding is that? If you can share.

**中文**

那……是什么样的理念呢？可以告诉我吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3316`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, you're not... you're not thinking about having more children, are you...?

**中文**

等等，你们该不是……你们该不是打算生出更多孩子吧……？

## `ZagreusHome_3317`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Mother, just how long has it been snowing on the surface, do you know? Because, it seems to me that Demeter... Grandmother, I should say, she... well, it's been very cold up there each time I've been.

**中文**

母亲，我想问问，凡间的大雪已经下了多久了，你知道吗？因为我觉得得墨忒尔……我的外婆，我觉得她……呃，我每次上去的时候，都觉得很冷。

## `ZagreusHome_3318`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's not your fault. You couldn't have known. And we can fix this, right?

**中文**

这不是你的错，你也预料不到这种事情。而且咱们可以解决这个问题，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3319`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Say, Mother, how's it been lately with you and Demeter...? With Grandmother. Still getting used to thinking of her that way.

**中文**

母亲，最近和得墨忒尔相处得如何……？我是说外婆，我还在努力适应这么称呼她。

## `ZagreusHome_3320`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Ah, that's a relief. Grandmother can be rather intense, but... she's been kind to me, and very helpful, too. Do you suppose she'll ease up on the whole eternal winter thing there on the surface, now that she knows you're safe?

**中文**

啊，这我就放心了。外婆有时候会咄咄逼人，不过……她一直都对我很好，也一直都在帮我。如今，她知道你平安无事了，你觉得她会不会让凡间无尽的寒冬缓和一些呢？

## `ZagreusHome_3321`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

At least she forgave.

**中文**

至少她还是选择了原谅。

## `ZagreusHome_3322`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

She's always managed, and quite gracefully at that. Never seen her break a sweat over anything. Even when I confronted her about you. Though, you two seem closer than I realized, I think.

**中文**

她总是井井有条，而且非常优雅。我从来没见过她因为任何事情流汗，就算是我去质问她关于你的事情，她也面不改色。不过我觉得，你们之间的关系比我想象得更加亲密。

## `ZagreusHome_3323`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm sure she did.

**中文**

她肯定会很欢迎的。

## `ZagreusHome_3324`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Mother, I think you're doing great. And I'm sure you know it's not a competition between you and Nyx. I just get the benefit of having two great mothers rather than one. Or zero.

**中文**

母亲，我觉得你做得很好啊。而且我确定，你明白你并不需要和倪克斯比什么。能有两位了不起的母亲照顾我，总比只有一位，或者根本没有要好。

## `ZagreusHome_3325`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hahaha, it's a deal.

**中文**

哈哈哈，这么说定了。

## `ZagreusHome_3326`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yes, I think so. Thanks, Mother.

**中文**

是啊，我想也是。谢谢，母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3327`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They are. Thank you, Mother.

**中文**

一切都好，谢谢，母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3328`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

She does. She's great, Mother.

**中文**

是啊，她真的很了不起，母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3329`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Urgh... yeah....

**中文**

呃……是啊……

## `ZagreusHome_3330`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Most definitely, yes.

**中文**

一点都没错，是啊。

## `ZagreusHome_3331`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I think so. If not for her... I can't even imagine, really. Did Nyx ever speak to you of Chaos, before I was born?

**中文**

是啊，如果没有她……我都想象不出会是什么样子，真的。我出生之前，倪克斯有没有和你提起过卡俄斯的事情？

## `ZagreusHome_3332`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Least I could do.

**中文**

至少这是我该做的。

## `ZagreusHome_3333`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you. He more than deserved it.

**中文**

谢谢你，这绝对是他应得的。

## `ZagreusHome_3334`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Is it even possible, for Father to return a mortal back to life if he so chose? Just by letting their shade escape, or something?

**中文**

如果父亲愿意的话，他真的能让凡人的灵魂回到凡间吗？比如说，放幽灵们逃跑之类的？

## `ZagreusHome_3335`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, we were so moved? You were there when Orpheus came to try and rescue Eurydice?

**中文**

等等，深深地打动了我们？俄耳甫斯打算救走欧律狄刻的时候，你也在场吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3336`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'd no idea.

**中文**

我想象不出来。

## `ZagreusHome_3337`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Mother, one thing I was wondering about was, how did you summon Charon to your surface residence? Especially without, you know, being dead.

**中文**

母亲，有件事我一直很好奇。你是怎么把卡戎召唤到你在凡间的住处的？尤其是，我是说，你还没死。

## `ZagreusHome_3338`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No idea what a calling card is, but, OK!

**中文**

我不知道“名片”是什么东西，不过，一切顺利就好！

## `ZagreusHome_3339`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Day-to-day, how can you even tell it's day around here, anyway, Mother?

**中文**

没日没夜？你是怎么知道什么时候是白天，什么时候是晚上的，母亲？

## `ZagreusHome_3340`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Never! Kidding. Of course.

**中文**

绝对没有！开玩笑呢，当然了。

## `ZagreusHome_3341`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So, Mother, all the pomegranates around here... those are your doing?

**中文**

母亲，这里所有的石榴……都是你种的吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3342`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...Fruit trees, huh. I grew up around all these poms and never thought much of it. Are there many other types of fruit trees on the surface, then?

**中文**

……果树，嗯，我长大的地方到处都是石榴树，可我却从来没怎么考虑过这个问题。那就是说，凡间还有很多其它的果树吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3343`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I like them, too.

**中文**

我也喜欢石榴。

## `ZagreusHome_3344`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

This garden isn't quite the sight yours was back on the surface, is it, Mother.

**中文**

这里的花园看起来不如上面的漂亮，是吧，母亲？

## `ZagreusHome_3345`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It was totally off limits to me. Though I guess Father must have taken care to maintain it as best he could.

**中文**

父亲根本不允许我来这里，不过我猜，他肯定尽他所能地照顾这座花园吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3346`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sounds good.

**中文**

听起来不错。

## `ZagreusHome_3347`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, that painting in Father's... I mean your bedchambers, it turned out rather well, didn't it? Some very talented shades around here.

**中文**

你知道，父亲卧室里那副画……我是说，你卧室里的画，其实画的还挺不错的，是吧？对明暗的处理很有天分。

## `ZagreusHome_3348`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Still can't believe he let me hold his spear.

**中文**

我还是不敢相信，他真的把他的长枪给我了。

## `ZagreusHome_3349`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mother's away I guess.

**中文**

我猜母亲不在这里。

## `ZagreusHome_3350`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mother's not here now, huh.

**中文**

看来母亲不在这里，嗯。

## `ZagreusHome_3351`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Guess Mother's off someplace.

**中文**

看来母亲去别的地方了。

## `ZagreusHome_3352`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mother must still be on Olympus.

**中文**

母亲肯定还在奥林匹斯山上呢。

## `ZagreusHome_3353`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mother's up on some mountaintop.

**中文**

母亲去了某座山上。

## `ZagreusHome_3354`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I did, I... she was there. She was... exactly where you said she'd be, but... I had so little time with her, I... was unable to survive up there, for very long. I had so many questions left for her!

**中文**

我找到她了，我……她在那里，她就在……你说她会在的地方，可是……我能和她在一起的时间并不多，我……没办法在上面呆太长时间，我还有好多问题没问过她呢！

## `ZagreusHome_3355`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

But... she is... she is so far away, I... don't know if I can.

**中文**

可是……她……她离我太远了。我……不知道能不能做得到。

## `ZagreusHome_3356`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You're right, Nyx. I'll find her again.

**中文**

你说得没错，倪克斯，我会再找到她的。

## `ZagreusHome_3357`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I have to know, Nyx... why not tell me I would never last up on the surface? That I'd just waste away and die! Wind up back here, regardless of how skillfully I fought! Father meant it with the 'there is no escape' thing after all?

**中文**

我必须知道，倪克斯……你为什么不告诉我，我在地上世界活不了多久？我会变得越来越虚弱，一命呜呼！无论我的战斗技术多么高超，我都只能回到这里！我父亲说“没人能逃出这个地方”，就是这个意思吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3358`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My father's fate? But he is smugly waiting for me every single time I make it there, and seems quite healthy by then, all in all! Though... you must mean, the further we get from this realm... the worse we get. Because he cast his lot... inheriting the Underworld?

**中文**

我父亲的命运？可我每次到那里的时候，他都会洋洋得意地等着我，而且他看起来都一点事都没有！不过……你肯定是想说，我们离开冥界越远……状况就会变得越差。因为他抽到的签……就是掌管冥界，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3359`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yes. Yes, I would.

**中文**

好的，好的，我会的。

## `ZagreusHome_3360`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

As mortals do...

**中文**

就像凡人一样……

## `ZagreusHome_3361`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Of course, Nyx, though... she said as much already, about you. I was about to tell you, I... she says she misses you, and Cerberus, and... I don't know who else. Why did she really leave, you think? Besides because of me? So what if she thought I died at birth?

**中文**

当然了，倪克斯，不过……她已经说了很多关于你的事情。我正打算告诉你呢，我……她说她很想念你和刻耳柏洛斯，还有……我也不知道还有谁了。你觉得她为什么要离开呢？仅仅是因为我吗？她那时会不会以为我一出生就死了？

## `ZagreusHome_3362`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

A war... wars have been fought for less, I understand. And, Father must have been a real burden, too.

**中文**

挑起战争……我明白，就算是更微不足道的事情都能引发战争。而且，我父亲肯定也是个要命的负担吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3363`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You've said more than enough. I'll see you, Nyx.

**中文**

你说得已经完全足够了，下次见吧，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_3364`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That means a lot to me, Nyx. Though I never could have done any of this without your help. Even after I lashed out at you after I learned the truth about Mother, you never stopped caring for me... and you put yourself directly in harm's way, defying Father's will. Why do all that, for me?

**中文**

对我来说，这些话意义重大，倪克斯。可是没有你的帮助，我根本做不到这些事情。即便我得知了母亲的真相之后大发雷霆，你也从未停止对我的关心……你还违背了父亲的意愿，把自己推到了风口浪尖。为什么要为我付出这一切呢？

## `ZagreusHome_3365`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

To get on better terms with the Olympians. They always used to frown on the Underworld, but... now that they know us, they seem much more willing to get involved and help with things.

**中文**

你希望能和奥林匹斯众神处好关系。他们以前总是对冥界不屑一顾，不过……如今他们已经和我们熟悉了，似乎也更愿意帮我们处理这里的事务了。

## `ZagreusHome_3366`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx... I love you, too. Thank you for everything.

**中文**

倪克斯……我也爱你，谢谢你为我做的一切。

## `ZagreusHome_3367`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I scarce believe it, too. What happens now? Pick up the pieces and begin again?

**中文**

我也不敢相信。现在该怎么办呢？收拾残局，重新开始吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3368`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sounds good.

**中文**

听起来不错。

## `ZagreusHome_3369`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How has it been for you of late, Nyx? Having Mother here again, for old time's sake?

**中文**

最近怎么样，倪克斯？母亲又回来了，你们可以好好叙叙旧？

## `ZagreusHome_3370`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, right. So, are you getting on OK, is everything all right?

**中文**

哦，好吧。所以，你们相处得还好吗？一切都还好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3371`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think so, too.

**中文**

我也这么想。

## `ZagreusHome_3372`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's more of a formality, I think. My responsibilities, such as they are, remain quite similar to those that got me to this point. But thank you, Nyx. It's strange... to think that I can serve the House, by trying to break from it, time after time.

**中文**

我觉得，这变得更加正规了。我的责任正是如此，和我以前一直在做的事情没什么差别。不过我要谢谢你，倪克斯。这种感觉很奇怪……想想看，我居然能靠一次次从这里逃出去来为这座圣殿提供服务。

## `ZagreusHome_3373`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, it does beat standing around in my chambers aimlessly. And... I daresay it feels rather good not to feel utterly incompetent, for a change.

**中文**

好吧，这总比呆在房间里无所事事要好。而且……我觉得，能感觉到自己有能力接受变化，这种感觉还挺好的。

## `ZagreusHome_3374`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Likewise, Nyx.

**中文**

我也一样，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_3375`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're right. We have to do something. We can't just keep pretending with them.

**中文**

你说得没错。我们不能一直把他们蒙在鼓里，必须做点什么才行。

## `ZagreusHome_3376`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I couldn't agree more. Fortunately, Mother has a plan! Asked me to get in touch with everybody on Olympus, one by one.

**中文**

我完全同意，不过幸好母亲有个计划！她要我一个接一个，和奥林匹斯山上的每位神明都取得联系。

## `ZagreusHome_3377`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

She says she promises to tell us soon.

**中文**

她说，她答应很快就告诉我们。

## `ZagreusHome_3378`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

A memorable night it was, indeed, Nyx! For most of us, at least. Dionysus, he won't remember any of it, I don't think. But, it was really good to see everyone having a good time. Getting along as though they didn't hold a grudge! So, then, we're clear, I think?

**中文**

确实是个难忘的夜晚，倪克斯！至少对大部分人来说是这样的，不过我估计迪奥尼索司肯定什么都记不住吧。不过很高兴看到大家欢聚一堂，都相处得很好，就好像没什么过节一样！所以说，我们已经没问题了，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3379`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, let's enjoy it while it does, I guess.

**中文**

那我猜，就趁它还在的时候，享受当下吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3380`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

She's doing well, I think. It's nice to visit her. Her residence is beautiful. A little home nestled amid the cold. All of these plants, in bloom! It's difficult even to describe. And very bright, as well.

**中文**

我觉得她过得很好，能见到她我很高兴。她的屋子很漂亮，是寒风之中的一栋小屋，而且所有的植物都盛开着！我都不知道该怎么描述。而且，那里很明亮。

## `ZagreusHome_3381`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They are.

**中文**

是这样的。

## `ZagreusHome_3382`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, why is it the mortals come here knowing all about Olympus, while knowing so little about what goes on in our realm? Is there something more we could be doing to help spread the word?

**中文**

倪克斯，为什么来这里的凡人对奥林匹斯山上的事情了如指掌，可对我们这里知之甚少？有没有什么办法能让大家更了解我们？

## `ZagreusHome_3383`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, I... I don't know, it's just, for all the famous gods and monsters and the like, it seems to me that someone such as you, who does so much, why... you should be at least as known as any one of them!

**中文**

哦，我……我也不知道，只是大家对那些著名的神灵和怪物都很了解。我觉得，像你这样付出了这么多的人……你至少也应该和他们一样有名才对！

## `ZagreusHome_3384`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I guess obscurity does have its perks.

**中文**

我猜不为人知也是有好处吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3385`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Harm to the Queen, wait... what are you talking about?

**中文**

让冥后受到伤害？等等……你在说什么呢？

## `ZagreusHome_3386`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No! Of course not. She... seems content to be all on her own.

**中文**

不！当然不是了。她……看起来一个人自得其乐。

## `ZagreusHome_3387`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The featured house servant board. Proud to have never once made the cut.

**中文**

是圣殿优秀员工的展板。我一次都没上过榜，真让我自豪。

## `ZagreusHome_3388`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I believe it, Nyx. I'm grateful that you put us into contact. I know you took a considerable risk in reaching out.

**中文**

我相信会是这样，倪克斯。我很高兴你让我们取得了联系，我知道你这么做冒了很大的风险。

## `ZagreusHome_3389`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yes. We are.

**中文**

我明白了。

## `ZagreusHome_3390`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm grateful that you put us into contact, Nyx. I know you took a considerable risk in reaching out.

**中文**

我很高兴你让我们取得了联系，倪克斯。我知道你这么做冒了很大的风险。

## `ZagreusHome_3391`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Glad there's no question anymore.

**中文**

我很高兴已经没有问题了。

## `ZagreusHome_3392`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

They all still think I'm headed to join them on Olympus. That's why they're helping me.

**中文**

他们依然以为我打算去奥林匹斯山和他们相聚，所以他们才会帮我。

## `ZagreusHome_3393`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's getting complicated pretty fast...

**中文**

马上就要开始有复杂的纠葛了……

## `ZagreusHome_3394`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx... the Olympians have been immensely helpful, but... did they really have to get mixed up in all this? I could have gone off on my own.

**中文**

倪克斯……奥林匹斯众神一直都在帮助我，不过……这件事情真的要把他们牵扯进来吗？我一个人也可以上路。

## `ZagreusHome_3395`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**
