But I'm misleading them. Taking advantage of their good graces trying to find Mother. What if they find out?

**中文**

可我在误导他们，我在利用他们的好意来寻找我的母亲。万一他们发现了怎么办？

## `ZagreusHome_3396`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Understood.

**中文**

……我明白了。

## `ZagreusHome_3397`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I did, I... she was there. She was... where you said she'd be. And, more than I imagined, but... I had so little time with her. I can't survive up there, for very long. Why didn't you tell me I'd just die, no matter what?

**中文**

我找到了，我……她就在那里……在你说的地方，而且比我想象得更美好。可是……我和她在一起的时间太短了，我没办法在上面存活太久。你为什么不告诉我，说无论发生什么，我都会死呢？

## `ZagreusHome_3398`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

My father's fate? But he is smugly waiting for me every single time I make it there, and seems quite healthy by then, all in all! Though... maybe the farther we get from this realm... the worse we get. Because he cast his lot... inheriting the Underworld?

**中文**

我父亲的命运？可我每次到那里的时候，他都会洋洋得意地等着我，而且他看起来都一点事都没有！不过……或许我们离开冥界越远……状况就会变得越差。因为他抽到的签……就是掌管冥界，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3399`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...Yes. Yes, I would. I had so many questions left for her!

**中文**

……会的，我会的。我还有那么多问题想要问她呢！

## `ZagreusHome_3400`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

As mortals do...

**中文**

就像凡人一样……

## `ZagreusHome_3401`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx... why did you never send word to Mother that I lived? You knew at least approximately where she went. You and Father... you could have spared her so much pain!

**中文**

倪克斯……你为什么从来没告诉过我母亲，说我还活着？你至少大概知道她去了哪里。你和父亲……你们本可以让她没这么痛苦才对！

## `ZagreusHome_3402`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Urgh...

**中文**

呃啊……

## `ZagreusHome_3403`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx... you saved my life. Gave me life. When I was born. Father told me. I don't know what to say. Why didn't you tell me?

**中文**

倪克斯……我出生的时候，是你救了我，赐予了我生命。是父亲告诉我的，我不知道该说什么。你为什么从来都没告诉过我？

## `ZagreusHome_3404`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I never should have been born... is that what the Fates wanted? Did you defy your own daughters?

**中文**

我从一开始就不应该生下来……这就是命运女神想要的结果吗？你是违抗了你女儿们的意愿吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3405`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

...Thank them for me.

**中文**

……替我谢谢他们吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3406`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, my mother was quite concerned to learn Olympus has been aiding me. Did you... haven't you... put her in danger by involving them? Was there no other way?

**中文**

倪克斯，听说奥林匹斯众神都在帮我，我的母亲很介意。把他们全都牵扯进来，这样会不会……让她遇到危险？没有别的办法了吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3407`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Ugh, what a mess. How are we going to fix this?

**中文**

呃，真是一团乱麻啊。咱们该怎么解决这个问题？

## `ZagreusHome_3408`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll be careful...

**中文**

我会小心的……

## `ZagreusHome_3409`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Of course, Nyx, though... why did she really leave the Underworld, do you think? Besides because of me. So what if she thought I died at birth?

**中文**

我当然明白，倪克斯，不过……你觉得她为什么要离开冥界呢？仅仅是因为我吗？她那时会不会以为我一出生就死了？

## `ZagreusHome_3410`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Mother doesn't want to see me again. I mean... she asked me not to return. Thinks the risk is too great.

**中文**

母亲不想再见我了。我是说……她希望我别再去找她了，她觉得这太冒险了。

## `ZagreusHome_3411`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...I'm going back.

**中文**

……我还是要找她。

## `ZagreusHome_3412`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father loved her, didn't he? Mother, I mean. Faith like that... he's just stubborn enough to hold onto the idea of her, isn't he? Though, what she saw in him, I'm not sure.

**中文**

父亲很爱她，是吗？我是说，爱我的母亲。他怀着强烈的信念……固执地思念着她，是吧？至于母亲到底看上了父亲的哪点，我就不清楚了。

## `ZagreusHome_3413`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sounds like a lot of pressure on Mother to fix all his failings... but, it sounds as though she cared for him, as well. And you, and Cerberus. She said she misses you.

**中文**

听起来像是母亲为了改正他的缺点承受了巨大的压力……不过，听起来她也很在乎他。她也很在乎你，还有刻耳柏洛斯。她说她很想你。

## `ZagreusHome_3414`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Nyx? You don't suppose Charon would hold it against me if... we had a little fight? To the death, I mean.

**中文**

呃，倪克斯？你觉得卡戎会不会记恨我，假设……我们小小地打了一架？我是说，打到至死方休的那种。

## `ZagreusHome_3415`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It... kind of already happened. And, I can confirm he's rather strong. Something I can do to make it up to him?

**中文**

我们……算是已经打过了吧，而且我觉得他很强壮。我应该怎么弥补和他之间的关系？

## `ZagreusHome_3416`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A friendly rivalry it is, then!

**中文**

那我们就是竞争对手了！

## `ZagreusHome_3417`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You could have told me more important things. Mother left because she never even knew I was alive. Thanks to you! She's got a lot of questions, herself, as you can probably imagine. I'm headed back to her. You going to cooperate?

**中文**

你本可以告诉我更重要的事。母亲之所以离开是因为她不知道我还活着，这都是因为你！你完全可以想象她心中有多少疑问。现在我要去往她身边，你或许乐意放我一马？

## `ZagreusHome_3418`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Let me go. Let me find her. She demands it!

**中文**

让我走。让我找到她。这也是她所希望的！

## `ZagreusHome_3419`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I never stopped to think about why pomegranates grew here in the Underworld. Mother's doing from before, then? And all that stuff she told the Olympians, about how they bind you to this place... it's all nonsense?

**中文**

我从一直都在想，为什么石榴可以在冥界生长，这是母亲的成果吗？还有，她和奥林匹斯众神说的那些事情，说他们把你束缚在这个地方……都是胡说八道吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3420`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You kept them growing all this time... flourishing, even, because of her.

**中文**

你一直种植着这些石榴……甚至让它们蓬勃生长，都是因为她。

## `ZagreusHome_3421`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yeah, well... I'm to attempt to escape this place by any means necessary, right? I figure if I can call on Olympus for aid, maybe some of my friends can help me in a pinch, as well.

**中文**

是的，这么说吧……我打算采取一切手段逃出这个地方，是吧？我觉得如果我呼唤奥林匹斯众神寻求援助的话，或许我的一部分朋友可以在危难时刻助我一臂之力。

## `ZagreusHome_3422`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, really? You're saying it's OK for your own loyal subjects to side against you up there? You won't take it out on them?

**中文**

等等，真的吗？你刚刚说即使你那些忠实下属在那里与你为敌也没关系？你真的不会追究他们的责任？

## `ZagreusHome_3423`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Commendable, me? An unseen power, huh. Surprised you're giving me the credit, Father.

**中文**

值得表扬，我吗？看不见的力量，嗯。你居然会表扬我，这真让我意外，父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3424`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So the more havoc I wreak in the Underworld, the more mortals hear about all the unpleasantness, and... it makes them fearful of this place? If more of them lead upstanding lives, it'll keep Tartarus from overflowing, I guess.

**中文**

所以说，我在冥界造成的破坏越多，凡人们就会听到越多的不满，然后……他们就会越害怕这个地方？如果能让更多人过上正直的生活的话，说不定塔耳塔罗斯就不会人满为患了。

## `ZagreusHome_3425`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Would you believe it if I told you she wants to have all of them over for Ambrosia?

**中文**

如果我说，她打算请每个人都喝些仙馔密酒的话，你能相信吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3426`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I did. But... it wasn't a one-way trip. I'll just be visiting from time to time.

**中文**

我去过了，不过……就算去了也得回来。我打算时不时地去见她一面。

## `ZagreusHome_3427`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I don't entirely understand yet either. Trust me on this while I get it sorted, but... looks like I'm not going anywhere. For very long.

**中文**

我也无法完全明白。相信我，我会想办法把问题弄清楚的，不过……看起来在很长一段时间之内，我都不会有什么进展了。

## `ZagreusHome_3428`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I know the feeling.

**中文**

我明白这种感受。

## `ZagreusHome_3429`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I mean, you're still trying just as hard as ever to prevent me from escaping past the threshold of your realm, so, I don't know if I'd exactly call the matter settled. But... it does feel different, now.

**中文**

我是想说，你依然和以前一样，尽你所能地阻止我逃出你的王国。所以，我也不知道这能不能算是事情都解决了，不过……现在的感觉确实和以前不一样了。

## `ZagreusHome_3430`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yeah, yeah.

**中文**

是啊，是啊。

## `ZagreusHome_3431`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I am. I figured it's the only way I could continue interrupting you while you try to do your job. You work too hard, Than, live a little, right?

**中文**

是啊，我发现只有这样，我才能趁你工作的时候打扰你。你工作实在是太卖力了，塔纳。享受一下生活，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3432`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll hold you to that.

**中文**

我要保证你说话算话。

## `ZagreusHome_3433`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thanatos, the world outside... didn't you say that it would make you sick, being out there for very long at all? I understand now what you meant by that. Beautiful place and all, but... it's good to be back.

**中文**

塔纳托斯，外面的世界……你之前不是说过，如果在那里呆得太久，就会感到浑身不舒服吗？我现在终于明白你说的是什么意思了。那里确实很美，一切都很好，但是……能回来真是太好了。

## `ZagreusHome_3434`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Couldn't if I tried!

**中文**

是啊，我已经体会过了！

## `ZagreusHome_3435`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, meant to ask... Charon was there to take Mother and me home the one time. So is he... has he been working for you? All part of some elaborate plan, or something?

**中文**

倪克斯，我想问的是……之前是卡戎把我和母亲送回来的。所以他……他一直都在侍奉你吗？这一切都是某个精心设计的计划的一部分吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3436`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Elaborate plan, then. Got it.

**中文**

是个煞费苦心的计划啊，我明白了。

## `ZagreusHome_3437`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Do you enjoy Orpheus' singing, Nyx? He seems a bit concerned that you might not.

**中文**

你喜欢俄耳甫斯的歌声吗，倪克斯？他好像有点怕你不喜欢。

## `ZagreusHome_3438`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

So that's a yes.

**中文**

也就是说，你很喜欢。

## `ZagreusHome_3439`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good work, Hypnos!

**中文**

干得漂亮，修普诺斯！

## `ZagreusHome_3440`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, it certainly took some doing, and several of those really were rather difficult, and took some time, but... what's so special about getting through them all? It's a list, right?

**中文**

这么说吧，这确实要花上一些工夫。而且其中一部分事情相当困难，也花了不少时间，不过……完成其中所有的内容，到底有什么特别之处呢？这只是份清单，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3441`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Any new prophecies need fulfilling, they know where to find me.

**中文**

如果有什么需要实现的新预言，他们知道该去哪里找我。

## `ZagreusHome_3442`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you.

**中文**

谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_3443`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How are you and Chaos getting on, lately, Nyx? Sounds like they expected an awful lot from you before. That you should shine as bright as day, something like that? I can't imagine.

**中文**

你最近和卡俄斯相处得怎么样，倪克斯？听起来祂之前很想听到你的消息。祂说你就应该想白昼一样熠熠生辉之类的？我实在想象不出来。

## `ZagreusHome_3444`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What limits are those? You seem capable of anything. Probably even shining bright.

**中文**

你还有什么极限呢？你看起来什么事情都做得到，说不定在夜晚熠熠生辉也做得到。

## `ZagreusHome_3445`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not me!

**中文**

我可没有！

## `ZagreusHome_3446`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You really asked Dusa to come back. Thank you, Nyx! I'm very grateful. And Megaera. Probably everybody here! I'm sure she won't let you down.

**中文**

你真的让杜莎回来了。谢谢你，倪克斯！我真的很感激，墨纪拉也是，说不定这里的所有人都很感激！我敢肯定她不会让你失望的。

## `ZagreusHome_3447`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You'll be impressed. You'll see.

**中文**

会让你大吃一惊的，等着吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3448`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sure was nice to see you and Hypnos speaking to each other. Praise from you must mean the world to him.

**中文**

看到你和修普诺斯聊天，我觉得很高兴。对他而言，你的赞赏肯定相当重要。

## `ZagreusHome_3449`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Have to say that sounds rather harsh when you put it that way, Nyx.

**中文**

不得不说，这种说法听起来很严厉，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusHome_3450`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm sure he will.

**中文**

我相信他会的。

## `ZagreusHome_3451`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Someday I'd like to meet more of your children, Nyx. Eris, Nemesis... Thanatos told me of several others. Have they ever visited the House?

**中文**

希望以后能与你其他的孩子们见面，倪克斯，像厄里斯、涅墨西斯……塔纳托斯和我提起过的其中几位，他们来过这座圣殿吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3452`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well I think you did great, if you ask me.

**中文**

在我看来，你已经做得相当好了。

## `ZagreusHome_3453`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Is this about all the House Contractor decorations? Look, it's one thing if Father objects, but if you do as well... just tell me, and I can have it taken down.

**中文**

这一切都是和圣殿承包商的装饰品有关吗？听我说，父亲反对我一点都不在乎，但如果你也不喜欢的话……告诉我就好，我会撤下来的。

## `ZagreusHome_3454`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Probably my mother's.

**中文**

或许这一点和我母亲很像。

## `ZagreusHome_3455`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, this feast Mother has planned... would it be all right with you if I were to invite Chaos?

**中文**

倪克斯，关于母亲策划的这场盛宴……如果我把卡俄斯也邀请来，你觉得可以吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3456`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

As long as it's OK.

**中文**

只要你觉得可以就好。

## `ZagreusHome_3457`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I was pleased to see even Chaos was able to attend our little feast there with the Olympians, in spirit, anyway! Were you able to chat at all?

**中文**

就连卡俄斯都以它的灵魂形态，参加我们与奥林匹斯众神的小小盛典了，我感到很开心！你们有没有聊上几句？

## `ZagreusHome_3458`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Of course!

**中文**

不客气！

## `ZagreusHome_3459`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Never thought that the administrative chamber would have been so invaluable, Nyx. Though, I have you to thank for all of it.

**中文**

我从来没想过管理大厅居然是这么重要的地方，倪克斯。不过，这一切都得感谢你才是。

## `ZagreusHome_3460`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I hope not.

**中文**

希望如此吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3461`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It must be difficult for her... returning to Olympus. She had no love for it at all. I hope she'll be all right.

**中文**

这对她来说肯定很难受……回到奥林匹斯山。她一点都不喜欢那里，我希望她一切都好。

## `ZagreusHome_3462`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...I'll miss her.

**中文**

……我会想念她的。

## `ZagreusHome_3463`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't know where I'd be without it. Probably still in Tartarus trying to get past Meg. I only wish I could get even stronger.

**中文**

要是没了它，我都不知道自己会在哪里，说不定还在塔耳塔罗斯和墨纪苦战呢。我只希望能变得更强大。

## `ZagreusHome_3464`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You really think so?

**中文**

你真的这么想吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3465`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

United with the Darkness? I've just... been picking it up here and there. Although, for quite a while, since you mention it! I'm not sure that I feel any different, though.

**中文**

与黑暗宝石融为一体？我只不过是……到处捡捡罢了。不过既然你提到了，那我得说，我已经捡了不少了！但我并不确定身上有什么变化。

## `ZagreusHome_3466`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Huh. I suppose.

**中文**

嗯，我想是吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3467`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father... the Pact... you were so blasted strong. I had no idea! All those other times we fought... I can't believe that you were holding back. You were desperate to stop me, you could have!

**中文**

父亲……关于惩罚契约……你实在是强得不像话，我之前一点都不知道。我们交手过那么多次……我真不敢相信你一直都在放水。你真的是很想阻止我，你根本就可以！

## `ZagreusHome_3468`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Must have been all our vicious battles to the death? Or maybe it's from Mother being back in your life?

**中文**

是因为我们之间的惨烈战斗吗？还是因为母亲回到了你身边？

## `ZagreusHome_3469`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Bet I can take you.

**中文**

希望我能胜你一筹吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3470`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles... if a friend of yours were in need of assistance, but the only way you could assist them was to somehow change the terms of an ancient pact Father probably forced on them, what would you do?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯……如果你的朋友需要帮助，但你唯一能帮助他的办法，就是想办法改变父亲很久以前强加在那个人身上的契约，你会怎么做？

## `ZagreusHome_3471`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx... you're right. She knows everything! Wouldn't hurt to ask her, I suppose...

**中文**

倪克斯……你说得没错，她什么都知道！我猜问问她应该也无妨吧……

## `ZagreusHome_3472`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's beautiful, mate. If only Eurydice could hear you right now.

**中文**

真是太美妙了，朋友。如果欧律狄克能听到你的歌声就好了。

## `ZagreusHome_3473`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Say, Father... back when you drew lots with your brothers, and wound up with the Underworld, rather than the heavens or the seas... which would you have claimed, if the choice was yours?

**中文**

我想问一下，父亲……当时你和你兄弟们抽签的时候，你抽到了这里，成了冥王，而没能成为掌管天空或海洋的神……如果你能自己选的话，你想要当哪个神？

## `ZagreusHome_3474`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Didn't you try to leave the Underworld, yourself, at some point? You once gave me that impression. I always thought it was because you hated it here.

**中文**

这么长时间以来，你自己就没试过逃出冥界吗？你之前暗示过我你也试过。我一直以为你想逃出这个地方，是因为你讨厌这里。

## `ZagreusHome_3475`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Can't wait!

**中文**

我等不及了！

## `ZagreusHome_3476`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Still showing up and lending their support. I think some of them are a little disappointed I'm not planning to come join them, though... I think they understand now that they owe the Underworld a debt. We all have to do our part, looking after the mortals. Whether alive or dead.

**中文**

一如既往。他们之中的一些神略显失望因为我并不打算加入他们，不过……我觉得他们应该已经明白他们欠冥界一个人情。在照料凡人的过程中——无论在他们生前还是死后，我们都有各自的职责。

## `ZagreusHome_3477`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They have.

**中文**

是啊。

## `ZagreusHome_3478`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Always wanted to ask you something, Father. Whose skull is that there on your shoulder? If it's not too personal.

**中文**

有一件事我想问很久了，父亲。如果你不介意的话，能不能告诉我你肩膀上的头骨是谁的？

## `ZagreusHome_3479`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So these are just the remains of the spawn of the most hideous monstrosity of all time, which you and your brothers slew, got it. I guess putting the dead in their place is all part of the job. I can hardly imagine what it was like before you and the Olympians took charge.

**中文**

所以你和你的兄弟们杀了史上最凶恶残暴的怪物，而这些就是他后代的遗骨咯？我猜你们想让他们死得其所。我简直无法想象在你和奥林匹斯众神掌管天地以前，世界是什么样的。

## `ZagreusHome_3480`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

The fabled Resources Director! So, now I get to give away the hard-earned resources I've painstakingly gathered over time?

**中文**

就是那个传说中的资源总监吗！所以，我那么长时间辛辛苦苦赚来的、来之不易的资源，我得把它们捐出去了吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3481`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What's the point in rising above my current station? I thought our work was to be done in secrecy.

**中文**

提高我的职级到底有什么意义？我还以为咱们的工作得私下里来做呢。

## `ZagreusHome_3482`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sounds great!

**中文**

听起来真不错！

## `ZagreusHome_3483`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you.

**中文**

谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_3484`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You always thought too little of me, Father. Though, thank you for taking note of that. I figured I ought to give back to the realm that made me what I am.

**中文**

你一直都太不把我当回事了，父亲。不过，谢谢你注意到了这点。我觉得我应该报答这个生我养我的王国才对。

## `ZagreusHome_3486`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Welcome home, Mother! How was your trip, things go all right with you and Demeter? With... Grandmother. Still getting used to thinking of her that way.

**中文**

欢迎回家，母亲！这一路还顺利吗？你和得墨忒尔的关系怎么样了？我是说……外婆，我正在努力适应这么称呼她。

## `ZagreusHome_3487`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Mother, you'll forgive my asking, but... your mother used to call you by a different name, didn't she?

**中文**

母亲，这么问可能很唐突，不过……外婆以前给你起了另外一个名字，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3488`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Cheers, I'm glad you like it!

**中文**

太好了！很高兴你喜欢！

## `ZagreusHome_3489`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Inhale And it smells fresh, too!

**中文**

吸气 而且闻起来也很新鲜！

## `ZagreusHome_3490`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Malphon: I see you overcome the wild and make peace with death!

**中文**

玛尔封，我见到你征服了荒野，与死亡谈笑风生！

## `ZagreusHome_3491`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The hidden aspect of an ancient god-king... Gilgamesh.

**中文**

是远古神王的隐藏形态……吉尔伽美什。

## `ZagreusHome_3492`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Grant me the aspect of Gilgamesh!

**中文**

赐给我吉尔伽美什的形态！

## `ZagreusHome_3493`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Gilgamesh.

**中文**

吉尔伽美什。

## `ZagreusHome_3494`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Gilgamesh...!

**中文**

吉尔伽美什……！

## `ZagreusHome_3495`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey watch it, Hypnos, that's my mother you're talking about!

**中文**

嘿，注意点，修普诺斯，你说的那个人可是我的母亲！

## `ZagreusHome_3496`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We don't.

**中文**

咱们可没有。

## `ZagreusHome_3497`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You should have been there, Hypnos! Not that I noticed your absence, though, what happened? Other pressing business?

**中文**

你当时也应该去才对，修普诺斯！虽然我并没注意到你缺席了，不过发生什么事情了？有更重要的事情要做吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3498`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Wow.

**中文**

哇哦。

## `ZagreusHome_3499`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hypnos... you think all these times I've died, it's because I was upset I was let go from a miserable job?

**中文**

修普诺斯……你觉得我死了那么多次是因为我很沮丧，是因为我失去了一份令人讨厌的工作，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3500`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm sure you can catch up.

**中文**

我敢肯定你能赶得上的。

## `ZagreusHome_3501`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Brilliant!

**中文**

太棒了！

## `ZagreusHome_3502`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Six hundred?

**中文**

六百次？

## `ZagreusHome_3503`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

The Bull of Minos led me to another hidden aspect, Achilles! A beastly form of the Twin Fists, belonging to an ancient god-king known as Gilgamesh. You ever hear that name? He had a half-beast friend, apparently. Like how Asterius is friend to Theseus.

**中文**

米诺斯的公牛为我解开了一个隐藏的武器形态，阿喀琉斯！是双子之拳的野兽形态，曾属于某位叫吉尔伽美什的远古神王。你听过这个名字吗？他有位半人半兽的朋友，就好像忒修斯与阿斯忒里俄斯一样……

## `ZagreusHome_3504`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, he is. He's loyal through and through to Theseus, and never holds back when we fight. But he seems to be the respect-for-his-opponent type. Unlike his companion.

**中文**

哦，他是敌人，对忒修斯忠心耿耿，在战斗中也从不手下留情。不过和他那位搭档不一样，他似乎懂得尊重对手。

## `ZagreusHome_3505`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Yes...

**中文**

很好……

## `ZagreusHome_3506`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

He has.

**中文**

他还清了。

## `ZagreusHome_3507`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oof, you showed me back there, boy. With Father on the surface? I know you were just playing, but... go easier on me, next time, will you?

**中文**

哎呀，你可让我尝到厉害了，乖狗狗。你和父亲在地面上的时候，记得吗？我知道你是在闹，不过……下次对我下手轻点，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3508`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Please...?

**中文**

拜托了……？

## `ZagreusHome_3509`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's nothing, Nyx, really. I know you've always believed in me, and it's not something I ever take for granted.

**中文**

别介意，倪克斯，真的。我知道你一直相信我，我可不打算把你的信任当做理所当然。

## `ZagreusHome_3510`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Your usefulness, what, you just mean the standing around, letting me lay into you with whatever vicious attack sequence I fancy at the time?

**中文**

你说你有用，怎么，你指的就是干站着，让我随心所欲地拿各种东西往你身上招呼吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3511`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

But only if I leave for good, isn't that right? So if I stuck around... you'd stay here, too?

**中文**

但只有我走了才会这样，对吧？所以如果我被困在这里……你也会留在这里，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3512`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sure looks that way.

**中文**

确实是这么回事。

## `ZagreusHome_3513`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, you must mean the Queen, and evidently you're aware of our relationship. You keep that quiet, understand, Skelly?

**中文**

呃嗯，你指的一定是冥后，而且很显然，你已经知道了我们之间的关系。这个秘密不准说出去，明白了吗，骨头？

## `ZagreusHome_3514`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It better be.

**中文**

最好这样。

## `ZagreusHome_3515`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It... went better than expected, all in all. I hope you didn't feel left out! It was a pretty limited guest list. Kind of a diplomatic mission, I would say. Not quite as good a time as it might have sounded from the outside.

**中文**

事情……比想象得要好，也就是这样了，希望你不会觉得被排挤在外！宾客的名单非常有限。要我说，这更像是某种外交任务之类的，实际上并不像外边传得那样其乐融融。

## `ZagreusHome_3516`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Same here.

**中文**

我也是。

## `ZagreusHome_3517`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Skelly, what with everything out in the open now, with the Queen and Charon, and all that... mind if I mention some of this to Thanatos? Charon's his brother, after all.

**中文**

嘿，骨头，既然所有的事情都公之于众了，包括皇后与卡戎的事情之类的……不介意我和塔纳托斯透露一些实情吧？毕竟他是卡戎的兄弟。

## `ZagreusHome_3518`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Appreciate you answering!

**中文**

谢谢你愿意回答！

## `ZagreusHome_3519`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Father... I don't know what to say...

**中文**

父亲……我不知道该说什么好……

## `ZagreusHome_3520`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Not here to gloat, Father. Well fought back there. I can't believe I got you.

**中文**

我不是来幸灾乐祸的，父亲大人。那真是一场精彩的战斗，难以置信我竟然打倒你了。

## `ZagreusHome_3521`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm thinking there shall.

**中文**

我想会有下次的。

## `ZagreusHome_3522`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey that's new...

**中文**

嘿，那些是新的……

## `ZagreusHome_3523`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Whoa, a Rod of Fishing? Here?

**中文**

哇，这地方还有鱼竿？

## `ZagreusHome_3524`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Schelemeus.

**中文**

瑟勒米乌斯。

## `ZagreusHome_3525`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Captain.

**中文**

船长。

## `ZagreusHome_3526`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Skelly.

**中文**

骨头。

## `ZagreusHome_3527`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's going on, Skelly.

**中文**

怎么样，骨头？

## `ZagreusHome_3528`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey.

**中文**

嘿。

## `ZagreusHome_3529`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

How's it going.

**中文**

最近怎么样？

## `ZagreusHome_3530`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

That Trout in there seems to be doing fine!

**中文**

那条鳟鱼看起来正自得其乐！

## `ZagreusHome_3531`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Look at that Bass swishing about in there.

**中文**

看那条鲈鱼在那边游来游去。

## `ZagreusHome_3532`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This Sturgeon's found himself a decent home.

**中文**

这条鲟鱼把这里当做他的安乐窝了。

## `ZagreusHome_3533`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The ever-shifting hell of Tartarus... you can't contain me, don't you know by now?

**中文**

塔耳塔罗斯不断变化的囚牢啊……你是关不住我的，你也该明白这一点了吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3534`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not like Mother's cottage, but not bad.

**中文**

和母亲的小屋不太一样，不过也不错。

## `ZagreusHome_3535`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The more I know about the Boons I'm offered, the better off I'll be.

**中文**

越了解我得到的这些祝福，情况就会对我越有利。

## `ZagreusHome_3536`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

If my father can be even more frustrating, that's something I have to see.

**中文**

如果父亲还能变得更难对付，那我一定得亲身感受感受。

## `ZagreusHome_3537`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This ought to let me ransack with more style.

**中文**

这应该能让我更潇洒地劫掠财物。

## `ZagreusHome_3538`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This moody look seems just the thing for me.

**中文**

这种富有情调的造型看起来正适合我。

## `ZagreusHome_3539`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

New one there.

**中文**

有新东西了。

## `ZagreusHome_3540`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's that one do?

**中文**

那东西是干什么用的？

## `ZagreusHome_3541`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

New keepsake.

**中文**

新的信物。

## `ZagreusHome_3542`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let's see what that one does.

**中文**

让我看看它是做什么的吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3543`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should I change things up?

**中文**

我应该做些改变吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3544`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Maybe I should switch.

**中文**

或许我应该更换一下。

## `ZagreusHome_3545`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Try another one maybe...

**中文**

试试别的吧……

## `ZagreusHome_3546`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Could try a different one...

**中文**

不如来试试别的……

## `ZagreusHome_3547`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Look, if you decide to start strumming on that lyre again, I'm sure nobody here is going to mind. Though you take care, OK?

**中文**

听我说，如果你打算再次奏响里拉琴的话，我敢肯定这里没人会介意的。不过你要多保重，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3548`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How about The Lament? You used to play that one a lot!

**中文**

不如就《哀婉之歌》吧？你以前总喜欢唱这首！

## `ZagreusHome_3549`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How about In the Blood? That's one of your old hits, right?

**中文**

不如就唱《鲜血之中》吧，这可是你以前最热门的曲子，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3550`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How about Argonauts, didn't you sail with them?

**中文**

不如就《阿尔戈船英雄》吧？你不是和他们一起出海了吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3551`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's been a while, Resources Director. I'll be sure to pay you a visit whenever I'm swimming in Underworld valuables and want to feel better about myself in my new role.

**中文**

已经有一段时间没见面了，资源总监。等我在冥界的宝藏中尽情畅游的时候，我一定会见你一面的，顺便祝愿我会更喜欢自己的新职位。

## `ZagreusHome_3552`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I found her, boy! My mother! Persephone, you remember her, don't you? She was pleased to hear about you. But I couldn't stay for long, and I have to find her again, so... guess I'll see you at the Temple, then?

**中文**

我找到她了，乖狗狗！我的母亲！珀尔塞福涅，你还记得她，是吧？她很高兴听到你的消息。不过我在上面待不了太久，所以我打算再去找她一次。所以我猜……到时候神殿里见吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3553`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, look at you, boy! Never seen you quite so perky, relatively speaking of course, big tough guy like yourself. You must have missed Mother just as much as she missed you. Well, you've got plenty of time for catching up on things, now!

**中文**

哎哟，看看你，乖狗狗！我从来没有见过你这么活泼。当然，对你这样的硬汉来说，只是相对活泼一些。你一定很想念母亲吧？她也一样很想念你。好了，你们现在有足够的时间可以叙叙旧了！

## `ZagreusHome_3554`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

We're all very proud of you, boy! You were so well-behaved at the feast. I was worried sick one uncle or the other was going to set you off at some point, but you were Patience Incarnate. Thanks for helping show our extended family a good time!

**中文**

我们都为你感到自豪，乖狗狗！你在宴会上的表现真不错。我本来很担心某个脾气古怪的叔叔可能会惹你大发雷霆，但你简直就是耐心的化身。谢谢你让我们新的大家庭度过了一段美好的时光！

## `ZagreusHome_3555`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Have to admit, this style really suits me, right?

**中文**

不得不承认，这种风格和我还真挺搭的，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3556`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I rather like this style, so let's go ahead and make it official.

**中文**

我很喜欢这种风格，咱们来正式确认吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3557`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This style reminds me of the surface. Good motivating factor!

**中文**

这样的风格让我想起了地上世界，很有助于激发斗志！

## `ZagreusHome_3558`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This style's a bit formal for my tastes, but I can get used to it!

**中文**

在我看来，这种风格有点太正式了，不过我可以努力适应！

## `ZagreusHome_3559`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'd like to think Master Chaos would approve of this style.

**中文**

我觉得卡俄斯君主应该会很喜欢这种风格。

## `ZagreusHome_3560`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Soon as I saw this lovely style, I knew I had to have it!

**中文**

我一看到这种可爱的风格，就知道一定要得到它。

## `ZagreusHome_3561`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What's that, there, something from the Fates?

**中文**

那是什么？是命运女神给我的礼物吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3562`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey that sounds handy, there...

**中文**

嘿，听起来很方便嘛……

## `ZagreusHome_3563`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The full might of Holy Excalibur...

**中文**

王者之圣剑的完全威力……

## `ZagreusHome_3564`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The sheer bloodthirst of the Frost Fair Blade...

**中文**

冷艳锯对鲜血纯粹的渴望……

## `ZagreusHome_3565`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The true strength of Naegling's Board...

**中文**

奈格灵之盾真正的力量……

## `ZagreusHome_3566`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The pure essence of Celestial Sharanga...

**中文**

天神之弓沙兰迦纯粹的本质……

## `ZagreusHome_3567`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The raw savagery of the Claws of Enkidu...

**中文**

恩奇都之爪的残暴本性……

## `ZagreusHome_3568`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The infernal fury of Igneus Eden...

**中文**

伊甸之火的熊熊烈焰……

## `ZagreusHome_3569`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You call my chambers a mess, look at your desk, Father! Whatever happened to your court musician, huh, where's his sentencing parchmentwork? Stuck in Tartarus, all because he stopped singing for you on command?

**中文**

你说我的房间像个猪圈，那你看看你自己的桌子吧，父亲！你知不知道你的宫廷乐师遇到了什么事情，嗯？他的判决书在什么地方？他被困在了塔耳塔罗斯，这都是因为他不愿听令为你唱歌，你知道吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3570`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sure, happy to oblige. I'll just ask the House Contractor since you're too busy.

**中文**

何乐而不为呢。既然你这么忙，我会先问问大殿里的承包商。

## `ZagreusHome_3571`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Contractor, mind locating the Court Musician's Sentence when you get the chance?

**中文**

承包商，你有空的话，可以帮我看看宫廷乐师的判决书在哪里吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3572`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Orpheus, you're back! Look, don't get on Father's bad side like that, and you're going to be fine. I've fought with him enough for all our sakes.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯，你回来了！听着，只要别惹父亲发火，你就不会有事的。为了我们的利益，我已经和他做过不少斗争了。

## `ZagreusHome_3573`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

In the name of Hades, I commute our court musician's sentencing, effective now!

**中文**

以哈迪斯的名义，我为我们的宫廷乐师减刑，立刻生效！

## `ZagreusHome_3574`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Poke.

**中文**

我碰。

## `ZagreusHome_3575`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Poke!

**中文**

我碰！

## `ZagreusHome_3576`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I paid for it, I can mess with it.

**中文**

我花钱买来的，我想怎么弄都行。

## `ZagreusHome_3577`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Greetings, Costly Artwork.

**中文**

你好啊，价格不菲的艺术品。

## `ZagreusHome_3578`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hello, Decorative Object.

**中文**

你好啊，装饰品。

## `ZagreusHome_3579`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just giving you a nudge.

**中文**

只是想推你一下。

## `ZagreusHome_3580`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Interesting texture.

**中文**

很有趣的纹理。

## `ZagreusHome_3581`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Still fancy.

**中文**

真不错。

## `ZagreusHome_3582`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No way.

**中文**

不可能。

## `ZagreusHome_3583`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Uh, no.

**中文**

呃，不行。

## `ZagreusHome_3584`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not sitting on that.

**中文**

我可不想坐在那上面。

## `ZagreusHome_3585`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll stand, thanks.

**中文**

我还是站着吧，多谢了。

## `ZagreusHome_3586`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No thanks.

**中文**

不了，谢谢。

## `ZagreusHome_3587`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No way whatsoever.

**中文**

想都不要想。

## `ZagreusHome_3588`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Tartarus is gloomy enough without Orpheus in it.

**中文**

少了俄耳甫斯，塔耳塔罗斯变得相当阴郁。

## `ZagreusHome_3589`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Still can't entirely believe Mother's come home....

**中文**

我还是不敢相信，母亲已经回来了……

## `ZagreusHome_3590`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We're one big happy family. No issues whatsoever!

**中文**

我们是个幸福的大家庭，永远都不会有问题！

## `ZagreusHome_3591`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The Claws of Enkidu helped me scrape by to the top.

**中文**

恩奇都之爪帮助我勉强来到了顶端。

## `ZagreusHome_3592`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The greatest Boons of the Olympians... what an honor.

**中文**

奥林匹斯众神最伟大的祝福……何等荣耀啊。

## `ZagreusHome_3593`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Bye, Mother.

**中文**

再见，母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3594`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Take care, Mother.

**中文**

多保重，母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3595`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Love you, Mother.

**中文**

我爱你，母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3596`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Let me know if you need anything.

**中文**

如果需要什么，告诉我就好。

## `ZagreusHome_3597`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good to see you, Mother.

**中文**

见到你很高兴，母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3598`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Come back soon, Mother.

**中文**

早点回来吧，母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3599`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Take care, OK?

**中文**

多保重，好吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3600`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Safe travels, Mother.

**中文**

祝你一路平安，母亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3601`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We'll take care of things while you're away.

**中文**

你不在的时候，我们会打理好一切的。

## `ZagreusHome_3602`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mother, you're back!

**中文**

母亲，你回来啦！

## `ZagreusHome_3603`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mother!

**中文**

母亲！

## `ZagreusHome_3604`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Glad you're home.

**中文**

很高兴你回家了。

## `ZagreusHome_3605`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good to have you back.

**中文**

你回来了真好。

## `ZagreusHome_3606`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

We all missed you.

**中文**

我们都想你了。

## `ZagreusHome_3607`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sigh

**中文**

叹气

## `ZagreusHome_3608`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sigh

**中文**

叹气

## `ZagreusHome_3609`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Can't believe Father kept this place locked up...

**中文**

真不敢相信，父亲一直都把这个地方锁起来……

## `ZagreusHome_3610`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sigh It's nice here...

**中文**

叹气 这里真不错……

## `ZagreusHome_3611`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Pom trees are coming in nicely.

**中文**

石榴树可真不错。

## `ZagreusHome_3612`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mother's taken good care of this place since she got back.

**中文**

母亲回来之后，她一直把这里打理得很好。

## `ZagreusHome_3613`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Peace and quiet...

**中文**

安静，祥和……

## `ZagreusHome_3614`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I like it here.

**中文**

我喜欢这个地方。

## `ZagreusHome_3615`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I believe I'm eligible for the rank of Warden, Resources Director?

**中文**

我相信我已经有资格担任典狱长的位置了，资源总监，是吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3616`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Make me a Fixer, Resources Director, I'm ready.

**中文**

把我升级为调停者吧，资源总监，我准备好了。

## `ZagreusHome_3617`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right, Resources Director, I'm ready to be an Agent.

**中文**

好了，资源总监，我已经准备好成为代理人了。

## `ZagreusHome_3618`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Need a new Cleaner, Resources Director? Because I'm ready.

**中文**

需要一位新的清道夫吗，资源总监？因为我已经准备好了。

## `ZagreusHome_3619`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

To the realm, Resources Director! Thus I become a Shadow.

**中文**

为了冥界，资源总监！让我成为暗影使者吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3620`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

With this donation, I accept the honorable rank of Dusk.

**中文**

捐了这笔资源之后，我愿意接受黄昏使者这一荣耀称号。

## `ZagreusHome_3621`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Resources Director, it's my humble privilege to achieve the rank of Wraith.

**中文**

资源总监，我很乐意接受魅影使者这一职级。

## `ZagreusHome_3622`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I scarce believe it but I'm eligible now to become Overseer.

**中文**

虽然不敢相信，但我已经足以胜任主管一职了。

## `ZagreusHome_3623`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This is for the realm, Resources Director. Thus I become a Specter.

**中文**

这是为了冥界，资源总监，让我成为幽魂使者吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3624`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The rarest rank achievable is mine... the rank of One.

**中文**

我达到了这最为杰出的等级……塑形者。

## `ZagreusHome_3625`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Somehow I've done it... the final rank... Unseen One... like Father.

**中文**

我做到了……最终等级……无形者……就像我的父亲一样。

## `ZagreusHome_3626`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Director, I believe I'm eligible for the next tier.

**中文**

总监，我相信我已经可以升到下一级了。

## `ZagreusHome_3627`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Badge me, Resources Director.

**中文**

把我的徽章给我吧，资源总监。

## `ZagreusHome_3628`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

For the realm!

**中文**

为了冥界！

## `ZagreusHome_3629`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here, you like resources, right?

**中文**

来，你喜欢资源，对不对？

## `ZagreusHome_3630`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Next tier, my good shade!

**中文**

升到下一级吧，我的好幽灵！

## `ZagreusHome_3631`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Next tier, if you please!

**中文**

下一级，拜托了！

## `ZagreusHome_3632`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I do believe I'm eligible now.

**中文**

我相信我已经能胜任了。

## `ZagreusHome_3633`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here you go, Director!

**中文**

来吧，总监！

## `ZagreusHome_3634`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My badge is due for an upgrade I think!

**中文**

我觉得，我的徽章可以升个级了！

## `ZagreusHome_3635`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Spruce up my badge, please!

**中文**

给我的徽章升级吧，拜托了！

## `ZagreusHome_3636`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Here, in the name of Hades.

**中文**

来吧，以哈迪斯之名。

## `ZagreusHome_3637`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Got a donation here.

**中文**

我要捐东西了。

## `ZagreusHome_3638`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Ready to badge me, Director?

**中文**

准备好给我戴上徽章了吗，总监？

## `ZagreusHome_3639`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

My badge, please, Resources Director?

**中文**

我的徽章，拜托了，资源总监？

## `ZagreusHome_3640`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Due for another upgrade here!

**中文**

可以再升一级了！

## `ZagreusHome_3641`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Not qualified yet, huh.

**中文**

还不够格，嗯。

## `ZagreusHome_3642`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm not there yet.

**中文**

还不行。

## `ZagreusHome_3643`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

In due time, I guess.

**中文**

我猜得再过一阵子。

## `ZagreusHome_3644`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No rush, I'll get there.

**中文**

不要急，我会升上去的。

## `ZagreusHome_3645`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I need how much...?

**中文**

还需要多少……？

## `ZagreusHome_3646`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Oof, that's steep.

**中文**

哎呀，这可太多了。

## `ZagreusHome_3647`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'll have to come back.

**中文**

我得下次再来了。

## `ZagreusHome_3648`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

You drive a hard bargain, Resources Director.

**中文**

你也太会砍价了，资源总监。

## `ZagreusHome_3649`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Can't you give me a better deal?

**中文**

就不能给我更优惠的价格吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3650`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

What if I ask really nicely, Resources Director?

**中文**

如果我好言相求呢，资源总监？

## `ZagreusHome_3651`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can still rank up within my heart, I guess.

**中文**

我觉得，我还可以在心里继续升级。

## `ZagreusHome_3652`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm at the highest rank achievable!

**中文**

我已经升到了最高等级！

## `ZagreusHome_3653`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Suppose I'm overdue to take a break...

**中文**

我猜我早该休息休息了……

## `ZagreusHome_3654`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Keeping busy, Resources Director?

**中文**

你挺忙的吧，资源总监？

## `ZagreusHome_3655`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just checking in, Resources Director.

**中文**

我只是打个卡，资源总监。

## `ZagreusHome_3656`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey, Resources Director.

**中文**

嘿，资源总监。

## `ZagreusHome_3657`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I can take on Father now using Extreme Measures... if I so dare.

**中文**

我可以在“极端措施”条款下和父亲交手了……如果我有这个胆量的话。

## `ZagreusHome_3658`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sign me up, Contractor. This ought to pay for itself in no time at all!

**中文**

签字吧，承包商，很快就能得到回报的！

## `ZagreusHome_3659`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Father... I know we can't exactly change the past, but we can try to move forward. Look, I know I'm not even supposed to have this stuff, but everyone likes it, won't you try it, at least? Here, take it, either way.

**中文**

呃，父亲……我知道过去的事情无法改变，但我们可以试着向前看。我想说，这东西我是不该有的，不过所有人都很喜欢，你要不要也试着尝尝？反正我先给你了。

## `ZagreusHome_3660`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

More Nectar for the old disposal service, Father, if you don't mind. Come on, you must have tried the stuff, at least in the days before you were here! Didn't you ever enjoy yourself, at any point?

**中文**

父亲，如果你不介意的话，帮忙把这些蜜露也处理掉吧。来嘛，你肯定已经尝过这种蜜酒了，至少在你来这里之前肯定尝过！在那段时间里，你一次都没好好享受过美酒吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3661`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Well, with that attitude...!

**中文**

嗯，既然这样的话……！

## `ZagreusHome_3662`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I can't imagine what I'd do without your blessing, Father! But, thank you again. I don't often inherit Fate-appointed honors, and will take good care of it. Though... what if the Olympians catch on? Wouldn't do for everybody knowing that you're aiding me even a little, right?

**中文**

真不敢想象没了你的庇佑我能做些什么，父亲！好吧，我仍要谢谢你。继承命中注定的荣誉并不常见，我会珍惜它的。但……要是被奥林匹斯众神知道了怎么办？是不是不能让所有人都知道你帮了我一点小忙？

## `ZagreusHome_3663`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...Fear is for the weak?

**中文**

……恐惧是弱者的专利？

## `ZagreusHome_3664`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So, what happened, when Mother first arrived, if I may ask? Some of your residents seem to remember the Queen from before. Yet the Olympians just never found out?

**中文**

我斗胆问一下，母亲第一次来冥界时发生了什么事？这里的一些居民好像记得冥后的往事。奥林匹斯众神莫非从来没弄清楚过？

## `ZagreusHome_3665`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right, all right.

**中文**

好吧，好吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3666`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father, all these shades... aren't they going to let on that Mother's back? What if word spreads... and the Olympians find out?

**中文**

父亲，这些幽灵……他们会不会泄露母亲回来的消息？要是流言传出……奥林匹斯众神知道了该怎么办？

## `ZagreusHome_3667`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sounds like you've got it all under control.

**中文**

听起来一切都已经在你的掌控之中了。

## `ZagreusHome_3668`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So, now that the festivities are over with, it's back to work like this, the same as ever?

**中文**

所以说，既然现在庆典已经结束，是不是要像平时那样继续工作了？

## `ZagreusHome_3669`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're responding to well-wishes? Aw. Don't you think some of your subjects are sincerely happy for you?

**中文**

你在应付那些祝福？哦…你难道不觉得你的一些属下其实是真心为你高兴吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3670`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yes, that should definitely come in handy, sir! I'm still getting a handle on everything I can do, with all the Olympians' powers fused with mine.

**中文**

好啊先生，那样肯定会非常便利！如今奥林匹斯众神的力量与我的力量融为一体，我还在试着驾驭我的能力。

## `ZagreusHome_3671`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

No. Can't go this way just yet...

**中文**

不行，现在还去不了……

## `ZagreusHome_3672`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Uncle Zeus and the others got us this lovely gift.

**中文**

宙斯叔叔和其他众神送给我们的可爱礼物。

## `ZagreusHome_3673`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's Mount Olympus...

**中文**

是奥林匹斯山……

## `ZagreusHome_3674`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Olympus must be bigger in real life.

**中文**

真正的奥林匹斯山，应该比这个要大得多。

## `ZagreusHome_3675`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Sure must be cold up there.

**中文**

山上肯定也很冷吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3676`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

A little slice of Olympus.

**中文**

奥林匹斯山的模型。

## `ZagreusHome_3677`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Made of real Olympian stone.

**中文**

用真正的奥林匹斯山石制成。

## `ZagreusHome_3678`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Guess I made it to Olympus after all.

**中文**

这样我也算是成功到达了奥林匹斯山。

## `ZagreusHome_3679`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Wonder what the real Olympus is like.

**中文**

真想看看真正的奥林匹斯山是什么样子。

## `ZagreusHome_3680`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

So detailed...

**中文**

真是精细……

## `ZagreusHome_3681`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Glad Father decided to keep this.

**中文**

真高兴父亲把这个留下来了。

## `ZagreusHome_3682`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Think I'm going to enjoy listening to this piece.

**中文**

这一首我应该会很爱听。

## `ZagreusHome_3683`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Think I'm going to enjoy listening to this piece, potentially over and over.

**中文**

这一首我应该会很爱听，甚至会单曲循环。

## `ZagreusHome_3684`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Always room for some new music in my life.

**中文**

新的乐曲越多越好啊。

## `ZagreusHome_3685`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Diamonds for a piece of music seems like a fair exchange!

**中文**

用钻石换音乐非常值当！

## `ZagreusHome_3686`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Time to load up the old music stand.

**中文**

该把旧乐谱架拿出来了。

## `ZagreusHome_3687`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Excited to give this one a listen.

**中文**

等不及要听一听这首了。

## `ZagreusHome_3688`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Still can't get over our gift from the Olympians. It's glorious!

**中文**

还是忘不了奥林匹斯众神的礼物，真是太壮观了！

## `ZagreusHome_3689`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Father changed the blasted lock already?

**中文**

父亲什么时候把这破锁给换了？

## `ZagreusHome_3690`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This is my home, Meg. I think I have a better understanding of that, now. Besides, I think that Father's going to let me have a bit more space.

**中文**

这是我的家，墨纪。我觉得我现在更理解它的含义了。另外，我觉得我父亲会让我有更大的空间。

## `ZagreusHome_3691`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

If by 'get back to work' you mean escape the Underworld again, then yes. I think I'm getting pretty good at it.

**中文**

如果“回去工作”指的是再次逃出冥界的话，那没错。我干这个可熟练了。

## `ZagreusHome_3692`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's not for him, I can assure you that.

**中文**

我可不是为了他才努力的，我可以向你保证。

## `ZagreusHome_3693`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Mother... she's doing well, I think. She's in good spirits every time we meet. Happy to let her know you're thinking of her, by the way!

**中文**

母亲……她一切都好。每次见面的时候，她看起来都很有精神。另外，我很乐于告诉她，说你还在惦记着她！

## `ZagreusHome_3694`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I think that she can be the judge of that.

**中文**

我觉得她可以自行判断。

## `ZagreusHome_3695`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Don't worry, Meg. I eventually managed to get home the painful way after all. Though... I managed to get much farther than you'd probably expect.

**中文**

没事，墨纪，反正后来我又惨死回家了。不过……我去的地方，恐怕比你想象得要远多了。

## `ZagreusHome_3696`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Bet you're happy to see Mother home again, huh, boy? Only wish you could have told me about her before.

**中文**

我猜你再次见到母亲，一定很开心吧，乖狗狗？要是你以前能告诉我和她有关的事情就好了。

## `ZagreusHome_3697`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey!

**中文**

嘿！

## `ZagreusHome_3698`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

See you out there, Father.

**中文**

外头见，父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3699`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Father.

**中文**

父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3700`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Take care, Father.

**中文**

多保重，父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3701`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

See you up top.

**中文**

上头见。

## `ZagreusHome_3702`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Nice chatting.

**中文**

和你聊天很愉快。

## `ZagreusHome_3703`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Good talk.

**中文**

聊得很开心。

## `ZagreusHome_3704`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

All right, then!

**中文**

好吧！

## `ZagreusHome_3705`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Thank you, Father.

**中文**

谢谢你，父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3706`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So tell me, Father. Just how much did it hurt your soul knowing my face is up there on the Featured Houseservant Board, for all in the lounge to see?

**中文**

父亲，我想问问：你看，我当上了优秀圣殿仆从，整个休息室都看着我的大头照，这事你难不难受？

## `ZagreusHome_3707`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Blood and darkness... all my life I thought that board was a joke. But seeing my face up there... it felt good. Thanks, Father. Maybe it won't be the last time.

**中文**

鲜血与黑暗啊……我一直以为那个展板不过是个笑话罢了。可看到我自己的照片挂在上面……感觉还挺不错的。谢了，父亲，或许我还会再上榜呢。

## `ZagreusHome_3708`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Why not!

**中文**

当然了！

## `ZagreusHome_3709`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

We're fortunate Uncle Zeus seemed to catch on right away, and went along with it. But, this whole elaborate tale you spun... I remember how hurt I felt when I found out Father lied to me about you. Now we're lying to Olympus. What if they find out? Even if they don't... I don't like it.

**中文**

我们很幸运，宙斯叔叔似乎马上就明白了我们的意图，配合我们一起圆谎。可这个故事是你精心编造出来的……我还记得，关于你的事情，当我发现父亲对我撒谎的时候，我当时有多伤心。现在我们又欺骗了奥林匹斯的众神。如果他们发现了呢？就算他们没发现……我也不喜欢这样。

## `ZagreusHome_3710`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're saying they all knew you weren't being perfectly honest, and went along with it anyway? I guess the pomegranate thing in particular was a bit of a stretch.

**中文**

你是说，他们明知道你没把所有的真相都说出来，但也还是接受了，是吗？我猜石榴那部分确实有点太牵强了。

## `ZagreusHome_3711`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You don't have hard feelings? What about Father? What about Demeter?

**中文**

你不觉得难受吗？那父亲的事情呢？得墨忒尔的事情呢？

## `ZagreusHome_3712`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

And Uncle Zeus is glad things didn't turn out worse for him. I get the sense he acts a bit impulsively at times, and deals with the consequences later.

**中文**

而且宙斯叔叔也很高兴，因为这一切并没有把他推到风口浪尖。我明白了，他有时会凭冲动做事，之后再去处理后果。

## `ZagreusHome_3713`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yeah. I know what you mean. Thanks, Mother. Think I'll be heading out again soon. See you when I get back?

**中文**

是啊，我明白你的意思。谢谢你，母亲。我觉得我马上就得再次出发了，等我回来之后再见吧？

## `ZagreusHome_3714`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father... all this stuff with the Olympians... will you just tell me what's going on, for once? How did Mother come to join you here? What's your problem with Uncle Zeus?

**中文**

父亲……和奥林匹斯众神的那些事情……你就不能告诉我发生了什么事情吗，哪怕一次也好？母亲怎么会和你来了这里？你和宙斯叔叔之间有什么过节？

## `ZagreusHome_3715`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

All right, all right, I swear. I swear, in your own name, I'll keep what you're about to say between us.... So?

**中文**

好了，好了，我发誓，我发誓就是了。以你本人之名，你说的事情我绝不外传……好了，所以呢？

## `ZagreusHome_3716`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You weren't expecting a gift like that. But she didn't want to return to Olympus, and you liked her, so you didn't send her away. And you're angry with Zeus for putting you in that position? Cut ties with him?

**中文**

你当时没想到会收到这样的礼物。不过她确实不想再回奥林匹斯山，你也很喜欢她，于是你并没有把她赶走。你是在怨恨宙斯把你置于这样的境地？于是就和他一刀两断了？

## `ZagreusHome_3717`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

And now if Demeter and the others found all this out, you and Mother... even Zeus would be in a bad spot, wouldn't he? He must have a temper on him to match yours.

**中文**

要是得墨忒尔和其他众神发现了真相，你和母亲……就连宙斯都会摊上麻烦，没错吧？他的脾气肯定和你的脾气差不多。

## `ZagreusHome_3718`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I will. Take care, Father.

**中文**

我会的。多保重，父亲。

## `ZagreusHome_3719`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

My father's fate? But he was smugly waiting for me up there, and seemed quite healthy, all in all! Though... maybe the farther we get from this realm... the worse we get. Because he cast his lot... inheriting the Underworld?

**中文**

我父亲的命运？可我每一次到那里的时候，他都会洋洋得意地等着我，而且他看起来都一点事都没有！不过……或许我们离开冥界越远……状况就会变得越差。因为他抽到的签……就是掌管冥界，是吗？

## `ZagreusHome_3720`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Still waiting for the festive occasion when we'll open one of these.

**中文**

等过节的时候，我们就会打开其中一个。

## `ZagreusHome_3721`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They fought bravely, but Lord Dionysus was too strong.

**中文**

他们很勇猛，但是迪奥尼索司大人更厉害。

## `ZagreusHome_3722`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Should check the garden.

**中文**

去看看花园吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3723`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The garden's always open now...

**中文**

现在花园已经常开了……

## `ZagreusHome_3724`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

After all this time, it's only been a few days! But it's been good. Mother and Father are back together, made some new friends, got this fancy painting! Easy. All is well in the Underworld!

**中文**

终于尘埃落定了，这不过是短短几天的事情！不过一切都很好。母亲和父亲重新团聚，我结识了一些新朋友，还弄到了这幅漂亮的画！真简单，在冥界一切都是那么美好！

## `ZagreusHome_3725`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

They say this one's the hottest style this side of Elysium.

**中文**

他们说这是至福乐土那边最流行的风格。

## `ZagreusHome_3726`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

This style seems appropriately grim for the job, I think.

**中文**

我觉得，这种阴沉的风格和工作的内容很搭。

## `ZagreusHome_3727`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm working on your pact, Achilles. Just need a little more time to sort it out.

**中文**

我还在忙你的契约的事情，阿喀琉斯。只要再给我点时间，我就能把它搞定。

## `ZagreusHome_3728`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The handiwork of Lord Hephaestus, himself! Sounds like he's been rather busy.

**中文**

竟然是赫菲斯托斯大人亲手所做！听上去感觉他最近很忙。

## `ZagreusHome_3729`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Father keeps things orderly I guess...

**中文**

父亲大人还挺会整理东西的……

## `ZagreusHome_3730`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

The master chambers...

**中文**

冥府主人的房间……

## `ZagreusHome_3731`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Mother didn't change the decor much.

**中文**

母亲没太改动这些装饰。

## `ZagreusHome_3732`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Resources Director!

**中文**

资源总监!

## `ZagreusHome_3733`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hello, Director.

**中文**

你好，总监。

## `ZagreusHome_3734`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Just me, Director.

**中文**

是我，总监。

## `ZagreusHome_3735`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Director.

**中文**

总监。

## `ZagreusHome_3736`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Director?

**中文**

总监？

## `ZagreusHome_3738`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx, you've known Megaera practically forever. Well, before my time at least, so could you... tell me more about what she was like, back then?

**中文**

倪克斯，你跟墨纪拉那么熟，至少肯定比我更熟。那你能不能告诉我……以前的她是什么样的？

## `ZagreusHome_3739`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's all right, Father. I'm not feeling very hungry at the moment.

**中文**

没事，父亲。我现在还不饿。

## `ZagreusHome_3740`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Took some figuring out, but so does any decent plan, I guess.

**中文**

花了点时间琢磨，但好事多磨吧。

## `ZagreusHome_3741`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Remember that Skelly character out in the courtyard, sir? Turns out he's been working with Charon all this time. Working for him more like! I had so much help in this, from all of you.

**中文**

先生，您记得在庭院里的那位骨头吗？原来他一直以来都是卡戎的同事！卡戎的手下，才对。您们给了我的帮助真是多呢。

## `ZagreusHome_3742`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

He won't tell me. He was either a venerable sea captain, or a petty thief. Maybe both?

**中文**

他不肯告诉我。他一就是个伟大的船长，不然就是个小偷。或许两者都对？

## `ZagreusHome_3743`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey Room, I'm back.

**中文**

嘿，房间，我回来了。

## `ZagreusHome_3744`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Greetings, Room.

**中文**

你好呀，房间。

## `ZagreusHome_3745`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Hey Room.

**中文**

嘿，房间。

## `ZagreusHome_3746`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Whew.

**中文**

哟。

## `ZagreusHome_3747`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I'm back, Room.

**中文**

我回来了，房间。

## `ZagreusHome_3748`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

I missed you, Room.

**中文**

我真想你，房间。

## `ZagreusHome_3749`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

Miss me, Room?

**中文**

小房间，你想我了没？

## `ZagreusHome_3750`

**Speaker:** ZagreusHome

**EN**

It's me, Room.

**中文**

我又回来了，小房间。
